      (olds.length ? '<details class="w199old"><summary>' + olds.length + (olds.length === 1 ? ' older version' : ' older versions') + '</summary><ul class="w199files">' + olds.map(function (f) { return '<li><a href="' + E(f.url || '#') + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(f.name) + '</a><small>' + E(day(f.modified_time)) + '</small></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></details>' : '') +
      '<label>In Applications <small>' + apps.length + '</small></label>' + (apps.length ? '<ul class="w199files">' + apps.map(function (c) { return '<li>' + btn(E(c.name || 'Someone'), 'data-w197c="' + c.id + '"', 'sm') + gradePill(c) + '<small>' + E(c.status || 'new') + (c.owner ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(c.owner) : '') + '</small></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0">Nobody yet. A CV in this position\'s Resumes folder, an upload below, or the position chosen on an application puts them here.</p>') +
      '<label>CVs in Drive <small>' + cvs.length + '</small></label>' + (cvs.length ? '<ul class="w199files">' + cvs.map(fileRow).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0">The Resumes folder is empty.</p>') +
      '<div class="w199upbox"><input type="text" id="w199upname" placeholder="Their name (optional)"><label class="w197b sm pri w199up">Upload a CV<input type="file" id="w199upf" accept=".pdf,.doc,.docx,.odt,.rtf,.txt,.png,.jpg,.jpeg" hidden></label>' + (p.resumes_id ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/' + E(p.resumes_id) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Resumes folder</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<label>People in this role</label>' + (ppl.length ? '<ul class="w199files">' + ppl.map(function (x) { return '<li><b>' + E(x.m.name) + '</b><small>' + (x.past ? (x.until ? 'until ' + E(day(x.until)) : 'earlier') + (x.since ? ', from ' + E(day(x.since)) : '') : (x.since ? 'since ' + E(day(x.since)) : 'now')) + (x.note ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(x.note) : '') + '</small>' + (x.id && !x.past ? btn('Moved on', 'data-w199end="' + x.id + '"', 'sm') : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0">Nobody recorded.</p>') +
      '<div class="w199upbox"><select id="w199who"><option value="">Add a person\u2026</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + m.id + '">' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select><input type="date" id="w199since" aria-label="Since">' + btn('Add', 'data-w199a="addperson"', 'sm') + '</div>' +
      '<label for="w199own">Handled by</label><select id="w199own" data-w199p="owner"><option value="">Nobody yet</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option' + (p.owner === m.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label for="w199need">Needed by</label><input type="date" id="w199need" data-w199p="needed_by" value="' + E(p.needed_by || '') + '">' +
      '<label for="w199notes">Notes</label><textarea id="w199notes" data-w199p="notes" placeholder="Where it is posted, the interview plan">' + E(p.notes || '') + '</textarea>' +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn('Save the notes', 'data-w199a="notes"', 'pri sm') + '<a class="w197b sm" href="https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/' + E(p.folder_id) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">The folder in Drive</a></div></div>';
    s.classList.add('open');
  }
  function newSheet() {
    P.open = null;
    var s = sheetEl();
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w199a="shut"></div><div class="pn w199pn">' + btn('Close', 'data-w199a="shut"', 'sm x') + '<h3>New position</h3>' +
      '<p class="role">Makes the position\'s folder in Drive (with its Resumes folder) under its department. Put the job description in the folder when it is written.</p>' +
      '<label for="w199nt">Title</label><input type="text" id="w199nt" placeholder="Volunteer Coordinator">' +
      '<label for="w199nd">Department</label><select id="w199nd">' + DEPTS.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d[0] + '">' + E(d[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Stage</label><div class="stg">' + PSTAGES.slice(0, 4).map(function (x, i) { return '<button type="button" data-w199ns="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (i === 0 ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn('Create the position', 'data-w199a="create"', 'pri') + '</div></div>';
    s.classList.add('open');
    var t = document.getElementById('w199nt'); if (t) try { t.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function shut() { var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (s) s.classList.remove('open'); P.open = null; }
  async function savePos(id, patch, msg) {
    try {
      patch.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); patch.updated_by = who();
      var r = await db.from('hub_positions').update(patch).eq('id', id); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var p = byId(id); if (p) Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { p[k] = patch[k]; });
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'position', entity_id: String(id), field: Object.keys(patch).filter(function (k) { return k !== 'updated_at' && k !== 'updated_by'; })[0] || 'update', old_value: null, new_value: String(patch.stage || patch.owner || patch.needed_by || (patch.notes ? 'notes' : '')), changed_by: who() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      if (msg) toastS(msg); return true;
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (e.message || e)); return false; }
  }
  function readFile(file) { return new Promise(function (ok, bad) { var r = new FileReader(); r.onload = function () { ok(String(r.result).replace(/^data:[^,]*,/, '')); }; r.onerror = bad; r.readAsDataURL(file); }); }
  async function upload(file, posId, candId, person) {
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) { toastS('That file is larger than 15 MB'); return; }
    toastS('Uploading ' + file.name + '\u2026');
    try {
      var b64 = await readFile(file);
      var r = await fn({ action: 'upload', position_id: posId, candidate_id: candId || null, add: !candId, person: person || null, name: file.name, mime: file.type || '', content_b64: b64 });
      toastS(candId ? 'CV saved in Drive' : 'CV saved in Drive and added to Applications');
      await load(true); if (window.w197) { await w197.loadCands(true); try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      if (candId && window.w197) w197.openSheet(candId); else if (P.open) openPos(P.open);
      if (onPage()) paint();
      return r;
    } catch (e) { toastS(String(e.message || e)); }
  }
  async function addFromFile(fileId) {
    var f = P.files.filter(function (x) { return x.file_id === fileId; })[0]; if (!f) return;
    var p = byId(f.position_id); if (!p) return;
    var name = String(f.name || 'Someone').replace(/\.[a-z0-9]{2,5}$/i, '').replace(/\b(cv|resume|curriculum vitae)\b/gi, ' ').replace(/[_()]+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/[-.,'\u2019s ]+$/, '').trim();
    var m = name.match(/^([A-Z' -]{2,})\s*[,.]\s*(.+)$/); if (m) name = m[2].trim() + ' ' + m[1].trim().toLowerCase().replace(/(^|[ '-])\S/g, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); });
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').insert({ name: name.slice(0, 120) || 'Someone', role: p.title, position_id: p.id, source: 'drive', status: 'new', stage_at: new Date().toISOString(), drive_file_id: f.file_id, links: f.url ? [f.url] : [], updated_by: who() }).select('id').single();
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      await db.from('hub_position_files').update({ candidate_id: r.data.id }).eq('id', f.id);
      f.candidate_id = r.data.id; toastS(name + ' added to Applications (New)');
      if (window.w197) await w197.loadCands(true);
      if (P.open) openPos(P.open); if (onPage()) paint();
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not add: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  /* in select mode a card click selects it instead of opening it (capture: before wave197's handler) */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (!P.selMode) return; var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var cd = t.closest('#w197board .w197cand, .w197closed .w197cand'); if (!cd) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    var id = cd.getAttribute('data-w197c'); P.sel[id] = !P.sel[id]; try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } bulkBar();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) { if (P.selMode && e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w197board .w197cand')) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); } }, true);
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (!/^#hrcentre\/applications/.test(String(location.hash || ''))) { P.selMode = false; P.sel = {}; } bulkBar(); });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var o = t.closest('[data-w199open]'); if (o) { e.preventDefault(); if (t.closest('#w197sheet')) { try { if (window.w197) w197.state().open = null; } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } load().then(function () { openPos(o.getAttribute('data-w199open')); }); return; }
    var st = t.closest('#w197sheet [data-w199st]'); if (st && P.open) { var k = st.getAttribute('data-w199st'); savePos(P.open, { stage: k }, stageLabel(k)).then(function () { openPos(P.open); if (onPage()) paint(); }); return; }
    var ns = t.closest('#w197sheet [data-w199ns]'); if (ns) { Array.prototype.forEach.call(ns.parentNode.children, function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', b === ns); }); return; }
    var cl = t.closest('[data-w199call]'); if (cl) { logCall(cl.getAttribute('data-w199call'), cl.getAttribute('data-o')); return; }
    var gr = t.closest('[data-w199grade]'); if (gr) { setGrade(gr.getAttribute('data-w199grade'), Number(gr.getAttribute('data-n'))); return; }
    var ad = t.closest('[data-w199add]'); if (ad) { addFromFile(ad.getAttribute('data-w199add')); return; }
    var en = t.closest('[data-w199end]'); if (en) { (async function () { await db.from('hub_position_people').update({ until: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) }).eq('id', en.getAttribute('data-w199end')); await load(true); if (P.open) openPos(P.open); toastS('Noted'); })(); return; }
    var a = t.closest('[data-w199a]'); if (!a) return;
    var k2 = a.getAttribute('data-w199a');
    if (k2 === 'selon') { setSelMode(true); return; }
    if (k2 === 'seloff') { setSelMode(false); return; }
    if (k2 === 'selnone') { P.sel = {}; try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } bulkBar(); return; }
    if (k2 === 'selall') { Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w197board .w197cand'), function (x) { P.sel[x.getAttribute('data-w197c')] = true; }); try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } bulkBar(); return; }
    if (k2 === 'shut') shut();
    else if (k2 === 'hiring' || k2 === 'all') { P.all = k2 === 'all'; paint(); }
    else if (k2 === 'unsorted') { e.preventDefault(); P.showUnsorted = !P.showUnsorted; paint(); }
    else if (k2 === 'new') newSheet();
    else if (k2 === 'sync') { P.busy = true; paint(); fn({ action: 'sync' }).then(function (r) { toastS('Drive read: ' + (r.positions || 0) + ' positions' + (r.added && r.added.length ? ', ' + r.added.length + ' new CVs added to Applications' : '') + (r.tidied && r.tidied.length ? ', ' + r.tidied.length + ' older JDs filed' : '')); return load(true); }).catch(function (x) { toastS('Could not read Drive: ' + (x.message || x)); }).then(function () { P.busy = false; if (onPage()) paint(); }); }
    else if (k2 === 'notes' && P.open) { var ta = document.getElementById('w199notes'); if (ta) savePos(P.open, { notes: ta.value.slice(0, 4000) }, 'Notes saved'); }
    else if (k2 === 'addperson' && P.open) {
      var sel = document.getElementById('w199who'), since = document.getElementById('w199since'); if (!sel || !sel.value) { toastS('Choose a person'); return; }
      (async function () {
        var r = await db.from('hub_position_people').insert({ position_id: P.open, member_id: Number(sel.value), since: since && since.value || null, created_by: who() });
        if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not add: ' + r.error.message); return; }
        await db.from('team_members').update({ position_id: P.open }).eq('id', Number(sel.value));
        try { var m = members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === sel.value; })[0]; if (m) m.position_id = P.open; } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
        await load(true); openPos(P.open); toastS('Added');
      })();
    }
    else if (k2 === 'create') {
      var ti = document.getElementById('w199nt'), dp = document.getElementById('w199nd'), on = document.querySelector('#w197sheet [data-w199ns].on');
      if (!ti || !ti.value.trim()) { toastS('A title, please'); return; }
      a.disabled = true; a.textContent = 'Creating\u2026';
      fn({ action: 'create_position', title: ti.value.trim(), dept: dp.value, stage: on ? on.getAttribute('data-w199ns') : 'requested', owner: myName() || null }).then(function (r) { toastS(r.position.title + ' created in Drive'); return load(true).then(function () { openPos(r.position.id); if (onPage()) paint(); }); }).catch(function (x) { toastS(String(x.message || x)); a.disabled = false; a.textContent = 'Create the position'; });
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t) return;
    if (t.id === 'w199dept') { P.dept = t.value; paint(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w199upf' && P.open) { var nm = document.getElementById('w199upname'); upload(t.files && t.files[0], P.open, null, nm && nm.value.trim()); return; }
    var uc = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w199upc'); if (uc) { var c = cands().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === uc; })[0]; if (c && c.position_id) upload(t.files && t.files[0], c.position_id, c.id); return; }
    var mv = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w199mv'); if (mv && t.value) { var to = t.value === '__unsorted' ? null : Number(t.value); t.disabled = true; fn({ action: 'move_file', file_id: mv, position_id: to }).then(function () { toastS('Moved'); return load(true); }).catch(function (x) { toastS(String(x.message || x)); }).then(function () { if (P.open) openPos(P.open); if (onPage()) paint(); }); return; }
    var pf = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w199p'); if (pf && P.open && pf !== 'notes') { var pt = {}; pt[pf] = t.value || null; savePos(P.open, pt, pf === 'owner' ? (t.value ? 'Handled by ' + t.value : 'Nobody handling it') : 'Saved'); return; }
    var bk = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w199bulk'); if (bk) { var v = t.value; t.value = ''; bulkApply(bk, v); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w199notcalled') { P.notCalled = t.checked; try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if (t.id === 'w199sortg') { P.sortGrade = t.checked; try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    var gn = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w199gnote');
    if (gn) {
      var mine = gradesOf(gn).filter(function (g) { return lower(g.grader_email) === myEmail(); })[0]; if (!mine || !mine.id) return;
      (async function () { var r = await db.from('hub_candidate_grades').update({ note: t.value.slice(0, 500) || null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', mine.id); if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + r.error.message); return; } mine.note = t.value.slice(0, 500) || null; toastS('Saved'); })();
      return;
    }
    var iv = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w199iv');
    if (iv) {
      var when = t.value ? new Date(t.value) : null; if (when && isNaN(when)) return;
      (async function () {
        var patch = { interview_at: when ? when.toISOString() : null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() };
        var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').update(patch).eq('id', Number(iv));
        if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + r.error.message); return; }
        var c = cands().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === iv; })[0]; if (c) Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = patch[k]; });
        try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: iv, field: 'interview_at', old_value: null, new_value: patch.interview_at || '', changed_by: who() }); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
        t.classList.remove('need'); var nt = document.getElementById('w199ivnote'); if (nt && when) nt.remove(); toastS(when ? 'Interview ' + ivText(when) : 'Interview time cleared'); try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      })();
      return;
    }
    var cf = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w199f');
    if (cf === 'position_id') {
      var st = window.w197 && w197.state(); if (!st || !st.open) return;
      var p = t.value ? byId(t.value) : null;
      (async function () {
        var patch = { position_id: p ? p.id : null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() }; if (p) patch.role = p.title;
        var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').update(patch).eq('id', st.open);
        if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not save: ' + r.error.message); return; }
        var c = cands().filter(function (x) { return x.id === st.open; })[0]; if (c) Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = patch[k]; });
        try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: String(st.open), field: 'position_id', old_value: null, new_value: p ? p.title : '', changed_by: who() }); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
        toastS(p ? 'Linked to ' + p.title : 'Unlinked'); try { w197.paintBoard(); w197.openSheet(st.open); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      })();
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.id === 'w199q') { P.q = t.value; paint(); } });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && P.open) shut(); });

  /* the page shows when its address does (wave197 draws the rail and the title) */
  var last = '';
  function tick() { var h = String(location.hash || ''); if (h === last) return; last = h; if (onPage()) setTimeout(show, 0); }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', tick);
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () { setTimeout(tick, 400); });
  setInterval(function () { if (onPage() && !document.getElementById('w199pos') && document.getElementById('w46body')) show(); }, 800);
  /* loaded early so Applications can show positions on its cards */
  setTimeout(function () { try { if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db) load(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 2500);

    '.w199old{margin:8px 0 0;font-size:13px;}.w199old summary{cursor:pointer;color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;}' +
    '.w199st{color:' + GOLD + ';font-weight:700;}.w199st.hi{color:' + GREEN + ';}.w199st.lo{color:' + FLAG + ';}' +
    '.w199grade{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;}.w199stars{display:inline-flex;gap:2px;}.w199star{border:none;background:none;cursor:pointer;font-size:26px;line-height:1;padding:2px;color:rgba(16,63,57,.22);}.w199star.on{color:' + GOLD + ';}.w199star:hover{transform:scale(1.12);}.w199grade small{color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '#w197sheet #w199gnote{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin-top:8px;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13.5px;padding:8px 12px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:12px;background:' + CREAM + ';}.w199glist li{gap:8px;}.w199sortg input{flex:none!important;min-width:0!important;padding:0!important;width:16px;height:16px;accent-color:' + GREEN + ';}.w199sortg{font-size:13px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w199sel{display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:18px;height:18px;border-radius:6px!important;border:2px solid rgba(16,63,57,.35);background:' + CREAM + '!important;color:' + CREAM + '!important;font-size:12px;padding:0!important;}.w199sel.on{background:' + GREEN + '!important;border-color:' + GREEN + ';}.w197cand:has(.w199sel.on){outline:2px solid ' + GREEN + ';outline-offset:1px;}' +
    '#w199bulk{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:18px;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:8500;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;background:' + CREAM + ';border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);box-shadow:0 18px 40px -18px rgba(16,63,57,.55);border-radius:18px;padding:10px 14px;width:max-content;max-width:calc(100vw - 32px);box-sizing:border-box;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13.5px;color:' + INK + ';}#w199bulk select{font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:999px;background:' + CREAM + ';max-width:170px;}' +
    '.w199called{color:' + TEAL + ';font-weight:700;}.w199tried{color:' + GOLD + ';font-weight:700;}.w199calls{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:center;font-size:13px;}#w197sheet #w199callnote{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;margin-top:8px;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13.5px;padding:8px 12px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:12px;background:' + CREAM + ';}' +
    '.w199iv{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;}.w199iv.past{color:var(--wa-muted);font-weight:600;}.w199ivno{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:700;}' +
    '#w197sheet input[type=datetime-local]{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:14px;padding:9px 12px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:12px;background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + INK + ';}#w197sheet input.need{border-color:' + FLAG + ';box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(179,64,47,.12);}' +
    '@media (max-width:600px){.w199grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.w199files select{margin-left:0;}}';
  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(css);

  window.w199 = { state: function () { return P; }, load: load, paint: paint, show: show, openPos: openPos, roleOf: roleOf, cardBits: cardBits, sortGrade: sortGrade, notCalled: notCalled, notCalledOnly: notCalledOnly, logCall: logCall, selBits: selBits, bulkApply: bulkApply, setSelMode: setSelMode, gradeAvg: gradeAvg, barBits: barBits, setGrade: setGrade, askTime: askTime, filterOptions: filterOptions, match: match, sheetBits: sheetBits, stages: PSTAGES, depts: DEPTS };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v201 (03/10/26): speak instead of typing, in every AI prompt box.
   Steel (03/10/26), on Your inbox's reply tool: "I'd like to use voice to text in prompts here"; asked where, he chose
   "Every AI prompt box". One shared mic, the same everywhere: tap it, talk, and the words appear in the box as you speak
   (the browser's own SpeechRecognition: Chrome, Edge and Safari; not Firefox, where no mic shows); tap again, or press Esc,
   to stop. It listens in the person's Hub language (wave198: Francais is fr-CA, else en-CA).
   The boxes (TARGETS): Ask the Hub (#w38in, which since wave38 replaced wave14's row and its mic), Your inbox (own words,
   anything else to say, the reply itself, the team's line, the writing guide), the content drafter's brief (#cdBrief),
   Suggest a change to the Hub (#w96in, #w96rin), Finance's import guidance (#w90gtext), the agenda's purpose (#w53purpose),
   the grant writer's notes (data-w193wnotes) and, since wave202, the email studio's boxes (data-w202say: what you want to say,
   anything else for a template, Ask the Hub to change it). The Loop (#w192say) keeps its own Speak button.
   A box is found again by its key when its page draws it anew mid-sentence, so the words keep landing. Each change fires
   the box's input event, so the page keeps what was said as if it were typed. Nothing is recorded or sent anywhere by the
   Hub: the browser turns speech into text (Chrome sends the audio to Google's speech service to do it).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa201) return;
  window.__wa201 = true;
  var SRC = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition || null;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', CREAM = '#FFFAF2';

  /* every box where people tell the Hub's AI what to do; key: the attribute that tells two boxes of one kind apart */
  var TARGETS = [
    { sel: '#w38in' },
    { sel: '#w170sheet [data-w170own]', key: 'data-w170own' },
    { sel: '#w170note' }, { sel: '#w170body' }, { sel: '#w170ask' }, { sel: '#w170voice' },
    { sel: '#cdBrief' },
    { sel: '#w96in' }, { sel: '#w96rin' },
    { sel: '#w90gtext' },
    { sel: '#w53purpose' },
    { sel: '[data-w193wnotes]' },
    { sel: '[data-w202say]', key: 'data-w202say' }
  ];
  var MIC = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path fill="currentColor" d="M12 14.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 0 3.5-3.5V5.5a3.5 3.5 0 1 0-7 0V11a3.5 3.5 0 0 0 3.5 3.5Zm6-3.5a1 1 0 1 0-2 0 4 4 0 0 1-8 0 1 1 0 1 0-2 0 6 6 0 0 0 5 5.91V19H8.5a1 1 0 1 0 0 2h7a1 1 0 1 0 0-2H13v-2.09A6 6 0 0 0 18 11Z"/></svg>';
  var STOP = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="14" height="14" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><rect x="6" y="6" width="12" height="12" rx="2.5" fill="currentColor"/></svg>';
  var L_IDLE = 'Speak instead of typing', L_ON = 'Listening: tap to stop';

  function css() {
    if (document.getElementById('w201css')) return;
    var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w201css';
    st.textContent = ''
      + '.w201h{display:flow-root;position:relative;height:0;padding:0;border:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:2;}'
      + '.w201mic{pointer-events:auto;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:6px;width:30px;height:30px;padding:0;border-radius:999px;'
      + 'border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.22);background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + GREEN + ';cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(16,63,57,.12);'
      + 'font:600 12px/1 "Space Grotesk",system-ui,sans-serif;white-space:nowrap;transition:background .15s,color .15s,width .15s;}'
      + '.w201mic:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);}'
      + '.w201mic:focus-visible{outline:2px solid ' + GOLD + ';outline-offset:2px;}'
      + '.w201h .w201mic{position:absolute;}'
      + '.w201mic.on{width:auto;padding:0 11px 0 9px;background:' + GREEN + ' !important;color:' + CREAM + ' !important;border-color:' + GREEN + ' !important;animation:w201pulse 1.4s ease-out infinite;}'
      + '@media (hover:none){.w201mic:hover{background:' + CREAM + ';}}'
      + '.w201mic .w201t{display:none;}.w201mic.on .w201t{display:inline;}'
      + '@keyframes w201pulse{0%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(220,165,39,.65);}70%{box-shadow:0 0 0 8px rgba(220,165,39,0);}100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(220,165,39,0);}}'
      + '.w201in{flex:none;align-self:center;margin:0 0 0 6px;}'
      + '.w201below{display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;margin:4px 0 0;}'
      + '.w201sr{position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0 0 0 0);white-space:nowrap;}'
      + '@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.w201mic.on{animation:none;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(220,165,39,.6);}}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  }
  function T(msg) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(msg); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function lang() {
    try { var s = window.w198 && window.w198.state && window.w198.state(); if (s && s.lang) return s.lang === 'fr' ? 'fr-CA' : 'en-CA'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return /^fr/i.test(navigator.language || '') ? 'fr-CA' : 'en-CA';
  }
  function keyOf(el, t) { return el.id ? '#' + el.id : t.sel + '|' + (t.key ? (el.getAttribute(t.key) || '') : ''); }
  function find(key) {
    if (key.charAt(0) === '#' && key.indexOf('|') < 0) return document.getElementById(key.slice(1));
    var i = key.lastIndexOf('|'), sel = key.slice(0, i), v = key.slice(i + 1);
    var t = TARGETS.filter(function (x) { return x.sel === sel; })[0]; if (!t) return null;
    var all = document.querySelectorAll(sel);
    for (var j = 0; j < all.length; j++) if (!t.key || all[j].getAttribute(t.key) === v) return all[j];
    return null;
  }
  function usable(el) { return el && el.isConnected && !el.disabled && !el.readOnly && el.offsetParent !== null; }

  /* ---------- placing the button ---------- */
  function px(v) { var n = parseFloat(v); return isFinite(n) ? n : 0; }
  function place(el, btn) {
    var p = el.parentNode, pcs = getComputedStyle(p), cs = getComputedStyle(el);
    var row = /flex/.test(pcs.display) && !/column/.test(pcs.flexDirection);
    if (row) { btn.classList.add('w201in'); p.insertBefore(btn, el.nextSibling); return { mode: 'in', node: btn }; }
    if (/grid/.test(pcs.display)) { var b = document.createElement('div'); b.className = 'w201below'; b.appendChild(btn); p.insertBefore(b, el.nextSibling); return { mode: 'below', node: b }; }
    /* over the box's corner: a zero-height holder right under it, the button lifted into the box (no layout shift, no timers) */
    var h = document.createElement('div'); h.className = 'w201h';
    var mb = px(cs.marginBottom), gap = /flex/.test(pcs.display) ? px(pcs.rowGap) : 0;
    h.style.marginTop = (-(mb + gap)) + 'px'; h.style.marginBottom = mb + 'px';
    h.appendChild(btn); p.insertBefore(h, el.nextSibling);
    var tall = el.tagName === 'TEXTAREA';
    var grip = tall && cs.resize !== 'none' ? 16 : 0;
    function fit() {
      if (!el.isConnected) return;
      h.style.width = el.offsetWidth + 'px';
      h.style.marginLeft = (el.offsetLeft - h.offsetLeft + px(h.style.marginLeft)) + 'px';
      var bh = btn.offsetHeight || 30;
      btn.style.right = (6 + grip) + 'px';
      btn.style.bottom = (tall ? 6 : Math.max(2, (el.offsetHeight - bh) / 2)) + 'px';
    }
    fit();
    el.style.paddingRight = (px(cs.paddingRight) + 36) + 'px';
    if (window.ResizeObserver) { try { new ResizeObserver(fit).observe(el); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    return { mode: 'over', node: h, fit: fit };
  }

  /* ---------- one box ---------- */
  function attach(el, t) {
    if (el.__w201 || el.hasAttribute('data-w201off')) return;
    if (!el.parentNode || el.offsetParent === null) return;   /* drawn hidden: try again when it shows */
    el.__w201 = true;
    var key = keyOf(el, t);
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.type = 'button'; btn.className = 'w201mic'; btn.setAttribute('data-w201mic', key);
    btn.setAttribute('aria-label', L_IDLE); btn.title = L_IDLE + ' (Esc stops)'; btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    btn.innerHTML = MIC + '<span class="w201t">Listening</span>';
    btn.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });   /* keep the caret in the box */
    btn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      var box = find(key) || el;
      if (REC && REC.key === key) stop(true); else start(box, key);
    });
    var pl = place(el, btn);
    el.__w201node = pl.node;
    if (REC && REC.key === key) paint(btn, true);
  }
  function paint(btn, on) {
    btn.classList.toggle('on', !!on); btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'true' : 'false');
    btn.setAttribute('aria-label', on ? L_ON : L_IDLE); btn.title = (on ? L_ON : L_IDLE) + ' (Esc stops)';
    btn.innerHTML = (on ? STOP : MIC) + '<span class="w201t">Listening</span>';
  }
  function paintAll() {
    var bs = document.querySelectorAll('[data-w201mic]');
    for (var i = 0; i < bs.length; i++) paint(bs[i], !!(REC && REC.key === bs[i].getAttribute('data-w201mic')));
  }
  function say(msg) { var r = document.getElementById('w201live'); if (r) r.textContent = msg; }

  /* ---------- listening ---------- */
  var REC = null;
  function now() { return (window.performance && performance.now) ? performance.now() : new Date().getTime(); }
  function tidy(s) { return String(s || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+([,.!?;:])/g, '$1').trim(); }
  function cap(s) { return s ? s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s; }
  function write(text) {
    var box = find(REC.key) || (REC.el.isConnected ? REC.el : null); if (!box) return;
    REC.el = box;
    var heard = tidy(text); if (!heard) return;
    if (REC.capital) heard = cap(heard);
    var v = REC.before + heard + REC.after;
    var max = Number(box.getAttribute('maxlength')) || 0; if (max > 0 && v.length > max) v = v.slice(0, max);
    if (box.value === v) return;
    box.value = v;
    var caret = Math.min(v.length, (REC.before + heard).length);
    try { box.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (box.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' && !REC.after) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
    try { box.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function start(el, key) {
    if (!SRC) return;
    if (REC) stop(true);
    var rec;
    try { rec = new SRC(); } catch (e) { T('Speaking is not available in this browser'); return; }
    rec.lang = lang(); rec.continuous = true; rec.interimResults = true; rec.maxAlternatives = 1;
    var v = el.value || '', at = (typeof el.selectionStart === 'number' && document.activeElement === el) ? el.selectionStart : v.length;
    var before = v.slice(0, at), after = v.slice(at);
    if (before && !/\s$/.test(before)) before += ' ';
    if (after && !/^\s/.test(after)) after = ' ' + after;
    REC = { key: key, el: el, rec: rec, before: before, after: after, fin: '', capital: !before.trim() || /[.!?]\s*$/.test(before), user: false, at: now() };
    var mine = REC;
    rec.onresult = function (ev) {
      if (REC !== mine) return;
      var interim = '';
      for (var i = ev.resultIndex; i < ev.results.length; i++) {
        var r = ev.results[i]; if (!r || !r[0]) continue;
        if (r.isFinal) mine.fin += ' ' + r[0].transcript; else interim += ' ' + r[0].transcript;
      }
      write(mine.fin + ' ' + interim);
    };
    rec.onerror = function (ev) {
      if (REC !== mine) return;
      var er = ev && ev.error;
      if (er === 'not-allowed' || er === 'service-not-allowed') T('Allow the microphone for the Hub in your browser, then tap the mic again');
      else if (er === 'no-speech') T('Nothing heard. Tap the mic and speak');
      else if (er === 'audio-capture') T('No microphone found on this device');
      else if (er === 'network') T('Speaking needs an internet connection');
      else if (er && er !== 'aborted') T('Speaking stopped: ' + er);
    };
    rec.onend = function () { if (REC === mine) finish(); };
    try { rec.start(); } catch (e) { REC = null; T('The microphone could not start'); return; }
    mine.timer = setInterval(function () { if (REC !== mine) { clearInterval(mine.timer); return; } gone(); }, 700);
    paintAll(); say(L_ON);
    try { el.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function stop(user) {
    if (!REC) return;
    REC.user = !!user;
    var r = REC.rec;
    try { r.stop(); } catch (e) { /* stopped */ }
    finish();
  }
  function finish() {
    var m = REC; if (!m) return;
    REC = null;
    if (m.timer) clearInterval(m.timer);
    try { m.rec.onresult = null; m.rec.onend = null; m.rec.onerror = null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var box = find(m.key) || (m.el.isConnected ? m.el : null);
    if (box) { try { box.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    paintAll(); say('Stopped listening');
  }

  /* Esc stops listening (and does not close the sheet or the dialog around the box) */
  window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (REC && (e.key === 'Escape' || e.key === 'Esc')) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation(); stop(true); }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- finding the boxes as pages draw them ---------- */
  function scan() {
    if (!SRC) return;
    for (var i = 0; i < TARGETS.length; i++) {
      var t = TARGETS[i], els = document.querySelectorAll(t.sel);
      for (var j = 0; j < els.length; j++) { var el = els[j]; if (el.tagName !== 'TEXTAREA' && el.tagName !== 'INPUT') continue; if (!el.__w201) attach(el, t); else if (el.__w201node && !el.__w201node.isConnected) { el.__w201 = false; attach(el, t); } }
    }
    /* a holder whose box is gone goes too; a box drawn hidden loses its button until it shows */
    var hs = document.querySelectorAll('.w201h,.w201below,.w201in');
    for (var k = 0; k < hs.length; k++) {
      var h = hs[k], prev = h.previousElementSibling;
      if (!prev || !prev.__w201) h.remove();
      else h.style.display = prev.offsetParent === null ? 'none' : '';
    }
    gone();
  }
  /* a box that goes away (or is hidden) for more than a second and a half stops the listening */
  function gone() {
    if (!REC) return;
    var b = find(REC.key), vis = !!(b && b.offsetParent !== null) || (REC.el.isConnected && REC.el.offsetParent !== null);
    if (vis) REC.goneAt = 0; else if (!REC.goneAt) REC.goneAt = now(); else if (now() - REC.goneAt > 1500) stop(false);
  }
  var queued = false;
  function soon() { if (queued) return; queued = true; (window.requestAnimationFrame || setTimeout)(function () { queued = false; try { scan(); } catch (e) { /* never break a page */ } }); }
  function boot() {
    if (!SRC || !document.body) return;
    css();
    if (!document.getElementById('w201live')) { var lv = document.createElement('div'); lv.id = 'w201live'; lv.className = 'w201sr'; lv.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite'); document.body.appendChild(lv); }
    try { new MutationObserver(soon).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    document.addEventListener('focusin', soon, true);
    document.addEventListener('click', soon, true);
    document.addEventListener('toggle', soon, true);
    soon();
  }
  window.w201 = { supported: !!SRC, targets: TARGETS.map(function (t) { return t.sel; }), scan: scan, state: function () { return REC ? { key: REC.key, lang: REC.rec.lang } : null; }, stop: function () { stop(true); }, lang: lang };
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot); else boot();
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v202 (03/10/26): the email studio. CRM > Studio (#crm/studio).
   =====================================================================
   Steel (03/10/26), with the Rally Paper 2026 emails as the example: "For our emails, I'd like to be able to produce these
   types of emails for one-off emails and template emails going forward for both our volunteers and donors." Then: "These
   are just examples of what we used at the rally paper, we need to create templates that follow the same brand guidelines.
   To start an email they should be given the option to put in what they wrote, and the photos for the email and then the
   AI puts it together. Or they use a template with their written information and the hub ai puts it together in an html
   structured email. I want there to be a few options they could take to build emails. Also, if there's missing
   information, I want the hub to prompt the user to ensure they've got all necessary and correct information for the
   newsletter. I'd like them to also have created emails used for automation workflows." His shelves: volunteers (welcome
   and serving; call-outs and events) and donors (thanks and impact; appeals and events). Where they go: saved templates,
   one-off to a list, one-off to one person.

   - Three ways to start: Tell the Hub (what you wrote and your photos; the Hub puts it together), Start from a template
     (the template plus your details; the Hub fills it in, or you do), Build it yourself (blocks).
   - The Hub checks before anything goes out: its own questions about missing or doubtful details (a date that is not that
     weekday, a link nobody gave, a number it would have to invent), and the studio's checks (anything left in [brackets],
     the subject, links, alt text, dates, Welfare Avenue's words and no em dashes, the CASL kind).
   - The editor: blocks in the brand (a headline with the E that moves and a doodle, text, details, a dot list, the gold
     button, photos, a video card, the green band of links, a quote, numbers, the sign-off) with the email beside it as it
     will look (mail-send's own layout, ported: MAIL202.layout), on a computer or a phone. Ask the Hub to change it.
   - Send: a test to yourself, one person (the permission rule decides, mail_mode still gates), a list (a campaign draft
     the Campaigns tab checks and sends), or save it as a template, which the automations' "Saved email" list picks up.
   - wave203 (04/10/26), the voices: Steel, "draft a WA voice code from the brand guide and keep mine too as options". Welfare
     Avenue (the house voice code, app_config mail_voice_wa, admins edit it in the Voices panel) or My voice (the writer's own
     guide from Your inbox). g2_voices.js; the choice is saved with the email (design.voice), never the guide's text.
   - wave204 (04/10/26), Soraya Tyree's review: Undo and Redo for every change, with Cmd/Ctrl+Z (l_history.js), and a way back
     from the Hub's draft to what you wrote or your details; a moving preview for the video card, a few silent seconds of your
     own video made into an animated GIF in the browser (m_gif.js, m_moving.js), since inboxes do not play video; text pasted
     from a document keeps its bold, links and paragraphs, and B and Link buttons mark text without typing the symbols (n_text.js).
     Steel, the same day: the costs in the studio "so they know how much they are spending" (o_costs.js: each email's AI spend,
     about 2 cents by every AI button, the What it costs panel with the month's total), and "come with their content first. It
     is unacceptable to only use AI. Bad input = bad output." (p_content.js: 40 words of your own before Tell the Hub, every
     detail before the Hub fills a template, and a prompt that never pads thin input).

  function ago(ts) {

  }

  /* ---------- the email itself: blocks in the brand, rendered for email clients (MAIL202) ----------
     Self-contained (no page, no db), so the seeding script and the tests load the same code: node runs this file alone.
     Every tag the renderer writes carries its own inline style, and links put style before href, so mail-send's styleInner
     (which only rewrites bare <p>, <h1>, <a href=...>, <strong> and the like) leaves the studio's HTML exactly as it is.
     The anatomy follows the Rally Paper 2026 emails (Steel's example): a hero with a doodle and the E that moves, section
     headings with the gold rule, a details table, a dot list, the gold button with a purple caption, photos with captions,
     video cards (wave204: with a moving preview when the writer made one), a dark green band of links, the sign-off. Inside mail-send's layout (600px, the green header with the logo,
     the CASL footer) the green pieces are rounded cards on the cream body. */
  var MAIL202 = (function () {
    var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', INK = '#1F201E', PURPLE = '#6F64AB', GOLD = '#DCA527', RULE = '#E6DFD3';
    var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
    var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";

      star: { gold: ST + 'brand/doodle-star-gold.png', purple: ST + 'brand/doodle-star-purple.png', cream: ST + 'brand/doodle-star-cream.png', w: 224, h: 238 },

    };

    var TYPES = ['hero', 'text', 'heading', 'details', 'list', 'button', 'photo', 'photos', 'video', 'band', 'quote', 'stats', 'signoff', 'divider'];
    var LABEL = { hero: 'Headline', text: 'Text', heading: 'Section heading', details: 'Details', list: 'Dot list', button: 'Button', photo: 'Photo', photos: 'Photos in a row',
      video: 'Video card', band: 'Green band of links', quote: 'Quote', stats: 'Numbers', signoff: 'Sign-off', divider: 'Divider' };
    /* the placeholders mail-send fills: a contact's own, then what an automation's trigger adds (wa157) */
    var KNOWN = ['first_name', 'full_name', 'email', 'hub_url', 'site_url', 'from_name', 'form_title', 'amount', 'gift_date', 'gift_date_line', 'campaign', 'event_day', 'event_when', 'event_title', 'role', 'event_location'];
    var SAMPLE = { first_name: 'Alex', full_name: 'Alex Tremblay', email: 'alex@example.org', hub_url: 'https://hub.welfareavenue.com', site_url: 'https://welfareavenue.com', from_name: 'Welfare Avenue',

    function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
    function str(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s); }
    function uid() { return 'b' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9); }
    /* a link the email may carry: http(s), mailto, tel or a placeholder; a bare domain gets https:// */
    function safeUrl(u) {
      u = str(u).trim();
      if (!u) return '';
      if (/^(https?:\/\/|mailto:|tel:)/i.test(u) || /^\{\{\s*[a-z_]+\s*(\|[^}]*)?\}\}/i.test(u)) return u;
      if (/^[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}(\/.*)?$/i.test(u)) return 'https://' + u;
      return '';
    }
    function safeImg(u) { u = str(u).trim(); return /^https:\/\//i.test(u) ? u : ''; }
    /* **bold** and [words](link) inside a line; everything else is text */
    function inline(s, o) {
      o = o || {};
      var linkC = o.link || PURPLE, boldC = o.bold || GREEN;
      var out = '', re = /\*\*([^*]+)\*\*|\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/g, last = 0, m;
      s = str(s);
      while ((m = re.exec(s))) {
        out += E(s.slice(last, m.index));
        if (m[1] != null) out += '<strong style="color:' + boldC + ';font-weight:700;">' + E(m[1]) + '</strong>';
        else { var u = safeUrl(m[3]); out += u ? '<a style="color:' + linkC + ';font-weight:700;text-decoration:underline;" href="' + E(u) + '" target="_blank">' + E(m[2]) + '</a>' : E(m[2]); }
        last = re.lastIndex;
      }
      return out + E(s.slice(last));
    }
    function plainInline(s) { return str(s).replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '$1').replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/g, function (_m, a, b) { var u = safeUrl(b); return u ? a + ' (' + u + ')' : a; }); }
    function paras(s) { return str(s).replace(/\r/g, '').split(/\n\s*\n/).map(function (p) { return p.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, ''); }).filter(Boolean); }
    function rule(m) { return '<table role="presentation" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" style="margin:' + (m || '8px 0 14px') + ';"><tr><td style="width:40px;height:3px;background-color:' + GOLD + ';font-size:0;line-height:0;">&nbsp;</td></tr></table>'; }
    function tbl(inner, style) { return '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" style="' + (style || 'margin:0 0 18px;') + '">' + inner + '</table>'; }
    function P(html, o) {
      o = o || {};
      return '<p style="margin:' + (o.m || '0 0 16px') + ';font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:' + (o.size || 16) + 'px;line-height:' + (o.lh || 26) + 'px;color:' + (o.color || INK) + ';' + (o.align ? 'text-align:' + o.align + ';' : '') + '">' + html + '</p>';
    }
    /* the E that moves: one E of a headline line becomes the long E in the line's colour (the last E that ends a word, as
       in the Rally Paper's "cause", else the last E, as in the brand book's EV\u2261RY), never inside a {{placeholder}} or an entity */
    function moveE(lineHtml, color, px) {
      var src = EIMG[color]; if (!src) return { html: lineHtml, done: false };
      var h = Math.round(px * 0.7), w = Math.round(h * 600 / 172);
      var parts = lineHtml.split(/(\{\{[^}]*\}\}|&[a-z#0-9]+;|<[^>]+>)/i), cands = [];
      parts.forEach(function (p, pi) {
        if (!p || /^(\{\{|&|<)/.test(p)) return;
        for (var k = 0; k < p.length; k++) {
          if (p.charAt(k) !== 'e' && p.charAt(k) !== 'E') continue;
          var next = p.charAt(k + 1);
          var end = next === '' ? !(parts[pi + 1] && /^[a-z0-9]/i.test(parts[pi + 1])) : !/[a-z0-9]/i.test(next);
          cands.push([pi, k, end]);
        }
      });
      if (!cands.length) return { html: lineHtml, done: false };
      var ends = cands.filter(function (c) { return c[2]; }), pick = ends.length ? ends[ends.length - 1] : cands[cands.length - 1];
      var word = parts[pick[0]], before = word.slice(0, pick[1]), after = word.slice(pick[1] + 1);
      var wsB = before.search(/\S*$/), wsA = after.search(/\s|$/);
      parts[pick[0]] = before.slice(0, wsB) + '<span style="white-space:nowrap;">' + before.slice(wsB) + '<img src="' + src + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" alt="E" style="display:inline-block;width:' + w + 'px;height:' + h +
        'px;vertical-align:baseline;margin:0 2px;border:0;">' + after.slice(0, wsA) + '</span>' + after.slice(wsA);
      return { html: parts.join(''), done: true };
    }
    function doodleImg(name, color, size, align) {
      var d = DOODLE[name]; if (!d) return '';
      var src = d[color] || d.gold, w = size || 64, h = Math.round(w * d.h / d.w);
      return '<div style="text-align:' + (align || 'right') + ';line-height:0;font-size:0;"><img src="' + src + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" alt="" style="display:inline-block;width:' + w + 'px;height:' + h + 'px;border:0;"></div>';
    }

    /* ---------- one block to HTML ---------- */
    var R = {};
    R.hero = function (b, ctx) {
      var green = b.style === 'green', lines = (b.lines || []).map(str).filter(function (x) { return x.trim(); });
      if (!lines.length && !b.lead) return '';
      var accent = b.accent === 'purple' ? PURPLE : GOLD, base = green ? CREAM : GREEN, gi = b.gold == null ? lines.length - 1 : Number(b.gold);
      var lineHtml = lines.map(function (l, i) {
        var c = i === gi ? accent : base, inner = E(l);
        if (b.moveE !== false && i === gi && !ctx.eDone) {
          var key = c === GOLD ? 'gold' : c === CREAM ? 'cream' : c === GREEN ? 'green' : '';
          var r = moveE(inner, key, 44); inner = r.html; if (r.done) ctx.eDone = true;
        }
        return '<div style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:500;font-size:44px;line-height:46px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:' + c + ';">' + inner + '</div>';
      }).join('');
      var kicker = b.kicker ? '<div style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-weight:700;font-size:11px;line-height:14px;letter-spacing:2px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + (green ? GOLD : PURPLE) + ';padding:0 0 12px;">' + E(b.kicker) + '</div>' : '';
      var doodle = b.doodle && b.doodle !== 'none' ? doodleImg(b.doodle, green ? 'gold' : (b.doodleColor === 'purple' ? 'purple' : 'gold'), 62) : '';
      var lead = b.lead ? '<div style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:17px;line-height:27px;color:' + (green ? CREAM : INK) + ';padding-top:16px;">' + inline(b.lead, green ? { link: GOLD, bold: GOLD } : {}) + '</div>' : '';
      var cell = (doodle ? '<div style="margin:-6px 0 -22px;">' + doodle + '</div>' : '') + kicker + lineHtml + lead;
      return green ? tbl('<tr><td style="background-color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:8px;padding:26px 28px 28px;">' + cell + '</td></tr>', 'margin:0 0 24px;border-collapse:separate;')
        : tbl('<tr><td style="padding:0 0 4px;">' + cell + '</td></tr>', 'margin:0 0 22px;');
    };
    R.text = function (b) { return paras(b.text).map(function (p) { return P(inline(p).replace(/\n/g, '<br>')); }).join(''); };
    R.heading = function (b) {
      if (!str(b.text).trim()) return '';
      return '<div style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:26px;line-height:28px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:' + GREEN + ';padding:10px 0 0;">' + E(b.text) + '</div>' + rule();
    };
    R.details = function (b) {
      var rows = (b.rows || []).filter(function (r) { return r && (str(r.label).trim() || str(r.value).trim()); });
      if (!rows.length) return '';
      var td = 'font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:15px;line-height:22px;vertical-align:top;border-bottom:1px solid ' + RULE + ';';
      return tbl(rows.map(function (r, i) {
        var top = i === 0 ? 'border-top:1px solid ' + RULE + ';' : '';
        return '<tr><td style="' + td + top + 'padding:10px 14px 10px 0;width:34%;font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';">' + E(r.label) + '</td><td style="' + td + top + 'padding:10px 0;color:' + PURPLE + ';">' + inline(r.value, { bold: PURPLE }).replace(/\n/g, '<br>') + '</td></tr>';
      }).join(''), 'margin:0 0 20px;border-collapse:collapse;');
    };
    R.list = function (b) {
      var items = (b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && (str(x.text).trim() || str(x.lead).trim()); });
      if (!items.length) return '';
      var dots = [GOLD, PURPLE, GREEN];
      return tbl(items.map(function (x, i) {
        return '<tr><td style="padding:3px 12px 3px 0;vertical-align:top;width:10px;"><table role="presentation" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" style="margin:8px 0 0;"><tr><td style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:5px;background-color:' + dots[i % 3] +
          ';font-size:0;line-height:0;">&nbsp;</td></tr></table></td><td style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:15px;line-height:24px;color:' + INK + ';padding:3px 0;">' +
          (str(x.lead).trim() ? '<strong style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;">' + E(x.lead) + '</strong> ' : '') + inline(x.text) + '</td></tr>';
      }).join(''), 'margin:0 0 18px;');
    };
    R.button = function (b) {
      var u = safeUrl(b.url) || '#', label = str(b.label).trim() || 'Find out more';
      return '<table role="presentation" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" align="center" style="margin:10px auto ' + (b.caption ? '6px' : '24px') + ';"><tr><td style="background-color:' + GOLD + ';border-radius:6px;" align="center">' +
        '<a style="display:inline-block;padding:15px 34px;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:20px;line-height:22px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';text-decoration:none;" href="' + E(u) + '" target="_blank">' + E(label) + '</a></td></tr></table>' +
        (b.caption ? P(inline(b.caption, { bold: PURPLE }), { m: '0 0 24px', size: 12, lh: 18, color: PURPLE, align: 'center' }) : '');
    };
    function imgTag(src, alt, w, round) {
      return '<img src="' + E(src) + '" width="' + w + '" alt="' + E(alt || '') + '" style="display:block;width:100%;max-width:' + w + 'px;height:auto;border:0;' + (round === false ? '' : 'border-radius:8px;') + '">';
    }
    R.photo = function (b) {
      var src = safeImg(b.src); if (!src) return '';
      var img = imgTag(src, b.alt, W), link = safeUrl(b.link);
      return tbl('<tr><td style="padding:0;">' + (link ? '<a style="display:block;text-decoration:none;" href="' + E(link) + '" target="_blank">' + img + '</a>' : img) + '</td></tr>' +
        (b.caption ? '<tr><td style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;line-height:20px;color:' + INK + ';padding:10px 0 0;">' + inline(b.caption) + '</td></tr>' : ''), 'margin:4px 0 22px;');
    };
    R.photos = function (b) {
      var items = (b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && safeImg(x.src); }).slice(0, 3);
      if (!items.length) return '';
      if (items.length === 1) return R.photo(items[0]);
      var n = items.length, gap = 10, w = Math.floor((W - gap * (n - 1)) / n), pct = Math.floor(100 / n);
      var pad = function (i) { return 'padding:0 ' + (i === n - 1 ? 0 : gap / 2) + 'px 0 ' + (i === 0 ? 0 : gap / 2) + 'px;'; };
      var imgs = items.map(function (x, i) { return '<td width="' + pct + '%" valign="top" style="' + pad(i) + '">' + imgTag(safeImg(x.src), x.alt, w) + '</td>'; }).join('');
      var caps = items.some(function (x) { return str(x.caption).trim(); }) ? '<tr>' + items.map(function (x, i) {
        return '<td width="' + pct + '%" valign="top" style="' + pad(i) + 'font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;line-height:20px;color:' + INK + ';padding-top:10px;">' + inline(x.caption) + '</td>';
      }).join('') + '</tr>' : '';
      return tbl('<tr>' + imgs + '</tr>' + caps, 'margin:4px 0 22px;');
    };
    /* wave204: with a moving preview (b.gif, an animated GIF the studio made from the writer's video, gw x gh pixels) the card
       shows it, play button drawn in, above the title; without one, the gold play button as before. Either way the whole card
       links to the video. */
    R.video = function (b) {
      var u = safeUrl(b.url) || '#', gif = safeImg(b.gif), top;
      if (gif) {
        var gw = Math.max(1, Math.min(W, Math.round(Number(b.gw)) || W)), gh = Math.round(Number(b.gh)) || 0, full = gw >= W;
        top = '<tr><td align="center" style="padding:' + (full ? '0' : '16px 16px 0') + ';line-height:0;font-size:0;"><img src="' + E(gif) + '" width="' + gw + '"' + (gh > 0 ? ' height="' + gh + '"' : '') +
          ' alt="' + E((str(b.title).trim() || 'Watch the video') + ' (video)') + '" style="display:block;margin:0 auto;width:100%;max-width:' + gw + 'px;height:auto;border:0;border-radius:' + (full ? '8px 8px 0 0' : '8px') + ';"></td></tr>';
      } else top = '<tr><td align="center" style="padding:22px 16px 6px;"><table role="presentation" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0"><tr><td align="center" style="background-color:' + GOLD + ';border-radius:32px;width:64px;height:64px;font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;font-size:26px;line-height:64px;color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:bold;">&#9654;</td></tr></table></td></tr>';
      return '<a style="display:block;text-decoration:none;margin:4px 0 22px;" href="' + E(u) + '" target="_blank">' +
        tbl(top + '<tr><td align="center" style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:22px;line-height:24px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + CREAM + ';padding:' + (gif ? '14px' : '4px') + ' 16px 4px;">' + E(str(b.title).trim() || 'Watch the video') + '</td></tr>' +
          '<tr><td align="center" style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12px;line-height:18px;color:' + GOLD + ';padding:0 16px ' + (gif ? '18px' : '22px') + ';">' + E(str(b.caption).trim() || 'Tap to watch') + '</td></tr>',
          'background-color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:8px;border-collapse:separate;') + '</a>';
    };
    R.band = function (b) {
      var links = (b.links || []).filter(function (l) { return l && str(l.label).trim() && safeUrl(l.url); });
      if (!str(b.title).trim() && !str(b.text).trim() && !links.length) return '';
      var inner = (str(b.title).trim() ? '<div style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:26px;line-height:28px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:' + GOLD + ';">' + E(b.title) + '</div>' + rule() : '') +
        paras(b.text).map(function (p) { return P(inline(p, { link: GOLD, bold: GOLD }).replace(/\n/g, '<br>'), { size: 14, lh: 22, color: CREAM, m: '0 0 12px' }); }).join('') +
        (links.length ? tbl(links.map(function (l) {
          return '<tr><td style="padding:0 0 8px;"><a style="display:block;border:1px solid ' + CREAM + ';border-radius:6px;padding:11px 16px;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:18px;line-height:22px;letter-spacing:.5px;color:' + CREAM +
            ';text-decoration:none;text-transform:uppercase;" href="' + E(safeUrl(l.url)) + '" target="_blank">&#8594;&nbsp; ' + E(l.label) + '</a></td></tr>';
        }).join(''), 'margin:4px 0 0;') : '');
      return tbl('<tr><td style="background-color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:8px;padding:24px 28px 16px;">' + inner + '</td></tr>', 'margin:4px 0 24px;border-collapse:separate;');
    };
    R.quote = function (b) {
      if (!str(b.quote).trim()) return '';
      return tbl('<tr><td style="border-left:3px solid ' + PURPLE + ';padding:4px 0 4px 18px;">' +
        P(inline(b.quote), { m: '0', size: 18, lh: 28, color: GREEN }) +
        (str(b.by).trim() ? '<div style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-weight:700;font-size:12px;line-height:18px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + PURPLE + ';padding-top:8px;">' + E(b.by) + '</div>' : '') +
        '</td></tr>', 'margin:6px 0 22px;');
    };
    R.stats = function (b) {
      var items = (b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && str(x.n).trim(); }).slice(0, 3);
      if (!items.length) return '';
      var green = b.style !== 'cream', n = items.length, pct = Math.floor(100 / n);
      var cells = items.map(function (x) {
        return '<td width="' + pct + '%" align="center" valign="top" style="padding:0 6px;"><div style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:40px;line-height:42px;color:' + (green ? GOLD : GREEN) + ';">' + E(x.n) + '</div>' +
          '<div style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;line-height:18px;color:' + (green ? CREAM : INK) + ';padding-top:6px;">' + E(x.label) + '</div></td>';
      }).join('');
      var head = str(b.title).trim() ? '<tr><td colspan="' + n + '" align="center" style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-weight:700;font-size:11px;line-height:14px;letter-spacing:2px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + (green ? GOLD : PURPLE) + ';padding:0 0 14px;">' + E(b.title) + '</td></tr>' : '';
      var note = str(b.note).trim() ? '<tr><td colspan="' + n + '" align="center" style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:11px;line-height:16px;color:' + (green ? CREAM : INK) + ';padding:14px 6px 0;opacity:.85;">' + E(b.note) + '</td></tr>' : '';
      var t = '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0">' + head + '<tr>' + cells + '</tr>' + note + '</table>';
      return green ? tbl('<tr><td style="background-color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:8px;padding:24px 14px 22px;">' + t + '</td></tr>', 'margin:6px 0 24px;border-collapse:separate;')
        : tbl('<tr><td style="border-top:1px solid ' + RULE + ';border-bottom:1px solid ' + RULE + ';padding:20px 0 18px;">' + t + '</td></tr>', 'margin:6px 0 24px;');
    };
    R.signoff = function (b) {
      var closing = str(b.closing).trim() || 'Warmly,';
      return P(E(closing) + (str(b.name).trim() ? '<br><strong style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;">' + E(b.name) + '</strong>' : '') +
        (str(b.role).trim() ? '<br><span style="color:' + PURPLE + ';">' + E(b.role) + '</span>' : ''), { m: '8px 0 16px' }) +
        paras(b.ps).map(function (p, i) { return P((i === 0 && !/^p\.?\s*s\.?/i.test(p) ? '<strong style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;">P.S.</strong> ' : '') + inline(p), { size: 15, lh: 24 }); }).join('');
    };
    R.divider = function (b) {
      return b.style === 'space' ? '<div style="height:18px;line-height:18px;font-size:0;">&nbsp;</div>' : rule('6px 0 22px');
    };

    /* ---------- one block to plain text ---------- */
    var TX = {};
    TX.hero = function (b) { return [str(b.kicker).trim(), (b.lines || []).map(str).filter(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).join(' ').toUpperCase(), plainInline(b.lead)].filter(Boolean).join('\n\n'); };
    TX.text = function (b) { return paras(b.text).map(plainInline).join('\n\n'); };
    TX.heading = function (b) { return str(b.text).trim().toUpperCase(); };
    TX.details = function (b) { return (b.rows || []).filter(function (r) { return r && (str(r.label).trim() || str(r.value).trim()); }).map(function (r) { return str(r.label).trim() + ': ' + plainInline(r.value).replace(/\n/g, ' '); }).join('\n'); };
    TX.list = function (b) { return (b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && (str(x.text).trim() || str(x.lead).trim()); }).map(function (x) { return '- ' + (str(x.lead).trim() ? str(x.lead).trim() + ' ' : '') + plainInline(x.text); }).join('\n'); };
    TX.button = function (b) { var u = safeUrl(b.url); return (str(b.label).trim() || 'Find out more') + (u ? ': ' + u : '') + (b.caption ? '\n' + plainInline(b.caption) : ''); };
    TX.photo = function (b) { return str(b.caption).trim() ? plainInline(b.caption) : ''; };
    TX.photos = function (b) { return (b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && str(x.caption).trim(); }).map(function (x) { return plainInline(x.caption); }).join('\n'); };
    TX.video = function (b) { var u = safeUrl(b.url); return (str(b.title).trim() || 'Watch the video') + (u ? ': ' + u : ''); };
    TX.band = function (b) {
      return [str(b.title).trim().toUpperCase(), paras(b.text).map(plainInline).join('\n\n'), (b.links || []).filter(function (l) { return l && str(l.label).trim() && safeUrl(l.url); }).map(function (l) { return str(l.label).trim() + ': ' + safeUrl(l.url); }).join('\n')].filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    };
    TX.quote = function (b) { return str(b.quote).trim() ? '"' + plainInline(b.quote) + '"' + (str(b.by).trim() ? '\n' + str(b.by).trim() : '') : ''; };
    TX.stats = function (b) { return [str(b.title).trim()].concat((b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && str(x.n).trim(); }).map(function (x) { return str(x.n).trim() + ' ' + str(x.label).trim(); }), [str(b.note).trim()]).filter(Boolean).join('\n'); };
    TX.signoff = function (b) { return [str(b.closing).trim() || 'Warmly,', str(b.name).trim(), str(b.role).trim()].filter(Boolean).join('\n') + (paras(b.ps).length ? '\n\n' + paras(b.ps).map(function (p, i) { return (i === 0 && !/^p\.?\s*s\.?/i.test(p) ? 'P.S. ' : '') + plainInline(p); }).join('\n\n') : ''); };
    TX.divider = function () { return ''; };

    function render(design) {
      var ctx = { eDone: false };
      return ((design && design.blocks) || []).map(function (b) { return b && R[b.type] ? R[b.type](b, ctx) : ''; }).join('');
    }
    /* the same, with each block marked for the studio's preview (a click opens that block); never sent */
    function renderMarked(design) {
      var ctx = { eDone: false };
      return ((design && design.blocks) || []).map(function (b, i) { var h = b && R[b.type] ? R[b.type](b, ctx) : ''; return '<div data-w202b="' + i + '">' + (h || '') + '</div>'; }).join('');
    }
    function renderText(design) {
      return ((design && design.blocks) || []).map(function (b) { return b && TX[b.type] ? TX[b.type](b) : ''; }).filter(function (t) { return str(t).trim(); }).join('\n\n').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim();
    }
    /* the placeholders an email uses ({{name}} or {{name|fallback}}), in order */
    function placeholders(s) {
      var out = [], re = /\{\{\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\s*(?:\|[^}]*)?\}\}/g, m;
      s = str(s);
      while ((m = re.exec(s))) if (out.indexOf(m[1]) < 0) out.push(m[1]);
      return out;
    }
    /* every piece of text in a design, with where it is (for the checks) */
    function texts(design) {
      var out = [];
      ((design && design.blocks) || []).forEach(function (b, i) {
        if (!b) return;
        var add = function (field, v) { if (str(v).trim()) out.push({ i: i, field: field, text: str(v) }); };
        ['kicker', 'lead', 'text', 'label', 'caption', 'title', 'quote', 'by', 'closing', 'name', 'role', 'ps', 'note', 'alt'].forEach(function (f) { add(f, b[f]); });
        (b.lines || []).forEach(function (l, k) { add('lines.' + k, l); });
        (b.rows || []).forEach(function (r, k) { if (r) { add('rows.' + k + '.label', r.label); add('rows.' + k + '.value', r.value); } });
        (b.items || []).forEach(function (x, k) { if (x) { add('items.' + k + '.lead', x.lead); add('items.' + k + '.text', x.text); add('items.' + k + '.caption', x.caption); add('items.' + k + '.label', x.label); add('items.' + k + '.n', x.n); add('items.' + k + '.alt', x.alt); } });
        (b.links || []).forEach(function (l, k) { if (l) add('links.' + k + '.label', l.label); });
      });
      return out;
    }
    /* every link and picture in a design */
    function links(design) {
      var out = [];
      ((design && design.blocks) || []).forEach(function (b, i) {
        if (!b) return;
        if (b.type === 'button') out.push({ i: i, field: 'url', url: str(b.url), label: str(b.label), need: true });
        if (b.type === 'video') out.push({ i: i, field: 'url', url: str(b.url), label: str(b.title), need: true });
        if (b.type === 'photo' && str(b.link).trim()) out.push({ i: i, field: 'link', url: str(b.link), label: 'the photo' });
        (b.links || []).forEach(function (l, k) { if (l) out.push({ i: i, field: 'links.' + k + '.url', url: str(l.url), label: str(l.label), need: !!str(l.label).trim() }); });
        texts({ blocks: [b] }).forEach(function (t) { var re = /\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/g, m; while ((m = re.exec(t.text))) out.push({ i: i, field: t.field, url: m[2], label: m[1], inText: true }); });
      });
      return out;
    }
    function images(design) {
      var out = [];
      ((design && design.blocks) || []).forEach(function (b, i) {
        if (!b) return;
        if (b.type === 'photo' && str(b.src).trim()) out.push({ i: i, k: null, src: str(b.src), alt: str(b.alt), lib: b.lib || null, usage: b.usage || null });
        if (b.type === 'photos') (b.items || []).forEach(function (x, k) { if (x && str(x.src).trim()) out.push({ i: i, k: k, src: str(x.src), alt: str(x.alt), lib: x.lib || null, usage: x.usage || null }); });
      });
      return out;
    }
    /* a new block of a type, with sensible starting words */
    function blank(type) {
      var b = { id: uid(), type: type };
      if (type === 'hero') { b.style = 'cream'; b.lines = ['Your headline', 'goes here']; b.lead = ''; b.doodle = 'star'; b.kicker = ''; b.accent = 'gold'; b.moveE = true; }
      if (type === 'text') b.text = 'Hi {{first_name|there}},\n\n';
      if (type === 'heading') b.text = 'What to know';
      if (type === 'details') b.rows = [{ label: 'When', value: '' }, { label: 'Where', value: '' }];
      if (type === 'list') b.items = [{ lead: '', text: '' }, { lead: '', text: '' }];
      if (type === 'button') { b.label = 'Sign up'; b.url = ''; b.caption = ''; }
      if (type === 'photo') { b.src = ''; b.alt = ''; b.caption = ''; }
      if (type === 'photos') b.items = [{ src: '', alt: '', caption: '' }, { src: '', alt: '', caption: '' }, { src: '', alt: '', caption: '' }];
      if (type === 'video') { b.url = ''; b.title = ''; b.caption = 'Tap to watch'; }
      if (type === 'band') { b.title = ''; b.text = ''; b.links = [{ label: '', url: '' }]; }
      if (type === 'quote') { b.quote = ''; b.by = ''; }
      if (type === 'stats') { b.style = 'green'; b.title = ''; b.items = [{ n: '', label: '' }, { n: '', label: '' }, { n: '', label: '' }]; b.note = ''; }
      if (type === 'signoff') { b.closing = 'Warmly,'; b.name = ''; b.role = 'Welfare Avenue'; b.ps = ''; }
      if (type === 'divider') b.style = 'rule';
      return b;
    }
    /* a design from anywhere (the AI, a saved row) made safe and complete: known types and fields only, text as text */
    function clean(d) {
      d = d && typeof d === 'object' ? d : {};
      var S = function (v, n) { return str(v).slice(0, n || 4000); };
      var blocks = (Array.isArray(d.blocks) ? d.blocks : []).filter(function (b) { return b && TYPES.indexOf(b.type) >= 0; }).slice(0, 40).map(function (b) {
        var o = { id: /^b[a-z0-9]{3,12}$/.test(str(b.id)) ? b.id : uid(), type: b.type };
        if (b.type === 'hero') { o.style = b.style === 'green' ? 'green' : 'cream'; o.kicker = S(b.kicker, 80); o.lines = (Array.isArray(b.lines) ? b.lines : [b.lines]).map(function (x) { return S(x, 60); }).filter(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).slice(0, 4); o.lead = S(b.lead, 800);
          o.doodle = ['star', 'heart', 'flowers', 'none'].indexOf(b.doodle) >= 0 ? b.doodle : 'star'; o.accent = b.accent === 'purple' ? 'purple' : 'gold'; o.moveE = b.moveE !== false; if (b.gold != null && !isNaN(Number(b.gold))) o.gold = Number(b.gold); }
        if (b.type === 'text' || b.type === 'heading') o.text = S(b.text, b.type === 'text' ? 6000 : 120);
        if (b.type === 'details') o.rows = (Array.isArray(b.rows) ? b.rows : []).slice(0, 12).map(function (r) { return { label: S(r && r.label, 60), value: S(r && r.value, 400) }; });
        if (b.type === 'list') o.items = (Array.isArray(b.items) ? b.items : []).slice(0, 12).map(function (x) { return { lead: S(x && x.lead, 80), text: S(x && x.text, 600) }; });
        if (b.type === 'button') { o.label = S(b.label, 60); o.url = S(b.url, 800); o.caption = S(b.caption, 200); }
        if (b.type === 'photo') { o.src = S(b.src, 800); o.alt = S(b.alt, 200); o.caption = S(b.caption, 400); o.link = S(b.link, 800); if (b.lib) o.lib = S(b.lib, 40); if (b.usage) o.usage = S(b.usage, 20); }
        if (b.type === 'photos') o.items = (Array.isArray(b.items) ? b.items : []).slice(0, 3).map(function (x) { var y = { src: S(x && x.src, 800), alt: S(x && x.alt, 200), caption: S(x && x.caption, 300) }; if (x && x.lib) y.lib = S(x.lib, 40); if (x && x.usage) y.usage = S(x.usage, 20); return y; });
        if (b.type === 'video') {
          o.url = S(b.url, 800); o.title = S(b.title, 80); o.caption = S(b.caption, 120);
          var gu = safeImg(b.gif);   /* wave204: the moving preview, its size in pixels and in KB */
          if (gu && gu.length <= 800) { o.gif = gu; var gw = Math.round(Number(b.gw)), gh = Math.round(Number(b.gh)), gk = Math.round(Number(b.gkb)); if (gw > 0 && gw <= 1200) o.gw = gw; if (gh > 0 && gh <= 1600) o.gh = gh; if (gk > 0) o.gkb = gk; }
        }
        if (b.type === 'band') { o.title = S(b.title, 80); o.text = S(b.text, 1200); o.links = (Array.isArray(b.links) ? b.links : []).slice(0, 6).map(function (l) { return { label: S(l && l.label, 60), url: S(l && l.url, 800) }; }); }
        if (b.type === 'quote') { o.quote = S(b.quote, 600); o.by = S(b.by, 120); }
        if (b.type === 'stats') { o.style = b.style === 'cream' ? 'cream' : 'green'; o.title = S(b.title, 80); o.items = (Array.isArray(b.items) ? b.items : []).slice(0, 3).map(function (x) { return { n: S(x && x.n, 16), label: S(x && x.label, 60) }; }); o.note = S(b.note, 200); }
        if (b.type === 'signoff') { o.closing = S(b.closing, 40); o.name = S(b.name, 80); o.role = S(b.role, 120); o.ps = S(b.ps, 600); }
        if (b.type === 'divider') o.style = b.style === 'space' ? 'space' : 'rule';
        return o;
      });
      var asks = (Array.isArray(d.asks) ? d.asks : []).slice(0, 12).map(function (a) { return { id: S(a && a.id, 40).replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/gi, '') || uid(), label: S(a && a.label, 160), hint: S(a && a.hint, 200) }; }).filter(function (a) { return a.label; });
      var out = { v: 1, blocks: blocks };
      if (asks.length) out.asks = asks;
      if (d.source) out.source = S(d.source, 20);
      if (d.from) out.from = S(d.from, 40);
      if (d.voice === 'wa' || d.voice === 'mine') out.voice = d.voice;   /* wave203: the voice the Hub writes it in (never the guide itself) */
      if (d.ai && typeof d.ai === 'object') {   /* wave204: what the Hub's AI has cost on this email (calls, US dollars) */
        var calls = Math.round(Number(d.ai.calls)), cost = Number(d.ai.cost);
        if (calls > 0 && calls < 100000 && cost >= 0 && cost < 10000) out.ai = { calls: calls, cost: Math.round(cost * 1e6) / 1e6 };
      }
      return out;
    }

    /* ---------- mail-send's own rendering, ported for the live preview (fn/mail-send/index.ts v5: fill, styleInner, layout) ---------- */
    var RAW_KEYS = ['body_html', 'extra_html'];
    function fill(tpl, vars, html) {
      return str(tpl).replace(/\{\{\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\s*(?:\|([^}]*))?\}\}/g, function (_m, key, fb) {
        var v = vars[key], has = v != null && str(v).trim() !== '';
        if (has) return html && RAW_KEYS.indexOf(key) < 0 ? E(v) : str(v);
        return fb != null ? fb : '';
      });
    }
    function styleInner(html) {
      var Pst = 'margin:0 0 16px;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:16px;line-height:26px;color:' + INK + ';';
      var rl = '<table role="presentation" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" style="margin:0 0 14px;"><tr><td style="width:40px;height:3px;background-color:' + GOLD + ';font-size:0;line-height:0;">&nbsp;</td></tr></table>';
      return str(html)
        .replace(/<a href="/g, '<a style="color:' + PURPLE + ';text-decoration:underline;" href="')
        .replace(/<a class="btn" href="([^"]*)">([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/g, function (_m, href, label) {
          return '<table role="presentation" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" align="center" style="margin:26px auto;"><tr><td style="background-color:' + GOLD + ';border-radius:6px;">' +
            '<a href="' + href + '" style="display:inline-block;padding:14px 28px;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:600;font-size:18px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';text-decoration:none;">' + label + '</a></td></tr></table>';
        })
        .replace(/<h1>/g, '<h1 style="margin:0 0 18px;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:500;font-size:40px;line-height:44px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:' + GREEN + ';">')
        .replace(/<h2>/g, '<h2 style="margin:24px 0 8px;font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-weight:500;font-size:26px;line-height:30px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:' + GREEN + ';">')
        .replace(/<\/h2>/g, '</h2>' + rl)
        .replace(/<h3>/g, '<h3 style="margin:22px 0 8px;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-weight:700;font-size:13px;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';">')
        .replace(/<p>/g, '<p style="' + Pst + '">')
        .replace(/<(ul|ol)>/g, '<$1 style="margin:0 0 16px 22px;padding:0;">')
        .replace(/<li>/g, '<li style="margin:0 0 8px;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:16px;line-height:26px;color:' + INK + ';">')
        .replace(/<strong>/g, '<strong style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;">')
        .replace(/<mark>/g, '<span style="background-color:' + GOLD + ';color:' + GREEN + ';padding:0 4px;">').replace(/<\/mark>/g, '</span>')
        .replace(/<hr\s*\/?>/g, rl);
    }
    var REASON = {
      transactional: 'of your relationship with Welfare Avenue: a sign-up, a serving or a gift.',
      newsletter: 'you said yes to our news, or gave or volunteered with us in the last two years.',
      fundraising: 'you said yes to our news, or gave or volunteered with us in the last two years.',
      volunteer: 'you applied to volunteer, or volunteered, with Welfare Avenue.',
      test: 'it is a test the Hub sent to a team address.'
    };
    var OVERLINE = { transactional: 'From the Welfare Avenue team', newsletter: 'News from Welfare Avenue', fundraising: 'From Welfare Avenue', volunteer: 'Volunteering at Welfare Avenue', test: 'A Hub test' };
    var BULK = ['newsletter', 'fundraising', 'volunteer'];
    var LOGO_CREAM = ST + 'brand/logo-primary-cream-600.png', LOCKUP_CREAM = ST + 'brand/logo-lockup-cream-600.png';
    function layout(o) {
      var bulk = BULK.indexOf(o.kind) >= 0, cfg = o.cfg || {};
      var addr = cfg.mail_postal_address || 'Welfare Avenue, 8200 av Bloomfield, Montreal QC H3N 2H9';
      var charity = cfg.mail_charity_number || '713048502RR0001';
      var small = 'font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;line-height:20px;color:' + CREAM + ';';
      return '<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"><meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge"><title>' + E(o.title) + '</title>' +
        '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Barlow+Condensed:wght@500;600&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@400;500;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">' +
        '<style>body{margin:0;padding:0;background:' + CREAM + ';}table{border-collapse:collapse;}img{border:0;line-height:100%;}@media only screen and (max-width:620px){.wa-wrap{width:100% !important;}.wa-pad{padding-left:20px !important;padding-right:20px !important;}h1{font-size:32px !important;line-height:36px !important;}}</style></head>' +
        '<body style="margin:0;padding:0;background-color:' + CREAM + ';">' +
        (o.preheader ? '<div style="display:none;max-height:0;overflow:hidden;opacity:0;color:' + CREAM + ';font-size:1px;line-height:1px;">' + E(o.preheader) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<table role="presentation" width="100%" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" style="background-color:' + CREAM + ';"><tr><td align="center" style="padding:24px 12px;">' +
        '<table role="presentation" class="wa-wrap" width="600" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" style="width:600px;max-width:600px;">' +
