        '<tr><td class="wa-pad" style="background-color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:8px 8px 0 0;padding:28px 36px 24px 36px;">' +
        '<img src="' + LOGO_CREAM + '" width="170" height="57" alt="Welfare Avenue" style="display:block;border:0;width:170px;height:auto;">' +
        '<div style="padding:20px 0 0;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-weight:700;font-size:11px;line-height:14px;letter-spacing:2px;color:' + GOLD + ';text-transform:uppercase;">' + E(OVERLINE[o.kind] || OVERLINE.transactional) + '</div></td></tr>' +
        '<tr><td class="wa-pad" style="background-color:' + CREAM + ';padding:32px 36px 20px 36px;border-left:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.12);border-right:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.12);">' + o.inner + '</td></tr>' +
        '<tr><td class="wa-pad" style="background-color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:0 0 8px 8px;padding:26px 36px 28px 36px;">' +
        '<img src="' + LOCKUP_CREAM + '" width="140" height="63" alt="Welfare Avenue, building pathways in our community" style="display:block;border:0;width:140px;height:auto;">' +
        '<div style="padding:16px 0 0;' + small + 'opacity:.85;">Welfare Avenue is a registered charity, no. ' + E(charity) + '. ' + E(addr) + '.<br>You are receiving this email because ' + E(REASON[o.kind] || REASON.transactional) + '</div>' +
        '<div style="padding-top:12px;' + small + '"><a href="https://welfareavenue.com" style="color:' + CREAM + ';text-decoration:underline;">welfareavenue.com</a>' +
        (o.unsub ? ' &nbsp;&middot;&nbsp; <a href="' + o.unsub + '" style="color:' + CREAM + ';text-decoration:underline;">' + (bulk ? 'Unsubscribe' : 'Email preferences') + '</a>' : '') +
        '</div></td></tr></table></td></tr></table></body></html>';
    }
    /* the whole email as an inbox would show it, with sample values for the placeholders */
    function preview(o) {
      var vars = Object.assign({}, SAMPLE, o.vars || {});
      var inner = styleInner(fill(o.html != null ? o.html : render(o.design), vars, true));
      return layout({ inner: inner, preheader: fill(o.preheader || '', vars, false).trim(), kind: o.kind || 'newsletter', unsub: '#', cfg: o.cfg || {}, title: fill(o.subject || '', vars, false) });
    }

    return { TYPES: TYPES, LABEL: LABEL, KNOWN: KNOWN, SAMPLE: SAMPLE, DOODLE: DOODLE, EIMG: EIMG, ST: ST, W: W,
      COLORS: { GREEN: GREEN, CREAM: CREAM, INK: INK, PURPLE: PURPLE, GOLD: GOLD, RULE: RULE },
      E: E, safeUrl: safeUrl, safeImg: safeImg, inline: inline, plainInline: plainInline, paras: paras, uid: uid,
      render: render, renderMarked: renderMarked, renderText: renderText, renderBlock: function (b) { return R[b.type] ? R[b.type](b, { eDone: false }) : ''; },
      placeholders: placeholders, texts: texts, links: links, images: images, blank: blank, clean: clean,
      fill: fill, styleInner: styleInner, layout: layout, preview: preview };
  })();
  /* ---------- the studio's own checks (no AI): what would go out wrong or unfinished ----------
     stop: the email cannot go out until it is fixed (anything left in [brackets], no subject, a button with no link, a weekday
     that is not that date); warn: worth a look, the writer decides; note: a reminder. Each one points at its block. */
  var MONTHS = { january: 0, jan: 0, janvier: 0, janv: 0, february: 1, feb: 1, fevrier: 1, 'f\u00e9vrier': 1, fevr: 1, 'f\u00e9vr': 1, march: 2, mar: 2, mars: 2, april: 3, apr: 3, avril: 3, avr: 3,
    may: 4, mai: 4, june: 5, jun: 5, juin: 5, july: 6, jul: 6, juillet: 6, juil: 6, august: 7, aug: 7, aout: 7, 'ao\u00fbt': 7, september: 8, sep: 8, sept: 8, septembre: 8,
    october: 9, oct: 9, octobre: 9, november: 10, nov: 10, novembre: 10, december: 11, dec: 11, decembre: 11, 'd\u00e9cembre': 11, 'd\u00e9c': 11 };
  var DAYS = { sunday: 0, sun: 0, dimanche: 0, monday: 1, mon: 1, lundi: 1, tuesday: 2, tue: 2, tues: 2, mardi: 2, wednesday: 3, wed: 3, mercredi: 3,
    thursday: 4, thu: 4, thur: 4, thurs: 4, jeudi: 4, friday: 5, fri: 5, vendredi: 5, saturday: 6, sat: 6, samedi: 6 };
  var DAYNAME = ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday'];
  var MONNAME = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
  /* words Welfare Avenue does not use (wa-brand: we say / we do not say), with what to say instead */
  var AVOID = [
    [/\bthe homeless\b|\bhomeless people\b/i, 'the homeless', 'people we serve, our community'], [/\bbeneficiar(y|ies)\b/i, 'beneficiaries', 'people we serve'],
    [/\bpeople in need\b|\bthose in need\b|\bthe needy\b/i, 'people in need', 'people we serve, our community'], [/\bless fortunate\b/i, 'the less fortunate', 'people we serve'],
    [/\bfeed(ing)? (the|people|them)\b/i, 'feed people', 'serve meals, share a meal'], [/\bhand(ing)? out food\b|\bgive away (food|snacks)\b/i, 'hand out food', 'serve meals; food as a starting point'],
    [/\bstudent club\b/i, 'student club', 'youth-powered movement'], [/\bjust volunteers\b/i, 'just volunteers', 'our volunteers, future leaders'],
    [/\bmaking a difference together\b|\bin these (challenging|difficult|uncertain) times\b/i, 'charity boilerplate', 'say what happened, specifically'],
    [/\bles sans-abri\b|\bles itin\u00e9rants\b/i, 'les sans-abri', 'les personnes que nous servons, notre communaut\u00e9'], [/\bles d\u00e9munis\b|\bles plus d\u00e9munis\b|\bles pauvres\b/i, 'les d\u00e9munis', 'les personnes que nous servons'],
    [/\bb\u00e9n\u00e9ficiaires\b/i, 'b\u00e9n\u00e9ficiaires', 'les personnes que nous servons']
  ];
  function todayParts() {
    try { var p = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date()).split('-'); return { y: +p[0], m: +p[1] - 1, d: +p[2] }; }
    catch (e) { var n = new Date(); return { y: n.getFullYear(), m: n.getMonth(), d: n.getDate() }; }
  }
  function strip(s) { return str(s).toLowerCase().normalize ? str(s).toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') : str(s).toLowerCase(); }
  /* "Sunday 12 October", "Sunday, October 12", "Sun Oct 12 2026", "dimanche 12 octobre 2026": weekday, day, month, year? */
  function datesIn(s) {
    var out = [], t = str(s);
    var dn = '(sunday|monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sun|mon|tues?|wed|thu(?:rs?)?|fri|sat|dimanche|lundi|mardi|mercredi|jeudi|vendredi|samedi)';
    var mn = '(january|february|march|april|may|june|july|august|september|october|november|december|jan|feb|mar|apr|jun|jul|aug|sept?|oct|nov|dec|janvier|f[e\u00e9]vrier|mars|avril|mai|juin|juillet|ao[u\u00fb]t|septembre|octobre|novembre|d[e\u00e9]cembre|janv|f[e\u00e9]vr|avr|juil|d[e\u00e9]c)';
    var re1 = new RegExp('\\b' + dn + '\\.?,?\\s+(?:le\\s+)?(\\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th|er)?\\s+' + mn + '\\.?(?:,?\\s+(\\d{4}))?', 'gi');
    var re2 = new RegExp('\\b' + dn + '\\.?,?\\s+' + mn + '\\.?\\s+(\\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?(?:,?\\s+(\\d{4}))?', 'gi');
    var m;
    while ((m = re1.exec(t))) out.push({ text: m[0], dow: DAYS[strip(m[1]).replace(/\.$/, '')], day: +m[2], mon: MONTHS[strip(m[3]).replace(/\.$/, '')], year: m[4] ? +m[4] : null });
    while ((m = re2.exec(t))) out.push({ text: m[0], dow: DAYS[strip(m[1]).replace(/\.$/, '')], day: +m[3], mon: MONTHS[strip(m[2]).replace(/\.$/, '')], year: m[4] ? +m[4] : null });
    return out.filter(function (x) { return x.dow != null && x.mon != null && x.day >= 1 && x.day <= 31; });
  }
  function checkDate(x) {
    var t = todayParts(), y = x.year;
    if (!y) { y = t.y; var cand = new Date(Date.UTC(y, x.mon, x.day)), today = new Date(Date.UTC(t.y, t.m, t.d)); if (cand < new Date(today.getTime() - 30 * 86400000)) y = t.y + 1; }
    var dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, x.mon, x.day));
    if (dt.getUTCMonth() !== x.mon) return { bad: x.day + ' ' + MONNAME[x.mon] + ' does not exist' };
    var past = dt < new Date(Date.UTC(t.y, t.m, t.d));
    if (dt.getUTCDay() !== x.dow) return { bad: x.day + ' ' + MONNAME[x.mon] + ' ' + y + ' is a ' + DAYNAME[dt.getUTCDay()] + ', not a ' + DAYNAME[x.dow] };
    return { past: past && x.year ? true : false, y: y };
  }
  function where(i) { var e = SS.d; var b = e && e.design && e.design.blocks[i]; return b ? (MAIL202.LABEL[b.type] || b.type) : ''; }
  function checks(e) {
    e = e || SS.d; if (!e) return [];
    var out = [], seen = {};
    var add = function (lvl, msg, i, key) { var k = key || (lvl + msg); if (seen[k]) return; seen[k] = 1; out.push({ lvl: lvl, msg: msg, i: i == null ? null : i }); };
    var subject = str(e.subject).trim(), pre = str(e.preheader).trim(), d = e.design || { blocks: [] };
    if (!subject) add('stop', 'Write a subject: it is what people see first in their inbox.', null);
    else {
      if (subject.length > 70) add('warn', 'The subject is ' + subject.length + ' characters: phones cut it after about 40 to 60.', null);
      if (/[A-Z]{4}/.test(subject) && !/[a-z]/.test(subject.replace(/\{\{[^}]*\}\}/g, ''))) add('warn', 'The subject is all capitals, which reads as shouting (and as spam to some inboxes).', null);
    }
    if (!pre) add('warn', 'Add a preview line: it shows after the subject in most inboxes.', null);
    else if (lower(pre) === lower(subject)) add('warn', 'The preview line repeats the subject: use it to add something.', null);
    if (!d.blocks.length) add('stop', 'The email is empty: add a block or ask the Hub to put it together.', null);
    /* [brackets] the writer still has to fill in */
    var all = [{ i: null, text: subject }, { i: null, text: pre }].concat(MAIL202.texts(d));
    d.blocks.forEach(function (b, i) { if (b.type === 'button' || b.type === 'video') all.push({ i: i, text: str(b.url) }); (b.links || []).forEach(function (l) { if (l) all.push({ i: i, text: str(l.url) }); }); });
    all.forEach(function (t) {
      var re = /\[([^\]\n]{1,90})\](?!\()/g, m;
      while ((m = re.exec(t.text))) add('stop', 'Fill in: ' + m[1] + (t.i != null ? ' (' + where(t.i) + ')' : ' (the subject or preview line)'), t.i, 'br' + m[1] + t.i);
    });
    /* placeholders */
    var ph = MAIL202.placeholders(subject + ' ' + pre + ' ' + MAIL202.render(d));
    ph.filter(function (p) { return MAIL202.KNOWN.indexOf(p) < 0; }).forEach(function (p) { add('warn', '{{' + p + '}} is not something the Hub fills in, so it will be empty. Write it out instead.', null, 'ph' + p); });
    var allText = all.map(function (t) { return t.text; }).join('\n');
    if (/\{\{\s*first_name\s*\}\}/.test(allText)) add('note', 'Give {{first_name}} a fallback, like {{first_name|there}}, for people without a first name in the CRM.', null);
    var trig = ['amount', 'gift_date', 'gift_date_line', 'campaign', 'event_day', 'event_when', 'event_title', 'role', 'form_title'].filter(function (p) { return ph.indexOf(p) >= 0; });
    if (trig.length) add('note', 'It uses ' + trig.map(function (p) { return '{{' + p + '}}'; }).join(', ') + ', which only an automation fills (a gift, a serving or a form). Sent any other way, the fallback words show.', null);
    /* links and pictures */
    MAIL202.links(d).forEach(function (l) {
      var u = str(l.url).trim();
      if (!u) { if (l.need) add('stop', (l.label ? '\u201c' + l.label + '\u201d' : 'A button') + ' has no link (' + where(l.i) + ').', l.i); return; }
      if (/^\[/.test(u)) return;
      if (!MAIL202.safeUrl(u)) add('stop', '\u201c' + u + '\u201d is not a web address (' + where(l.i) + ').', l.i);
      else if (/example\.(com|org)|yourlink|lien ici|link here/i.test(u)) add('warn', '\u201c' + u + '\u201d looks like a stand-in link (' + where(l.i) + ').', l.i);
      else if (/^http:\/\//i.test(u)) add('note', u + ' is not https: a secure link is safer and fewer inboxes flag it.', l.i);
      if (l.inText && /^(click here|here|ici|cliquez ici)$/i.test(str(l.label).trim())) add('warn', 'Say where a link goes instead of \u201c' + l.label + '\u201d: it reads better and is clearer for screen readers.', l.i);
    });
    var imgs = MAIL202.images(d);
    imgs.forEach(function (p) {
      if (!str(p.alt).trim()) add('warn', 'A photo has no description (alt text): people using screen readers, and inboxes that block images, get nothing (' + where(p.i) + ').', p.i, 'alt' + p.i + '.' + p.k);
      if (p.usage && p.usage !== 'ready') add('warn', 'A Photo library picture is marked \u201c' + p.usage + '\u201d, not ready for public use: check it first (' + where(p.i) + ').', p.i, 'use' + p.i + '.' + p.k);
    });
    if (imgs.some(function (p) { return !p.lib; })) add('note', 'Uploaded photos: no close-ups of the people we serve, and anyone recognizable has said yes (the Photo library\u2019s rule).', null);
    d.blocks.forEach(function (b, i) { if ((b.type === 'photo' && !str(b.src).trim()) || (b.type === 'photos' && !(b.items || []).some(function (x) { return x && str(x.src).trim(); }))) add('warn', 'A photo block has no photo yet, so it will not show (' + where(i) + ').', i); });
    /* wave204: the moving preview (an animated GIF from the writer's own video) */
    d.blocks.forEach(function (b, i) {
      if (b.type !== 'video' || !str(b.gif).trim()) return;
      if (Number(b.gkb) > 2500) add('warn', 'The moving preview is ' + kbText(b.gkb) + ': heavy for a phone on data. Make a lighter or shorter one (' + where(i) + ').', i, 'mvkb' + i);
      add('note', 'A moving preview comes from your own video: the photo rule applies (no close-ups of the people we serve, and anyone recognizable has said yes).', i, 'mvrule');
    });
    /* dates */
    all.forEach(function (t) {
      datesIn(t.text).forEach(function (x) {
        var r = checkDate(x);
        if (r.bad) add('stop', '\u201c' + x.text.trim() + '\u201d: ' + r.bad + '. Which is right?', t.i, 'dt' + x.text);
        else if (r.past) add('warn', '\u201c' + x.text.trim() + '\u201d has passed.', t.i, 'dp' + x.text);
      });
    });
    /* Welfare Avenue's words */
    all.forEach(function (t) {
      if (/\u2014|\s\u2013\s/.test(t.text)) add('warn', 'An em dash: Welfare Avenue writes without them. Use a comma, a colon or a full stop' + (t.i != null ? ' (' + where(t.i) + ')' : '') + '.', t.i, 'dash' + t.i);
      AVOID.forEach(function (a) { if (a[0].test(t.text)) add('warn', '\u201c' + a[1] + '\u201d: we say ' + a[2] + '.', t.i, 'av' + a[1]); });
      if ((t.text.match(/!/g) || []).length > 2) add('note', 'Lots of exclamation marks: one is plenty.', t.i, 'excl');
    });
    /* the kind under CASL */
    var asks = MAIL202.links(d).some(function (l) { return /\/donate|\/give|\bdon\b|donner/i.test(str(l.url)); });
    if (e.kind === 'transactional' && asks) add('warn', 'It asks for gifts, which makes it an appeal under Canada\u2019s anti-spam law: choose \u201cAn appeal for gifts\u201d as its kind.', null);
    d.blocks.forEach(function (b, i) { if (b.type === 'hero') (b.lines || []).forEach(function (l) { if (str(l).replace(/\{\{[^}]*\}\}/g, 'xxxxxxxx').length > 20) add('note', 'A headline line over 20 letters wraps on a phone (' + where(i) + ').', i, 'hl' + i); }); });
    if (!d.blocks.some(function (b) { return b.type === 'signoff'; }) && d.blocks.length) add('note', 'No sign-off: people reply more to an email signed by a person.', null);
    var order = { stop: 0, warn: 1, note: 2 };
    return out.sort(function (a, b) { return order[a.lvl] - order[b.lvl]; });
  }
  function stops(e) { return checks(e).filter(function (c) { return c.lvl === 'stop'; }); }
  /* ---------- the Hub's AI: putting an email together, filling a template, changing it, asking what is missing ----------
     One tool (build_email) through the llm router (x-wa-task email_studio, x-wa-caller email-studio). The model returns the
     blocks, the subject and preview line, and questions about anything missing or doubtful; it never invents a date, a
     place, a number, a name or a link: it leaves a [bracket] where one goes and asks. Photos go by their index; uploaded
     ones are sent as small pictures so the model can describe them. */
  var BLOCK_PROPS = {
    type: { type: 'string', enum: ['hero', 'text', 'heading', 'details', 'list', 'button', 'photo', 'photos', 'video', 'band', 'quote', 'stats', 'signoff', 'divider'] },
    style: { type: 'string', description: 'hero: cream (default) or green; stats: green or cream' },
    kicker: { type: 'string', description: 'hero: a small line above the headline, 2 to 5 words (a date, a place, the occasion)' },
    lines: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' }, description: 'hero: the headline in 2 short lines (under 16 letters each); the last line is the coloured one' },
    lead: { type: 'string', description: 'hero: one sentence under the headline' },
    doodle: { type: 'string', enum: ['star', 'heart', 'flowers', 'none'] },
    text: { type: 'string', description: 'text: paragraphs separated by a blank line; **bold** and [words](https://link) allowed. heading: the heading words.' },
    title: { type: 'string', description: 'video, band and stats: the title' },
    rows: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { label: { type: 'string' }, value: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['label', 'value'] }, description: 'details: label and value rows (When, Where, Bring...)' },
    items: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { lead: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, n: { type: 'string' }, label: { type: 'string' } } }, description: 'list: {lead (a bold start), text}; stats: {n (the number as written), label}' },
    label: { type: 'string', description: 'button: the words on it, 2 to 4 words' },
    url: { type: 'string', description: 'button and video: the full https link' },
    caption: { type: 'string', description: 'button: a short purple line under it; photo: a caption; video: the line under the title' },
    photo: { type: 'integer', description: 'photo: the index of one of the photos given' },
    photos: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'integer' }, description: 'photos: 2 or 3 indexes of the photos given' },
    alt: { type: 'string', description: 'photo: what the photo shows, for screen readers' },
    captions: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' }, description: 'photos: a caption per photo (or empty strings)' },
    links: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { label: { type: 'string' }, url: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['label', 'url'] }, description: 'band: link buttons' },
    quote: { type: 'string' }, by: { type: 'string' },
    note: { type: 'string', description: 'stats: a small note under the numbers' },
    closing: { type: 'string', description: 'signoff: Warmly, / With gratitude, / Merci,' }, name: { type: 'string', description: 'signoff: who signs it' }, role: { type: 'string', description: 'signoff: their role or Welfare Avenue' },
    ps: { type: 'string', description: 'signoff: an optional P.S. line' }
  };
  var AI_TOOL = {
    name: 'build_email',
    description: 'Return the whole email: its subject, preview line, blocks in order, and questions for the writer about anything missing or doubtful.',
    input_schema: { type: 'object', properties: {
      name: { type: 'string', description: 'A short name for the team, e.g. "12 October call-out"' },
      subject: { type: 'string' }, preheader: { type: 'string', description: 'The preview line after the subject in the inbox' },
      blocks: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: BLOCK_PROPS, required: ['type'] } },
      questions: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { ask: { type: 'string' }, why: { type: 'string' }, hint: { type: 'string', description: 'An example answer' } }, required: ['ask'] } },
      notes: { type: 'string', description: 'One or two lines for the writer about what you did' }
    }, required: ['subject', 'preheader', 'blocks', 'questions'] }
  };
  /* the voice part of the prompt (wave203): the house voice code, the writer's own guide, or (neither loaded) the built-in paragraph */
  var BUILTIN_VOICE = 'Write in Welfare Avenue\'s voice: warm, specific, credible, plain. Short paragraphs. Impact first. Solidarity, never charity from above. Say "people we serve", "our community", "support", "dignity", "youth-powered". Never say "the homeless", "beneficiaries", "people in need", "the less fortunate", "feed people", "hand out food", "student club", "just volunteers". No em dashes or en dashes as punctuation: use a comma, a colon or a full stop. No hashtags, at most one exclamation mark, no boilerplate like "in these challenging times" or "making a difference together".';
  function voiceBlock(v) {
    if (v && v.key === 'mine' && str(v.text).trim()) return 'VOICE: write as ' + str(v.name || 'the writer') + ', who signs this email, in their own voice. Their writing guide is between the two lines below. Use it for how the words sound: word choice, rhythm, openings and closings. The shape of the email, the facts rule, the links and Welfare Avenue\'s words after it still apply, and anything in the guide about replies, threads, attachments, plain text or tools does not.\n-----\n' + str(v.text).trim() + '\n-----';
    if (v && str(v.text).trim()) return 'VOICE: Welfare Avenue\'s house voice, the Voice Code between the two lines below. Use it for how the words sound. The shape of the email, the facts rule and the links after it still apply.\n-----\n' + str(v.text).trim() + '\n-----';
    return BUILTIN_VOICE;
  }
  function aiSystem(lang, voice) {
    var fr = lang === 'fr';
    return [
      'You put together emails for Welfare Avenue, a youth-powered registered charity in Montreal that fights food insecurity and poverty through direct community action: Sunday servings of meals cooked, driven and served by young volunteers, and the connections to support that follow. Food is the starting point.',
      voiceBlock(voice),
      'Whatever the voice: never "the homeless", "beneficiaries", "people in need", "the less fortunate", "feed people", "hand out food", "student club" or "just volunteers"; no em dashes or en dashes as punctuation (a comma, a colon or a full stop instead); no hashtags; at most one exclamation mark.',
      'Facts: never invent a date, a time, a place, an amount, a number, a name, a quote or a link. Use only what the writer gave, the template\'s own words, and these public figures: 20,000+ meals since April 2020, 250+ servings, 600+ volunteers (our best estimates from our records, rounded down); giving: $25 covers one recipe (a fruit salad for 8 to 10 people), $75 one volunteer cook\'s batch (a dish for 30 to 35 people), $250 the groceries for a whole Sunday serving (about 50 plates), $20 a month about 100 portions a year. Add no claims the writer did not make about how things go (no experience needed, training, who will be there, what happens after signing up, what to bring, food, parking, how long it takes): leave them out, or write a [placeholder] and ask when the email needs one. When something the email needs is missing, write a short [placeholder in square brackets] where it goes and ask about it in questions. When two things disagree (a weekday that is not that date, times that do not add up, a number that changed), ask. Today is ' + todayLine() + ' in Montreal; the next three weeks: ' + nextDays(21) + '. A date without a year is the next one to come. Work out "this Saturday" or "next Sunday" from that list, never from memory.',
      'Content: the writer\'s own content comes first. Build the email from what they gave, keeping their words, facts and order where they work. Never write what they did not give: no filler paragraphs, no invented stories, examples, feelings or reasons. When what they gave is thin, keep the email short and ask for more in your questions rather than padding it.',
      'Links you may use when they fit: https://welfareavenue.com (the website), https://welfareavenue.com/volunteer/ (volunteer sign-up), https://welfareavenue.com/donate/ (giving). Any other link only if the writer gave it.',
      'Shape: one hero first (a kicker, a headline in two short lines, the last line the coloured one, a lead sentence, a doodle), then "Hi {{first_name|there}}," and short paragraphs; details for when and where (and what to bring, when the writer said); a dot list for two to five parallel points; one gold button for the one thing to do (a caption under it only when the writer gave one or it is plainly true, like "On our website"); the writer\'s photos where they help; a sign-off with the sender\'s name when known, else "[Who signs it]". 120 to 300 words unless the material needs more. Keep every {{placeholder}} exactly as written, and use {{first_name|there}} for the greeting.',
      'Subject: under 60 characters, specific, honest, may use {{first_name|friend}}. Preview line: under 90 characters, adds to the subject.',
      'Questions: at most five, only what would block the email or embarrass us if wrong, each a short plain question with a one-line why and an example answer. No questions when nothing is missing.',
      fr ? 'Write the email in French as spoken in Quebec, with "vous", natural and warm (not a translation). Keep the placeholders as they are: {{first_name|there}} becomes {{first_name|}} after "Bonjour".' : 'Write in Canadian English.',
      'Answer only by calling build_email once.'
    ].join('\n\n');
  }
  function todayLine() {
    var t = todayParts(); var dt = new Date(Date.UTC(t.y, t.m, t.d));
    return DAYNAME[dt.getUTCDay()] + ' ' + t.d + ' ' + MONNAME[t.m] + ' ' + t.y;
  }
  /* the coming days written out, so the model never works out a weekday itself (wave202: the first real call called 11 October 2026 a Saturday) */
  function nextDays(n) {
    var t = todayParts(), out = [];
    for (var k = 1; k <= n; k++) { var dt = new Date(Date.UTC(t.y, t.m, t.d + k)); out.push(DAYNAME[dt.getUTCDay()] + ' ' + dt.getUTCDate() + ' ' + MONNAME[dt.getUTCMonth()]); }
    return out.join(', ');
  }
  /* the writer's dates checked by the Hub (d_checks' datesIn and checkDate): a weekday that is not that date, a date that does not exist */
  function dateNotes(texts) {
    var seen = {}, out = [];
    (texts || []).forEach(function (t) { datesIn(t).forEach(function (x) { var c = checkDate(x); if (c.bad && !seen[c.bad]) { seen[c.bad] = 1; out.push('- "' + x.text.trim() + '": ' + c.bad + '.'); } }); });
    return out;
  }
  function photoLines(photos) {
    return (photos || []).map(function (p, i) { return i + ': ' + (p.lib ? 'from our Photo library, "' + str(p.title || p.alt) + '"' + (p.alt ? ' (' + p.alt + ')' : '') : 'uploaded by the writer' + (p.data ? ' (image ' + (i + 1) + ' below)' : '')) + (p.caption ? ', caption: ' + p.caption : ''); }).join('\n');
  }
  /* the design as the model reads it: photos by index into the photo list (added to it when new), no ids */
  function designForAi(d, photos) {
    var idx = function (src, alt, lib, usage) {
      for (var k = 0; k < photos.length; k++) if (photos[k].src === src) return k;
      photos.push({ src: src, alt: alt || '', lib: lib || null, usage: usage || null, title: '' }); return photos.length - 1;
    };
    return JSON.stringify((d && d.blocks || []).map(function (b) {
      var o = {};
      Object.keys(b).forEach(function (k) { if (['id', 'lib', 'usage', 'src', 'items', 'gif', 'gw', 'gh', 'gkb'].indexOf(k) < 0 && b[k] !== '' && b[k] != null) o[k] = b[k]; });   /* wave204: a moving preview stays with the card (keepMoving), the model never sees it */
      if (b.type === 'photo' && str(b.src).trim()) o.photo = idx(b.src, b.alt, b.lib, b.usage);
      if (b.type === 'photos') { var its = (b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && str(x.src).trim(); }); o.photos = its.map(function (x) { return idx(x.src, x.alt, x.lib, x.usage); }); o.captions = its.map(function (x) { return str(x.caption); }); }
      else if (b.items) o.items = b.items;
      return o;
    }));
  }
  function aiMessages(o, photos) {
    var who = myName(), k = KIND[o.kind] || KIND.newsletter;
    var head = 'Audience: ' + (AUD[o.audience] || 'Everyone') + '. Kind: ' + k.l + ' (' + k.s + '). Language: ' + (o.lang === 'fr' ? 'French' : 'English') + '. The writer: ' + who + ' (' + myEmail() + ').';
    var parts = [head];
    if (o.mode === 'new') parts.push('Put this email together from what the writer wrote:\n"""\n' + str(o.brief).trim() + '\n"""');
    if (o.mode === 'template') {
      parts.push('Fill in this template for the writer. Keep its shape and its fixed words; replace every [bracket] with the writer\'s details, rewrite what their details change, and drop a block only when it cannot apply. The template, "' + str(o.tplName) + '":\n' + designForAi(o.design, photos) + '\nSubject: ' + str(o.subject) + '\nPreview line: ' + str(o.preheader));
      var asks = (o.asks || []).filter(function (a) { return str(a.answer).trim(); }).map(function (a) { return '- ' + a.label + ' ' + str(a.answer).trim(); }).join('\n');
      parts.push('The writer\'s details:\n' + (asks || '(none given)') + (str(o.brief).trim() ? '\nAnything else they want to say:\n"""\n' + str(o.brief).trim() + '\n"""' : ''));
    }
    if (o.mode === 'change' || o.mode === 'answers') {
      parts.push('The email as it stands:\n' + designForAi(o.design, photos) + '\nSubject: ' + str(o.subject) + '\nPreview line: ' + str(o.preheader));
      if (o.mode === 'change') parts.push('The writer asks: ' + str(o.instruction).trim() + '\nReturn the whole email with that change, and keep everything else as it is (photos by the same index).');
      else parts.push('The writer answered your questions:\n' + (o.qa || []).map(function (q) { return '- ' + q.ask + ' ' + (str(q.answer).trim() || '(no answer)'); }).join('\n') + '\nPut the answers in, remove the [brackets] they fill, and keep everything else as it is. Ask again only about what is still missing.');
    }
    var said = [str(o.brief), str(o.instruction)].concat((o.asks || []).map(function (a) { return str(a.answer); }), (o.qa || []).map(function (q) { return str(q.answer); }));
    if (o.mode === 'answers' && o.design) said = said.concat(MAIL202.texts(o.design).map(function (t) { return t.text; }));
    var dn = dateNotes(said);
    if (dn.length) parts.push('The Hub checked the dates (trust this over your own reckoning):\n' + dn.join('\n') + '\nUnless the writer\'s answers settle it, keep their words in the email and ask which is right.');
    if (photos.length) parts.push('Photos (use them by index in photo and photos blocks):\n' + photoLines(photos));
    var content = [{ type: 'text', text: parts.join('\n\n') }];
    photos.forEach(function (p) { if (p.data) content.push({ type: 'image', source: { type: 'base64', media_type: 'image/jpeg', data: p.data } }); });
    return [{ role: 'user', content: content }];
  }
  async function aiCall(o) {
    var photos = (o.photos || []).slice();
    try { await loadVoices(); } catch (e) { /* the built-in voice then */ }
    var body = { max_tokens: 6000, temperature: 0.4, system: aiSystem(o.lang, voiceFor(o.voice)), tools: [AI_TOOL], tool_choice: { type: 'tool', name: 'build_email' }, messages: aiMessages(o, photos) };
    var r;
    try { r = await db.functions.invoke('llm', { headers: { 'x-wa-task': 'email_studio', 'x-wa-caller': 'email-studio' }, body: body }); }
    catch (e) { return { error: msgOf(e) }; }
    if (!r || r.error) { var m = msgOf(r && r.error); if (/401|unauthor/i.test(m)) m = 'Writing with the Hub is not open to this account.'; return { error: m }; }
    var tu = ((r.data && r.data.content) || []).filter(function (c) { return c && c.type === 'tool_use' && c.input; })[0];
    var cost = r.data && r.data._wa && typeof r.data._wa.cost === 'number' ? r.data._wa.cost : null;   /* wave204: what this call cost (US dollars) */
    if (!tu) return { error: 'The Hub did not send an email back. Try again, or build it yourself.', cost: cost };
    return { input: tu.input, photos: photos, cost: cost };
  }
  /* the model's answer made into a studio design: blocks cleaned, photos put back by index */
  function fromAi(input, photos) {
    photos = photos || [];
    var raw = (input && Array.isArray(input.blocks) ? input.blocks : []).map(function (b) {
      b = Object.assign({}, b || {});
      if (b.type === 'photo') { var p = photos[Number(b.photo)]; if (p) { b.src = p.src; b.lib = p.lib || undefined; b.usage = p.usage || undefined; if (!str(b.alt).trim()) b.alt = p.alt || ''; } else b.src = ''; }
      if (b.type === 'photos') {
        var idx = Array.isArray(b.photos) ? b.photos : [], caps = Array.isArray(b.captions) ? b.captions : [];
        b.items = idx.map(function (n, k) { var p = photos[Number(n)]; return p ? { src: p.src, alt: p.alt || '', caption: str(caps[k] || ''), lib: p.lib || undefined, usage: p.usage || undefined } : null; }).filter(Boolean);
      }
      if (b.type === 'hero') { if (!Array.isArray(b.lines)) b.lines = str(b.lines).split(/\n/); b.moveE = true; }
      if (b.type === 'stats' && Array.isArray(b.items)) b.items = b.items.map(function (x) { return { n: str(x && (x.n || x.lead)), label: str(x && (x.label || x.text)) }; });
      return b;
    });
    var design = MAIL202.clean({ v: 1, blocks: raw });
    var qs = (input && Array.isArray(input.questions) ? input.questions : []).slice(0, 5).map(function (q, i) { return { id: 'q' + i + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), ask: str(q && q.ask).slice(0, 300), why: str(q && q.why).slice(0, 300), hint: str(q && q.hint).slice(0, 200), answer: '' }; }).filter(function (q) { return q.ask; });
    return { design: design, subject: str(input && input.subject).slice(0, 300), preheader: str(input && input.preheader).slice(0, 300), name: str(input && input.name).slice(0, 120), questions: qs, notes: str(input && input.notes).slice(0, 600) };
  }
  /* ---------- photos: the Photo library (ready ones first) or an upload, made light for email ----------
     An upload is shrunk in the browser (1200 px at most, JPEG) and stored in the public content-images bucket under
     email/<year>/<month>/, so every inbox can load it; a small copy stays in memory for the Hub's AI to look at. */
  var LIBCOLS = 'id,title,alt_text,caption,usage,url_web,url_thumb,orientation,scene,tags,season,is_pick';
  function loadLibrary() {
    if (SS.lib || SS.libBusy) return Promise.resolve(SS.lib);
    SS.libBusy = true;
    return Promise.resolve(db.from('wa_photos_list').select(LIBCOLS).limit(400)).then(function (r) {
      SS.libBusy = false;
      var rows = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (p) { return p && (p.url_web || p.url_thumb); });
      var rank = function (p) { return (p.usage === 'ready' ? 0 : p.usage === 'crop' ? 1 : 2) * 10 + (p.is_pick ? 0 : 1); };
      SS.lib = rows.sort(function (a, b) { return rank(a) - rank(b) || (Number(b.id) - Number(a.id)); });
      return SS.lib;
    }, function () { SS.libBusy = false; SS.lib = []; return SS.lib; });
  }
  function libMatch(p, q) {
    if (!q) return true;
    var hay = lower([p.title, p.alt_text, p.caption, p.scene, p.season, (p.tags || []).join(' ')].join(' '));
    return q.split(/\s+/).every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; });
  }
  function fromLib(p) { return { src: p.url_web || p.url_thumb, thumb: p.url_thumb || p.url_web, alt: str(p.alt_text || p.title).slice(0, 200), title: p.title || '', lib: 'photo:' + p.id, usage: p.usage || null, caption: '' }; }
  function readImage(file) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var url = URL.createObjectURL(file), img = new Image();
      img.onload = function () { res({ img: img, url: url }); };
      img.onerror = function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); rej(new Error('This photo could not be read here: use a JPG or PNG.')); };
      img.src = url;
    });
  }
  function shrink(img, max, q) {
    var w = img.naturalWidth || img.width, h = img.naturalHeight || img.height, s = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(w, h));
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * s)); c.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * s));
    var g = c.getContext('2d'); g.fillStyle = CREAM; g.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height); g.drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    return { canvas: c, w: c.width, h: c.height, q: q };
  }
  function toBlob(sh) { return new Promise(function (res) { sh.canvas.toBlob(function (b) { res(b); }, 'image/jpeg', sh.q); }); }
  async function uploadPhoto(file) {
    if (!file || !/^image\//.test(file.type || 'image/')) throw new Error('That is not a photo.');
    if (file.size > 25 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('That photo is over 25 MB.');
    var r = await readImage(file);
    try {
      var big = shrink(r.img, 1200, 0.82), small = shrink(r.img, 512, 0.7);
      var blob = await toBlob(big);
      var now = new Date(), path = 'email/' + now.getFullYear() + '/' + ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '/' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10) + Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4) + '.jpg';
      var up = await db.storage.from('content-images').upload(path, blob, { contentType: 'image/jpeg', upsert: false, cacheControl: '31536000' });
      if (up && up.error) throw new Error('The upload did not go through: ' + msgOf(up.error));
      var pub = db.storage.from('content-images').getPublicUrl(path);
      var src = pub && pub.data && pub.data.publicUrl;
      if (!src) throw new Error('The upload did not give back a link.');
      var data = small.canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.7).split(',')[1] || '';
      return { src: src, thumb: src, alt: '', title: str(file.name).replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, ''), lib: null, usage: null, caption: '', data: data.length < 400000 ? data : '' };
    } finally { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(r.url); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  /* the picker: where the chosen photo goes is SS.pick ({to: 'start'} or {to: 'block', i, k}) */
  function pickerHtml() {
    var p = SS.pick; if (!p) return '';
    var lib = SS.lib, q = lower(SS.libQ);
    var grid = !lib ? busyHtml('Loading the Photo library\u2026') : (function () {
      var rows = lib.filter(function (x) { return libMatch(x, q); }).slice(0, 120);
      if (!rows.length) return '<p class="m">No photo matches \u201c' + E(SS.libQ) + '\u201d.</p>';
      return '<div class="w202pgrid" data-w198skip>' + rows.map(function (x) {
        var ready = x.usage === 'ready';
        return '<button class="w202pcell' + (ready ? '' : ' notready') + '" data-w202libpick="' + E(x.id) + '" title="' + E(x.title || '') + '"><img src="' + E(x.url_thumb || x.url_web) + '" alt="' + E(x.alt_text || x.title || '') + '" loading="lazy">' +
          '<span>' + E(x.title || '') + '</span>' + (ready ? '' : '<em>' + E(x.usage || 'check') + '</em>') + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    })();
    return '<div class="w202modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Choose a photo"><div class="w202mbox">' +
      '<div class="w202mh"><h3>' + I('image') + ' Choose a photo</h3><button class="w202x" data-w202pickx aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w202prow"><label class="w202upl w152btn ghost">' + I('upload') + ' Upload ' + (p.to === 'start' ? 'photos' : 'a photo') + '<input type="file" accept="image/*"' + (p.to === 'start' ? ' multiple' : '') + ' data-w202file="pick" hidden></label>' +
      '<input class="w152in" data-w202libq placeholder="Search the Photo library: volunteers, food, winter\u2026" value="' + E(SS.libQ) + '" aria-label="Search the Photo library"></div>' +
      (SS.uploads ? busyHtml('Uploading ' + plural(SS.uploads, 'photo', 'photos') + '\u2026') : '') +
      '<p class="m w202pnote">Ready photos first: our volunteers, food and setups, no close-ups of the people we serve. A photo marked otherwise needs a check before it goes out.</p>' + grid + '</div></div>';
  }
  function photoChosen(ph) {
    var p = SS.pick; if (!p || !ph) return;
    if (p.to === 'start') { if (SS.start.photos.length < 6) SS.start.photos.push(ph); }
    else if (p.to === 'block' && SS.d) {
      var b = SS.d.design.blocks[p.i]; if (!b) return;
      var tgt = b.type === 'photos' ? (b.items[p.k] = b.items[p.k] || { src: '', alt: '', caption: '' }) : b;
      tgt.src = ph.src; tgt.alt = ph.alt || tgt.alt || ''; if (ph.lib) tgt.lib = ph.lib; else delete tgt.lib; if (ph.usage) tgt.usage = ph.usage; else delete tgt.usage;
      changed(true);
    }
  }
  async function uploadFiles(files, to) {
    files = Array.prototype.slice.call(files || [], 0, to === 'start' ? Math.max(0, 6 - SS.start.photos.length) : 1);
    if (!files.length) return;
    SS.uploads = files.length; SS.err = ''; paint();
    for (var i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
      try {
        var ph = await uploadPhoto(files[i]);
        if (to === 'start-direct') SS.start.photos.push(ph); else photoChosen(ph);
      } catch (e) { SS.err = msgOf(e); }
      SS.uploads = Math.max(0, SS.uploads - 1);
    }
    SS.pick = null;
    paint();
  }
  /* ---------- the studio's front page: three ways to start, the templates on their shelves, the recent emails ---------- */
  function studioTemplates() { return templates().filter(function (t) { return t.design && !t.one_off; }); }
  function recentOneOffs() {
    return templates().filter(function (t) { return t.design && t.one_off; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return str(b.updated_at).localeCompare(str(a.updated_at)); });
  }
  function hasBrackets(t) { return /\[[^\]\n]{1,90}\](?!\()/.test(str(t.subject) + ' ' + str(t.preheader) + ' ' + str(t.html_body)); }
  function miniHero(t) {
    var h = ((t.design && t.design.blocks) || []).filter(function (b) { return b.type === 'hero'; })[0];
    var lines = h ? (h.lines || []) : [t.name], green = h && h.style === 'green';
    var sv = function (s) { return MAIL202.fill(s, MAIL202.SAMPLE, false); };   /* placeholders shown as a reader would see them */
    return '<div class="w202mini' + (green ? ' g' : '') + '" aria-hidden="true">' + (h && h.kicker ? '<i>' + E(sv(h.kicker)) + '</i>' : '') +
      lines.slice(0, 3).map(function (l, k) { return '<b' + (k === lines.length - 1 ? ' class="a"' : '') + '>' + E(sv(l)) + '</b>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function tplCard(t) {
    var k = KIND[t.kind] || { short: t.kind }, mine = canEdit(t), fill = hasBrackets(t);
    return '<div class="w202card" role="listitem">' + miniHero(t) +
      '<div class="w202cb" data-w198skip><b>' + E(t.name) + '</b><span class="m">' + E(t.description || t.subject) + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w202chips"><span class="w202chip">' + E(k.short) + '</span>' + (t.audience ? '<span class="w202chip p">' + E(AUD[t.audience] || t.audience) + '</span>' : '') +
      (t.created_by === 'starter' ? '<span class="w202chip g">Starter</span>' : '') + (!t.active ? '<span class="w202chip x">Switched off</span>' : '') + (fill ? '' : '<span class="w202chip t" title="No [brackets] left: an automation can use it as it is">Ready as is</span>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w202ca">' + (fill ? '<button class="w152btn sm" data-w202fill="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('spark') + ' Fill it in</button>' : '<button class="w152btn sm" data-w202use="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('copy') + ' Use it</button>') +
      '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202peek="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('eye') + ' Look</button>' +
      (mine ? '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202edit="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('edit') + ' Edit</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function shelvesHtml(list, compact) {
    var f = SS.filter;
    var rows = list.filter(function (t) { return f === 'all' || (t.audience === f) || (f === 'donors' && t.audience === 'everyone') || (f === 'volunteers' && t.audience === 'everyone'); });
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="m">No templates here yet.</p>';
    return SHELF.map(function (s) {
      var on = rows.filter(function (t) { return (t.category || 'other') === s[0]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0) || str(a.name).localeCompare(str(b.name)); });
      if (!on.length) return '';
      return '<div class="w202shelf"><h4>' + E(s[1]) + ' <span class="m">' + on.length + '</span></h4><div class="w202grid' + (compact ? ' c' : '') + '" role="list">' + on.map(compact ? tplRow : tplCard).join('') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function tplRow(t) {
    var on = SS.start.tpl === t.key;
    return '<button class="w202trow' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" role="listitem" data-w202tplpick="' + E(t.key) + '" aria-pressed="' + on + '">' + miniHero(t) + '<span data-w198skip><b>' + E(t.name) + '</b><span class="m">' + E(t.description || t.subject) + '</span></span></button>';
  }
  function filterHtml() {
    return '<div class="w202filt" role="group" aria-label="Who the templates are for">' + [['all', 'All'], ['volunteers', 'Volunteers'], ['donors', 'Donors']].map(function (f) {
      return '<button class="w202fb' + (SS.filter === f[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w202filter="' + f[0] + '" aria-pressed="' + (SS.filter === f[0]) + '">' + E(f[1]) + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function homeHtml() {
    var mine = recentOneOffs(), me = myEmail();
    var rec = mine.filter(function (t) { return lower(t.created_by) === me; }).concat(mine.filter(function (t) { return lower(t.created_by) !== me; })).slice(0, SS.allRecent ? 60 : 8);
    return '<div class="w202">' +
      '<section class="w202top" data-w202sec="start"><div class="w202intro"><span class="w202kick">Email studio</span><h2>Emails in the brand, put together with you</h2>' +
      '<p>For one person, a list or an automation. Bring your content first: your words, the facts and your photos. The Hub shapes them into a Welfare Avenue email and checks it with you before anything goes out; it does not write the email for you.</p>' +
      '<div class="w204ibtns"><button class="w202vbtn" data-w202voices="wa">' + I('voice') + ' Voices <span>Welfare Avenue' + (SS.voices.mine ? ' \u00b7 My voice' : '') + '</span></button>' + costsChip() + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w202ways">' +
      '<button class="w202way" data-w202go="#crm/studio/ai">' + I('spark') + '<b>Tell the Hub</b><span>Paste or write what you want to say and add your photos. The Hub designs the email, no HTML needed, and asks about anything missing.</span></button>' +
      '<button class="w202way" data-w202go="#crm/studio/tpl">' + I('layout') + '<b>Start from a template</b><span>Pick one of our templates and give it your details. The Hub fills it in, or you do.</span></button>' +
      '<button class="w202way" data-w202blank>' + I('blocks') + '<b>Build it yourself</b><span>Blocks you arrange: a headline, text, details, a button, photos, a video, a sign-off.</span></button>' +
      '</div></section>' +
      (SS.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(SS.msg) + '</div>' : '') + (SS.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(SS.err) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<section class="w202sec" data-w202sec="templates"><div class="w202sh"><h3>' + I('layout') + ' Templates</h3>' + filterHtml() + '</div>' +
      '<p class="m">Our starters and the templates the team saved. \u201cReady as is\u201d ones have nothing left to fill in, so an automation can send them; the others start you off with [brackets] to fill.</p>' +
      shelvesHtml(studioTemplates()) + '</section>' +
      '<section class="w202sec" data-w202sec="recent"><div class="w202sh"><h3>' + I('mail') + ' Recent emails</h3></div>' +
      (rec.length ? '<div class="w202recent" role="list">' + rec.map(function (t) {
        return '<button class="w202rrow" role="listitem" data-w202open="' + E(t.key) + '"><span data-w198skip><b>' + E(t.name) + '</b><span class="m">' + E(t.subject) + '</span></span>' +
          '<span class="m">' + E(KIND[t.kind] ? KIND[t.kind].short : t.kind) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(lower(t.created_by) === me ? 'you' : str(t.created_by).split('@')[0]) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(ago(t.updated_at)) + '</span>' + I('chev') + '</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' + (mine.length > 8 && !SS.allRecent ? '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202allrecent>Show all ' + mine.length + '</button>' : '')
        : '<p class="m">The emails you write here show up in this list, ready to finish or send again.</p>') + '</section>' +
      peekHtml() + voicesHtml() + costsHtml() + '</div>';
  }
  /* a quick look at a template as it will arrive */
  function peekHtml() {
    if (!SS.peek) return '';
    var t = tplByKey(SS.peek); if (!t) return '';
    var html = MAIL202.preview({ html: t.html_body, subject: t.subject, preheader: t.preheader, kind: t.kind, cfg: mailSettings() || {} });
    return '<div class="w202modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="' + E(t.name) + '"><div class="w202mbox wide">' +
      '<div class="w202mh"><h3 data-w198skip>' + E(t.name) + '</h3><button class="w202x" data-w202peekx aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<div class="m" data-w198skip>Subject: <b>' + E(MAIL202.fill(t.subject, MAIL202.SAMPLE, false)) + '</b>' + (t.preheader ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(MAIL202.fill(t.preheader, MAIL202.SAMPLE, false)) : '') + '</div>' +
      '<iframe class="w202frame peek" sandbox="allow-same-origin" title="The template as it will look" srcdoc="' + E(html) + '"></iframe>' +
      '<div class="w154acts">' + (hasBrackets(t) ? '<button class="w152btn" data-w202fill="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('spark') + ' Fill it in</button>' : '<button class="w152btn" data-w202use="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('copy') + ' Use it</button>') +
      (canEdit(t) ? '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w202edit="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('edit') + ' Edit the template</button>' : '') + '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w202peekx>Close</button></div></div></div>';
  }
  /* ---------- wave203 (04/10/26): the voices. Steel: "draft a WA voice code from the brand guide and keep mine too as options."
     Welfare Avenue: the house voice code (app_config mail_voice_wa; every staff member reads it, only admins change it, as
     wa_app_config_guard keeps mail_* keys for admins), the default for everyone. My voice: the writer's own writing guide from
     Your inbox (mybox_owners.voice through the mybox function's voice action), offered only to people who have one, sent to
     the llm router with their own emails and never stored with an email (the design keeps only the choice: voice 'wa' or
     'mine'). Whatever the voice, the facts rule, the links, the shape and Welfare Avenue's words stay in the prompt. ---------- */
  var VOICE_KEY = 'mail_voice_wa';
  SS.voices = { wa: null, mine: null, loaded: false, p: null, open: false, tab: 'wa', editing: false, draft: '', saving: false, err: '' };
  try { var lastVoice = localStorage.getItem('wa203voice'); if (lastVoice === 'mine') SS.start.voice = 'mine'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  if (!SS.start.voice) SS.start.voice = 'wa';
  function rememberVoice(v) { try { localStorage.setItem('wa203voice', v === 'mine' ? 'mine' : 'wa'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function loadVoices(force) {
    var V = SS.voices;
    if (V.p) return V.p;
    if (V.loaded && !force) return Promise.resolve(V);
    V.p = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.from('app_config').select('key, value, updated_at').eq('key', VOICE_KEY).limit(1);
        var row = r && Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data[0] : null, v = row && row.value;
        if (typeof v === 'string') { try { v = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { v = null; } }   /* app_config.value is text holding JSON */
        V.wa = v && typeof v === 'object' && str(v.text).trim() ? { label: str(v.label || 'Welfare Avenue'), text: str(v.text), version: Number(v.version) || 1, source: str(v.source || ''),
          updated_by: str(v.updated_by || ''), updated_at: v.updated_at || row.updated_at || null } : null;
      } catch (e) { V.wa = null; }
      try {
        var o = await db.from('mybox_owners').select('email, enabled').limit(1);
        var own = o && Array.isArray(o.data) ? o.data[0] : null;
        if (own && own.enabled && lower(own.email) === myEmail()) {
          var g = await db.functions.invoke('mybox', { body: { action: 'voice' } });
          var d = g && !g.error && g.data && !g.data.error ? g.data : null;
          V.mine = d && str(d.voice).trim() ? { text: str(d.voice), framework: str(d.framework || '') } : null;
        } else V.mine = null;
      } catch (e) { V.mine = null; }
      V.loaded = true; V.p = null;
      if (SS.start.voice === 'mine' && !V.mine) SS.start.voice = 'wa';
      return V;
    })();
    return V.p;
  }
  /* the voice an email is written in for this person: their own only when they have a guide and it is their email */
  function effVoice(e) {
    var want = e && e.design && e.design.voice === 'mine' ? 'mine' : 'wa';
    if (want === 'mine' && SS.voices.mine && (!e.created_by || lower(e.created_by) === myEmail())) return 'mine';
    return 'wa';
  }
  /* what goes to the model: { key, text, name } (null when the house voice is not set: aiSystem keeps its built-in voice) */
  function voiceFor(key) {
    var V = SS.voices;
    if (key === 'mine' && V.mine) return { key: 'mine', text: V.mine.text, name: myName() };
    if (V.wa) return { key: 'wa', text: V.wa.text, name: 'Welfare Avenue' };
    return null;
  }
  function voiceCtl(cur) {
    var V = SS.voices;
    if (!V.loaded) loadVoices().then(function () { paint(); });
    var opts = [['wa', 'Welfare Avenue']]; if (V.mine) opts.push(['mine', 'My voice']);
    var note = cur === 'mine' && V.mine ? 'Your own writing guide, for emails you sign.' : 'Our house voice from the brand guide: the default for anything written as Welfare Avenue.';
    return '<div class="w155lb">Voice ' + segHtml('data-w202voice', opts, cur === 'mine' && V.mine ? 'mine' : 'wa') +
      '<span class="m">' + E(note) + ' <button class="w202link" data-w202voices>About the voices</button></span></div>';
  }
  function voicesHtml() {
    var V = SS.voices; if (!V.open) return '';
    var tab = V.tab === 'mine' ? 'mine' : 'wa', body;
    var tabs = '<div class="w202seg" role="tablist" aria-label="Voices">' + [['wa', 'Welfare Avenue'], ['mine', 'My voice']].map(function (o) {
      return '<button role="tab" class="' + (o[0] === tab ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w202vtab="' + o[0] + '" aria-selected="' + (o[0] === tab) + '">' + E(o[1]) + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (!V.loaded) body = busyHtml('Loading the voices\u2026');
    else if (tab === 'wa') {
      if (V.editing) body = '<p class="m">The house voice the Hub uses for anything written as Welfare Avenue. A change applies to the next email the Hub puts together or changes.</p>' +
        '<textarea class="w152in w202vtext" data-w202vdraft rows="22" aria-label="The Welfare Avenue voice code">' + E(V.draft) + '</textarea>' +
        (V.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(V.err) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202vsave' + (V.saving ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('save') + ' ' + (V.saving ? 'Saving\u2026' : 'Save the voice') + '</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w202vcancel>Cancel</button></div>';
      else body = (V.wa ? '<p class="m">The house voice for anything written as Welfare Avenue, and the default for everyone. Version ' + V.wa.version + (V.wa.updated_at ? ', ' + E(fmtDay(V.wa.updated_at)) : '') +
          (V.wa.updated_by ? ', ' + E(/@/.test(V.wa.updated_by) ? V.wa.updated_by.split('@')[0] : V.wa.updated_by) : '') + '.</p><div class="w202vread" data-w198skip tabindex="0">' + E(V.wa.text) + '</div>'
        : '<p class="m">The house voice is not set yet, so the Hub uses its short built-in Welfare Avenue voice.</p>') +
        (amAdmin() ? '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn sm" data-w202vedit>' + I('edit') + ' ' + (V.wa ? 'Edit the voice' : 'Write the voice') + '</button></div>' : '<p class="m">Admins look after it. Tell one of them what you would change.</p>');
    } else {
      body = V.mine ? '<p class="m">Your own voice: your writing guide from Your inbox (Settings, Your writing guide). Only you see it, and only emails you write in the studio use it.</p>' +
          '<div class="w202vread" data-w198skip tabindex="0">' + E(V.mine.text) + '</div><div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202vinbox>' + I('edit') + ' Edit it in Your inbox</button></div>'
        : '<p class="m">Your own voice comes from a writing guide. People with the inbox assistant write theirs in Your inbox, Settings, Your writing guide; \u201cMy voice\u201d then appears beside Welfare Avenue when the Hub writes an email.</p>';
    }
    return '<div class="w202modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Voices"><div class="w202mbox wide w202voices">' +
      '<div class="w202mh"><h3>' + I('voice') + ' Voices</h3><button class="w202x" data-w202voicesx aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<p class="m">How the Hub sounds when it puts an email together. Whatever the voice, it never invents a fact, and the studio\u2019s checks still run.</p>' + tabs + body + '</div></div>';
  }
  function openVoices(tab) {
    var V = SS.voices; V.open = true; V.editing = false; V.err = ''; V.tab = tab || (SS.start.voice === 'mine' && V.mine ? 'mine' : 'wa');
    loadVoices().then(function () { paint(); }); paint();
  }
  async function saveVoice() {
    var V = SS.voices, text = str(V.draft).replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').trim();
    if (!amAdmin()) { V.err = 'Only an admin can change the house voice.'; paint(); return; }
    if (text.length < 200) { V.err = 'That is very short for a voice code: keep at least a few lines on how we sound.'; paint(); return; }
    if (text.length > 20000) { V.err = 'Keep the voice under 20,000 characters: the Hub sends it with every email it writes.'; paint(); return; }
    if (/[\u2014\u2013]/.test(text)) { V.err = 'The voice code has an em dash or an en dash in it. Our own rules say no dashes as punctuation: use a comma, a colon or a full stop.'; paint(); return; }
    V.saving = true; V.err = ''; paint();
    var prev = V.wa, now = new Date().toISOString();
    var val = { label: 'Welfare Avenue', text: text, version: ((prev && prev.version) || 0) + 1, source: (prev && prev.source) || 'Welfare Avenue Brand Guides v1 2026', updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: now };
    var r; try { r = await db.from('app_config').upsert({ key: VOICE_KEY, value: JSON.stringify(val), updated_at: now }, { onConflict: 'key' }); } catch (e) { r = { error: e }; }
    V.saving = false;
    if (!r || r.error) { var m = msgOf(r && r.error); V.err = /admin/i.test(m) ? 'Only an admin can change the house voice.' : 'The voice did not save: ' + m; paint(); return; }
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'writing_voice', entity_id: VOICE_KEY, field: 'text', old_value: prev ? 'v' + prev.version + ', ' + prev.text.length + ' characters' : null, new_value: 'v' + val.version + ', ' + text.length + ' characters', changed_by: myEmail() }); } catch (e) { /* the audit line is best effort */ }
    V.wa = { label: val.label, text: text, version: val.version, source: val.source, updated_by: val.updated_by, updated_at: now };
    V.editing = false; V.draft = ''; T('The Welfare Avenue voice is saved');
    paint();
  }
  /* ---------- starting an email: Tell the Hub (words and photos), Start from a template (details), Build it yourself ---------- */
  function segHtml(attr, opts, cur) {
    return '<div class="w202seg" role="group">' + opts.map(function (o) { return '<button class="' + (o[0] === cur ? 'on' : '') + '" ' + attr + '="' + o[0] + '" aria-pressed="' + (o[0] === cur) + '">' + E(o[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function kindSel(attr, cur) {
    return '<select class="w152sel" ' + attr + ' aria-label="What kind of email">' + Object.keys(KIND).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (k === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(KIND[k].l) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';
  }
  function langSel(attr, cur) { return '<select class="w152sel" ' + attr + ' aria-label="Language"><option value="en"' + (cur !== 'fr' ? ' selected' : '') + '>English</option><option value="fr"' + (cur === 'fr' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Fran\u00e7ais</option></select>'; }
  function startPhotosHtml() {
    var ps = SS.start.photos;
    return '<div class="w155lb">Photos <span class="m">Up to six. The Hub places them and describes them; you can change everything after.</span>' +
      '<div class="w202sph" data-w198skip>' + ps.map(function (p, i) {
        return '<div class="w202spc"><img src="' + E(p.thumb || p.src) + '" alt="' + E(p.alt || p.title || 'Photo ' + (i + 1)) + '"><button class="w202x sm" data-w202sdel="' + i + '" aria-label="Remove this photo">' + I('x') + '</button>' +
          '<input class="w152in" data-w202scap="' + i + '" value="' + E(p.caption || '') + '" placeholder="What is in it? (optional)" aria-label="What is in photo ' + (i + 1) + '"></div>';
      }).join('') + (ps.length < 6 ? '<button class="w202spadd" data-w202pickstart>' + I('image') + '<span>Add photos</span></button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function startBar(title) {
    return '<div class="w202bar"><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202go="#crm/studio">' + I('back') + ' The studio</button><h2>' + E(title) + '</h2></div>';
  }
  function aiStartHtml() {
    var s = SS.start, k = KIND[s.kind] || KIND.newsletter;
    return '<div class="w202">' + startBar('Tell the Hub') + (SS.startNote && SS.startNote.view === 'ai' ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(SS.startNote.text) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w202cols st"><div class="w154card w202form" data-w202sec="ai">' +
      '<div class="w155lb">Who is it for?' + segHtml('data-w202aud', [['volunteers', 'Volunteers'], ['donors', 'Donors'], ['everyone', 'Everyone']], s.audience) + '</div>' +
      '<label class="w155lb">What kind of email?' + kindSel('data-w202kind="start"', s.kind) + '<span class="m">' + E(k.s) + '</span></label>' +
      '<label class="w155lb">Language' + langSel('data-w202lang="start"', s.lang) + '</label>' +
      voiceCtl(s.voice) +
      '<label class="w155lb">What do you want to say? <span class="m">Paste your text from a doc or an email (bold and links come along), or write it here. No HTML needed: the Hub designs the email.</span><textarea class="w152in w202say" data-w202say="brief" data-w202s="brief" rows="10" placeholder="Your content, in your own words: what is happening, why it matters, the day and date, the times, the place, and what people should do (with the link). The Hub shapes what you bring; it never makes up the facts.">' + E(s.brief) + '</textarea></label>' +
      meterHtml() +
      startPhotosHtml() +
      (SS.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(SS.err) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn' + (ownWords(s.brief) < CONTENT_MIN ? ' notyet' : '') + '" data-w202startai' + (SS.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('spark') + ' Put it together ' + aboutStep() + '</button>' +
      '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w202startself>Build it myself with these words ' + aboutStep(true) + '</button></div>' +
      (SS.busy ? busyHtml(SS.busy) : '') + '</div>' +
      '<aside class="w202tips"><h4>Your content first</h4><p class="m">The Hub shapes what you bring; it does not write the email for you. Bad input, bad output: thin words make a thin email, and each try costs about 2\u00a2.</p><ul><li><b>Your own words:</b> what is happening and why it matters, three or four sentences at least.</li><li><b>The facts:</b> the day and date, the times, the place, how many people you need, the link to sign up or give.</li>' +
      '<li><b>Who signs it,</b> if not you.</li><li><b>One thing to do:</b> what should people do after reading?</li><li><b>Photos</b> of our volunteers, food and setups (no close-ups of the people we serve).</li></ul>' +
      '<p class="m">The Hub writes it in the voice you pick (Welfare Avenue\u2019s house voice, or your own when you have a writing guide), leaves [brackets] where something is missing and asks you about it. Nothing is sent until you choose to.</p></aside></div>' +
      pickerHtml() + voicesHtml() + '</div>';
  }
  function tplStartHtml() {
    var s = SS.start, t = s.tpl ? tplByKey(s.tpl) : null;
    var asks = t && t.design && t.design.asks ? t.design.asks : [];
    return '<div class="w202">' + startBar('Start from a template') + (SS.startNote && SS.startNote.view === 'tpl' ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(SS.startNote.text) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w202cols st"><div class="w202pickcol" data-w202sec="pick">' + filterHtml() + shelvesHtml(studioTemplates(), true) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w154card w202form" data-w202sec="details">' + (!t ? '<p class="m">Pick a template. Then give it your details, and the Hub fills it in.</p>' :
        '<div class="w202tsel">' + miniHero(t) + '<div data-w198skip><h3>' + E(t.name) + '</h3><p class="m">' + E(t.description || '') + '</p></div></div>' +
        '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202peek="' + E(t.key) + '">' + I('eye') + ' Look at the template</button>' +
        (asks.length ? '<h4 class="w202h4">Your details</h4>' + asks.map(function (a) {
          return '<label class="w155lb" data-w198skip>' + E(a.label) + '<input class="w152in" data-w202ask="' + E(a.id) + '" value="' + E(s.asks[a.id] || '') + '" placeholder="' + E(a.hint ? 'For example: ' + a.hint : '') + '"></label>';
        }).join('') : '<p class="m">This one is ready as it is. Add anything you want to say, or use it straight away.</p>') +
        '<label class="w155lb">Anything else to say? <span class="m">Optional</span><textarea class="w152in w202say" data-w202say="extra" data-w202s="extra" rows="4" placeholder="A story, a thank-you, a detail the template does not ask for.">' + E(s.extra) + '</textarea></label>' +
        '<label class="w155lb">Language' + langSel('data-w202lang="start"', s.lang) + '</label>' +
        voiceCtl(s.voice) +
        startPhotosHtml() +
        (SS.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(SS.err) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<p class="m">Your details first: the Hub fills in what you give it and never makes up the rest.</p>' +
        '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202starttpl="ai"' + (SS.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('spark') + ' Fill it in with the Hub ' + aboutStep() + '</button>' +
        '<button class="w152btn ghost" data-w202starttpl="self">Fill it in myself ' + aboutStep(true) + '</button></div>' + (SS.busy ? busyHtml(SS.busy) : '')) + '</div></div>' +
      pickerHtml() + peekHtml() + voicesHtml() + '</div>';
  }
  /* a new email in memory, then saved (it needs a subject to be saved) */
  function draftFrom(o) {
    return { key: null, name: o.name || 'New email', kind: o.kind || 'newsletter', audience: o.audience || null, lang: o.lang || 'en', subject: o.subject || '', preheader: o.preheader || '',
      design: MAIL202.clean(o.design || { blocks: [] }), one_off: true, category: null, description: '', active: true, created_by: myEmail(), updated_at: null };
  }
  function photosForAi(ps) { return ps.map(function (p) { return { src: p.src, alt: p.alt || '', title: p.title || '', lib: p.lib || null, usage: p.usage || null, caption: p.caption || '', data: p.data || '' }; }); }
  async function openDraft(d, opts) {
    SS.d = d; SS.questions = (opts && opts.questions) || []; SS.notes = (opts && opts.notes) || ''; SS.open = {}; SS.send = null; SS.ro = false; SS.err = ''; SS.msg = '';
    histReset(); SS.startNote = null;   /* wave204: a new history; the step before, when the Hub made it from what you gave */
    SS.back = opts && opts.back ? Object.assign({ d: d }, opts.back) : null;
    if (d.design.blocks[0]) SS.open[d.design.blocks[0].id] = !SS.questions.length && !!(opts && opts.openFirst);
    SS.view = 'edit'; SS.busy = '';
    var key = str(d.subject).trim() ? await saveNow() : null;
    go('#crm/studio/e/' + (key || 'new'));
    if (SS.to) openSend('person'); else paint();
  }
  async function startAi() {
    var s = SS.start;
    var nw = ownWords(s.brief);
    if (nw < CONTENT_MIN) { SS.err = contentShort(nw); paint(); return; }   /* wave204: your content first */
    SS.startNote = null;
    SS.err = ''; SS.busy = 'The Hub is putting your email together. This takes about half a minute\u2026'; paint();
    var photos = photosForAi(s.photos), back = { view: 'ai', brief: s.brief, photos: s.photos.slice(), audience: s.audience, kind: s.kind, lang: s.lang, voice: s.voice };
    var r = await aiCall({ mode: 'new', brief: s.brief, audience: s.audience, kind: s.kind, lang: s.lang, photos: photos, voice: s.voice });
    if (r.error) { SS.busy = ''; SS.err = r.error; paint(); return; }
    var res = fromAi(r.input, r.photos);
    if (!res.design.blocks.length) { SS.busy = ''; SS.err = 'The Hub sent back an empty email. Try again with a few more details, or build it yourself.'; paint(); return; }
    res.design.source = 'ai'; res.design.voice = s.voice === 'mine' && SS.voices.mine ? 'mine' : 'wa'; res.design.ai = addCost(null, r.cost);
    await openDraft(draftFrom({ name: res.name || 'New email', kind: s.kind, audience: s.audience, lang: s.lang, subject: res.subject, preheader: res.preheader, design: res.design }), { questions: res.questions, notes: res.notes, back: back });
    SS.start.brief = ''; SS.start.photos = []; paint();
  }
  function startSelf() {
    var s = SS.start, blocks = [MAIL202.blank('hero')];
    var text = str(s.brief).trim();
    var t = MAIL202.blank('text'); t.text = 'Hi {{first_name|there}},' + (text ? '\n\n' + text : '\n\n'); blocks.push(t);
    s.photos.forEach(function (p) { var b = MAIL202.blank('photo'); b.src = p.src; b.alt = p.alt || ''; b.caption = p.caption || ''; if (p.lib) b.lib = p.lib; if (p.usage) b.usage = p.usage; blocks.push(b); });
    var btn = MAIL202.blank('button'); btn.label = s.kind === 'fundraising' ? 'Give today' : s.kind === 'volunteer' ? 'Sign up to serve' : 'Find out more'; btn.url = s.kind === 'fundraising' ? 'https://welfareavenue.com/donate/' : s.kind === 'volunteer' ? 'https://welfareavenue.com/volunteer/' : ''; blocks.push(btn);
    var so = MAIL202.blank('signoff'); so.name = myName(); blocks.push(so);
    var d = draftFrom({ name: 'New email', kind: s.kind, audience: s.audience, lang: s.lang, subject: '', preheader: '', design: { blocks: blocks, source: 'blocks', voice: s.voice === 'mine' && SS.voices.mine ? 'mine' : 'wa' } });
    var back = { view: 'ai', brief: s.brief, photos: s.photos.slice(), audience: s.audience, kind: s.kind, lang: s.lang, voice: s.voice };
    SS.start.brief = ''; SS.start.photos = [];
    openDraft(d, { openFirst: true, back: back });
  }
  function startBlank() {
    var s = SS.start, so = MAIL202.blank('signoff'); so.name = myName();
    openDraft(draftFrom({ name: 'New email', kind: s.kind, audience: s.audience, lang: s.lang, design: { blocks: [MAIL202.blank('hero'), MAIL202.blank('text'), MAIL202.blank('button'), so], source: 'blocks', voice: s.voice === 'mine' && SS.voices.mine ? 'mine' : 'wa' } }), { openFirst: true });
  }
  function copyOf(t, o) {
    var d = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.design || { blocks: [] }));
    d.from = t.key; d.source = (o && o.source) || 'template'; delete d.asks; delete d.ai;   /* wave204: a copy starts with nothing spent */
    (d.blocks || []).forEach(function (b) { b.id = MAIL202.uid(); });
    return draftFrom({ name: (o && o.name) || t.name, kind: t.kind === 'test' ? 'newsletter' : t.kind, audience: t.audience, lang: t.lang || 'en', subject: t.subject, preheader: t.preheader || '', design: d });
  }
  async function startTpl(useAi) {
    var s = SS.start, t = tplByKey(s.tpl); if (!t) return;
    var asks = (t.design && t.design.asks) || [], back = { view: 'tpl', tpl: t.key, asks: Object.assign({}, s.asks), extra: s.extra, photos: s.photos.slice(), lang: s.lang, voice: s.voice };
    if (!useAi) { var d0 = copyOf(t); d0.design.voice = s.voice === 'mine' && SS.voices.mine ? 'mine' : 'wa'; s.photos.forEach(function (p) { var b = MAIL202.blank('photo'); b.src = p.src; b.alt = p.alt || ''; if (p.lib) b.lib = p.lib; if (p.usage) b.usage = p.usage; d0.design.blocks.splice(Math.max(1, d0.design.blocks.length - 1), 0, b); }); SS.start.photos = []; await openDraft(d0, { openFirst: false, back: back }); return; }
    var given = asks.map(function (a) { return { label: a.label, answer: s.asks[a.id] || '' }; });
    var short = tplShort(t, s); if (short) { SS.err = short; paint(); return; }   /* wave204: your content first */
    SS.err = ''; SS.busy = 'The Hub is filling in \u201c' + t.name + '\u201d. This takes about half a minute\u2026'; paint();
    var photos = photosForAi(s.photos);
    var r = await aiCall({ mode: 'template', design: t.design, subject: t.subject, preheader: t.preheader, tplName: t.name, asks: given, brief: s.extra, audience: t.audience, kind: t.kind, lang: s.lang || t.lang, photos: photos, voice: s.voice });
    if (r.error) { SS.busy = ''; SS.err = r.error; paint(); return; }
    var res = fromAi(r.input, r.photos);
    if (!res.design.blocks.length) { SS.busy = ''; SS.err = 'The Hub sent back an empty email. Try again, or fill it in yourself.'; paint(); return; }
    res.design.from = t.key; res.design.source = 'template+ai'; res.design.voice = s.voice === 'mine' && SS.voices.mine ? 'mine' : 'wa'; res.design.ai = addCost(null, r.cost);
    keepMoving(t.design, res.design);   /* wave204: a template's moving preview stays */
    await openDraft(draftFrom({ name: res.name || t.name, kind: t.kind, audience: t.audience, lang: s.lang || t.lang, subject: res.subject || t.subject, preheader: res.preheader || t.preheader, design: res.design }), { questions: res.questions, notes: res.notes, back: back });
    SS.start.asks = {}; SS.start.extra = ''; SS.start.photos = []; paint();
  }
  /* ---------- the editor: settings, the Hub's questions, the checks, the blocks, Ask the Hub, the email beside it ---------- */
  function summary(b) {
    var cut = function (s, n) { s = str(s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return s.length > (n || 60) ? s.slice(0, (n || 60) - 1) + '\u2026' : s; };
    if (b.type === 'hero') return cut((b.lines || []).join(' / ')) || 'No headline yet';
    if (b.type === 'text') return cut(b.text) || 'Empty';
    if (b.type === 'heading') return cut(b.text) || 'Empty';
    if (b.type === 'details') return cut((b.rows || []).map(function (r) { return r.label; }).filter(Boolean).join(', ')) || 'No rows yet';
    if (b.type === 'list') return plural((b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return str(x.text).trim() || str(x.lead).trim(); }).length, 'point', 'points');
    if (b.type === 'button') return cut((b.label || 'A button') + (b.url ? ' \u2192 ' + b.url : ' (no link yet)'), 70);
    if (b.type === 'photo') return b.src ? cut(b.caption || b.alt || 'A photo') : 'No photo yet';
    if (b.type === 'photos') return plural((b.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x && x.src; }).length, 'photo', 'photos');
    if (b.type === 'video') return cut(b.title || b.url || 'No link yet');
    if (b.type === 'band') return cut(b.title || b.text) || 'Empty';
    if (b.type === 'quote') return cut(b.quote) || 'Empty';
    if (b.type === 'stats') return cut((b.items || []).map(function (x) { return x.n; }).filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) || 'No numbers yet';
    if (b.type === 'signoff') return cut([b.closing, b.name].filter(Boolean).join(' ')) || 'Sign-off';
    return b.style === 'space' ? 'Space' : 'Gold rule';
  }
  function fv(v) { return E(v == null ? '' : v); }
  function inp(i, path, label, value, o) {
    o = o || {};
    var at = 'data-w202f="' + i + '.' + path + '"' + (o.fmt && o.area ? ' data-w204fmt' : '');
    var ph = o.ph ? ' placeholder="' + E(o.ph) + '"' : '';
    var ctrl = o.area ? '<textarea class="w152in" ' + at + ' rows="' + (o.rows || 4) + '"' + ph + '>' + fv(value) + '</textarea>' : '<input class="w152in" ' + at + ' value="' + fv(value) + '"' + ph + (o.type ? ' type="' + o.type + '"' : '') + '>';
    return '<label class="w155lb' + (o.half ? ' half' : '') + '">' + E(label) + (o.help ? ' <span class="m">' + E(o.help) + '</span>' : '') + (o.fmt && o.area ? fmtBar() : '') + ctrl + '</label>';
  }
  function sel(i, path, label, value, opts) {
    return '<label class="w155lb half">' + E(label) + '<select class="w152sel" data-w202f="' + i + '.' + path + '">' + opts.map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + E(o[0]) + '"' + (String(o[0]) === String(value) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(o[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>';
  }
  function chk(i, path, label, on) { return '<label class="w202ck"><input type="checkbox" data-w202f="' + i + '.' + path + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '> ' + E(label) + '</label>'; }
  function photoSlot(i, k, p) {
    var at = k == null ? String(i) : i + ',' + k;
    return '<div class="w202slot">' + (p && p.src ? '<img src="' + E(p.src) + '" alt="' + E(p.alt || '') + '">' : '<div class="w202noimg">' + I('image') + '</div>') +
      '<div class="w202slb"><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202pickfor="' + at + '">' + I('image') + (p && p.src ? ' Change' : ' Choose a photo') + '</button>' + (p && p.src ? '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202rmphoto="' + at + '">' + I('x') + ' Remove</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  var TEXTHELP = 'Paste from a doc and bold and links come along. Select words, then B or Link. A blank line starts a paragraph; {{first_name|there}} is their first name.';
  function blockForm(b, i) {
    var h = '';
    if (b.type === 'hero') {
      h += '<div class="w202two">' + sel(i, 'style', 'Background', b.style, [['cream', 'Cream'], ['green', 'Green card']]) + sel(i, 'doodle', 'Doodle', b.doodle, [['star', 'Star'], ['heart', 'Heart'], ['flowers', 'Flowers'], ['none', 'None']]) + '</div>';
      h += inp(i, 'kicker', 'Small line above', b.kicker, { ph: 'Sunday 12 October \u00b7 Cabot Square' });
      h += inp(i, 'lines', 'Headline', (b.lines || []).join('\n'), { area: true, rows: 2, help: 'Two short lines; the last one takes the colour', ph: 'Sunday\nneeds you' });
      h += '<div class="w202two">' + sel(i, 'accent', 'Colour of the last line', b.accent, [['gold', 'Gold'], ['purple', 'Purple']]) + '<div class="w155lb half">The E that moves' + chk(i, 'moveE', 'Stretch one E (once per email)', b.moveE !== false) + '</div></div>';
      h += inp(i, 'lead', 'The sentence under it', b.lead, { area: true, rows: 2, fmt: true });
    }
    if (b.type === 'text') h += inp(i, 'text', 'Text', b.text, { area: true, rows: 7, help: TEXTHELP, fmt: true });
    if (b.type === 'heading') h += inp(i, 'text', 'Heading', b.text);
    if (b.type === 'details' || b.type === 'list' || b.type === 'stats' || b.type === 'band') {
      var field = b.type === 'details' ? 'rows' : b.type === 'band' ? 'links' : 'items', rows = b[field] || [];
      if (b.type === 'band') { h += inp(i, 'title', 'Title', b.title); h += inp(i, 'text', 'Text', b.text, { area: true, rows: 3, fmt: true }); }
      if (b.type === 'stats') { h += '<div class="w202two">' + sel(i, 'style', 'Background', b.style, [['green', 'Green card'], ['cream', 'Cream with rules']]) + inp(i, 'title', 'Small title', b.title, { half: true, ph: 'Since April 2020' }) + '</div>'; }
      h += '<div class="w202rows">' + rows.map(function (r, k) {
        var a, c;
        if (b.type === 'details') { a = ['label', 'Label', 'When']; c = ['value', 'Value', 'Sunday 12 October, 11 am']; }
        else if (b.type === 'list') { a = ['lead', 'Bold start', 'Cooks:']; c = ['text', 'Text', 'start at 9 am']; }
        else if (b.type === 'stats') { a = ['n', 'Number', '20,000+']; c = ['label', 'What it counts', 'meals served']; }
        else { a = ['label', 'Button words', 'Cooks']; c = ['url', 'Link', 'https://']; }
        return '<div class="w202row"><input class="w152in" data-w202f="' + i + '.' + field + '.' + k + '.' + a[0] + '" value="' + fv(r[a[0]]) + '" placeholder="' + E(a[2]) + '" aria-label="' + E(a[1]) + '">' +
