          '<input class="w152in" data-w202f="' + i + '.' + field + '.' + k + '.' + c[0] + '" value="' + fv(r[c[0]]) + '" placeholder="' + E(c[2]) + '" aria-label="' + E(c[1]) + '">' +
          '<button class="w202x sm" data-w202rowdel="' + i + ',' + field + ',' + k + '" aria-label="Remove this row">' + I('x') + '</button></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' + ((b.type === 'stats' && rows.length >= 3) ? '' : '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202rowadd="' + i + ',' + field + '">' + I('plus') + ' Add ' + (b.type === 'details' ? 'a row' : b.type === 'band' ? 'a link' : b.type === 'stats' ? 'a number' : 'a point') + '</button>');
      if (b.type === 'stats') h += inp(i, 'note', 'Small note under the numbers', b.note, { ph: 'Our best estimates from our records, rounded down.' });
    }
    if (b.type === 'button') { h += '<div class="w202two">' + inp(i, 'label', 'Button words', b.label, { half: true }) + inp(i, 'url', 'Link', b.url, { half: true, ph: 'https://welfareavenue.com/volunteer/' }) + '</div>' + inp(i, 'caption', 'Small line under it', b.caption, { ph: 'Two minutes \u00b7 your volunteer hours count' }); }
    if (b.type === 'photo') { h += photoSlot(i, null, b) + inp(i, 'alt', 'What the photo shows (for screen readers)', b.alt) + inp(i, 'caption', 'Caption', b.caption, { help: 'Optional' }) + inp(i, 'link', 'Link when tapped', b.link, { help: 'Optional', ph: 'https://' }); }
    if (b.type === 'photos') {
      h += '<div class="w202slots">' + [0, 1, 2].map(function (k) { var p = (b.items || [])[k] || {}; return '<div class="w202slotw">' + photoSlot(i, k, p) +
        '<input class="w152in" data-w202f="' + i + '.items.' + k + '.alt" value="' + fv(p.alt) + '" placeholder="What it shows" aria-label="What photo ' + (k + 1) + ' shows">' +
        '<input class="w152in" data-w202f="' + i + '.items.' + k + '.caption" value="' + fv(p.caption) + '" placeholder="Caption (optional)" aria-label="Caption for photo ' + (k + 1) + '"></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    if (b.type === 'video') { h += inp(i, 'url', 'Link to the video', b.url, { ph: 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=...', help: 'Where a tap goes: YouTube, Instagram, Google Drive or our website' }) + '<div class="w202two">' + inp(i, 'title', 'Title', b.title, { half: true, ph: 'What is a serving?' }) + inp(i, 'caption', 'Line under it', b.caption, { half: true, ph: 'Tap to watch' }) + '</div>' + movingSlot(i, b); }
    if (b.type === 'quote') { h += inp(i, 'quote', 'The quote', b.quote, { area: true, rows: 3, fmt: true }) + inp(i, 'by', 'Who said it', b.by, { ph: 'A volunteer, fall 2025' }); }
    if (b.type === 'signoff') { h += '<div class="w202two">' + inp(i, 'closing', 'Closing', b.closing, { half: true, ph: 'Warmly,' }) + inp(i, 'name', 'Name', b.name, { half: true }) + '</div>' + inp(i, 'role', 'Under the name', b.role, { ph: 'Welfare Avenue' }) + inp(i, 'ps', 'P.S.', b.ps, { area: true, rows: 2, help: 'Optional', fmt: true }); }
    if (b.type === 'divider') h += sel(i, 'style', 'Kind', b.style, [['rule', 'A short gold rule'], ['space', 'Just space']]);
    return h;
  }
  function blocksHtml() {
    var bl = SS.d.design.blocks, ro = SS.ro;
    return '<div class="w202blocks" data-w202sec="blocks">' + bl.map(function (b, i) {
      var open = !!SS.open[b.id];
      return '<div class="w202blk' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '" data-w202blk="' + i + '" id="w202b' + i + '">' +
        '<div class="w202bh"><button class="w202bt" data-w202tog="' + i + '" aria-expanded="' + open + '"><b>' + E(MAIL202.LABEL[b.type] || b.type) + '</b><span class="m" data-w198skip>' + E(summary(b)) + '</span></button>' +
        (ro ? '' : '<span class="w202ba"><button class="w202ib" data-w202mv="' + i + ',-1" aria-label="Move up"' + (i === 0 ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('up') + '</button><button class="w202ib" data-w202mv="' + i + ',1" aria-label="Move down"' + (i === bl.length - 1 ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('down') + '</button>' +
          '<button class="w202ib" data-w202dup="' + i + '" aria-label="Duplicate">' + I('copy') + '</button><button class="w202ib" data-w202del="' + i + '" aria-label="Remove">' + I('trash') + '</button></span>') + '</div>' +
        (open ? '<div class="w202bf"' + (ro ? ' inert' : '') + '>' + blockForm(b, i) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' + (ro ? '' : addHtml());
  }
  function addHtml() {
    return '<div class="w202add" data-w202sec="add"><span class="m">Add a block</span><div class="w202addb">' + MAIL202.TYPES.map(function (t) { return '<button class="w202chipb" data-w202add="' + t + '">' + I('plus') + E(MAIL202.LABEL[t]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function questionsHtml() {

      }).join('') + '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202answers' + (SS.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('spark') + ' Put my answers in ' + aboutStep() + '</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w202qx>I\u2019ll fix it myself</button></div>'
        : '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn ghost sm" data-w202qx>Got it</button></div>') + '</div>';

  function checksHtml() {

'<b class="w202cwn">' + I('alert') + ' Ready, with ' + plural(wn, 'thing', 'things') + ' to look at</b>' : '<b class="w202cok">' + I('check') + ' Ready to send</b>';
    return '<div class="w154card w202checks" data-w202sec="checks" id="w202checks">' + head + (cs.length ? '<ul>' + cs.map(function (c) {

    }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function settingsHtml() {

' selected' : '') + '>' + E(AUD[a]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' +

      '<p class="m wide">' + E((KIND[e.kind] || KIND.newsletter).s) + ' Every email carries our charity number, postal address, why they got it and an unsubscribe link.</p></div>';

  function saveState() {
    var e = SS.d; if (!e) return '';
    if (SS.ro) return '<span class="w202ss">Read only</span>';
    if (SS.saving) return '<span class="w202ss">Saving\u2026</span>';
    if (SS.saveNote) return '<span class="w202ss warn">' + E(SS.saveNote) + '</span>';
    if (!e.key) return '<span class="w202ss warn">Not saved yet: it needs a subject</span>';
    if (SS.dirty) return '<span class="w202ss">Unsaved changes</span>';
    return '<span class="w202ss ok">' + I('check') + ' Saved' + (SS.savedAt ? ' ' + ago(SS.savedAt) : '') + '</span>';
  }
  function updateSaveState() { var el = document.getElementById('w202ss'); if (el) el.innerHTML = saveState(); }
  function previewDoc() {
    var e = SS.d, d = e.design;
    var html = MAIL202.preview({ html: MAIL202.renderMarked(d), subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader, kind: e.kind, cfg: mailSettings() || {} });
    return html.replace('</head>', '<style>[data-w202b]{cursor:pointer;border-radius:6px;}[data-w202b]:hover{outline:2px dashed rgba(220,165,39,.85);outline-offset:4px;}[data-w202b].hl{outline:2px solid #DCA527;outline-offset:4px;}</style></head>');
  }
  function previewHtml() {

      '<br>Subject: <b>' + E(MAIL202.fill(e.subject || '(no subject yet)', MAIL202.SAMPLE, false)) + '</b>' + (e.preheader ? '<br>Preview: ' + E(MAIL202.fill(e.preheader, MAIL202.SAMPLE, false)) : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w202dev" role="group" aria-label="Preview size"><button class="' + (SS.device !== 'phone' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w202dev="desktop" aria-pressed="' + (SS.device !== 'phone') + '">' + I('desktop') + '<span>Computer</span></button><button class="' + (SS.device === 'phone' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w202dev="phone" aria-pressed="' + (SS.device === 'phone') + '">' + I('phone') + '<span>Phone</span></button></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w202fw' + (SS.device === 'phone' ? ' phone' : '') + '"><iframe id="w202frame" class="w202frame" sandbox="allow-same-origin" title="The email as it will look, with Alex as the reader" srcdoc="' + E(previewDoc()) + '"></iframe></div>' +
      '<p class="m">Shown with Alex as the reader. Click a part of the email to edit it.</p></div>';

  function editorHtml() {
    var e = SS.d; if (!e) return busyHtml('Opening the email\u2026');
    var owner = !SS.ro ? '' : '<div class="w202ro">' + I('alert') + ' <span>' + (e.created_by === 'starter' ? 'This is one of our starters: only admins change it.' : 'This is ' + E(str(e.created_by).split('@')[0]) + '\u2019s email: only they or an admin change it.') + ' Copy it to make it yours.</span><button class="w152btn sm" data-w202copy>' + I('copy') + ' Copy it</button></div>';
    return '<div class="w202 w202ed">' +
      '<div class="w202bar"><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202go="#crm/studio">' + I('back') + ' The studio</button>' + backBtn() +
      '<input class="w202name" data-w202e="name" value="' + E(e.name) + '" aria-label="The email\u2019s name (for the team)"' + (SS.ro ? ' disabled' : '') + ' data-w198skip>' +
      '<span id="w202ss">' + saveState() + '</span>' + histBar() + '<span class="w202sp"></span>' +

      '<button class="w152btn sm" data-w202sendopen>' + I('send') + ' Send or save\u2026</button></div>' + owner +
      (SS.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(SS.msg) + (SS.undo ? ' <button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202undo>Undo</button>' : '') + '</div>' : '') + (SS.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(SS.err) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w202tabs" role="tablist"><button role="tab" class="' + (SS.tab === 'edit' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w202tab="edit">Edit</button><button role="tab" class="' + (SS.tab === 'preview' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w202tab="preview">Preview</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w202cols ed tab-' + SS.tab + '"><div class="w202left">' + sendHtml() + settingsHtml() + questionsHtml() + checksHtml() + blocksHtml() +
      (SS.ro ? '' : '<div class="w154card w202chg" data-w202sec="change"><h3>' + I('spark') + ' Ask the Hub to change it</h3>' +
        '<textarea class="w152in w202say" data-w202say="change" rows="3" placeholder="Make it shorter. Warmer. Put the date first. Write it in French. Add a P.S. about bringing a friend.">' + E(SS.change) + '</textarea>' +
        '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202change' + (SS.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('spark') + ' Change it ' + aboutStep() + '</button></div>' + (SS.busy ? busyHtml(SS.busy) : '') +
        '<p class="m w204cost" id="w204cost">' + emailCostLine(e) + ' <button class="w202link" data-w204costs>What it costs</button></p></div>') +
      '<div class="w202foot">' + (SS.ro ? '' : '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202dupe>' + I('copy') + ' Make a copy</button>') +
      (!SS.ro && e.key ? (SS.confirmDel ? '<span class="w202cf">' + (e.one_off ? 'Delete this email?' : 'Remove this template? Automations that use it stop sending it.') + ' <button class="w152btn sm" data-w202delyes>Yes, remove it</button><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202delno>Keep it</button></span>'
        : '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202rm>' + I('trash') + ' ' + (e.one_off ? 'Delete' : 'Remove the template') + '</button>') : '') + '</div>' +
      '</div><div class="w202right">' + previewHtml() + '</div></div>' + pickerHtml() + voicesHtml() + movingHtml() + '</div>';
  }
  /* ---------- edits ---------- */
  var timers = {};
  function later(name, ms, fn) { clearTimeout(timers[name]); timers[name] = setTimeout(fn, ms); }
  function changed(structural, field) {
    if (!SS.d || SS.ro) return;
    histRecord(field);   /* wave204: every change is a step for Undo (typing in one field: one step until a pause) */
    SS.dirty = true; SS.saveNote = ''; updateSaveState();
    if (structural) { paint(); } else { later('pv', 300, refreshPreview); later('ck', 500, refreshChecks); }
    later('save', 2500, function () { if (SS.dirty) saveNow(); });
  }
  function refreshChecks() { var el = document.getElementById('w202checks'); if (el && SS.d) el.outerHTML = checksHtml(); }
  function refreshPreview() {
    var f = document.getElementById('w202frame'); if (!f || !SS.d) return;
    var y = 0; try { y = f.contentWindow.scrollY || 0; } catch (e) { y = 0; }
    f.setAttribute('data-y', String(y));
    f.srcdoc = previewDoc();
    var pvh = document.querySelector('.w202pvh .m'); if (pvh) { var st = mailSettings() || {}; pvh.innerHTML = 'From: ' + E((st.mail_from_name || 'Welfare Avenue') + ' <' + (st.mail_from_email || 'info@welfareavenue.com') + '>') + '<br>Subject: <b>' + E(MAIL202.fill(SS.d.subject || '(no subject yet)', MAIL202.SAMPLE, false)) + '</b>' + (SS.d.preheader ? '<br>Preview: ' + E(MAIL202.fill(SS.d.preheader, MAIL202.SAMPLE, false)) : ''); }
    var blk = document.querySelectorAll('.w202bt .m'); SS.d.design.blocks.forEach(function (b, i) { if (blk[i]) blk[i].textContent = summary(b); });
  }
  /* the preview's own clicks: a link never opens inside it; a part of the email opens its block */
  function wireFrame(f) {
    try {
      var doc = f.contentDocument; if (!doc || doc.__w202) return; doc.__w202 = true;
      var y = Number(f.getAttribute('data-y') || 0); if (y) f.contentWindow.scrollTo(0, y);
      doc.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
        var a = ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('a'); if (a) ev.preventDefault();
        var m = ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('[data-w202b]'); if (!m) return;
        var i = Number(m.getAttribute('data-w202b')); openBlock(i);
      });
      doc.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);   /* wave204: Cmd/Ctrl+Z works with the preview in focus too */
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function openBlock(i) {
    var b = SS.d && SS.d.design.blocks[i]; if (!b) return;
    SS.open[b.id] = true; if (SS.tab === 'preview') SS.tab = 'edit'; paint();
    setTimeout(function () { var el = document.getElementById('w202b' + i); if (el) { el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); var f = el.querySelector('input,textarea,select'); if (f) try { f.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }, 60);
  }
  function setPath(i, path, value) {
    var b = SS.d.design.blocks[i]; if (!b) return;
    var p = path.split('.'), o = b;
    if (p[0] === 'lines') { b.lines = str(value).split(/\n/).slice(0, 4); return; }
    for (var k = 0; k < p.length - 1; k++) { var key = /^\d+$/.test(p[k]) ? Number(p[k]) : p[k]; if (o[key] == null) o[key] = /^\d+$/.test(p[k + 1]) ? [] : {}; o = o[key]; }
    var last = p[p.length - 1];
    if (last === 'moveE') { o.moveE = !!value; return; }
    o[last] = value;
  }
  function addBlock(type) {
    var bl = SS.d.design.blocks, nb = MAIL202.blank(type);
    if (type === 'signoff') nb.name = myName();
    var openIdx = -1; bl.forEach(function (b, i) { if (SS.open[b.id]) openIdx = i; });
    var at = openIdx >= 0 ? openIdx + 1 : (bl.length && bl[bl.length - 1].type === 'signoff' ? bl.length - 1 : bl.length);
    if (type === 'hero') at = 0;
    bl.splice(at, 0, nb); SS.open = {}; SS.open[nb.id] = true;
    changed(true);
    setTimeout(function () { var el = document.getElementById('w202b' + at); if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); }, 60);
  }
  async function saveNow() {
    var e = SS.d; if (!e || SS.ro) return null;
    if (!str(e.subject).trim()) { SS.saveNote = ''; updateSaveState(); return e.key || null; }
    if (SS.saving) { SS.saveAgain = true; return SS.savingP; }
    SS.saving = true; updateSaveState();
    var html = MAIL202.render(e.design), text = MAIL202.renderText(e.design);
    var payload = { key: e.key || '', name: str(e.name).trim() || str(e.subject).trim(), kind: e.kind, subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader || '', html_body: html, text_body: text, design: e.design,
      vars: MAIL202.placeholders(e.subject + ' ' + (e.preheader || '') + ' ' + html), one_off: e.one_off !== false, audience: e.audience || null, category: e.category || null, lang: e.lang || 'en', description: e.description || '', active: e.active !== false };
    SS.savingP = rpc('email_studio_save', { p: payload }).then(function (r) {
      SS.saving = false;
      if (r.error) { SS.saveNote = 'Not saved: ' + words(r.error); updateSaveState(); return null; }
      var data = r.data || {};
      e.key = data.key || e.key; e.updated_at = data.updated_at || new Date().toISOString(); SS.dirty = false; SS.savedAt = Date.now(); SS.saveNote = '';
      if (SS.view === 'edit' && location.hash.indexOf('#crm/studio/e/') === 0 && location.hash !== '#crm/studio/e/' + e.key) setHash('#crm/studio/e/' + e.key);
      reloadTemplates(); updateSaveState();
      if (SS.saveAgain) { SS.saveAgain = false; return saveNow(); }
      return e.key;
    });
    return SS.savingP;
  }
  async function aiChange(mode) {
    var e = SS.d; if (!e) return;
    var qa = (SS.questions || []).map(function (q) { return { ask: q.ask, answer: q.answer }; });
    if (mode === 'change' && !str(SS.change).trim()) { SS.err = 'Tell the Hub what to change.'; paint(); return; }
    if (mode === 'answers' && !qa.some(function (q) { return str(q.answer).trim(); })) { SS.err = 'Answer at least one question first.'; paint(); return; }
    SS.err = ''; SS.busy = mode === 'change' ? 'The Hub is changing your email\u2026' : 'The Hub is putting your answers in\u2026'; paint();
    var r = await aiCall({ mode: mode, design: e.design, subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader, instruction: SS.change, qa: qa, audience: e.audience, kind: e.kind, lang: e.lang, photos: [], voice: effVoice(e) });
    SS.busy = '';
    if (r.error) { spent(e, r.cost); SS.err = r.error; paint(); return; }
    var res = fromAi(r.input, r.photos);
    if (!res.design.blocks.length) { spent(e, r.cost); SS.err = 'The Hub sent back an empty email, so nothing changed.'; paint(); return; }
    res.design.ai = addCost(e.design.ai, r.cost);   /* wave204: what the Hub's work on this email has cost */
    keepMoving(e.design, res.design);   /* wave204: a video card keeps its moving preview */
    SS.undo = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({ design: e.design, subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader }));
    res.design.from = e.design.from; res.design.source = e.design.source; if (e.design.voice) res.design.voice = e.design.voice;
    e.design = res.design; if (res.subject) e.subject = res.subject; if (res.preheader) e.preheader = res.preheader;
    SS.questions = res.questions; SS.notes = res.notes; SS.open = {};
    if (mode === 'change') SS.change = '';
    SS.msg = mode === 'change' ? 'Changed. Not what you wanted? Undo puts it back.' : 'Your answers are in.';
    changed(true);
  }
  /* ---------- where it goes: a test, one person, a list (a campaign), or a template (and so the automations) ----------
     Every send goes through mail-send: the permission rule and mail_mode decide, as for any email the Hub sends. */
  var STABS = [['test', 'Send me a test', 'mail'], ['person', 'One person', 'person'], ['list', 'A list', 'list'], ['template', 'Save as a template', 'save']];
  function contactMatches(q) {
    q = lower(q); if (q.length < 2) return [];
    return contacts().filter(function (c) {
      if (c.merged_into) return false;
      var hay = lower([c.full_name, (c.emails || []).join(' '), c.primary_email].join(' '));
      return q.split(/\s+/).every(function (w) { return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0; });
    }).slice(0, 8);
  }
  function permLine(c) {
    var c152 = crm(), p = null;
    try { p = c152 && c152.permWhy ? c152.permWhy(c) : null; } catch (e) { p = null; }
    if (!p) return '';
    var kind = SS.d.kind, ok = kind === 'transactional' ? p.trans : kind === 'volunteer' ? p.trans && !/unsubscribed/.test(p.why) : p.news;
    return '<div class="w202perm ' + (ok ? 'ok' : 'no') + '">' + I(ok ? 'check' : 'alert') + ' ' + (ok ? 'They can get it (' + E(p.why) + ').' : 'The permission rule will hold it back: ' + E(p.why) + '.') + '</div>';
  }
  function modeNote() {
    var st = mailSettings(); if (!st) return '';
    return st.mail_mode === 'live' ? '' : '<div class="w155mode test">' + I('alert') + ' Test mode: email goes only to the test recipients (' + E(st.mail_test_recipients || 'none set') + ') until an admin switches the Hub to live in the Email tab.</div>';
  }
  function stopsHtml() {
    var st = stops(); if (!st.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w202stops"><b>' + I('alert') + ' Fix ' + (st.length === 1 ? 'this' : 'these') + ' first:</b><ul>' + st.slice(0, 6).map(function (c) { return '<li>' + (c.i != null ? '<button class="w202lnk" data-w202goto="' + c.i + '">' + E(c.msg) + '</button>' : E(c.msg)) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
  }
  function sendHtml() {
    var s = SS.send; if (!s || !SS.d) return '';
    var e = SS.d, blocked = stops().length > 0, h = '';
    if (s.tab === 'test') {
      h = '<p>A test goes to you, <b>' + E(myEmail()) + '</b>, marked as a test, with your own name in it.</p>' + stopsHtml() +
        '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202dotest' + (blocked || s.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('mail') + ' Send me a test</button></div>';
    }
    if (s.tab === 'person') {
      var c = s.contact ? contacts().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(s.contact); })[0] : null;
      h = modeNote() + (c ? '<div class="w202who" data-w198skip><b>' + E(c.full_name || '(no name)') + '</b> <span class="m">' + E(c.primary_email || (c.emails || [])[0] || 'no email address') + '</span>' +
        '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w202whox>Someone else</button></div>' + permLine(c)
        : '<label class="w155lb">Who?<input class="w152in" data-w202who value="' + E(s.q || '') + '" placeholder="A name or an email address" autocomplete="off"></label><div class="w202whol" data-w202whores data-w198skip>' + whoResults(s.q) + '</div>') +
        stopsHtml() + (c ? '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202doperson' + (blocked || s.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('send') + ' Send it to ' + E(String(c.full_name || 'them').split(' ')[0]) + '</button></div>' : '');
    }
    if (s.tab === 'list') {
      var ls = lists();
      h = (e.kind === 'transactional' ? '<div class="w155mode test">' + I('alert') + ' A thank-you, reminder or confirmation goes person by person (One person) or through an automation. To send it to a list, change its kind to News, A volunteer call-out or An appeal.</div>'
        : '<p>This makes a campaign draft with this email: the Campaigns tab then checks who on the list can get it, sends you a test and sends it, at Resend\u2019s pace.</p>' +
          (ls.length ? '<label class="w155lb">The list<select class="w152sel" data-w202list><option value="">Pick a list\u2026</option>' + ls.map(function (l) { return '<option value="' + E(l.id) + '"' + (String(l.id) === String(s.list) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(l.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>'
            : '<p class="m">No list yet: make one in the Lists tab first.</p>') + stopsHtml() +
          '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202docamp' + (blocked || s.busy || !s.list ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('list') + ' Make the campaign</button></div>');
    }
    if (s.tab === 'template') {
      var isT = e.one_off === false;
      h = '<p>' + (isT ? 'It is a template: on the studio\u2019s shelf, and in the automations\u2019 list as \u201cSaved email: ' + E(e.name) + '\u201d.' : 'A template stays on the studio\u2019s shelf for the team to use again, and the automations can send it (as a \u201cSaved email\u201d).') + '</p>' +
        '<label class="w155lb">Name<input class="w152in" data-w202t="name" value="' + E(s.t.name) + '" data-w198skip></label>' +
        '<label class="w155lb">When to use it<input class="w152in" data-w202t="description" value="' + E(s.t.description) + '" placeholder="The day after a serving: what the volunteers made possible" data-w198skip></label>' +
        '<div class="w202two"><label class="w155lb half">Shelf<select class="w152sel" data-w202t="category">' + SHELF.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x[0] + '"' + (s.t.category === x[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' +
        '<label class="w155lb half">For<select class="w152sel" data-w202t="audience"><option value="">Anyone</option>' + Object.keys(AUD).map(function (a) { return '<option value="' + a + '"' + (s.t.audience === a ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(AUD[a]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label></div>' +
        (isT ? '<label class="w202ck"><input type="checkbox" data-w202t="active"' + (s.t.active !== false ? ' checked' : '') + '> On (an automation can send it)</label>' : '') +
        (hasBrackets({ subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader, html_body: MAIL202.render(e.design) }) ? '<p class="m">It still has [brackets]: fine for a template people fill in, but an automation would send them as they are.</p>' : '') +
        '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w202dotpl' + (s.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('save') + (isT ? ' Save the template' : ' Save as a template') + '</button></div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w154card w202send" data-w202sec="send"><div class="w202sh"><h3>' + I('send') + ' Send or save</h3><button class="w202x" data-w202sendx aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w202stabs" role="tablist">' + STABS.map(function (t) { return '<button role="tab" class="' + (s.tab === t[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w202stab="' + t[0] + '" aria-selected="' + (s.tab === t[0]) + '">' + I(t[2]) + E(t[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      h + (s.busy ? busyHtml(s.busy) : '') + (s.msg ? '<div class="w154msg">' + E(s.msg) + '</div>' : '') + (s.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(s.err) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function whoResults(q) {
    var rows = contactMatches(q);
    if (!str(q).trim() || str(q).trim().length < 2) return '<p class="m">Type at least two letters.</p>';
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="m">Nobody in the CRM matches.</p>';
    return rows.map(function (c) { return '<button class="w202whob" data-w202whopick="' + E(c.id) + '"><b>' + E(c.full_name || '(no name)') + '</b><span class="m">' + E(c.primary_email || (c.emails || [])[0] || 'no email address') + '</span></button>'; }).join('');
  }
  function openSend(tab) {
    var e = SS.d; if (!e) return;
    SS.send = { tab: tab || (SS.to ? 'person' : 'test'), q: '', contact: SS.to || null, list: null, busy: '', msg: '', err: '', t: { name: e.name, description: e.description || '', category: e.category || 'other', audience: e.audience || '', active: e.active !== false } };
    loadMailSettings().then(function () { if (SS.send) paint(); });
    paint();
    setTimeout(function () { var el = document.querySelector('.w202send'); if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); }, 40);
  }
  async function doTest() {
    var s = SS.send; if (!s || stops().length) return;
    s.busy = 'Saving and sending you a test\u2026'; s.err = ''; s.msg = ''; paint();
    var key = await saveNow();
    if (!key) { s.busy = ''; s.err = 'It did not save, so no test went: ' + (SS.saveNote || 'it needs a subject'); paint(); return; }
    var r = await mailFn('test', { template_key: key, to: myEmail() });
    s.busy = ''; if (r.error) s.err = 'The test did not go: ' + r.error; else s.msg = 'Test sent to ' + (r.to || myEmail()) + '. It can take a minute to arrive.'; paint();
  }
  async function doPerson() {
    var s = SS.send; if (!s || !s.contact || stops().length) return;
    var c = contacts().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(s.contact); })[0]; if (!c) return;
    s.busy = 'Saving and sending it to ' + (c.full_name || 'them') + '\u2026'; s.err = ''; s.msg = ''; paint();
    var key = await saveNow();
    if (!key) { s.busy = ''; s.err = 'It did not save, so nothing went.'; paint(); return; }
    var r = await mailFn('send', { contact_id: c.id, template_key: key });
    s.busy = '';
    if (r.error) s.err = r.error;
    else if (r.status === 'suppressed') s.err = 'Not sent: the permission rule held it back (' + (r.basis || 'no permission') + ').';
    else if (r.status === 'sent') s.msg = 'Sent to ' + (r.to || c.full_name) + '. It is on their timeline in the CRM.';
    else s.err = 'It did not go: ' + (r.error || r.status || 'no answer');
    paint();
  }
  async function doCampaign() {
    var s = SS.send, e = SS.d; if (!s || !s.list || stops().length || e.kind === 'transactional') return;
    s.busy = 'Saving and making the campaign\u2026'; s.err = ''; paint();
    var key = await saveNow();
    if (!key) { s.busy = ''; s.err = 'It did not save, so no campaign was made.'; paint(); return; }
    var row = { name: str(e.name).trim() || e.subject, list_id: Number(s.list), template_key: key, kind: e.kind, subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader || null, vars: { studio: true },
      status: 'draft', created_by: myEmail(), updated_by: myEmail(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    var r; try { r = await db.from('crm_campaigns').insert(row).select().single(); } catch (er) { r = { error: er }; }
    s.busy = '';
    if (!r || r.error || !r.data) { s.err = 'The campaign was not made: ' + msgOf(r && r.error); paint(); return; }
    var c = crm(); try { if (c && c.load) await c.load('campaigns', true); } catch (er) { /* fine */ }
    SS.send = null;
    go('#crm/campaigns/' + r.data.id);
  }
  async function doTemplate() {
    var s = SS.send, e = SS.d; if (!s) return;
    if (!str(s.t.name).trim()) { s.err = 'Give the template a name.'; paint(); return; }
    var was = { one_off: e.one_off, name: e.name, description: e.description, category: e.category, audience: e.audience, active: e.active };
    e.one_off = false; e.name = s.t.name.trim(); e.description = s.t.description; e.category = s.t.category || 'other'; e.audience = s.t.audience || null; e.active = s.t.active !== false;
    s.busy = 'Saving the template\u2026'; s.err = ''; s.msg = ''; paint();
    SS.dirty = true;
    var key = await saveNow();
    s.busy = '';
    if (!key) { Object.keys(was).forEach(function (k) { e[k] = was[k]; }); s.err = 'Not saved: ' + (SS.saveNote || 'it needs a subject'); paint(); return; }
    s.msg = 'Saved as a template: it is on the studio\u2019s shelf, and the automations can send it as \u201cSaved email: ' + e.name + '\u201d.';
    paint();
  }
  /* ---------- wave204 (04/10/26): Undo and Redo, and a way back to the step before. Soraya Tyree's review: "some sort of
     backspace button or button to go back to the previous step in case I added something but want to go back. I tried command
     Z on my keyboard but nothing happened". Every change to the open email is a step (a block added, moved, copied or removed,
     a field typed in, a photo or a moving preview, the settings, the Hub's own changes): Undo and Redo sit in the editor's bar,
     with Cmd/Ctrl+Z and Shift+Cmd/Ctrl+Z (Ctrl+Y on Windows). Typing in one field is one step until a pause or another field.
     The history belongs to the open email and starts again when another one opens. The keys are the studio's only when the
     email has the focus (or nothing does): in the Ask the Hub box, the answers and the send panel the browser's own undo works.
     When the Hub put the email together from your words or a template, "What you wrote" (or "Your details") takes you back to
     that step with everything you gave it; the email stays in Recent emails. ---------- */
  var HIST_MAX = 80;
  function histNow() { try { return performance.now(); } catch (e) { return Date.now(); } }   /* a pause is measured on the page's own clock */
  function histSnap(e) {
    e = e || SS.d; if (!e) return null;
    var d = e.design; if (d && d.ai) { d = Object.assign({}, d); delete d.ai; }   /* what was spent is spent: never a step */
    return JSON.stringify({ design: d, subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader, name: e.name, kind: e.kind, audience: e.audience, lang: e.lang });
  }
  function histReset() { var H = SS.hist; H.past = []; H.future = []; H.cur = histSnap(); H.field = null; H.at = 0; }
  /* from changed(): the state before this change goes on the Undo list, unless it continues a burst of typing in one field */
  function histRecord(field) {
    var H = SS.hist; if (!SS.d) return;
    var now = histSnap(); if (H.cur == null) { H.cur = now; return; }
    if (now === H.cur) return;
    var t = histNow(), burst = !!field && H.field === field && t - H.at < 1500;
    if (!burst) { H.past.push(H.cur); if (H.past.length > HIST_MAX) H.past.shift(); }
    H.future = []; H.cur = now; H.field = field || null; H.at = t;
    histButtons();
  }
  function histApply(s) {
    var e = SS.d, o = JSON.parse(s), ai = e.design && e.design.ai;
    e.design = o.design; if (ai) e.design.ai = ai; e.subject = o.subject; e.preheader = o.preheader; e.name = o.name; e.kind = o.kind; e.audience = o.audience; e.lang = o.lang;
  }
  /* dir -1: undo, 1: redo */
  function histGo(dir) {
    var H = SS.hist, e = SS.d; if (!e || SS.ro) return false;
    var now = histSnap(); if (H.cur != null && now !== H.cur) { H.past.push(H.cur); H.future = []; H.cur = now; }
    var from = dir < 0 ? H.past : H.future, to = dir < 0 ? H.future : H.past;
    if (!from.length) { T(dir < 0 ? 'Nothing to undo' : 'Nothing to redo'); return false; }
    to.push(H.cur); H.cur = from.pop(); H.field = null; H.at = 0;
    histApply(H.cur);
    var ids = {}; e.design.blocks.forEach(function (b) { ids[b.id] = 1; }); Object.keys(SS.open).forEach(function (k) { if (!ids[k]) delete SS.open[k]; });
    SS.msg = ''; SS.undo = null; SS.err = ''; SS.confirmDel = false;
    SS.dirty = true; SS.saveNote = ''; paint();
    later('save', 2500, function () { if (SS.dirty) saveNow(); });
    return true;
  }
  function histMac() { try { return /Mac|iPhone|iPad/i.test(navigator.platform || navigator.userAgent || ''); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function histBar() {
    if (SS.ro || !SS.d) return '';
    var H = SS.hist, mac = histMac();
    return '<span class="w204hist" role="group" aria-label="Undo and redo">' +
      '<button class="w152btn sm ghost w204hb" id="w204undo" data-w204hist="-1" title="Undo the last change (' + (mac ? '\u2318Z' : 'Ctrl+Z') + ')"' + (H.past.length ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + I('undo') + '<span>Undo</span></button>' +
      '<button class="w152btn sm ghost w204hb" id="w204redo" data-w204hist="1" title="Redo (' + (mac ? '\u21e7\u2318Z' : 'Ctrl+Y') + ')"' + (H.future.length ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + I('redo') + '<span>Redo</span></button></span>';
  }
  function histButtons() {
    var u = document.getElementById('w204undo'), r = document.getElementById('w204redo');
    if (u) u.disabled = !SS.hist.past.length; if (r) r.disabled = !SS.hist.future.length;
  }
  /* may Cmd/Ctrl+Z be the studio's here? Yes on the email's own fields, the page itself or the preview; no in other boxes */
  function histKeyOk(a) {
    if (!a || a === document.body || a === document.documentElement) return true;
    if (a.id === 'w202frame') return true;
    if (!within(a)) return false;
    if (a.hasAttribute('data-w202f') || a.hasAttribute('data-w202e') || a.hasAttribute('data-w202voicesel')) return true;
    return !/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(a.tagName) && !a.isContentEditable;
  }
  /* ---------- back to the step before the Hub's draft ---------- */
  function backBtn() {
    var B = SS.back; if (!B || !SS.d || B.d !== SS.d) return '';
    return '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w204back title="Back to the step before, with everything you gave the Hub">' + I('back') + ' ' + (B.view === 'tpl' ? 'Your details' : 'What you wrote') + '</button>';
  }
  function backToStart() {
    var B = SS.back; if (!B) return;
    var s = SS.start, e = SS.d;
    if (B.view === 'tpl') { s.tpl = B.tpl; s.asks = Object.assign({}, B.asks || {}); s.extra = B.extra || ''; }
    else { s.brief = B.brief || ''; s.audience = B.audience || s.audience; s.kind = B.kind || s.kind; }
    s.photos = (B.photos || []).slice(); s.lang = B.lang || s.lang; s.voice = B.voice || s.voice;
    SS.back = null;
    if (e && SS.dirty && !SS.ro) saveNow();
    SS.startNote = { view: B.view, text: e && e.key ? 'Here is what you gave the Hub, to change and try again. The email it made is kept in Recent emails.' : 'Here is what you gave the Hub, to change and try again.' };
    go(B.view === 'tpl' ? '#crm/studio/tpl/' + B.tpl : '#crm/studio/ai');
  }
  /* ---------- wave204 (04/10/26): GIF204, the animated GIF encoder behind the moving preview ----------
     Self-contained (no page, no db), so node runs this file alone for the tests. One palette for the whole clip (median cut
     over a 15-bit histogram: up to 255 colours, index 255 kept for transparency), so colours hold still from frame to frame.
     Every later frame carries only the pixels that changed by more than a little (the rest transparent, the frame cropped to
     what changed), which keeps a few seconds of video small enough for an inbox. LZW as in the GIF89a spec (the variable code
     size timing follows omggif's encoder). Loops forever; disposal "do not dispose". */
  var GIF204 = (function () {
    var TI = 255;   /* the transparent index */
    function chan(x, c) { return c === 0 ? x >> 10 : c === 1 ? (x >> 5) & 31 : x & 31; }
    /* frames: RGBA arrays of one size; up to max colours; step: every step-th pixel goes into the histogram */
    function palette(frames, max, step, keep) {
      keep = keep || [];
      var hist = new Uint32Array(32768), sr = new Float64Array(32768), sg = new Float64Array(32768), sb = new Float64Array(32768), f, d, p, i, key;
      for (f = 0; f < frames.length; f++) { d = frames[f]; for (p = 0; p < d.length; p += 4 * step) { key = ((d[p] >> 3) << 10) | ((d[p + 1] >> 3) << 5) | (d[p + 2] >> 3); hist[key]++; sr[key] += d[p]; sg[key] += d[p + 1]; sb[key] += d[p + 2]; } }
      var bins = []; for (i = 0; i < 32768; i++) if (hist[i]) bins.push(i);
      function box(list) {
        var lo = [31, 31, 31], hi = [0, 0, 0], n = 0;
        for (var k = 0; k < list.length; k++) { var x = list[k]; n += hist[x]; for (var c = 0; c < 3; c++) { var v = chan(x, c); if (v < lo[c]) lo[c] = v; if (v > hi[c]) hi[c] = v; } }
        var r = [hi[0] - lo[0], hi[1] - lo[1], hi[2] - lo[2]], ch = r[1] >= r[0] && r[1] >= r[2] ? 1 : r[0] >= r[2] ? 0 : 2;
        return { list: list, n: n, ch: ch, range: r[ch] };
      }
      var boxes = bins.length ? [box(bins)] : [], room = Math.max(1, max - keep.length);
      while (boxes.length < room) {
        var bi = -1, best = 0;
        for (i = 0; i < boxes.length; i++) { var s = boxes[i].list.length > 1 ? boxes[i].n * (boxes[i].range + 1) : 0; if (s > best) { best = s; bi = i; } }
        if (bi < 0) break;
        var b = boxes[bi], c0 = b.ch, half = b.n / 2, acc = 0, cut = 1;
        b.list.sort(function (x, y) { return chan(x, c0) - chan(y, c0); });
        for (i = 0; i < b.list.length - 1; i++) { acc += hist[b.list[i]]; if (acc >= half) { cut = i + 1; break; } }
        boxes.splice(bi, 1, box(b.list.slice(0, cut)), box(b.list.slice(cut)));
      }
      var pal = new Uint8Array(256 * 3);
      boxes.forEach(function (bx, k) {
        var r = 0, g = 0, bl = 0;
        bx.list.forEach(function (x) { r += sr[x]; g += sg[x]; bl += sb[x]; });   /* the true average of the pixels in the box */
        pal[k * 3] = Math.round(r / bx.n); pal[k * 3 + 1] = Math.round(g / bx.n); pal[k * 3 + 2] = Math.round(bl / bx.n);
      });
      var n = boxes.length;
      keep.slice(0, max - n).forEach(function (c) { pal[n * 3] = c[0]; pal[n * 3 + 1] = c[1]; pal[n * 3 + 2] = c[2]; n++; });
      return { pal: pal, n: Math.max(1, n) };
    }
    /* the nearest palette colour for a pixel, remembered per 15-bit colour */
    function mapper(pal, n) {
      var cache = new Int16Array(32768); for (var z = 0; z < 32768; z++) cache[z] = -1;
      return function (r, g, b) {
        var key = ((r >> 3) << 10) | ((g >> 3) << 5) | (b >> 3), v = cache[key];
        if (v >= 0) return v;
        var cr = ((r >> 3) << 3) + 4, cg = ((g >> 3) << 3) + 4, cb = ((b >> 3) << 3) + 4, best = 0, bd = 1e12;
        for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) { var dr = pal[k * 3] - cr, dg = pal[k * 3 + 1] - cg, db = pal[k * 3 + 2] - cb, dd = dr * dr * 2 + dg * dg * 4 + db * db * 3; if (dd < bd) { bd = dd; best = k; } }
        cache[key] = best; return best;
      };
    }
    /* one frame's indexes as LZW sub-blocks, written into out at pos; returns the new pos */
    function lzw(idx, len, out, pos) {
      var min = 8, clear = 256, eoi = 257, next = 258, size = 9, cur = 0, shift = 0;
      out[pos++] = min;
      var sub = pos++;
      function flush(bits) { while (shift >= bits) { out[pos++] = cur & 255; cur >>= 8; shift -= 8; if (pos === sub + 256) { out[sub] = 255; sub = pos++; } } }
      function emit(c) { cur |= c << shift; shift += size; flush(8); }
      var table = new Map(), ib = idx[0];
      emit(clear);
      for (var i = 1; i < len; i++) {
        var k = idx[i], key = (ib << 8) | k, code = table.get(key);
        if (code === undefined) {
          emit(ib);
          if (next === 4096) { emit(clear); next = 258; size = 9; table.clear(); }
          else { if (next >= (1 << size)) size++; table.set(key, next++); }
          ib = k;
        } else ib = code;
      }
      emit(ib); emit(eoi); flush(1);
      if (sub + 1 === pos) out[sub] = 0; else { out[sub] = pos - sub - 1; out[pos++] = 0; }
      return pos;
    }
    /* frames: RGBA arrays (w x h); delay in hundredths of a second; o.thr: how far a colour may drift before a pixel is
       redrawn (squared RGB distance); o.keep: [r, g, b] colours that must be in the palette exactly (a small gold button
       would otherwise be averaged away by the median cut); o.onProg(fraction); o.yield(): a promise between frames */
    async function encode(frames, w, h, delay, o) {
      o = o || {};
      if (!frames.length || w < 1 || h < 1) throw new Error('Nothing to make a preview from.');
      var px = w * h, step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(frames.length * px / 200000));
      var P = palette(frames, 255, step, o.keep), pal = P.pal, map = mapper(pal, P.n), thr = o.thr == null ? 300 : o.thr;
      var shown = new Uint8Array(px), idx = new Uint8Array(px), crop = new Uint8Array(px);
      var out = new Uint8Array(1024 + frames.length * (Math.ceil(px * 1.6) + 64)), pos = 0;
      var u8 = function (v) { out[pos++] = v; }, u16 = function (v) { out[pos++] = v & 255; out[pos++] = (v >> 8) & 255; }, txt = function (s) { for (var c = 0; c < s.length; c++) u8(s.charCodeAt(c)); };
      txt('GIF89a'); u16(w); u16(h); u8(0xF7); u8(0); u8(0);
      out.set(pal, pos); pos += 768;
      u8(0x21); u8(0xFF); u8(11); txt('NETSCAPE2.0'); u8(3); u8(1); u16(0); u8(0);
      for (var f = 0; f < frames.length; f++) {
        var d = frames[f], x0 = w, y0 = h, x1 = -1, y1 = -1;
        for (var p = 0, q = 0; p < px; p++, q += 4) {
          var ni = map(d[q], d[q + 1], d[q + 2]);
          if (f === 0) { shown[p] = ni; idx[p] = ni; continue; }
          var si = shown[p];
          if (ni === si) { idx[p] = TI; continue; }
          var dr = pal[ni * 3] - pal[si * 3], dg = pal[ni * 3 + 1] - pal[si * 3 + 1], db = pal[ni * 3 + 2] - pal[si * 3 + 2];
          if (dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db < thr) { idx[p] = TI; continue; }
          idx[p] = ni; shown[p] = ni;
          var x = p % w, y = (p / w) | 0;
          if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x; if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y;
        }
        var fx = 0, fy = 0, fw = w, fh = h, data = idx, len = px;
        if (f > 0) {
          if (x1 < 0) { fw = 1; fh = 1; crop[0] = TI; data = crop; len = 1; }
          else {
            fx = x0; fy = y0; fw = x1 - x0 + 1; fh = y1 - y0 + 1; len = fw * fh;
            for (var yy = 0, z = 0; yy < fh; yy++) { var row = (fy + yy) * w + fx; for (var xx = 0; xx < fw; xx++) crop[z++] = idx[row + xx]; }
            data = crop;
          }
        }
        u8(0x21); u8(0xF9); u8(4); u8(4 | (f > 0 ? 1 : 0)); u16(delay); u8(TI); u8(0);
        u8(0x2C); u16(fx); u16(fy); u16(fw); u16(fh); u8(0);
        pos = lzw(data, len, out, pos);
        if (o.onProg) o.onProg((f + 1) / frames.length);
        if (o.yield) await o.yield();
      }
      u8(0x3B);
      return out.slice(0, pos);
    }
    return { encode: encode, palette: palette, lzw: lzw };
  })();
  /* ---------- wave204 (04/10/26): the moving preview. Soraya Tyree's review: "something we discussed in last week's marketing
     meeting was the possibility of having a video that plays in the email so people don't have to click on anything." Gmail and
     Outlook do not play video inside an email (Apple Mail is about the only one that does), but they play an animated picture.
     So the video card can carry a moving preview: a few silent seconds of the writer's own video, read here in the browser
     (frames from the file at 10 or 8 a second, cropped to the shape picked, our gold play button drawn on), made into an animated
     GIF (GIF204) and uploaded to content-images/email/ like a photo. It plays as soon as the email opens (Outlook on Windows
     shows its first frame) and a tap opens the full video, with sound, at the card's link. The video file never leaves the
     computer. The Hub page's Content-Security-Policy allows media from blob: URLs for this (build/site/_headers). ---------- */
  var MV = { std: { delay: 10, px: 157000, fps: 10 }, light: { delay: 12, px: 96000, fps: 8 } }, MV_LEN = [2, 3, 4, 5];
  function mvDims(shape, size) {
    var ar = shape === 'square' ? 1 : shape === 'tall' ? 0.8 : 16 / 9, px = (MV[size] || MV.std).px;
    var w = Math.min(MAIL202.W, Math.round(Math.sqrt(px * ar))), h = Math.round(w / ar);
    return { w: w - (w % 2), h: h - (h % 2) };
  }
  function mvShapeFor(w, h) { var a = h ? w / h : 1.7; return a > 1.25 ? 'wide' : a < 0.85 ? 'tall' : 'square'; }
  function mvTime(t) { t = Math.max(0, Number(t) || 0); var m = Math.floor(t / 60), s = t - m * 60; return m + ':' + (s < 9.95 ? '0' : '') + s.toFixed(1); }
  function mvSpan(M) { return mvTime(M.start) + ' to ' + mvTime(Math.min(M.dur, M.start + M.len)) + ' of ' + mvTime(M.dur); }
  function kbText(kb) { kb = Number(kb) || 0; return kb >= 1000 ? (kb / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' MB' : Math.max(1, Math.round(kb)) + ' KB'; }
  function mvVideo(url) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var v = document.createElement('video'), done = false, tm;
      var fin = function (err) { if (done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(tm); v.onloadeddata = null; v.onerror = null; if (err) rej(err); else res(v); };
      tm = setTimeout(function () { fin(new Error('The video took too long to open. Try a shorter clip, or an MP4 file.')); }, 20000);
      v.muted = true; v.playsInline = true; v.preload = 'auto';
      v.onloadeddata = function () { fin(null); };
      v.onerror = function () { fin(new Error('This video could not be read here. An MP4 file works best, in Chrome or Safari.')); };
      v.src = url;
    });
  }
  function mvSeek(v, t) {
    return new Promise(function (res) {
      var done = false, tm, fin = function () { if (done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(tm); v.removeEventListener('seeked', fin); res(); };
      tm = setTimeout(fin, 4000);
      v.addEventListener('seeked', fin);
      try { v.currentTime = t; } catch (e) { fin(); }
    });
  }
  /* our gold play button, drawn on every frame (so even Outlook's first frame says "video") */
  function mvBadge(g, w, h) {
    var r = Math.round(Math.max(16, Math.min(w, h) * 0.11)), cx = Math.round(w / 2), cy = Math.round(h / 2), s = r * 0.46;
    g.save();
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(220,165,39,0.95)'; g.fill();
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(cx - s * 0.62, cy - s); g.lineTo(cx - s * 0.62, cy + s); g.lineTo(cx + s * 0.95, cy); g.closePath(); g.fillStyle = GREEN; g.fill();
    g.restore();
  }
  function mvFree(M) {
    if (!M) return;
    if (M.out) { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(M.out.url); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    try { URL.revokeObjectURL(M.url); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function closeMoving() { mvFree(SS.mv); SS.mv = null; }
  function mvStale() { var M = SS.mv; if (M && M.out) { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(M.out.url); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } M.out = null; } }
  async function openMoving(i, file) {
    if (!file || !SS.d || SS.ro) return;
    var b = SS.d.design.blocks[i]; if (!b || b.type !== 'video') return;
    if (!/^video\//i.test(file.type || '') && !/\.(mp4|m4v|mov|webm|ogv)$/i.test(str(file.name))) { SS.err = 'That is not a video file. Use an MP4 (or a MOV or WebM).'; paint(); return; }
    closeMoving();
    var M = SS.mv = { id: b.id, name: str(file.name) || 'Your video', url: URL.createObjectURL(file), dur: 0, vw: 0, vh: 0, start: 0, len: 3, shape: 'wide', size: 'std', busy: '', prog: 0, err: '', out: null };
    SS.err = ''; paint();
    try {
      var v = await mvVideo(M.url);
      if (!isFinite(v.duration)) await mvSeek(v, 1e6);   /* some recordings only learn their length at the end */
      if (SS.mv !== M) return;
      M.dur = isFinite(v.duration) ? v.duration : 0; M.vw = v.videoWidth; M.vh = v.videoHeight;
      if (!M.dur || !M.vw) throw new Error('This video has no picture the Hub can read. Try an MP4 file.');
      M.shape = mvShapeFor(M.vw, M.vh);
      M.len = M.dur >= 3 ? 3 : Math.max(0.5, Math.floor(M.dur * 10) / 10);
      M.start = M.dur > M.len + 2 ? 1 : 0;
      try { v.removeAttribute('src'); v.load(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (e) { if (SS.mv === M) M.err = msgOf(e); }
    if (SS.mv === M) paint();
  }
  function mvProg(M, pct) { M.prog = pct; var el = document.getElementById('w204mvprog'); if (el) el.textContent = pct + '%'; }
  async function makeMoving() {
    var M = SS.mv; if (!M || M.busy || !M.dur) return;
    mvStale();
    var cfg = MV[M.size] || MV.std, dm = mvDims(M.shape, M.size), n = Math.max(2, Math.round(M.len * 100 / cfg.delay));
    M.busy = 'Making the moving preview\u2026'; M.prog = 0; M.err = ''; paint();
    try {
      var v = await mvVideo(M.url);
      if (SS.mv !== M) return;
      var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = dm.w; c.height = dm.h;
      var g = c.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
      var vw = v.videoWidth, vh = v.videoHeight, ar = dm.w / dm.h, sw, sh, sx, sy;
      if (vw / vh > ar) { sh = vh; sw = vh * ar; sx = (vw - sw) / 2; sy = 0; } else { sw = vw; sh = vw / ar; sx = 0; sy = (vh - sh) / 2; }
      var frames = [], end = Math.max(0, (isFinite(v.duration) ? v.duration : M.dur) - 0.04);
      for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) {
        await mvSeek(v, Math.min(end, M.start + k * cfg.delay / 100));
        if (SS.mv !== M) return;
        g.fillStyle = GREEN; g.fillRect(0, 0, dm.w, dm.h);
        g.drawImage(v, sx, sy, sw, sh, 0, 0, dm.w, dm.h);
        mvBadge(g, dm.w, dm.h);
        frames.push(g.getImageData(0, 0, dm.w, dm.h).data);
        mvProg(M, Math.round((k + 1) / n * 70));
      }
      try { v.removeAttribute('src'); v.load(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      var bytes = await GIF204.encode(frames, dm.w, dm.h, cfg.delay, { keep: [[220, 165, 39], [16, 63, 57], [255, 250, 242]], onProg: function (p) { mvProg(M, 70 + Math.round(p * 30)); }, yield: function () { return new Promise(function (r) { setTimeout(r, 0); }); } });
      if (SS.mv !== M) return;
      var blob = new Blob([bytes], { type: 'image/gif' });
      M.out = { blob: blob, url: URL.createObjectURL(blob), kb: Math.max(1, Math.round(blob.size / 1024)), w: dm.w, h: dm.h, frames: n, secs: M.len };
    } catch (e) { M.err = msgOf(e); }
    M.busy = ''; paint();
  }
  async function useMoving() {
    var M = SS.mv; if (!M || !M.out || M.busy || !SS.d) return;
    var b = SS.d.design.blocks.filter(function (x) { return x.id === M.id; })[0];
    if (!b || b.type !== 'video') { closeMoving(); paint(); return; }
    M.busy = 'Uploading the preview\u2026'; M.err = ''; paint();
    try {
      var now = new Date(), path = 'email/' + now.getFullYear() + '/' + ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '/' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10) + Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4) + '.gif';
      var up = await db.storage.from('content-images').upload(path, M.out.blob, { contentType: 'image/gif', upsert: false, cacheControl: '31536000' });
      if (up && up.error) throw new Error('The upload did not go through: ' + msgOf(up.error));
      var pub = db.storage.from('content-images').getPublicUrl(path), src = pub && pub.data && pub.data.publicUrl;
      if (!src) throw new Error('The upload did not give back a link.');
      if (SS.mv !== M) return;
      b.gif = src; b.gw = M.out.w; b.gh = M.out.h; b.gkb = M.out.kb;
      closeMoving(); SS.open[b.id] = true;
      T('The moving preview is in the email');
      changed(true);
    } catch (e) { if (SS.mv === M) { M.busy = ''; M.err = msgOf(e); paint(); } }
  }
  /* the slot in the video card's form */
  function movingSlot(i, b) {
    var has = !!str(b.gif).trim();
    return '<div class="w204mvslot">' + (has ? '<img src="' + E(b.gif) + '" alt="The moving preview">' : '<div class="w202noimg">' + I('film') + '</div>') +
      '<div class="w204mvtx"><b>Moving preview</b><span class="m">' + (has ? 'Plays as soon as the email opens' + (b.gkb ? ' (' + kbText(b.gkb) + ')' : '') + '. A tap opens the full video at the link.'
        : 'Inboxes like Gmail and Outlook do not play video inside an email, but they play a moving picture: a few silent seconds of your video that start as soon as the email opens. A tap opens the full video, with sound, at the link.') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="w202slb"><label class="w152btn sm' + (has ? ' ghost' : '') + '">' + I('upload') + (has ? ' Make a new one' : ' Make one from a video file') + '<input type="file" accept="video/*,.mp4,.mov,.m4v,.webm" data-w204vfile="' + i + '" hidden></label>' +
      (has ? '<button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w204mvrm="' + i + '">' + I('x') + ' Remove it</button>' : '') + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function movingHtml() {
    var M = SS.mv; if (!M) return '';
    var body;
    if (!M.dur) body = M.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(M.err) + '</div><div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w204mvx>Close</button></div>' : busyHtml('Opening the video\u2026');
    else {
      var maxStart = Math.max(0, M.dur - M.len), dm = mvDims(M.shape, M.size), cfg = MV[M.size] || MV.std, dis = M.busy ? ' disabled' : '';
      var lens = MV_LEN.filter(function (l) { return l <= M.dur + 0.05; }); if (!lens.length) lens = [M.len];
      var left = '<div class="w204mvl"><video class="w204mvv" data-w204mvvideo src="' + E(M.url) + '" muted playsinline controls preload="auto" aria-label="Your video"></video>' +
        '<label class="w155lb">Start at <span class="m" id="w204mvt">' + E(mvSpan(M)) + '</span><input type="range" class="w204range" data-w204mvstart min="0" max="' + maxStart.toFixed(1) + '" step="0.1" value="' + Math.min(M.start, maxStart).toFixed(1) + '"' + dis + ' aria-label="Where the preview starts"></label>' +
        '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn sm ghost" data-w204mvhere' + dis + '>Start where the video is paused</button></div>' +
        '<div class="w155lb">Length ' + segHtml('data-w204mvlen', lens.map(function (l) { return [String(l), l + ' seconds']; }), String(M.len)) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w155lb">Shape ' + segHtml('data-w204mvshape', [['wide', 'Wide'], ['square', 'Square'], ['tall', 'Tall']], M.shape) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w155lb">Size ' + segHtml('data-w204mvsize', [['std', 'Standard'], ['light', 'Lighter']], M.size) + '<span class="m">' + dm.w + ' \u00d7 ' + dm.h + ' pixels, ' + cfg.fps + ' frames a second. Lighter loads faster on a phone.</span></div></div>';
      var right;
      if (M.busy) right = '<div class="w204mvwait">' + busyHtml(M.busy) + '<p class="m" id="w204mvprog" aria-live="polite">' + (M.prog ? M.prog + '%' : '') + '</p></div>';
      else if (M.out) {
        var kb = M.out.kb;
        right = '<img class="w204mvout" src="' + E(M.out.url) + '" alt="The moving preview" width="' + M.out.w + '">' +
          '<p class="m"><b>' + kbText(kb) + '</b> \u00b7 ' + M.out.frames + ' frames \u00b7 ' + M.out.w + ' \u00d7 ' + M.out.h + '. ' + (kb > 2500 ? 'Heavy for a phone on data: try Lighter, or fewer seconds.' : kb > 1500 ? 'Fine for email; Lighter would load faster.' : 'Light enough for any inbox.') + '</p>' +
          (M.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(M.err) + '</div>' : '') +
          '<div class="w154acts"><button class="w152btn" data-w204mvuse>' + I('check') + ' Use this preview</button><button class="w152btn ghost" data-w204mvmake>Make it again</button></div>';
      } else right = '<div class="w204mvph">' + I('film') + '<p class="m">Pick where it starts and how long it runs, then make it. It takes a few seconds, here on your computer: your video is not uploaded, only the preview.</p>' +
        (M.err ? '<div class="w152err">' + E(M.err) + '</div>' : '') + '<button class="w152btn" data-w204mvmake>' + I('spark') + ' Make the preview</button></div>';
      body = '<div class="w204mvgrid">' + left + '<div class="w204mvr">' + right + '</div></div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w202modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Moving preview"><div class="w202mbox w204mv">' +
      '<div class="w202mh"><h3>' + I('film') + ' Moving preview</h3><button class="w202x" data-w204mvx aria-label="Close"' + (M.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + I('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<p class="m" data-w198skip><b>' + E(M.name) + '</b>. A few silent seconds that play as soon as the email opens, with our gold play button on top; a tap opens the full video at the card\u2019s link.</p>' + body + '</div></div>';
  }
  /* after a paint, the video in the panel shows where the preview starts */
  function movingAfterPaint() {
    var M = SS.mv, v = M && SS.root && SS.root.querySelector('[data-w204mvvideo]'); if (!v) return;
    var set = function () { try { if (Math.abs(v.currentTime - M.start) > 0.05) v.currentTime = M.start; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } };
    if (v.readyState >= 1) set(); else v.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', set, { once: true });
  }
  /* the Hub's changes keep a card's moving preview: matched by the link, else by order */
  function keepMoving(from, to) {
    var olds = ((from && from.blocks) || []).filter(function (b) { return b && b.type === 'video' && str(b.gif).trim(); });
    if (!olds.length || !to) return to;
    var used = [];
    (to.blocks || []).filter(function (b) { return b && b.type === 'video' && !str(b.gif).trim(); }).forEach(function (b) {
      var free = olds.filter(function (o) { return used.indexOf(o) < 0; });
      var m = free.filter(function (o) { return str(o.url).trim() && str(o.url).trim() === str(b.url).trim(); })[0] || free[0];
      if (!m) return; used.push(m);
      b.gif = m.gif; if (m.gw) b.gw = m.gw; if (m.gh) b.gh = m.gh; if (m.gkb) b.gkb = m.gkb;
    });
    return to;
  }
  /* ---------- wave204 (04/10/26): text without the markup. Soraya Tyree's review: "a place where I can drop the text and it
     generates the html for me would be very helpful because I'm not too familiar with that kind of work". Tell the Hub is that
     place, and the front page now says so (paste your text, the Hub designs the email: no HTML). In the editor, and in the
     Tell the Hub and template boxes, text pasted from a document or an email keeps its bold, its links and its paragraphs,
     turned into the studio's **bold** and [words](link); the B and Link buttons (Cmd/Ctrl+B, Cmd/Ctrl+K) mark selected words
     without typing the symbols. ---------- */
  function htmlToMark(html) {
    var doc; try { doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(str(html), 'text/html'); } catch (e) { return null; }
    if (!doc || !doc.body) return null;
    var runs = [], BLOCK = /^(P|DIV|H[1-6]|LI|UL|OL|TR|TABLE|BLOCKQUOTE|SECTION|ARTICLE|HEADER|FOOTER|PRE)$/;
    function weight(el) { var fw = str(el.style && el.style.fontWeight).trim(); return fw === 'bold' || fw === 'bolder' ? 700 : fw === 'normal' || fw === 'lighter' ? 400 : fw ? Number(fw) || 0 : 0; }
    function bold(el, inherited) { var w = weight(el); if (w) return w >= 600; if (/^(B|STRONG|H[1-6])$/.test(el.tagName)) return true; return inherited; }
    function walk(node, b, inLi) {
      for (var c = node.firstChild; c; c = c.nextSibling) {
        if (c.nodeType === 3) { var t = c.nodeValue.replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ').replace(/[\r\n\t ]+/g, ' '); if (t) runs.push({ t: t, b: b }); continue; }
        if (c.nodeType !== 1) continue;
        var tag = c.tagName;
        if (/^(STYLE|SCRIPT|META|TITLE|HEAD|NOSCRIPT|TEMPLATE|IMG|SVG)$/.test(tag)) continue;
        if (tag === 'BR') { runs.push({ br: 1 }); continue; }
        var b2 = bold(c, b);
        if (tag === 'A') {
          var href = str(c.getAttribute('href')).trim(), words = str(c.textContent).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
          if (words && /^(https?:|mailto:|tel:)/i.test(href)) { runs.push({ t: words === href ? href : '[' + words.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '') + '](' + href.replace(/[()\s]/g, function (x) { return encodeURIComponent(x); }) + ')', raw: true }); continue; }
          walk(c, b2, inLi); continue;
        }
        var blk = BLOCK.test(tag) && !(inLi && /^(P|DIV)$/.test(tag));   /* a list point's own paragraph stays on its line */
        if (blk) runs.push({ br: tag === 'LI' ? 1 : 2 });
        if (tag === 'LI') runs.push({ t: '\u2022 ', raw: true });
        walk(c, b2, inLi || tag === 'LI');
        if (blk) runs.push({ br: tag === 'LI' ? 1 : 2 });
      }
    }
    walk(doc.body, false);
    var toks = [];
    runs.forEach(function (r) {
      if (r.br) { toks.push(r); return; }
      var last = toks[toks.length - 1], b = r.raw ? false : !!r.b;
      if (last && !last.br && !last.raw && !r.raw && last.b === b) last.t += r.t; else toks.push({ t: r.t, b: b, raw: !!r.raw });
    });
    var out = '', pend = 0;
    toks.forEach(function (k) {
      if (k.br) { if (out) pend = Math.max(pend, k.br); return; }
      var t = k.t;
      if (!out || pend) t = t.replace(/^\s+/, '');
      if (!t) return;
      if (pend) { out = out.replace(/[ \t]+$/, '') + (pend === 2 ? '\n\n' : '\n'); pend = 0; }
      if (k.b) { var m = t.match(/^(\s*)([\s\S]*?)(\s*)$/); if (m[2]) t = m[1] + '**' + m[2].replace(/\*\*/g, '') + '**' + m[3]; }
      out += t;
    });
    return out.replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim();
  }
  /* the boxes that take pasted formatting: the editor's marked text fields, and Tell the Hub's and the template's own words */
  function pasteBox(t) { return t && t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' && (t.hasAttribute('data-w204fmt') || /^(brief|extra)$/.test(t.getAttribute('data-w202s') || '')); }
  function insertText(t, text, selFrom, selTo, cutFrom, cutTo) {
    var a = cutFrom != null ? cutFrom : t.selectionStart, z = cutTo != null ? cutTo : t.selectionEnd, v = t.value;
    t.value = v.slice(0, a) + text + v.slice(z);
    try { t.focus({ preventScroll: true }); t.setSelectionRange(a + (selFrom != null ? selFrom : text.length), a + (selTo != null ? selTo : selFrom != null ? selFrom : text.length)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    t.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
  }
  function onPaste(ev) {
    var t = ev.target; if (!within(t) || !pasteBox(t) || t.disabled || t.readOnly) return;
    var cd = ev.clipboardData; if (!cd) return;
    var html = cd.getData('text/html'); if (!html || !/<(b|strong|a|span|p|div|li|h[1-6]|br)\b/i.test(html)) return;
    var mark = htmlToMark(html); if (mark == null || !mark.trim()) return;
    if (mark.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() === str(cd.getData('text/plain')).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()) return;   /* nothing to keep: the browser pastes it */
    ev.preventDefault();
    insertText(t, mark);
  }
  /* B and Link on the selected words */
  function fmtApply(t, kind) {
    if (!t || t.disabled) return;
    var a = t.selectionStart, z = t.selectionEnd, v = t.value, sel = v.slice(a, z);
    if (kind === 'b') {
      if (sel && v.slice(a - 2, a) === '**' && v.slice(z, z + 2) === '**') { insertText(t, sel, 0, sel.length, a - 2, z + 2); return; }
      if (!sel) { insertText(t, '****', 2, 2); return; }
      var m = sel.match(/^(\s*)([\s\S]*?)(\s*)$/);
      if (/^\*\*[\s\S]+\*\*$/.test(m[2])) { var inner = m[2].slice(2, -2); insertText(t, m[1] + inner + m[3], m[1].length, m[1].length + inner.length); return; }
      insertText(t, m[1] + '**' + m[2] + '**' + m[3], m[1].length, m[1].length + m[2].length + 4);
      return;
    }
    if (kind === 'link') {
      var u = sel.trim();
      if (/^(https?:\/\/|www\.)\S+$/i.test(u)) { insertText(t, '[words](' + (/^www\./i.test(u) ? 'https://' + u : u) + ')', 1, 6); return; }
      var w = sel.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '') || 'words';
      insertText(t, '[' + w + '](https://)', w.length + 3, w.length + 11);
    }
  }
  function fmtBar() {
    var mac = histMac();
    return '<span class="w204fmt" role="group" aria-label="Formatting"><button type="button" class="w204fb" data-w204fmtb="b" title="Bold: select words, then B (' + (mac ? '\u2318B' : 'Ctrl+B') + ')" aria-label="Bold"><b>B</b></button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w204fb" data-w204fmtb="link" title="Link: select words, then Link (' + (mac ? '\u2318K' : 'Ctrl+K') + ')" aria-label="Link">' + I('link') + '</button></span>';
  }
  /* ---------- wave204 (04/10/26): what the Hub's AI costs. Steel: "Could you add these costs somewhere in the studio so they
     know how much they are spending". Every call through the llm router reports its cost (_wa.cost, US dollars from
     OpenRouter). The studio adds each call to the email it was for (design.ai: calls and cost, saved with the email, kept
     out of Undo since the money is spent), shows that beside Ask the Hub to change it, puts "about 2\u00a2" by every button that
     calls the AI ("free" by the ones that do not), and the front page's What it costs panel shows the price of each step,
     what the whole studio spent this month, last month and in 90 days (email_studio_costs(), from ai_calls, for staff with
     the CRM; admins can read ai_calls themselves until that function is there), and what the emails here have cost, the
     writer's own among them. Measured on 04/10/26 with the voice code in the prompt: 1.6 to 1.9 cents a call. ---------- */
  SS.costs = { open: false, loaded: false, p: null, data: null };
  function money(d) {
    d = Number(d) || 0;
    if (d >= 1) return '$' + d.toFixed(2);
    var c = d * 100;
    return (c === 0 ? '0' : c < 0.1 ? 'under 0.1' : c < 10 ? String(Math.round(c * 10) / 10) : String(Math.round(c))) + '\u00a2';
  }
  function aboutStep(free) { return '<span class="w204about' + (free ? ' free' : '') + '" title="' + (free ? 'Done by hand: nothing to pay' : 'One call to the Hub, in US dollars') + '">' + (free ? 'free' : 'about 2\u00a2') + '</span>'; }
  function addCost(ai, cost) {
    ai = ai && typeof ai === 'object' ? ai : {};
    return { calls: (Number(ai.calls) || 0) + 1, cost: Math.round(((Number(ai.cost) || 0) + (Number(cost) || 0)) * 1e6) / 1e6 };
  }
  /* a call that changed nothing still cost something: on the email, saved, not a step for Undo */
  function spent(e, cost) {
    if (!e || cost == null) return;
    e.design.ai = addCost(e.design.ai, cost); SS.dirty = true; updateSaveState();
    later('save', 2500, function () { if (SS.dirty) saveNow(); });
  }
  function emailCostLine(e) {
    var ai = e && e.design && e.design.ai;
    return ai && ai.calls ? 'The Hub\u2019s work on this email so far: <b>' + money(ai.cost) + '</b> (' + plural(ai.calls, 'call', 'calls') + ').' : 'Nothing spent on this email yet.';
  }
  function torontoMonth(ts) { try { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Toronto', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit' }).format(new Date(ts)).slice(0, 7); } catch (e) { return str(ts).slice(0, 7); } }
  function loadCosts(force) {
    var C = SS.costs; if (C.p) return C.p; if (C.loaded && !force) return Promise.resolve(C);
    C.p = (async function () {
      var d = null;
      try { var r = await db.rpc('email_studio_costs', {}); if (r && !r.error && r.data && r.data.month) d = r.data; } catch (e) { d = null; }
      if (!d && amAdmin()) {
        try {
          var q = await db.from('ai_calls').select('created_at, cost, ok').eq('task', 'email_studio').gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 86400000).toISOString()).limit(5000);
          if (q && !q.error && Array.isArray(q.data)) {
            var now = torontoMonth(Date.now()), y = Number(now.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(now.slice(5, 7)), prev = (m === 1 ? (y - 1) + '-12' : y + '-' + ('0' + (m - 1)).slice(-2));
            d = { month: { calls: 0, cost: 0 }, last_month: { calls: 0, cost: 0 }, d90: { calls: 0, cost: 0 } };
            q.data.forEach(function (row) { var c = Number(row.cost) || 0, mo = torontoMonth(row.created_at); d.d90.calls++; d.d90.cost += c; if (mo === now) { d.month.calls++; d.month.cost += c; } else if (mo === prev) { d.last_month.calls++; d.last_month.cost += c; } });
          }
        } catch (e) { d = null; }
      }
      C.data = d; C.loaded = true; C.p = null;
      var chip = document.getElementById('w204costchip'); if (chip && d) chip.textContent = money(d.month.cost) + ' this month';
      return C;
    })();
    return C.p;
  }
  function costsChip() {
    var C = SS.costs; if (!C.loaded) loadCosts();
    return '<button class="w202vbtn" data-w204costs>' + I('coin') + ' What it costs <span id="w204costchip">' + (C.data ? E(money(C.data.month.cost) + ' this month') : 'about 2\u00a2 a step') + '</span></button>';
  }
  function costsHtml() {
    var C = SS.costs; if (!C.open) return '';
    var d = C.data, me = myEmail(), team = 0, teamn = 0, mine = 0, minen = 0;
    templates().forEach(function (t) { var ai = t.design && t.design.ai, c = ai ? Number(ai.cost) || 0 : 0; if (!c) return; team += c; teamn++; if (lower(t.created_by) === me) { mine += c; minen++; } });
    var rows = [['Tell the Hub: putting it together from your content', 'about 2\u00a2'], ['A round of answers (Put my answers in)', 'about 2\u00a2'], ['Ask the Hub to change it', 'about 2\u00a2 each time'],
      ['Start from a template, filled in by the Hub', 'about 2\u00a2'], ['A photo the Hub looks at', 'about 0.1\u00a2 more'], ['A typical email: Tell the Hub, one round of answers, one change', 'about 5\u00a2'],
      ['Build it yourself, Fill it in myself, Use it, any editing by hand, a moving preview', 'free']];
    var spend = !C.loaded ? busyHtml('Adding it up\u2026') : d ? '<div class="w204spend"><div><b>' + E(money(d.month.cost)) + '</b><span>this month so far, ' + E(plural(d.month.calls, 'call', 'calls')) + '</span></div>' +
      '<div><b>' + E(money(d.last_month.cost)) + '</b><span>last month, ' + E(plural(d.last_month.calls, 'call', 'calls')) + '</span></div><div><b>' + E(money(d.d90.cost)) + '</b><span>the last 90 days</span></div></div>'
      : '<p class="m">The studio\u2019s totals are not open to this account yet; each email still shows its own.</p>';
    return '<div class="w202modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="What it costs"><div class="w202mbox wide w204costs">' +
      '<div class="w202mh"><h3>' + I('coin') + ' What it costs</h3><button class="w202x" data-w204costsx aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<p class="w204first">' + I('alert') + ' <span><b>Your content first.</b> The Hub shapes what you bring: your own words, the facts and your photos. It does not write the email for you, and thin input costs the same and makes a weaker email: bad input, bad output.</span></p>' +
      '<p class="m">The Hub\u2019s writing help is paid for by use (Claude Sonnet 5 through OpenRouter, in US dollars), not by subscription: each call costs a little, and anything done by hand is free.</p>' +
      '<h4 class="w202h4">Each step</h4><table class="w204ct">' + rows.map(function (r) { return '<tr><td>' + E(r[0]) + '</td><td>' + E(r[1]) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' +
      '<h4 class="w202h4">What the studio has spent</h4>' + spend +
      '<p class="m">On the emails here now: ' + E(money(team)) + ' across ' + E(plural(teamn, 'email', 'emails')) + (minen ? ', yours ' + E(money(mine)) + ' across ' + E(plural(minen, 'email', 'emails')) : ', none of them yours') + '. Deleted emails and drafts never saved show only in the totals above.</p>' +
      '<h4 class="w202h4">Sending</h4><p class="m">Sending is separate from the writing help: Resend\u2019s free plan covers 3,000 emails a month and 100 a day, and the Hub\u2019s daily cap is set to match.</p></div></div>';
  }
  function openCosts() { SS.costs.open = true; loadCosts(true).then(function () { paint(); }); paint(); }
  /* ---------- wave204 (04/10/26): your content first. Steel: "ensure that it is important to come with their content first. It
     is unacceptable to only use AI. Bad input = bad output." The Hub shapes what the writer brings; it never writes the email
     for them. Tell the Hub asks for at least 40 words of the writer's own (three or four sentences, links not counted) before it
     calls the AI, with a meter under the box (the words, a date or day, a link); Fill it in with the Hub needs every detail the
     template asks for (a template with nothing to fill needs 20 words of your own); the prompt tells the model to build from
     the writer's content and never pad thin input. Building by hand stays open to everyone, any time. ---------- */
  var CONTENT_MIN = 40, EXTRA_MIN = 20;
  function ownWords(s) {
    return (str(s).replace(/\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]*\)/g, '$1').replace(/https?:\/\/\S+|www\.\S+/gi, ' ').match(/[A-Za-z0-9\u00c0-\u024f][A-Za-z0-9\u00c0-\u024f'\u2019-]*/g) || []).length;
  }
  function contentFacts(s) {
    s = str(s);
    return { words: ownWords(s),
      date: datesIn(s).length > 0 || /\b(today|tomorrow|tonight|(sun|mon|tues|wednes|thurs|fri|satur)days?|\d{1,2}(st|nd|rd|th)?\s+(of\s+)?(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)[a-z]*|(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|oct|nov|dec)[a-z]*\.?\s+\d{1,2}|dimanche|lundi|mardi|mercredi|jeudi|vendredi|samedi|demain|aujourd)/i.test(s),
      link: /https?:\/\/\S+|www\.\S+|\b[a-z0-9-]+\.(com|org|ca|net|io)\b/i.test(s) };
  }
  function meterHtml() {
    var f = contentFacts(SS.start.brief), ok = f.words >= CONTENT_MIN, pct = Math.min(100, Math.round(f.words / CONTENT_MIN * 100));
    return '<div class="w204meter' + (ok ? ' ok' : '') + '" id="w204meter" aria-live="polite"><span class="w204bar" aria-hidden="true"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></span>' +
      '<span><b>' + (ok ? 'Your content is in' : 'Your content first') + ':</b> ' + E(plural(f.words, 'word', 'words')) + (ok ? '' : ' of at least ' + CONTENT_MIN) +
      ' \u00b7 ' + (f.date ? '\u2713 a date or day' : '\u25cb no date yet') + ' \u00b7 ' + (f.link ? '\u2713 a link' : '\u25cb no link yet') + '</span></div>';
  }
  function updateMeter() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w204meter'); if (el) el.outerHTML = meterHtml();
    var btn = SS.root && SS.root.querySelector('[data-w202startai]'); if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('notyet', ownWords(SS.start.brief) < CONTENT_MIN);
  }
  function contentShort(n) {
    return 'Bring your content first: write or paste at least ' + CONTENT_MIN + ' words of your own, about three or four sentences (you have ' + n + '). What is happening, why it matters, the facts and what people should do. The Hub shapes what you bring; it does not write the email for you. Bad input, bad output.';
  }
  /* Fill it in with the Hub: every detail the template asks for, or (nothing to fill) a few sentences of your own */
  function tplShort(t, s) {
    var asks = (t.design && t.design.asks) || [];
    var missing = asks.filter(function (a) { return str(s.asks[a.id]).trim().length < 2; });
    if (asks.length && missing.length) return 'Give the template your details first: ' + missing.length + ' of ' + asks.length + ' still empty (' + missing.map(function (a) { return a.label; }).join(' / ') + '). The Hub fills in your content; it does not make it up. Or choose Fill it in myself.';
    if (!asks.length && ownWords(s.extra) < EXTRA_MIN) return 'This template is ready as it is. For the Hub to work your own words in, write a few sentences under Anything else to say (' + EXTRA_MIN + ' words or more), or choose Fill it in myself.';
    return '';
  }
