  /* ---------- drawing, the routes (#crm/studio, /ai, /tpl[/key], /e/<key>, .../to/<contact>), the clicks and keys ---------- */
  function keyOf(el) {
    var attrs = ['data-w202f', 'data-w202e', 'data-w202s', 'data-w202say', 'data-w202qa', 'data-w202ask', 'data-w202who', 'data-w202libq', 'data-w202t', 'data-w202scap', 'data-w202vdraft', 'data-w204mvstart'];
    for (var i = 0; i < attrs.length; i++) if (el.hasAttribute && el.hasAttribute(attrs[i])) return '[' + attrs[i] + '="' + String(el.getAttribute(attrs[i])).replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]';
    return null;
  }
  function fromRow(t) {
    return { key: t.key, name: t.name, kind: t.kind === 'test' ? 'newsletter' : t.kind, audience: t.audience || null, lang: t.lang || 'en', subject: t.subject || '', preheader: t.preheader || '',
      design: MAIL202.clean(t.design || { blocks: [] }), one_off: !!t.one_off, category: t.category || null, description: t.description || '', active: t.active !== false, created_by: t.created_by || '', updated_at: t.updated_at };
  }
  function openKey(key) {
    if (key === 'new') { if (!SS.d || SS.d.key) { SS.view = 'home'; setHash('#crm/studio'); } return; }
    if (SS.d && SS.d.key === key) return;
    var t = tplByKey(key);
    if (!t) { SS.d = null; SS.pendingKey = key; return; }
    SS.pendingKey = null;
    SS.d = fromRow(t); SS.ro = !canEdit(t); SS.questions = []; SS.notes = ''; SS.open = {}; SS.send = null; SS.undo = null; SS.dirty = false; SS.savedAt = null; SS.saveNote = ''; SS.change = '';
    histReset();   /* wave204: a new history for this email */
    if (SS.to && !SS.ro) setTimeout(function () { openSend('person'); }, 0);
  }
  function open(parts) {
    parts = parts || [];
    var sub = parts[2] || '', ti = parts.indexOf('to');
    if (SS.d && SS.dirty && !SS.ro) saveNow();
    try { var c0 = crm(); if (c0 && c0.close && c0.state().sheet) c0.close(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    SS.msg = SS.view === 'home' && sub === '' ? SS.msg : ''; SS.err = ''; SS.pick = null; SS.peek = null; SS.confirmDel = false;
    SS.to = ti >= 0 && parts[ti + 1] ? parts[ti + 1] : (sub === 'e' ? SS.to : null);
    if (sub === 'ai') SS.view = 'ai';
    else if (sub === 'tpl') { SS.view = 'tpl'; if (parts[3] && parts[3] !== 'to') SS.start.tpl = parts[3]; }
    else if (sub === 'e') { SS.view = 'edit'; openKey(parts[3] || 'new'); }
    else SS.view = 'home';
    SS.voices.open = false; SS.voices.editing = false;
    closeMoving(); SS.costs.open = false; if (SS.startNote && SS.startNote.view !== SS.view) SS.startNote = null;   /* wave204: the note shows on the step you went back to, once */
    if (!SS.voices.loaded) loadVoices().then(function () { if (SS.root && SS.root.isConnected) paint(); });
    css202();
  }
  function paint() {
    var root = SS.root;
    if (!root || !root.isConnected) {
      var c = crm(); try { if (c && c.state().ui.tab === 'studio') c.paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return;
    }
    css202();
    if (!cstate().templates) {
      root.innerHTML = busyHtml('Loading the templates\u2026');
      var c2 = crm(); if (c2 && c2.load) Promise.resolve(c2.load('templates')).then(function () { paint(); });
      return;
    }
    if (SS.view === 'edit' && !SS.d && SS.pendingKey) {
      var t = tplByKey(SS.pendingKey);
      if (t) openKey(SS.pendingKey);
      else { SS.pendingKey = null; SS.view = 'home'; SS.err = 'That email is not there any more.'; setHash('#crm/studio'); }
    }
    if (SS.view === 'edit' && !SS.d) { SS.view = 'home'; setHash('#crm/studio'); }
    var act = document.activeElement, k = null, caret = null;
    if (act && root.contains(act)) { k = keyOf(act); try { caret = [act.selectionStart, act.selectionEnd]; } catch (e) { caret = null; } }
    root.innerHTML = SS.view === 'ai' ? aiStartHtml() : SS.view === 'tpl' ? tplStartHtml() : SS.view === 'edit' ? editorHtml() : homeHtml();
    if (k) { var el = root.querySelector(k); if (el) { try { el.focus({ preventScroll: true }); if (caret && caret[0] != null && el.setSelectionRange) el.setSelectionRange(caret[0], caret[1]); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('iframe.w202frame'), function (f) { f.addEventListener('load', function () { wireFrame(f); }); });
    movingAfterPaint();
    try { if (window.w201 && window.w201.scan) window.w201.scan(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function within(t) { return t && t.closest && t.closest('.w202root'); }
  function blockAt(s) { var p = String(s).split(','); return { i: Number(p[0]), k: p.length > 1 ? Number(p[1]) : null }; }
  function copyCurrent() {
    var e = SS.d; if (!e) return;
    var d = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(e.design)); (d.blocks || []).forEach(function (b) { b.id = MAIL202.uid(); }); delete d.ai;   /* wave204: a copy starts with nothing spent */
    openDraft(draftFrom({ name: e.name + ' (copy)', kind: e.kind, audience: e.audience, lang: e.lang, subject: e.subject, preheader: e.preheader, design: d }), {});
  }
  async function removeEmail() {
    var e = SS.d; if (!e || !e.key) return;
    var r = await rpc('email_studio_remove', { p_key: e.key });
    if (r.error) { SS.err = words(r.error); SS.confirmDel = false; paint(); return; }
    var d = r.data || {}; SS.d = null; SS.confirmDel = false; SS.dirty = false;
    SS.msg = d.deleted ? '\u201c' + e.name + '\u201d is deleted.' : '\u201c' + e.name + '\u201d is switched off: it was sent, or a campaign or an automation uses it, so its words stay in the history.';
    await reloadTemplates(); SS.view = 'home'; go('#crm/studio'); paint();
  }
  var ROW = { rows: { label: '', value: '' }, items: { lead: '', text: '' }, links: { label: '', url: '' } };
  document.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var t = ev.target; if (!within(t)) return;
    var el, v;
    var hit = function (a) { el = t.closest('[' + a + ']'); if (el) { v = el.getAttribute(a); ev.preventDefault(); return true; } return false; };
    if (hit('data-w202go')) { go(v); return; }
    if (hit('data-w202blank')) { startBlank(); return; }
    if (hit('data-w202filter')) { SS.filter = v; paint(); return; }
    if (hit('data-w202use')) { var tu = tplByKey(v); SS.peek = null; if (tu) openDraft(copyOf(tu), { openFirst: false }); return; }
    if (hit('data-w202fill')) { SS.peek = null; SS.start.tpl = v; SS.start.asks = {}; SS.err = ''; go('#crm/studio/tpl/' + v); return; }
    if (hit('data-w202peekx')) { SS.peek = null; paint(); return; }
    if (hit('data-w202peek')) { SS.peek = v; loadMailSettings(); paint(); return; }

  function onKey(ev) {
    if (!SS.root || !SS.root.isConnected) return;
    var mod = ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey, k = String(ev.key || '').toLowerCase();
    /* wave204: Cmd/Ctrl+Z, Shift+Cmd/Ctrl+Z and Ctrl+Y step through the email's history; Cmd/Ctrl+B and K mark text */
    if (mod && !ev.altKey && (k === 'z' || k === 'y') && SS.view === 'edit' && SS.d && !SS.ro && !SS.mv && !SS.pick && !SS.voices.open && histKeyOk(document.activeElement)) {
      var redo = k === 'y' ? (ev.ctrlKey && !ev.metaKey && !ev.shiftKey) : ev.shiftKey;
      if (k === 'y' && !redo) return;
      ev.preventDefault(); histGo(redo ? 1 : -1); return;
    }
    if (mod && !ev.altKey && !ev.shiftKey && (k === 'b' || k === 'k') && ev.target && ev.target.hasAttribute && ev.target.hasAttribute('data-w204fmt') && !SS.ro) { ev.preventDefault(); fmtApply(ev.target, k === 'b' ? 'b' : 'link'); return; }
    if (ev.key === 'Escape') {
      if (SS.mv) { if (!SS.mv.busy) { closeMoving(); paint(); } return; }
      if (SS.costs.open) { SS.costs.open = false; paint(); return; }
      if (SS.voices.open) { SS.voices.open = false; SS.voices.editing = false; SS.voices.err = ''; paint(); return; }
      if (SS.peek) { SS.peek = null; paint(); return; }
      if (SS.pick) { SS.pick = null; paint(); return; }
      if (SS.send && within(document.activeElement)) { SS.send = null; paint(); return; }
    }
    if ((ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey) && (ev.key === 's' || ev.key === 'S') && SS.view === 'edit' && SS.d && !SS.ro) { ev.preventDefault(); saveNow(); }
  }

    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  if (!addTour()) { var tries = 0, iv = setInterval(function () { if (addTour() || ++tries > 40) clearInterval(iv); }, 500); }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v205 (04/10/26): the Strategy app rebuilt; the organizational goals edited live, Google-Doc style.
   Steel (04/10/26): a rebuilt, interactive Strategy app where the organizational goals can be edited live. His decisions:
   - "organizational goals, and departments own projects": the five FY27 to FY31 goals with their yearly milestones;
     department goals are retired as a tier (kept, archived, out of every default view, wins and comments kept; one-time
     Make it a project); each department's project links straight to an org goal, ideally to one milestone.
   - Live editing: click-to-edit with autosave on every field, milestone, target, owner and path (no edit dialog; saves as
     you type, quietly); who is on the page and which goal they are in, with other people's saves arriving without a
     refresh (Supabase Realtime: presence plus postgres_changes); a per-field check so two people never silently lose
     words ("Ian just changed this"); threaded comments on goals and milestones with @mentions (wave138's flow); every
     change in a history with Restore (goal_history, written by a database trigger).
   - "each goal's owners and admins edit; all staff read and comment; board viewers read-only": enforced in the database
     (strategy_* functions check inside; restrictive policies on goals and goal_comments); the page only mirrors it.
   - The cleanest page of the Hub: the mission at the top, the five goals as cards, the roadmap with the checkpoint and
     the FY29 gate, and the pinned Strategy Library (the Hub's first important-document flag, strategy_docs.pinned).
   The content is the approved draft's (build/data/wa205_seed.py), marked "Draft for board approval (19 Oct 2026)" until
   an admin marks the goals approved.

   #orggoals/library (Strategy Library), #orggoals/retired (Retired goals). #goals (Department goals) opens Retired goals.

   Data: goals (org tier), goal_owners, goal_milestones, goal_paths, goal_history, goal_comments, projects.goal_id and
   milestone_id, strategy_plan, strategy_docs (wa205_strategy). Writers: strategy_save, strategy_add, strategy_remove,
   strategy_owner_set, strategy_link_project, strategy_comment_add, strategy_comment_edit, strategy_restore,

   "This year" view at the top of Goals with the current quarter, what is due next and what is overdue; checkpoint comments
   through strategy_checkpoint_comment_add; projects link to a checkpoint through strategy_link_checkpoint). g_year.js.

(function () {
  if (window.__wa205) return; window.__wa205 = true;

  var MS_ST = ['not started', 'in progress', 'done', 'at risk', 'carried forward'];
  var ST_LABEL = { 'not started': 'Not started', 'in progress': 'In progress', 'on track': 'On track', 'at risk': 'At risk', 'delayed': 'Delayed', 'done': 'Done', 'carried forward': 'Carried forward' };
  var FIELD_LABEL = { title: 'the title', detail: 'the SMART statement', why: 'Why', measure: 'the headline measure', baseline: 'the baseline', target_fy29: 'the FY29 target', target_fy31: 'the FY31 target', status: 'the status', due_date: 'the due date', progress: 'progress', approval: 'board approval', retired_at: 'retired', body: 'the text', fy: 'the year', archived_at: 'removed', added: 'added', removed: 'removed', is_lead: 'the lead', focus: 'the focus', goal_id: 'the goal link', milestone_id: 'the milestone link', reason: 'the reason', owners: 'the owners', activities_note: 'the note on the activities', checkpoint_id: 'the checkpoint link' };
  var CP_ST = ['not started', 'on track', 'at risk', 'done'];   /* wave207: a quarterly checkpoint's status */
  var SAVE_MS = 800;

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function str(v) { return v == null ? '' : String(v); }
  function lsGet(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function lsSet(k, v) { try { if (v == null) localStorage.removeItem(k); else localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function byId(list, id) { for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) { if (String(list[i].id) === String(id)) return list[i]; } return null; }
  function clip(s, n) { s = str(s); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1) + '\u2026' : s; }
  function plural(n, one, many) { return n + ' ' + (n === 1 ? one : (many || one + 's')); }
  function fmtDay(d) { if (!d) return ''; try { var x = new Date(String(d).length <= 10 ? String(d) + 'T12:00:00' : d); return x.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(d); } }
  function fmtWhen(ts) {
    if (!ts) return '';
    try {
      var d = new Date(ts), now = new Date(), diff = (now - d) / 1000;
      if (diff < 45) return 'just now';
      if (diff < 3600) return Math.round(diff / 60) + ' min ago';
      var t = d.toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
      if (d.toDateString() === now.toDateString()) return 'today ' + t;
      return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + ' ' + t;
    } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }

  /* ---------- who ---------- */
  function members() { try { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function memberById(id) { return byId(members(), id); }
  function memberByEmail(em) { em = lower(em); if (!em) return null; return members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === em; })[0] || null; }
  function nameOf(email) { var m = memberByEmail(email); return m ? m.name : (email ? String(email).split('@')[0] : 'Someone'); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function me() { var em = myEmail(), m = memberByEmail(em); var name = ''; try { name = (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || ''; } catch (e) { name = ''; } return { email: em, name: (m && m.name) || name || em.split('@')[0], id: m ? m.id : null, avatar_url: m ? m.avatar_url : null }; }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function boardMode() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function isAdmin() { return staffOk() && role() === 'admin'; }
  function isLeader() { var r = role(); return staffOk() && (r === 'admin' || r === 'leader'); }
  /* the database decides (wa205_can_comment, wa205_can_edit); the page mirrors it so nobody types into a box that will refuse */
  function canComment() { return staffOk() && !boardMode() && role() !== 'viewer' && role() !== 'blocked'; }
  function isOwner(g) { var em = myEmail(); if (!g || !em) return false; return S.owners.some(function (o) { if (String(o.goal_id) !== String(g.id) || o.removed_at) return false; var m = memberById(o.member_id); return m && lower(m.email) === em; }); }
  function canEdit(g) { return !!g && !g.retired_at && canComment() && (isAdmin() || isOwner(g)); }
  function readOnlyWhy() {
    if (boardMode() || role() === 'viewer') return 'You are reading the goals as a board viewer.';
    if (!staffOk()) return 'Sign in with a Welfare Avenue account to work on the goals.';
    return 'Each goal\u2019s owners and the admins edit it; you can comment and link projects.';
  }

  /* ---------- loading ---------- */
  function q(p) { return Promise.resolve(p).then(function (r) { return r || { data: null }; }, function (e) { return { data: null, error: e }; }); }
  function isActiveOrg(g) { return g && g.department === 'org' && !g.retired_at && g.number != null; }
  function loadAll(force) {
    if (S.loading && !force) return S.loading;
    if (S.loaded && !force) return Promise.resolve();
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db) return Promise.resolve();
    S.loading = Promise.all([
      q(db.from('goals').select('*').order('number', { ascending: true })),
      q(db.from('goal_owners').select('*').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('goal_milestones').select('*').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('goal_paths').select('*').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('projects').select('id,name,department,status,goal_id,milestone_id,checkpoint_id,leads,parent_id,workstream,banked_at').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('strategy_plan').select('*')),
      q(db.from('strategy_docs').select('*').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('goal_comments').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true })),
      q(db.from('goal_wins').select('id,goal_id')),
      q(db.from('charitable_activities').select('*').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('goal_activities').select('*').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('goal_year_targets').select('*').order('sort')),
      q(db.from('goal_checkpoints').select('*').order('quarter'))
    ]).then(function (r) {
      var all = (r[0].data || []);
      S.err = r[0].error ? (r[0].error.message || String(r[0].error)) : null;
      S.goals = all.filter(isActiveOrg).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.number || 99) - (b.number || 99); });
      S.retired = all.filter(function (g) { return !!g.retired_at; });
      S.allGoals = all;
      S.owners = (r[1].data || []).filter(function (o) { return !o.removed_at; });
      S.milestones = (r[2].data || []).filter(function (m) { return !m.archived_at; });
      S.paths = (r[3].data || []).filter(function (p) { return !p.archived_at; });
      S.projects = (r[4].data || []).filter(function (p) { return !p.banked_at; });
      S.plan = {}; (r[5].data || []).forEach(function (x) { S.plan[x.key] = x.value; });
      S.docs = (r[6].data || []).filter(function (d) { return !d.archived_at; });
      S.comments = r[7].data || [];
      S.wins = {}; (r[8].data || []).forEach(function (w) { S.wins[w.goal_id] = (S.wins[w.goal_id] || 0) + 1; });
      S.acts = r[9].data || []; S.gacts = (r[10].data || []).filter(function (a) { return !a.archived_at; });
      S.targets = (r[11].data || []).filter(function (t) { return !t.archived_at; }); S.checkpoints = (r[12].data || []).filter(function (c) { return !c.archived_at; });
      S.goals.forEach(baseGoal); S.milestones.forEach(function (m) { baseRow('milestone', m, ['body', 'status', 'fy']); }); S.paths.forEach(function (p) { baseRow('path', p, ['body']); });
      ['activity', 'target', 'checkpoint'].forEach(function (e) { ENTS[e].list().forEach(function (row) { baseRow(e, row, ENTS[e].fields); }); });
      S.loaded = Date.now(); S.loading = null;
    });
    return S.loading;
  }
  var GOAL_FIELDS = ['title', 'detail', 'why', 'measure', 'baseline', 'target_fy29', 'target_fy31', 'status', 'due_date', 'activities_note'];
  /* every kind of row the page edits: its list in S and the fields autosave sends (wave207 adds activity, target, checkpoint) */
  var ENTS = {
    goal: { list: function () { return S.goals; }, fields: GOAL_FIELDS },
    milestone: { list: function () { return S.milestones; }, fields: ['body', 'status', 'fy'] },
    path: { list: function () { return S.paths; }, fields: ['body'] },
    activity: { list: function () { return S.gacts; }, fields: ['reason'] },
    target: { list: function () { return S.targets; }, fields: ['body', 'owners'] },
    checkpoint: { list: function () { return S.checkpoints; }, fields: ['body', 'status', 'due_date'] }
  };
  function key(e, id, f) { return e + ':' + id + ':' + f; }
  function baseGoal(g) { baseRow('goal', g, GOAL_FIELDS); }
  function baseRow(e, row, fields) { fields.forEach(function (f) { var k = key(e, row.id, f); if (!S.dirty[k]) S.base[k] = str(row[f]); }); }
  function goalOf(e, id) {
    if (e === 'goal') return byId(S.goals, id);
    var row = rowOf(e, id);
    return row ? byId(S.goals, row.goal_id) : null;
  }
  function rowOf(e, id) { return ENTS[e] ? byId(ENTS[e].list(), id) : null; }
  function msOf(gid) { return S.milestones.filter(function (m) { return String(m.goal_id) === String(gid); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.fy < b.fy ? -1 : a.fy > b.fy ? 1 : 0) || (a.sort - b.sort) || (a.id - b.id); }); }
  function pathsOf(gid) { return S.paths.filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(gid); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort - b.sort) || (a.id - b.id); }); }
  function ownersOf(gid) { return S.owners.filter(function (o) { return String(o.goal_id) === String(gid); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.is_lead ? 1 : 0) - (a.is_lead ? 1 : 0) || (a.sort - b.sort); }); }
  function projOf(gid) { return S.projects.filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(gid); }); }
  function commentsOf(gid, mid) { return S.comments.filter(function (c) { return String(c.goal_id) === String(gid) && (mid === undefined || String(c.milestone_id || '') === String(mid || '')); }); }
  function progress(gid) {
    var ms = msOf(gid).filter(function (m) { return m.fy !== 'FY30' && m.fy !== 'FY31'; });
    var done = ms.filter(function (m) { return m.status === 'done'; }).length;
    var y = currentFy(), yr = ms.filter(function (m) { return m.fy === y; }), yd = yr.filter(function (m) { return m.status === 'done'; }).length;
    return { total: ms.length, done: done, pct: ms.length ? Math.round(done / ms.length * 100) : 0, fy: y, yTotal: yr.length, yDone: yd };
  }
  function currentFy() { var d = new Date(), y = d.getFullYear() + (d.getMonth() >= 9 ? 1 : 0); return 'FY' + String(y).slice(2); }   /* the fiscal year starts October 1 */
  function rpc(fn, args) {
    return Promise.resolve(db.rpc(fn, args)).then(function (r) {
      if (r && r.error) throw new Error(r.error.message || String(r.error));
      return r ? r.data : null;
    });
  }
  function errText(e) { var m = String((e && e.message) || e || 'Something went wrong'); return m.replace(/^Error:\s*/, ''); }

  /* ---------- autosave: one field at a time, debounced, with the per-field check ---------- */
  function fieldEl(k) { return document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205k="' + k + '"]') || document.querySelector('#w205drawer [data-w205k="' + k + '"]'); }
  function parseKey(k) { var p = k.split(':'); return { e: p[0], id: p[1], f: p.slice(2).join(':') }; }
  function schedule(k, ms) {
    clearTimeout(S.timers[k]);
    S.timers[k] = setTimeout(function () { save(k); }, ms == null ? SAVE_MS : ms);
    savedState(k);
  }
  function flushAll() { Object.keys(S.timers).forEach(function (k) { if (S.dirty[k]) { clearTimeout(S.timers[k]); save(k); } }); }
  function save(k) {
    var el = fieldEl(k), P = parseKey(k);
    if (!el) { delete S.dirty[k]; return Promise.resolve(); }
    if (S.conflict[k]) return Promise.resolve();   /* waits for the person to choose */
    if (S.inflight[k]) { S.inflight[k].again = true; return S.inflight[k].p; }
    var val = el.value, old = S.base[k] == null ? '' : S.base[k];
    if (val === old) { delete S.dirty[k]; savedState(k); return Promise.resolve(); }
    var rec = { again: false };
    rec.p = rpc('strategy_save', { p_entity: P.e, p_id: Number(P.id), p_field: P.f, p_value: val, p_old: old }).then(function (res) {
      delete S.inflight[k];
      if (res && res.conflict) {
        S.conflict[k] = { current: str(res.current), by: res.by || nameOf(res.by_email) || 'Someone', at: res.at, mine: val };
        S.base[k] = str(res.current);
        paintConflict(k); savedState(k);
        return;
      }
      if (!res || res.ok === false) throw new Error((res && res.error) || 'Not saved');
      S.base[k] = val; delete S.errors[k];
      var row = rowOf(P.e, P.id); if (row) { row[P.f] = P.f === 'due_date' ? (val || null) : val; row.updated_by = myEmail(); }
      S.savedAt[goalKey(P)] = Date.now();
      var cur = fieldEl(k);
      if (cur && cur.value === val) delete S.dirty[k];
      savedState(k);
      afterSave(P);
      if (rec.again || (cur && cur.value !== val)) schedule(k, 250);
    }).catch(function (e) {
      delete S.inflight[k];
      S.errors[k] = errText(e);
      savedState(k);
      paintFieldError(k);
    });
    S.inflight[k] = rec;
    savedState(k);
    return rec.p;
  }
  function goalKey(P) { var g = goalOf(P.e, P.id); return g ? String(g.id) : ''; }
  function savedState(k) {
    var P = parseKey(k), gid = goalKey(P); if (!gid) return;
    var box = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205saved="' + gid + '"]'); if (!box) return;
    var keys = Object.keys(S.dirty).filter(function (x) { return goalKey(parseKey(x)) === gid; });
    var busy = Object.keys(S.inflight).some(function (x) { return goalKey(parseKey(x)) === gid; });
    var errs = Object.keys(S.errors).filter(function (x) { return goalKey(parseKey(x)) === gid; });
    var conf = Object.keys(S.conflict).some(function (x) { return goalKey(parseKey(x)) === gid; });
    box.className = 'w205saved' + (errs.length ? ' err' : busy || keys.length ? ' busy' : '');
    box.textContent = errs.length ? 'Not saved' : conf ? 'Check the note' : busy ? 'Saving\u2026' : keys.length ? 'Editing\u2026' : (S.savedAt[gid] ? 'Saved' : '');
    if (!errs.length && !busy && !keys.length && S.savedAt[gid]) box.title = 'Saved ' + fmtWhen(S.savedAt[gid]); else box.removeAttribute('title');
  }
  function paintFieldError(k) {
    var el = fieldEl(k); if (!el) return;
    var host = el.parentNode, old = host.querySelector('.w205err[data-for="' + k + '"]'); if (old) old.remove();
    if (S.errors[k]) { var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'w205err'; d.setAttribute('data-for', k); d.setAttribute('role', 'alert'); d.textContent = 'Not saved: ' + S.errors[k]; el.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', d); }
  }
  /* "Ian just changed this": their words and yours, side by side; nothing is lost either way */
  function paintConflict(k) {
    var el = fieldEl(k); if (!el) return;
    var host = el.parentNode, old = host.querySelector('.w205conf[data-for="' + k + '"]'); if (old) old.remove();
    var c = S.conflict[k]; if (!c) return;
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'w205conf'; d.setAttribute('data-for', k); d.setAttribute('role', 'status');
    d.innerHTML = '<b>' + E(c.by) + ' just changed this.</b> ' + (c.current ? 'Their version:' : 'They cleared it.') +
      (c.current ? '<span class="theirs" data-w198skip>' + E(c.current) + '</span>' : '') +
      '<div class="acts"><button type="button" class="w205btn sm pri" data-w205keep="' + E(k) + '">Keep mine</button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205theirs="' + E(k) + '">Use theirs</button>' +
      '<small style="color:' + MUTED + ';align-self:center">Your words stay in the box until you choose.</small></div>';
    el.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', d);
  }
  function resolveConflict(k, keepMine) {
    var c = S.conflict[k]; if (!c) return;
    delete S.conflict[k];
    var el = fieldEl(k);
    var note = document.querySelector('#w205 .w205conf[data-for="' + k + '"]'); if (note) note.remove();
    if (keepMine) { S.dirty[k] = true; save(k); }
    else { if (el) { el.value = c.current; grow(el); } S.base[k] = c.current; delete S.dirty[k]; var P = parseKey(k), row = rowOf(P.e, P.id); if (row) row[P.f] = c.current; savedState(k); afterSave(P); }
  }
  /* the at-a-glance table and the progress bar follow the fields */
  function afterSave(P) {
    try {
      var g = goalOf(P.e, P.id); if (!g) return;
      if (P.e === 'goal' && ['title', 'measure', 'baseline', 'target_fy29', 'target_fy31'].indexOf(P.f) >= 0) paintGlance();
      if (P.e === 'milestone' && P.f === 'status') { paintProgress(g.id); var ms = byId(S.milestones, P.id), box = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205ms="' + P.id + '"]'); if (box && ms) box.classList.toggle('done', ms.status === 'done'); paintGlance(); }
      if (P.e === 'goal' && P.f === 'status') { var sel = fieldEl(key('goal', g.id, 'status')); if (sel) sel.className = 'w205stsel s-' + stKey(g.status); }
      if (P.e === 'milestone' && P.f === 'fy') repaintGoal(g.id, true);
      if (P.e === 'checkpoint' || P.e === 'target') { afterYearSave(P); paintYear(); }
      if (P.e === 'activity') { paintActs(g.id); paintCoverage(); paintGlance(); }
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }
  function stKey(s) { return String(s || 'not started').replace(/ (\w)/g, function (m, c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }).replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g, ''); }
  function grow(el) { if (!el || el.tagName !== 'TEXTAREA') return; el.style.height = 'auto'; el.style.height = (el.scrollHeight + 2) + 'px'; }

  /* ---------- live: other people's saves and who is here ---------- */
  function onRemoteRow(e, row, kind) {
    var fields = ENTS[e].fields;
    var by = nameOf(row.updated_by);
    var mine = lower(row.updated_by) === myEmail();
    var list = ENTS[e].list();
    var cur = byId(list, row.id);
    if (e === 'goal') {
      if (!isActiveOrg(row)) { if (cur || row.retired_at) { S.loaded = 0; loadAll(true).then(paintSoon); } return; }
      if (!cur) { S.loaded = 0; loadAll(true).then(paintSoon); return; }
      ['approval', 'approved_at', 'owner', 'number'].forEach(function (f) { cur[f] = row[f]; });
    }
    if (e !== 'goal' && (row.archived_at || !cur)) {
      if (row.archived_at) { var i = list.indexOf(cur); if (i >= 0) list.splice(i, 1); }
      else list.push(row);
      if (row.archived_at || !cur) { baseRow(e, row, fields); repaintGoal(row.goal_id, true); if (e === 'activity') { paintActs(row.goal_id); paintCoverage(); paintGlance(); } if (e === 'target' || e === 'checkpoint') paintYear(); if (S.drawer && S.drawer.kind === 'acts') paintDrawer(); return; }
    }
    fields.forEach(function (f) {
      var k = key(e, row.id, f), v = str(row[f]);
      if (v === str(S.base[k])) { if (cur) cur[f] = row[f]; return; }   /* our own save coming back */
      var el = fieldEl(k);
      var busy = el && (document.activeElement === el || S.dirty[k] || S.inflight[k]) && el.value !== v;
      if (busy && !mine) {
        /* they are typing here: keep their words, show the other person's */
        S.conflict[k] = { current: v, by: by, at: row.updated_at, mine: el.value };
        S.base[k] = v; paintConflict(k); savedState(k);
      } else {
        S.base[k] = v; if (cur) cur[f] = row[f];
        if (el && el.value !== v) { el.value = v; grow(el); if (!mine) { el.classList.remove('flash'); void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add('flash'); el.title = 'Changed by ' + by + ' ' + fmtWhen(row.updated_at); } }
        afterSave({ e: e, id: row.id, f: f });
      }
      if (cur) cur[f] = row[f];
    });
  }
  function onRemote(table, payload) {
    var row = payload.new || {}, ev = payload.eventType;
    if (!row || !row.id) return;
    if (table === 'goals') onRemoteRow('goal', row, ev);
    else if (table === 'goal_milestones') onRemoteRow('milestone', row, ev);
    else if (table === 'goal_paths') onRemoteRow('path', row, ev);
    else if (table === 'goal_activities') onRemoteRow('activity', row, ev);
    else if (table === 'goal_year_targets') onRemoteRow('target', row, ev);
    else if (table === 'goal_checkpoints') onRemoteRow('checkpoint', row, ev);
    else if (table === 'goal_owners') {
      var o = byId(S.owners, row.id);
      if (row.removed_at) { if (o) S.owners.splice(S.owners.indexOf(o), 1); }
      else if (o) Object.assign(o, row); else S.owners.push(row);
      paintOwners(row.goal_id);
    } else if (table === 'goal_comments') {
      var c = byId(S.comments, row.id); if (c) Object.assign(c, row); else S.comments.push(row);
      paintCounts(row.goal_id); paintYear();
      if (S.drawer && S.drawer.kind === 'comments' && String(S.drawer.goal) === String(row.goal_id)) paintDrawer();
    } else if (table === 'projects') {
      var p = byId(S.projects, row.id);
      if (row.banked_at) { if (p) S.projects.splice(S.projects.indexOf(p), 1); }
      else if (p) Object.assign(p, row); else S.projects.push(row);
      S.goals.forEach(function (g) { paintProjects(g.id); });
      paintUnlinked(); paintYear();
      if (S.drawer && S.drawer.kind === 'link') paintDrawer();
    }
  }
  var paintT = null;
  function paintSoon() { clearTimeout(paintT); paintT = setTimeout(function () { if (onPage()) paint(); }, 60); }

  /* presence travels on a Realtime channel anyone holding the page's public key could listen to: it carries the roster id and
     the first name only (as the public website does), never an email; who you are comes from the roster on each side */
  var TAB = 't' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
  function myKey() { var m = me(); return m.id ? 'm' + m.id : TAB; }
  function presenceMeta() {
    var m = me();
    return { mid: m.id || null, name: String(m.name || '').split(' ')[0], goal: S.focus.goal, field: S.focus.field, view: S.view, at: Date.now() };
  }
  function track() { try { if (S.channel && S.live) S.channel.track(presenceMeta()); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  var trackT = null;
  function trackSoon() { clearTimeout(trackT); trackT = setTimeout(track, 250); }
  function readPresence() {
    var st = {}; try { st = S.channel && S.channel.presenceState ? S.channel.presenceState() : {}; } catch (e) { st = {}; }
    var seen = {}, out = [], mine = me().id;
    Object.keys(st || {}).forEach(function (k) {
      (st[k] || []).forEach(function (m) {
        var id = m.mid ? 'm' + m.mid : k; if (!id || k === TAB || (mine && String(m.mid) === String(mine))) return;
        if (!seen[id] || (m.at || 0) > (seen[id].at || 0)) seen[id] = m;
      });
    });
    Object.keys(seen).forEach(function (id) { out.push(seen[id]); });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    S.presence = out;
    paintPresence();
  }
  function goLive() {
    if (S.channel || typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || typeof db.channel !== 'function') return;
    try {
      var ch = db.channel('wa205-strategy', { config: { presence: { key: myKey() } } });
      ['goals', 'goal_milestones', 'goal_paths', 'goal_owners', 'goal_comments', 'projects', 'goal_activities', 'goal_year_targets', 'goal_checkpoints'].forEach(function (t) {
        var opt = { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: t };
        if (t === 'goals') opt.filter = 'department=eq.org';
        ch.on('postgres_changes', opt, function (payload) { try { onRemote(t, payload); } catch (e) { console.warn('wave205 live', e); } });
      });
      ch.on('presence', { event: 'sync' }, readPresence);
      ch.on('presence', { event: 'join' }, readPresence);
      ch.on('presence', { event: 'leave' }, readPresence);
      S.channel = ch;
      ch.subscribe(function (status) {
        S.live = status === 'SUBSCRIBED';
        if (S.live) track();
        paintPresence();
      });
    } catch (e) { console.warn('wave205 realtime', e); S.channel = null; }
  }
  function goQuiet() {
    var ch = S.channel; if (!ch) return;
    S.channel = null; S.live = false; S.presence = [];
    try { if (ch.untrack) ch.untrack(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (db.removeChannel) db.removeChannel(ch); else if (ch.unsubscribe) ch.unsubscribe(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  function deptLabel(d) { try { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || d || 'Other'; } catch (e) { return d || 'Other'; } }
  function deptColor(d) { try { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || MUTED; } catch (e) { return MUTED; } }
  function av(p, size) { try { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size) : ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function onPage() { var a = document.querySelector('.panel.active'); return !!(a && a.id === 'orggoals'); }
  function host() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('orggoals'); if (!sec) return null;
    var h = document.getElementById('w205');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w205'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function approvalLabel() { var a = S.plan.approval || {}; return a.label || 'Draft for board approval (19 Oct 2026)'; }
  function approved() { return S.goals.length && S.goals.every(function (g) { return g.approval === 'approved'; }); }

  function mastHtml(title, line) {
    return '<header class="w205mast"><div><div class="crumb">' + (boardMode() ? 'Board' : 'Strategy') + '</div><h1>' + E(title) + '</h1>' + (line ? '<p>' + E(line) + '</p>' : '') + '</div><span class="sp"></span>' +
      '<div class="w205pres" data-w205pres></div></header>';
  }
  /* a field you click and type in; it saves itself (the database checks who may) */
  function ed(e, row, f, o) {
    o = o || {};
    var g = e === 'goal' ? row : byId(S.goals, row.goal_id);
    var k = key(e, row.id, f), can = canEdit(g), v = str(row[f]);
    var ph = can ? (o.ph || 'Click to write') : (o.empty || 'Not written yet');
    return '<textarea class="w205ed' + (o.cls ? ' ' + o.cls : '') + '" rows="1" data-w205k="' + E(k) + '" aria-label="' + E(o.label || f) + '" placeholder="' + E(ph) + '"' + (can ? '' : ' readonly tabindex="-1"') + ' data-w198skip spellcheck="true">' + E(v) + '</textarea>';
  }
  function stSel(e, row, f, list) {
    var g = e === 'goal' ? row : byId(S.goals, row.goal_id), can = canEdit(g), v = row[f] || list[0];
    var cls = e === 'goal' ? 'w205stsel' : 'w205dot';
    return '<select class="' + cls + ' s-' + stKey(v) + '" data-w205k="' + E(key(e, row.id, f)) + '" aria-label="' + (e === 'goal' ? 'Goal status' : 'Milestone status') + '" title="' + E(ST_LABEL[v] || v) + '"' + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' +
      list.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + E(s) + '"' + (s === v ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(ST_LABEL[s] || s) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';
  }
  function heroHtml() {
    var p = S.plan;
    return '<section class="w205hero" aria-label="Our mission"><div class="lab">Our mission</div><blockquote data-w198skip>' + E(p.mission || '') + '</blockquote><div class="bar"></div>' +
      '<p>' + E(p.mission_note || '') + (p.window ? ' ' + E(p.window) : '') + '</p></section>';
  }
  function statusHtml() {
    var ok = approved(), a = S.goals[0] || {};
    return '<div class="w205status">' +
      (ok ? '<span class="w205badge ok">' + I('check') + ' Approved by the board' + (a.approved_at ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(fmtDay(a.approved_at)) : '') + '</span>'
          : '<span class="w205badge draft">' + I('flag') + ' ' + E(approvalLabel()) + '</span><span class="note">The board votes on these goals on Oct 19, 2026.</span>') +
      (isAdmin() && S.goals.length ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205approve="' + (ok ? 'draft' : 'approved') + '">' + (ok ? 'Back to draft' : 'Mark approved by the board') + '</button>' : '') +
      '</div>' +
      (canComment() && S.goals.some(function (g) { return canEdit(g); }) ? '' : '<div class="w205ro" role="note">' + E(readOnlyWhy()) + '</div>');
  }
  function whyHtml() {
    var p = S.plan; if (!p.why_reset) return '';
    return '<details class="w205why" style="margin:0 0 6px"><summary class="w205link" style="list-style:none;display:inline-block">Why these goals</summary>' +
      '<div style="background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;margin-top:8px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.55">' +
      '<p style="margin:0 0 8px">' + E(p.why_reset) + '</p>' + (p.long_range ? '<p style="margin:0 0 8px">' + E(p.long_range) + '</p>' : '') +
      (p.rule ? '<p style="margin:0"><b>Rule for every goal:</b> ' + E(p.rule) + '</p>' : '') + '</div></details>';
  }
  function ownerNames(gid) { return ownersOf(gid).map(function (o) { var m = memberById(o.member_id); return (m ? m.name : '?') + (o.is_lead ? ' (lead)' : ''); }).join(', '); }
  function glanceHtml() {
    if (!S.goals.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w205sec"><h2>The goals at a glance</h2><span class="hint">FY29 is the 3-year target; FY31 applies only if the FY29 gate is met.</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w205glance" data-w205glance><table><thead><tr><th>Goal</th><th>Headline measure</th><th>Baseline</th><th>FY29 (3-year)</th><th>FY31 (5-year, gated)</th><th>Owners</th><th>Charitable activities</th><th>Milestones</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
      S.goals.map(function (g) {
        var pr = progress(g.id);
        return '<tr data-w205jump="' + g.id + '" tabindex="0" role="link" aria-label="Open goal ' + E(g.number) + '">' +
          '<td><span style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center"><span class="w205num">' + E(g.number) + '</span><b data-w198skip>' + E(g.title) + '</b></span></td>' +
          '<td data-l="Measure" data-w198skip>' + E(g.measure) + '</td><td data-l="Baseline" data-w198skip>' + E(g.baseline) + '</td>' +
          '<td data-l="FY29" class="t29" data-w198skip>' + E(g.target_fy29) + '</td><td data-l="FY31" class="g31" data-w198skip>' + E(g.target_fy31) + '</td>' +
          '<td data-l="Owners" data-w198skip>' + E(ownerNames(g.id)) + '</td><td data-l="Activities">' + actsMini(g.id) + '</td><td data-l="Milestones">' + pr.done + ' of ' + pr.total + ' done</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
  }
  function paintGlance() { var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205glance]'); if (!el) return; var tmp = document.createElement('div'); tmp.innerHTML = glanceHtml(); var n = tmp.querySelector('[data-w205glance]'); if (n) el.innerHTML = n.innerHTML; }

  function ownersHtml(g) {
    var can = canEdit(g);
    return ownersOf(g.id).map(function (o) {
      var m = memberById(o.member_id) || { name: 'Someone' };
      var inner = av(m, 24) + '<span data-w198skip>' + E(m.name) + '</span>' + (o.is_lead ? '<span class="lead">Lead</span>' : '') + (o.focus ? '<span class="focus" data-w198skip>' + E(o.focus) + '</span>' : '');
      return can ? '<button type="button" class="w205chip" data-w205owner="' + g.id + ':' + o.member_id + '" aria-label="' + E(m.name) + ': owner options">' + inner + '</button>' : '<span class="w205chip">' + inner + '</span>';
    }).join('') + (can ? '<button type="button" class="w205chip add" data-w205addowner="' + g.id + '">' + I('plus') + ' Owner</button>' : '') +
      (ownersOf(g.id).length ? '' : '<span style="font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + '">No owner yet.</span>');
  }
  function paintOwners(gid) { var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205owners="' + gid + '"]'); var g = byId(S.goals, gid); if (el && g) el.innerHTML = ownersHtml(g); paintGlance(); }
  function progHtml(g) {
    var pr = progress(g.id);
    return '<div class="trk" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="' + pr.pct + '" aria-label="Milestones done, FY27 to FY29"><i style="width:' + pr.pct + '%"></i></div>' +
      '<span><b style="color:' + GREEN + '">' + pr.done + ' of ' + pr.total + '</b> milestones done, FY27 to FY29' + (pr.yTotal ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(pr.fy) + ': ' + pr.yDone + ' of ' + pr.yTotal : '') + '</span>';
  }
  function paintProgress(gid) { var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205prog="' + gid + '"]'); var g = byId(S.goals, gid); if (el && g) el.innerHTML = progHtml(g); }
  function projChip(p) {
    return '<button type="button" class="w205pj" data-w205proj="' + p.id + '" title="' + E(deptLabel(p.department)) + ': ' + E(p.name) + '"><i class="d" style="background:' + E(deptColor(p.department)) + '"></i><span data-w198skip>' + E(p.name) + '</span></button>';
  }
  function cntBtn(gid, mid) {
    var n = commentsOf(gid, mid == null ? undefined : mid).length;
    if (mid == null) n = commentsOf(gid).length;
    return '<button type="button" class="w205cnt' + (n ? ' has' : '') + '" data-w205cmts="' + gid + (mid != null ? ':' + mid : '') + '" aria-label="' + (mid != null ? 'Comments on this milestone' : 'Comments on this goal') + ' (' + n + ')">' + I('chat') + (n ? n : '') + '</button>';
  }
  function msHtml(m, g) {
    var can = canEdit(g), ps = S.projects.filter(function (p) { return String(p.milestone_id) === String(m.id); });
    return '<div class="w205ms' + (m.status === 'done' ? ' done' : '') + '" data-w205ms="' + m.id + '"><div class="row">' + stSel('milestone', m, 'status', MS_ST) +
      ed('milestone', m, 'body', { label: m.fy + ' milestone', ph: 'Write the milestone' }) +
      (can ? '<button type="button" class="w205rm" data-w205rm="milestone:' + m.id + '" aria-label="Remove this milestone">' + I('x') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205msft">' + ps.map(projChip).join('') + cntBtn(g.id, m.id) +
      (canComment() ? '<button type="button" class="w205cnt" data-w205link="' + g.id + ':' + m.id + '" aria-label="Link a project to this milestone">' + I('link') + '</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function yearsHtml(g) {
    var ms = msOf(g.id), now = currentFy(), can = canEdit(g);
    var fys = FYS.slice(); ms.forEach(function (m) { if (fys.indexOf(m.fy) < 0) fys.push(m.fy); }); fys.sort();
    return fys.map(function (fy) {
      var list = ms.filter(function (m) { return m.fy === fy; }), done = list.filter(function (m) { return m.status === 'done'; }).length;
      return '<div class="w205yr' + (fy === 'FY31' || fy === 'FY30' ? ' gated' : '') + (fy === now ? ' now' : '') + '"><div class="yh"><b>' + fy + '</b><span>' + E(FY_LABEL[fy] || '') + '</span>' + (list.length ? '<em>' + done + '/' + list.length + '</em>' : '') + '</div>' +
        list.map(function (m) { return msHtml(m, g); }).join('') +
        (list.length ? '' : '<p style="font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:2px 2px 8px">' + (can ? 'No milestone yet.' : 'No milestone for this year.') + '</p>') +
        (can ? '<button type="button" class="w205addms" data-w205addms="' + g.id + ':' + fy + '">' + I('plus') + ' Add a milestone</button>' : '') +
        (fy === 'FY31' ? '<p style="font-size:11px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:6px 2px 0">Only if the FY29 gate is met.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function pathsHtml(g) {
    var can = canEdit(g), ps = pathsOf(g.id);
    return '<ol class="w205paths">' + ps.map(function (p) {
      return '<li data-w205path="' + p.id + '">' + ed('path', p, 'body', { label: 'Path', ph: 'Write the path' }) + (can ? '<button type="button" class="w205rm" data-w205rm="path:' + p.id + '" aria-label="Remove this path">' + I('x') + '</button>' : '') + '</li>';
    }).join('') + '</ol>' + (ps.length ? '' : '<p style="font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:0">' + (can ? 'No path yet: add how we get there.' : 'No path written yet.') + '</p>');
  }
  function projectsHtml(g) {
    var ms = {}; msOf(g.id).forEach(function (m) { ms[m.id] = 1; });
    var ps = projOf(g.id).filter(function (p) { return !p.milestone_id || !ms[p.milestone_id]; });
    var all = projOf(g.id);
    return '<div class="w205projs">' + (ps.length ? ps.map(projChip).join('') : '') +
      (canComment() ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205link="' + g.id + '">' + I('link') + ' Link a project</button>' : '') +
      (all.length ? '' : '<span style="font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + '">' + (canComment() ? 'No project serves this goal yet.' : 'No project linked yet.') + '</span>') + '</div>';
  }
  function paintProjects(gid) {
    var g = byId(S.goals, gid); if (!g) return;
    var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205projs="' + gid + '"]'); if (el) el.innerHTML = projectsHtml(g);
    msOf(gid).forEach(function (m) {
      var box = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205ms="' + m.id + '"] .w205msft'); if (!box) return;
      var ps = S.projects.filter(function (p) { return String(p.milestone_id) === String(m.id); });
      box.innerHTML = ps.map(projChip).join('') + cntBtn(g.id, m.id) + (canComment() ? '<button type="button" class="w205cnt" data-w205link="' + g.id + ':' + m.id + '" aria-label="Link a project to this milestone">' + I('link') + '</button>' : '');
    });
    var c = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205pcount="' + gid + '"]'); if (c) c.textContent = projOf(gid).length ? '(' + projOf(gid).length + ')' : '';
    paintCpFoot(gid);
  }
  function paintCounts(gid) {
    var b = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205cmtsft="' + gid + '"]'); var n = commentsOf(gid).length;
    if (b) b.innerHTML = I('chat') + ' Comments' + (n ? ' (' + n + ')' : '');
    msOf(gid).forEach(function (m) { var x = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205cmts="' + gid + ':' + m.id + '"]'); if (x) x.outerHTML = cntBtn(gid, m.id); });
  }
  function goalBodyHtml(g) {
    return '<div class="w205prog" data-w205prog="' + g.id + '">' + progHtml(g) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205two"><div class="w205smart"><div class="w205lab">SMART <span class="who" data-w205who="' + E(key('goal', g.id, 'detail')) + '" hidden></span></div>' + ed('goal', g, 'detail', { label: 'SMART statement', ph: 'Write the goal as the board will read it' }) + '</div>' +
      '<div><div class="w205lab">Why <span class="who" data-w205who="' + E(key('goal', g.id, 'why')) + '" hidden></span></div>' + ed('goal', g, 'why', { label: 'Why', ph: 'Why this goal matters' }) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w205meas">' + [['measure', 'Headline measure'], ['baseline', 'Baseline'], ['target_fy29', 'FY29 target'], ['target_fy31', 'FY31 target, gated']].map(function (x) {
        return '<div' + (x[0] === 'target_fy29' ? ' class="t29"' : '') + '><div class="w205lab">' + E(x[1]) + ' <span class="who" data-w205who="' + E(key('goal', g.id, x[0])) + '" hidden></span></div>' + ed('goal', g, x[0], { label: x[1], ph: 'Add it' }) + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205h4"><h4>Paths</h4><span style="font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + '">How we get there</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (canEdit(g) ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205addpath="' + g.id + '">' + I('plus') + ' Add a path</button>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div data-w205paths="' + g.id + '">' + pathsHtml(g) + '</div>' +
      yearBlockHtml(g) +
      '<div class="w205h4"><h4>Milestones</h4><span style="font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + '">By fiscal year (Oct 1 to Sep 30)</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w205years" data-w205years="' + g.id + '">' + yearsHtml(g) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205h4"><h4>Projects serving this goal <span data-w205pcount="' + g.id + '" style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + ';text-transform:none">' + (projOf(g.id).length ? '(' + projOf(g.id).length + ')' : '') + '</span></h4></div>' +
      '<div data-w205projs="' + g.id + '">' + projectsHtml(g) + '</div>';
  }
  function goalHtml(g) {
    var folded = !!S.folded[g.id] && String(S.goalId) !== String(g.id);
    var n = commentsOf(g.id).length;
    return '<article class="w205goal' + (folded ? ' folded' : '') + (String(S.goalId) === String(g.id) ? ' target' : '') + '" id="w205g-' + g.id + '" data-w205goal="' + g.id + '" aria-labelledby="w205gt-' + g.id + '">' +
      '<div class="w205ghd"><span class="w205num" aria-hidden="true">' + E(g.number) + '</span><div class="tw" id="w205gt-' + g.id + '">' + ed('goal', g, 'title', { cls: 'title', label: 'Goal ' + g.number + ' title', ph: 'Name the goal' }) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="side"><span class="w205pres" style="margin:0" data-w205gpres="' + g.id + '"></span>' + stSel('goal', g, 'status', GOAL_ST) + '<span class="w205saved" data-w205saved="' + g.id + '" aria-live="polite"></span>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w205fold" data-w205fold="' + g.id + '" aria-expanded="' + (folded ? 'false' : 'true') + '" aria-label="Fold or open this goal">' + I('chev') + '</button></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w205owners" data-w205owners="' + g.id + '">' + ownersHtml(g) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w207acts" data-w207acts="' + g.id + '">' + actsHtml(g) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205gbody" data-w205gbody="' + g.id + '">' + goalBodyHtml(g) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205gft"><button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205cmtsft="' + g.id + '" data-w205cmts="' + g.id + '">' + I('chat') + ' Comments' + (n ? ' (' + n + ')' : '') + '</button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205hist="' + g.id + '">' + I('clock') + ' History</button>' +
      '<span class="sp"></span><span>Due ' + E(fmtDay(g.due_date)) + (canEdit(g) ? '' : ' \u00b7 read only') + '</span></div></article>';
  }
  /* repaint one goal's body without losing what someone is typing */
  function repaintGoal(gid, keepFocus) {
    var g = byId(S.goals, gid), box = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205gbody="' + gid + '"]'); if (!g || !box) return;
    var act = document.activeElement, actKey = act && box.contains(act) ? act.getAttribute('data-w205k') : null, sel = null;
    if (actKey && act.selectionStart != null) sel = [act.selectionStart, act.selectionEnd];
    var keep = {};
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-w205k]').forEach(function (el) { var k = el.getAttribute('data-w205k'); if (S.dirty[k] || S.conflict[k]) keep[k] = el.value; });
    box.innerHTML = goalBodyHtml(g);
    Object.keys(keep).forEach(function (k) { var el = fieldEl(k); if (el) el.value = keep[k]; if (S.conflict[k]) paintConflict(k); });
    box.querySelectorAll('textarea.w205ed').forEach(grow);
    if (keepFocus && actKey) { var n = fieldEl(actKey); if (n) { n.focus(); if (sel) try { n.setSelectionRange(sel[0], sel[1]); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    paintGlance(); paintPresence();
  }
  function roadHtml() {
    var r = S.plan.roadmap; if (!r) return '';
    var ph = r.phases || [];
    return '<div class="w205sec"><h2>Roadmap</h2><span class="hint">Four phases, one checkpoint, one gate.</span></div>' +
      '<section class="w205road" aria-label="Roadmap"><h3>' + E(r.title || '') + '</h3><div class="bar"></div>' +
      '<div class="w205phases">' + ph.map(function (p) {
        return '<div class="w205ph' + (p.gated ? ' gated' : '') + '"><div class="k">' + E(p.key + ' ' + p.name) + '</div><div class="d">' + E(p.dates) + (p.gated ? ' \u00b7 gated' : '') + '</div><ul>' + (p.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + E(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205marks"><div></div>' +
      (r.checkpoint ? '<div class="w205mk"><b>' + E(r.checkpoint.name) + '</b><ul>' + (r.checkpoint.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + E(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>' : '<div></div>') + '<div></div>' +
      (r.gate ? '<div class="w205mk gt"><b>' + E(r.gate.name) + '</b><ul>' + (r.gate.items || []).map(function (x) { return '<li>' + E(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (r.gate_rule ? '<p class="w205rule">' + E(r.gate_rule) + '</p>' : '') + '</section>' +
      (S.plan.cadence && S.plan.cadence.length ? '<div class="w205list" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="it" style="font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + '">How we track it</div>' + S.plan.cadence.map(function (c) { return '<div class="it">' + E(c) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
  }
  function unlinkedList() {
    return S.projects.filter(function (p) {
      var s = lower(p.status || 'active'); if (/done|complete|closed|archiv/.test(s)) return false;
      return !p.goal_id || !byId(S.goals, p.goal_id);
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.department).localeCompare(String(b.department)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
  }
  function unlinkedHtml() {
    var list = unlinkedList();
    return '<div class="w205sec"><h2>Projects not linked to a goal yet</h2><span class="hint">Teams own projects; each one serves a goal.</span></div>' +
      (list.length ? '<div class="w205list" data-w205unlinked>' + list.map(function (p) {
        var was = p.goal_id ? byId(S.allGoals || [], p.goal_id) : null;
        return '<div class="it"><i style="width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:' + E(deptColor(p.department)) + ';flex:none"></i><div class="t"><b data-w198skip>' + E(p.name) + '</b><small>' + E(deptLabel(p.department)) + (was ? ' \u00b7 was linked to a retired goal: <span data-w198skip>' + E(clip(was.title, 70)) + '</span>' : '') + '</small></div>' +
          (canComment() ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205linkp="' + p.id + '">' + I('link') + ' Link</button>' : '') + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w205empty" data-w205unlinked><b>Every project serves a goal</b>Nothing to link.</div>');
  }
  function paintUnlinked() { var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205unlinked]'); if (!el) return; var t = document.createElement('div'); t.innerHTML = unlinkedHtml(); var n = t.querySelector('[data-w205unlinked]'); if (n) el.replaceWith(n); }
  function libPreviewHtml() {
    var pinned = S.docs.filter(function (d) { return d.pinned; }).slice(0, 6);
    return '<div class="w205sec"><h2>Strategy Library</h2><span class="hint">The documents that set our direction, pinned for everyone.</span><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205go="#orggoals/library">' + I('doc') + ' Open the library (' + S.docs.length + ')</button></div>' +
      (pinned.length ? '<div class="w205lib">' + pinned.map(function (d) { return docHtml(d, true); }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="w205empty"><b>Nothing pinned yet</b>' + (isAdmin() ? 'Pin the documents everyone should read from the library.' : 'Admins pin the documents everyone should read.') + '</div>');
  }
  function goalsViewHtml() {
    var p = S.plan;
    if (!S.goals.length) {
      return mastHtml('Goals', p.window || '') + heroHtml() + '<div class="w205empty"><b>No organizational goals yet</b>' + (S.err ? 'The goals could not be read (' + E(clip(S.err, 120)) + ').' : 'When the goals are written, they show here.') + '</div>';
    }
    return mastHtml('Goals', 'Five goals for FY27 to FY29, with a gated view to FY31. Teams own projects; each one serves a goal.') +
      heroHtml() + statusHtml() + yearViewHtml() + whyHtml() + glanceHtml() + coverageHtml() +
      '<div class="w205sec"><h2>Goal by goal</h2><span class="hint">' + (S.goals.some(canEdit) ? 'Click any text to change it; it saves as you type.' : 'Owners keep these up to date.') + '</span></div>' +
      S.goals.map(goalHtml).join('') + roadHtml() + libPreviewHtml() + unlinkedHtml() +
      '<div class="w205more"><button type="button" class="w205tile" data-w205go="#goalmap"><b>Goal alignment</b><span>Every project and the goal it serves, on one map.</span></button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w205tile" data-w205go="#projects"><b>Projects</b><span>Every department\u2019s projects, their leads and their goals.</span></button>' +
      '<button type="button" class="w205tile" data-w205go="#orggoals/retired"><b>Retired goals (' + S.retired.length + ')</b><span>The department goals and the three earlier org goals, kept with their wins and comments.</span></button></div>' +
      (p.source && p.source.title ? '<p style="font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:18px 0 0">From the draft "' + E(p.source.title) + '".</p>' : '');
  }

  /* ---------- the drawer: comments, history, link a project, owners, a library document ---------- */
  function ensureDrawer() {
    if (document.getElementById('w205drawer')) return;
    var s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w205scrim'; s.setAttribute('data-w205close', '1');
    var d = document.createElement('aside'); d.id = 'w205drawer'; d.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); d.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); d.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'w205dt'); d.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    document.body.appendChild(s); document.body.appendChild(d);
  }
  function openDrawer(st) {
    ensureDrawer();
    S.drawer = st; S.drawer.opener = document.activeElement;
    paintDrawer();
    var d = document.getElementById('w205drawer'), s = document.getElementById('w205scrim');
    d.classList.add('open'); s.classList.add('open'); d.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
    setTimeout(function () { var f = d.querySelector('[data-w205autofocus]') || d.querySelector('textarea,input,button'); if (f) try { f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 60);
  }
  function closeDrawer() {
    var d = document.getElementById('w205drawer'), s = document.getElementById('w205scrim');
    if (!d || !S.drawer) return;
    var op = S.drawer.opener; S.drawer = null;
    d.classList.remove('open'); s.classList.remove('open'); d.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    if (onPage() && /\/(comments|history)/.test(location.hash)) { try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#orggoals/goal/' + S.goalId); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    if (op && document.body.contains(op)) try { op.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function drawerShell(title, sub, body, foot) {
    return '<div class="dh"><div><h3 id="w205dt">' + title + '</h3>' + (sub ? '<small>' + sub + '</small>' : '') + '</div><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205close="1" aria-label="Close">' + I('x') + '</button></div>' +
      '<div class="db">' + body + '</div>' + (foot ? '<div class="df">' + foot + '</div>' : '');
  }
  function paintDrawer() {
    var d = document.getElementById('w205drawer'); if (!d || !S.drawer) return;
    var st = S.drawer, html = '';
    var keepVal = null, ta = d.querySelector('textarea[data-w205cmt]'); if (ta) keepVal = { v: ta.value, focus: document.activeElement === ta, s: ta.selectionStart };
    var keepReply = {}; d.querySelectorAll('textarea[data-w205reply]').forEach(function (t) { keepReply[t.getAttribute('data-w205reply')] = t.value; });
    if (st.kind === 'comments') html = commentsDrawer(st);
    else if (st.kind === 'history') html = historyDrawer(st);
    else if (st.kind === 'link') html = linkDrawer(st);
    else if (st.kind === 'owner') html = ownerDrawer(st);
    else if (st.kind === 'doc') html = docDrawer(st);
    else if (st.kind === 'acts') html = actsDrawer(st);
    d.innerHTML = html;
    if (keepVal) { var t2 = d.querySelector('textarea[data-w205cmt]'); if (t2) { t2.value = keepVal.v; if (keepVal.focus) { t2.focus(); try { t2.setSelectionRange(keepVal.s, keepVal.s); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } } }
    Object.keys(keepReply).forEach(function (k) { var t = d.querySelector('textarea[data-w205reply="' + k + '"]'); if (t) t.value = keepReply[k]; });
  }

  /* comments, threaded; @mentions through wave138 (the picker and the DM it already sends) */
  function markText(s) { var h = E(s); try { if (window.w138Mark) h = window.w138Mark(h); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return h; }
  function commentHtml(c, gid, canReply) {
    var mine = c.author_email && lower(c.author_email) === myEmail();
    var m = memberByEmail(c.author_email) || { name: c.author || 'Someone' };
    var editing = S.drawer && String(S.drawer.editing) === String(c.id);
    return '<div class="w205cm" data-w205c="' + c.id + '">' + av(m, 28) + '<div class="bd"><div class="who" data-w198skip>' + E(c.author || m.name) + '<small>' + E(fmtWhen(c.created_at)) + (c.edited_at ? ' \u00b7 edited' : '') + '</small></div>' +
      (editing ? '<textarea data-w205edit="' + c.id + '" aria-label="Edit your comment">' + E(c.body) + '</textarea><div class="acts"><button type="button" class="w205btn sm pri" data-w205editsave="' + c.id + '">Save</button><button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205editcancel="1">Cancel</button></div>'
        : '<div class="tx" data-w198skip>' + markText(c.body) + '</div>') +
      (!editing ? '<div class="ln">' + (canReply ? '<button type="button" class="w205link" data-w205replyto="' + c.id + '">Reply</button>' : '') + ((mine || isAdmin()) && canComment() ? '<button type="button" class="w205link" data-w205editc="' + c.id + '">Edit</button>' : '') + '</div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function commentsDrawer(st) {
    var g = byId(S.goals, st.goal) || byId(S.allGoals || [], st.goal);
    if (!g) return drawerShell('Comments', '', '<p>That goal is not here.</p>');
    var ms = msOf(g.id), mid = st.milestone || null, cid = st.checkpoint || null, can = canComment();
    var all = commentsOf(g.id);
    var roots = all.filter(function (c) { return !c.parent_id && (cid ? String(c.checkpoint_id) === String(cid) : mid ? String(c.milestone_id) === String(mid) : true); });
    var seg = '<div class="w205seg" role="tablist"><button type="button" role="tab" class="' + (!mid && !cid ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w205cseg="" aria-selected="' + (!mid && !cid) + '">Everything (' + all.length + ')</button>' +
      cpsOfGoal(g.id).filter(function (c) { return all.some(function (x) { return String(x.checkpoint_id) === String(c.id); }) || String(c.id) === String(cid); }).map(function (c) {
        var n = all.filter(function (x) { return String(x.checkpoint_id) === String(c.id); }).length;
        return '<button type="button" role="tab" class="' + (String(c.id) === String(cid) ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w207cseg="' + c.id + '" title="' + E(c.body) + '" aria-selected="' + (String(c.id) === String(cid)) + '">' + E(cpLabel(c)) + ' (' + n + ')</button>';
      }).join('') +
      ms.filter(function (m) { return all.some(function (c) { return String(c.milestone_id) === String(m.id); }) || String(m.id) === String(mid); }).map(function (m) {
        var n = all.filter(function (c) { return String(c.milestone_id) === String(m.id); }).length;
        return '<button type="button" role="tab" class="' + (String(m.id) === String(mid) ? 'on' : '') + '" data-w205cseg="' + m.id + '" title="' + E(m.body) + '" aria-selected="' + (String(m.id) === String(mid)) + '">' + E(m.fy) + ': ' + E(clip(m.body, 26)) + ' (' + n + ')</button>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    var threads = roots.map(function (c) {
      var reps = all.filter(function (r) { return String(r.parent_id) === String(c.id); });
      var mm = c.milestone_id ? byId(S.milestones, c.milestone_id) : null, cc = c.checkpoint_id ? byId(S.checkpoints, c.checkpoint_id) : null;
      return '<div class="w205th">' + (mm && !mid ? '<span class="w205ms-tag" data-w198skip>' + E(mm.fy) + ': ' + E(clip(mm.body, 40)) + '</span>' : '') + (cc && !cid ? '<span class="w205ms-tag" data-w198skip>' + E(cpLabel(cc)) + ': ' + E(clip(cc.body, 40)) + '</span>' : '') + commentHtml(c, g.id, can) +
        (reps.length || String(st.replyTo) === String(c.id) ? '<div class="w205replies">' + reps.map(function (r) { return commentHtml(r, g.id, false); }).join('') +
          (String(st.replyTo) === String(c.id) ? '<div style="margin-top:10px"><textarea data-w205reply="' + c.id + '" data-w205cmt="reply" placeholder="Write a reply. Type @ to bring someone in." aria-label="Your reply" data-w205autofocus></textarea><div class="acts"><button type="button" class="w205btn sm pri" data-w205sendreply="' + c.id + '">Reply</button><button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205replyto="">Cancel</button></div></div>' : '') + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    var msNow = mid ? byId(S.milestones, mid) : null, cpNow = cid ? byId(S.checkpoints, cid) : null;
    var body = seg + (threads || '<div class="w205empty"><b>No comments yet</b>' + (can ? 'Start the conversation: a question, a link, an @mention.' : 'When the team talks about this goal, it shows here.') + '</div>');
    var foot = can ? '<textarea data-w205cmt="new" placeholder="' + (cpNow ? 'Comment on this checkpoint' : msNow ? 'Comment on this milestone' : 'Comment on this goal') + '. Type @ to bring someone in." aria-label="Your comment"' + (st.replyTo ? '' : ' data-w205autofocus') + '></textarea>' +
      '<div class="acts"><small>' + (cpNow ? 'On ' + E(cpLabel(cpNow)) + ': ' + E(clip(cpNow.body, 40)) : msNow ? 'On ' + E(msNow.fy) + ': ' + E(clip(msNow.body, 40)) : 'On the whole goal') + ' \u00b7 Ctrl+Enter posts</small><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w205btn pri sm" data-w205post="1">Post</button></div>' +
      '<small style="display:block;color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:11px;margin-top:6px">People you @mention hear about it the way every Hub @mention works.</small>'
      : '<small style="color:' + MUTED + '">' + E(readOnlyWhy()) + '</small>';
    return drawerShell('Comments', '<span data-w198skip>Goal ' + E(g.number || '') + ': ' + E(g.title) + '</span>', body, foot);
  }
  function postComment(parent) {
    var d = document.getElementById('w205drawer'); if (!d || !S.drawer) return;
    var ta = parent ? d.querySelector('textarea[data-w205reply="' + parent + '"]') : d.querySelector('textarea[data-w205cmt="new"]');
    var body = ta ? ta.value.trim() : ''; if (!body) { if (ta) ta.focus(); return; }
    var st = S.drawer, btn = d.querySelector(parent ? '[data-w205sendreply="' + parent + '"]' : '[data-w205post]'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    var pc = parent ? byId(S.comments, parent) : null, cpId = parent ? (pc && pc.checkpoint_id) : st.checkpoint;
    (cpId ? rpc('strategy_checkpoint_comment_add', { p_checkpoint: Number(cpId), p_parent: parent ? Number(parent) : null, p_body: body })
      : rpc('strategy_comment_add', { p_goal: Number(st.goal), p_milestone: parent ? null : (st.milestone ? Number(st.milestone) : null), p_parent: parent ? Number(parent) : null, p_body: body })).then(function (row) {
      if (row && row.id && !byId(S.comments, row.id)) S.comments.push(row);
      if (ta) ta.value = '';
      if (parent) st.replyTo = null;
      paintDrawer(); paintCounts(st.goal); paintYearCounts(st.goal);
      toastS('Comment posted');
    }).catch(function (e) { toastS('Not posted: ' + errText(e)); if (btn) btn.disabled = false; });
  }
  function saveEdit(id) {
    var d = document.getElementById('w205drawer'), ta = d && d.querySelector('textarea[data-w205edit="' + id + '"]'); if (!ta) return;
    rpc('strategy_comment_edit', { p_id: Number(id), p_body: ta.value }).then(function (row) {
      var c = byId(S.comments, id); if (c && row) Object.assign(c, row);
      S.drawer.editing = null; paintDrawer();
    }).catch(function (e) { toastS('Not saved: ' + errText(e)); });
  }

  /* the history: every change, who and when, with Restore */
  function histLabel(h) {
    var f = FIELD_LABEL[h.field] || h.field;
    if (h.entity === 'goal') return 'changed ' + f;
    if (h.entity === 'milestone') return h.field === 'added' ? 'added a milestone' : h.field === 'archived_at' ? (h.new_value ? 'removed a milestone' : 'brought back a milestone') : 'changed a milestone\u2019s ' + (h.field === 'body' ? 'text' : f.replace(/^the /, ''));
    if (h.entity === 'path') return h.field === 'added' ? 'added a path' : h.field === 'archived_at' ? (h.new_value ? 'removed a path' : 'brought back a path') : 'changed a path';
    if (h.entity === 'owner') return h.field === 'added' ? 'added an owner' : h.field === 'removed' ? 'took an owner off' : h.field === 'is_lead' ? 'changed the lead' : 'changed an owner\u2019s focus';
    if (h.entity === 'project') return h.field === 'goal_id' ? (h.new_value ? 'linked a project' : 'unlinked a project') : h.field === 'checkpoint_id' ? (h.new_value ? 'linked a project to a checkpoint' : 'took a project off a checkpoint') : 'moved a project to a milestone';
    if (h.entity === 'activity') return h.field === 'added' ? 'anchored the goal on a charitable activity' : h.field === 'archived_at' ? (h.new_value ? 'took a charitable activity off' : 'brought back a charitable activity') : 'changed why it serves a charitable activity';
    if (h.entity === 'target') return h.field === 'owners' ? 'changed who reports on the yearly target' : 'changed the yearly target';
    if (h.entity === 'checkpoint') return 'changed a checkpoint\u2019s ' + (h.field === 'body' ? 'text' : h.field === 'due_date' ? 'due date' : f.replace(/^the /, ''));
    return 'changed ' + f;
  }
  function restorable(h) {
    if (h.entity === 'project') return canComment();
    var g = byId(S.goals, h.goal_id); if (!canEdit(g)) return false;
    if (h.entity === 'goal') return ['title', 'detail', 'why', 'measure', 'baseline', 'target_fy29', 'target_fy31', 'status', 'due_date', 'activities_note'].indexOf(h.field) >= 0;
    return true;
  }
  function histVal(h, v) {
    if (v == null || v === '') return '';
    if (h.field === 'archived_at') return '';
    if (h.field === 'status') return ST_LABEL[v] || v;
    if (h.field === 'is_lead') return v === 'true' ? 'Lead' : 'Not the lead';
    return v;
  }
  function historyDrawer(st) {
    var g = byId(S.goals, st.goal); if (!g) return drawerShell('History', '', '<p>That goal is not here.</p>');
    var body;
    if (st.loading) body = '<p style="color:' + MUTED + '">Reading the history\u2026</p>';
    else if (st.err) body = '<div class="w205empty"><b>The history could not be read</b>' + E(st.err) + '</div>';
    else if (!st.rows || !st.rows.length) body = '<div class="w205empty"><b>No changes yet</b>Every change to this goal shows here, with who made it and when.</div>';
    else {
      var day = '', out = [];
      st.rows.forEach(function (h) {
        var dd = fmtDay(h.changed_at); if (dd !== day) { day = dd; out.push('<div class="w205hday">' + E(dd) + '</div>'); }
        var ov = histVal(h, h.old_value), nv = histVal(h, h.new_value), simple = (h.entity === 'project' && h.field !== 'checkpoint_id') || h.entity === 'owner' || h.field === 'added' || (h.field === 'archived_at' && h.entity !== 'activity');
        if (h.entity === 'activity' && h.field === 'archived_at') { simple = true; nv = h.new_value || h.old_value || ''; }
        var cpx = h.entity === 'checkpoint' ? cpLabel(byId(S.checkpoints, h.entity_id)) : '';
        out.push('<div class="w205h" data-w205h="' + h.id + '"><div class="l1"><span><b data-w198skip>' + E(h.changed_by_name || h.changed_by || 'Someone') + '</b> ' + E(histLabel(h)) + '</span><span class="sp"></span><small>' + E(new Date(h.changed_at).toLocaleTimeString('en-CA', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })) + '</small></div>' +
          (h.restored_from ? '<span class="rs">A restore</span>' : '') + (cpx ? '<span class="w205ms-tag">' + E(cpx) + '</span>' : '') +
          (simple ? ((nv || ov) ? '<span class="nv" data-w198skip>' + E(nv || ov) + '</span>' : '') : (ov ? '<span class="ov" data-w198skip>' + E(ov) + '</span>' : '') + (nv ? '<span class="nv" data-w198skip>' + E(nv) + '</span>' : '<span class="nv"><i>cleared</i></span>')) +
          (restorable(h) ? '<div style="margin-top:6px"><button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205restore="' + h.id + '">' + I('undo') + ' Restore</button></div>' : '') + '</div>');
      });
      body = out.join('');
    }
    return drawerShell('History', '<span data-w198skip>Goal ' + E(g.number) + ': ' + E(g.title) + '</span> \u00b7 every change, newest first', body, canEdit(g) ? '<small style="color:' + MUTED + '">Restore puts the old version back; the restore is itself a change here, so nothing is lost.</small>' : '');
  }
  function loadHistory() {
    var st = S.drawer; if (!st || st.kind !== 'history') return;
    st.loading = true; paintDrawer();
    Promise.resolve(db.from('goal_history').select('*').eq('goal_id', Number(st.goal)).order('changed_at', { ascending: false }).limit(300)).then(function (r) {
      if (S.drawer !== st) return;
      st.loading = false; st.err = r && r.error ? (r.error.message || String(r.error)) : null; st.rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      st.rows.sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.changed_at).localeCompare(String(a.changed_at)) || b.id - a.id; });
      paintDrawer();
    }, function (e) { st.loading = false; st.err = errText(e); paintDrawer(); });
  }
  function restore(id) {
    rpc('strategy_restore', { p_history: Number(id) }).then(function (res) {
      if (res && res.conflict) { toastS('Someone changed that field again; read the latest first.'); }
      else toastS('Restored');
      S.loaded = 0;
      return loadAll(true).then(function () { paint(); if (S.drawer && S.drawer.kind === 'history') loadHistory(); });
    }).catch(function (e) { toastS('Not restored: ' + errText(e)); });
  }

  /* link a project to a goal and, ideally, one milestone */
  function linkDrawer(st) {
    var g = st.goal ? byId(S.goals, st.goal) : null;
    var qv = lower(st.q || '');
    var p = st.project ? byId(S.projects, st.project) : null;
    var body = '';
    if (!p) {
      var list = S.projects.filter(function (x) { var s = lower(x.status || 'active'); return !/done|complete|closed|archiv/.test(s); })
        .filter(function (x) { return !qv || lower(x.name + ' ' + deptLabel(x.department)).indexOf(qv) >= 0; })
        .sort(function (a, b) { return String(deptLabel(a.department)).localeCompare(String(deptLabel(b.department))) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
      var groups = {}; list.forEach(function (x) { (groups[x.department] = groups[x.department] || []).push(x); });
      body = '<label class="w205search" style="max-width:none;margin:0 0 10px">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w205linkq value="' + E(st.q || '') + '" placeholder="Find a project" aria-label="Find a project" data-w205autofocus /></label>' +
        (list.length ? Object.keys(groups).map(function (d) {
          return '<div class="w205grp">' + E(deptLabel(d)) + '</div><div class="w205pick">' + groups[d].map(function (x) {
            var cur = x.goal_id ? byId(S.goals, x.goal_id) : null;
            return '<label><input type="radio" name="w205lp" data-w205pickp="' + x.id + '" /><span class="t"><span data-w198skip>' + E(x.name) + '</span><small>' + (cur ? 'Serves goal ' + E(cur.number) + (g && cur.id === g.id ? ' (this goal)' : '') : 'Not linked to a goal yet') + '</small></span></label>';
          }).join('') + '</div>';
        }).join('') : '<div class="w205empty"><b>No project matches</b>Projects are added on a team\u2019s page or on Projects.</div>');
      return drawerShell('Link a project', g ? '<span data-w198skip>To goal ' + E(g.number) + ': ' + E(g.title) + '</span>' : 'Pick the project first', body, '<small style="color:' + MUTED + '">Teams own their projects; linking one says which goal (and milestone) it serves.</small>');
    }
    var goalSel = st.goalPick != null ? st.goalPick : (g ? g.id : p.goal_id);
    var gg = byId(S.goals, goalSel);
    var curG = p.goal_id ? byId(S.goals, p.goal_id) : null;
    body = '<div class="w205th"><b data-w198skip>' + E(p.name) + '</b><div style="font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin-top:2px">' + E(deptLabel(p.department)) + ' \u00b7 ' + (curG ? 'serves goal ' + E(curG.number) : 'not linked to a goal yet') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:8px"><a class="w205btn sm" href="#project/' + p.id + '">Open the project</a></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w205grp">The goal it serves</div><div class="w205pick">' + S.goals.map(function (x) {
        return '<label class="' + (String(x.id) === String(goalSel) ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w205lg" data-w205pickg="' + x.id + '"' + (String(x.id) === String(goalSel) ? ' checked' : '') + ' /><span class="w205num" style="width:24px;height:24px;font-size:13px">' + E(x.number) + '</span><span class="t" data-w198skip>' + E(x.title) + '</span></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' +
      (gg ? '<div class="w205grp">The milestone (optional)</div><div class="w205pick"><label class="' + (!st.msPick ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w205lm" data-w205pickm=""' + (!st.msPick ? ' checked' : '') + ' /><span class="t">The goal as a whole</span></label>' +
        msOf(gg.id).map(function (m) { return '<label class="' + (String(st.msPick) === String(m.id) ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w205lm" data-w205pickm="' + m.id + '"' + (String(st.msPick) === String(m.id) ?
