' checked' : '') + ' /><span class="t"><span data-w198skip>' + E(m.body) + '</span><small>' + E(m.fy) + '</small></span></label>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      (gg ? cpPickHtml(gg, st) : '');
    var foot = '<div class="acts" style="margin:0">' + (curG ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205unlink="' + p.id + '">Unlink</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w205btn pri" data-w205dolink="1"' + (gg ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + I('link') + ' Link it</button></div>';
    return drawerShell(curG ? 'Move or unlink a project' : 'Link a project', '<span data-w198skip>' + E(p.name) + '</span>', body, foot);
  }
  function doLink(unlink) {
    var st = S.drawer; if (!st || st.kind !== 'link' || !st.project) return;
    var p = byId(S.projects, st.project), g = unlink ? null : (st.goalPick != null ? st.goalPick : (st.goal || (p && p.goal_id)));
    var m = unlink ? null : (st.msPick || null);
    var cp = unlink ? null : (st.cpPick || null);
    rpc('strategy_link_project', { p_project: Number(st.project), p_goal: g ? Number(g) : null, p_milestone: m ? Number(m) : null }).then(function () {
      /* wave207: the checkpoint after the goal (moving the goal clears a checkpoint of another goal) */
      var keep = p && p.checkpoint_id && cpsOfGoal(g).some(function (c) { return String(c.id) === String(p.checkpoint_id); }) ? p.checkpoint_id : null;
      if (String(cp || '') === String(keep || '')) return null;
      return rpc('strategy_link_checkpoint', { p_project: Number(st.project), p_checkpoint: cp ? Number(cp) : null });
    }).then(function () {
      if (p) { p.goal_id = g ? Number(g) : null; p.milestone_id = m ? Number(m) : null; p.checkpoint_id = cp ? Number(cp) : null; }
      paintYear();
      closeDrawer();
      S.goals.forEach(function (x) { paintProjects(x.id); }); paintUnlinked();
      /* the shared project store (wave116i): Projects, the team pages and Goal alignment repaint from the same rows */
      try {
        var PS = window.__waProjects;
        if (PS && PS.rows) { PS.rows.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(st.project)) { x.goal_id = g ? Number(g) : null; x.milestone_id = m ? Number(m) : null; x.checkpoint_id = cp ? Number(cp) : null; } }); if (window.w116ProjectsChanged) window.w116ProjectsChanged(PS.rows); }
        else if (window.w116Goals && window.w116Goals.refresh) window.w116Goals.refresh();
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      toastS(unlink ? 'Unlinked' : 'Linked to goal ' + ((byId(S.goals, g) || {}).number || ''));
    }).catch(function (e) { toastS('Not linked: ' + errText(e)); });
  }

  /* owners: add someone from the roster, make the lead, take someone off */
  function ownerDrawer(st) {
    var g = byId(S.goals, st.goal); if (!g) return drawerShell('Owners', '', '');
    var qv = lower(st.q || ''), own = ownersOf(g.id);
    var current = own.map(function (o) {
      var m = memberById(o.member_id) || { name: '?' };
      return '<div class="w205th" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">' + av(m, 30) + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b data-w198skip>' + E(m.name) + '</b>' + (o.is_lead ? ' <span class="w205tag g">Lead</span>' : '') +
        '<label class="w205field" style="margin:6px 0 0;font-weight:600">What they carry <input type="text" data-w205focus="' + o.member_id + '" value="' + E(o.focus || '') + '" placeholder="Optional, e.g. Toronto" /></label></div>' +
        '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px">' + (o.is_lead ? '' : '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205lead="' + o.member_id + '">Make lead</button>') + '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205ownoff="' + o.member_id + '">Take off</button></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    var ids = {}; own.forEach(function (o) { ids[o.member_id] = 1; });
    var pool = members().filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false && !ids[m.id] && (!qv || lower(m.name + ' ' + (m.role || '') + ' ' + deptLabel(m.department)).indexOf(qv) >= 0); })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); }).slice(0, 40);
    var body = '<div class="w205grp">Owners now</div>' + (current || '<p style="font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + '">No owner yet.</p>') +
      '<div class="w205grp">Add an owner</div><label class="w205search" style="max-width:none;margin:0 0 8px">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w205ownq value="' + E(st.q || '') + '" placeholder="Find someone on the roster" aria-label="Find someone on the roster" data-w205autofocus /></label>' +
      '<div class="w205pick">' + pool.map(function (m) { return '<label data-w205ownon="' + m.id + '" tabindex="0" role="button">' + av(m, 26) + '<span class="t"><span data-w198skip>' + E(m.name) + '</span><small>' + E(m.role || deptLabel(m.department)) + '</small></span>' + I('plus') + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    return drawerShell('Owners', '<span data-w198skip>Goal ' + E(g.number) + ': ' + E(g.title) + '</span> \u00b7 one to three, one lead', body, '<small style="color:' + MUTED + '">Owners and admins edit the goal. Every change is in its history.</small>');
  }
  function ownerSet(gid, mid, on, lead, focus) {
    return rpc('strategy_owner_set', { p_goal: Number(gid), p_member: Number(mid), p_on: !!on, p_lead: lead == null ? null : !!lead, p_focus: focus == null ? null : focus }).then(function () {
      return Promise.resolve(db.from('goal_owners').select('*').eq('goal_id', Number(gid))).then(function (r) {
        S.owners = S.owners.filter(function (o) { return String(o.goal_id) !== String(gid); }).concat(((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (o) { return !o.removed_at; }));
        paintOwners(gid); if (S.drawer && S.drawer.kind === 'owner') paintDrawer(); paintSoonIfRights();
      });
    }).catch(function (e) { toastS('Not changed: ' + errText(e)); });
  }
  function paintSoonIfRights() { /* someone who took themselves off loses the pen: redraw the fields */ var g = S.goals.filter(function (x) { return !canEdit(x); }).length; if (g !== S.lastNoEdit) { S.lastNoEdit = g; paintSoon(); } }

  /* a library document (admins) */
  function docDrawer(st) {
    var d = st.id ? byId(S.docs, st.id) : {};
    var f = function (n, l, ph, ta) { return '<label class="w205field">' + E(l) + (ta ? '<textarea data-w205df="' + n + '" rows="3" placeholder="' + E(ph) + '">' + E(d[n] || '') + '</textarea>' : '<input type="' + (n === 'url' ? 'url' : 'text') + '" data-w205df="' + n + '" value="' + E(d[n] || '') + '" placeholder="' + E(ph) + '"' + (n === 'title' ? ' data-w205autofocus' : '') + ' />') + '</label>'; };
    var body = f('title', 'Title', 'The document\u2019s name') + f('url', 'Link', 'https://docs.google.com/...') + f('group_label', 'Shelf', 'Our plans, Consulting work or Sources') + f('doc_date', 'When it was written', 'e.g. Jun 2026') +
      f('direction', 'The direction it sets', 'What it says we will do', true) + f('status_note', 'Where it stands', 'e.g. Concept; no dates or targets', true) +
      '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600"><input type="checkbox" data-w205df="pinned"' + (st.id ? (d.pinned ? ' checked' : '') : ' checked') + ' /> Pinned for everyone (shows on the Goals page)</label>';
    var foot = '<div class="acts" style="margin:0">' + (st.id ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205docarch="' + st.id + '">Take it out of the library</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="w205btn pri" data-w205docsave="1">' + (st.id ? 'Save' : 'Add it') + '</button></div>';
    return drawerShell(st.id ? 'Edit a document' : 'Add a document', 'The Strategy Library', body, foot);
  }
  function docSave(archive) {
    var st = S.drawer, d = document.getElementById('w205drawer'); if (!st || !d) return;
    var patch = {};
    if (archive) patch.archived = true;
    else d.querySelectorAll('[data-w205df]').forEach(function (el) { var n = el.getAttribute('data-w205df'); patch[n] = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : el.value; });
    if (!archive && !String(patch.title || '').trim()) { toastS('A document needs a title'); return; }
    rpc('strategy_doc_save', { p_id: st.id ? Number(st.id) : null, p_patch: patch }).then(function (row) {
      S.docs = S.docs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(row && row.id); });
      if (row && !row.archived_at) S.docs.push(row);
      S.docs.sort(function (a, b) { return a.sort - b.sort; });
      closeDrawer(); paint(); toastS(archive ? 'Taken out of the library' : 'Saved');
    }).catch(function (e) { toastS('Not saved: ' + errText(e)); });
  }
  function togglePin(id) {
    var d = byId(S.docs, id); if (!d) return;
    rpc('strategy_doc_save', { p_id: Number(id), p_patch: { pinned: !d.pinned } }).then(function (row) { if (row) Object.assign(d, row); paint(); toastS(d.pinned ? 'Pinned for everyone' : 'Unpinned'); }).catch(function (e) { toastS('Not changed: ' + errText(e)); });
  }

  /* ---------- the Strategy Library ---------- */
  function docHtml(d, compact) {
    var admin = isAdmin();
    var gl = (d.goals || []).filter(Boolean).map(function (n) { var g = S.goals.filter(function (x) { return String(x.number) === String(n); })[0]; return g ? '<a class="w205tag g" href="#orggoals/goal/' + g.id + '" data-w205go="#orggoals/goal/' + g.id + '">Goal ' + E(n) + '</a>' : ''; }).join(' ');
    return '<article class="w205doc' + (d.pinned ? '' : ' unpinned') + '" data-w205doc="' + d.id + '"><div class="hd"><span class="ic">' + I(d.url ? 'doc' : 'archive') + '</span><div style="flex:1;min-width:0">' +
      '<h4 data-w198skip>' + (d.url ? '<a href="' + E(d.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(d.title) + '</a>' : E(d.title)) + '</h4>' +
      '<div class="meta">' + E(d.group_label || '') + (d.doc_date ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(d.doc_date) : '') + (d.url ? '' : ' \u00b7 no link yet') + '</div></div>' +
      (admin ? '<button type="button" class="w205pinb' + (d.pinned ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w205pin="' + d.id + '" aria-pressed="' + (d.pinned ? 'true' : 'false') + '" aria-label="' + (d.pinned ? 'Unpin' : 'Pin') + ' this document" title="' + (d.pinned ? 'Pinned for everyone: unpin' : 'Pin it for everyone') + '">' + I('star') + '</button>' : (d.pinned ? '<span class="w205pinb on" title="Pinned">' + I('star') + '</span>' : '')) + '</div>' +
      (d.direction ? '<div class="dir" data-w198skip>' + E(compact ? clip(d.direction, 150) : d.direction) + '</div>' : '') +
      (d.status_note && !compact ? '<div class="st" data-w198skip>' + E(d.status_note) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="ft">' + gl + '<span style="flex:1"></span>' + (d.url ? '<a class="w205btn sm ghost" href="' + E(d.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + I('ext') + ' Open</a>' : '') +
      (admin && !compact ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm ghost" data-w205docedit="' + d.id + '">Edit</button>' : '') + '</div></article>';
  }
  function libraryViewHtml() {
    var qv = lower(S.libQ), admin = isAdmin();
    var list = S.docs.filter(function (d) {
      if (!qv) return true;
      return lower([d.title, d.direction, d.status_note, d.group_label, d.doc_date].join(' ')).indexOf(qv) >= 0;
    });
    var shelves = []; list.forEach(function (d) { var s = d.group_label || 'Other'; if (shelves.indexOf(s) < 0) shelves.push(s); });
    var order = ['Our plans', 'Consulting work', 'Sources']; shelves.sort(function (a, b) { var ia = order.indexOf(a), ib = order.indexOf(b); return (ia < 0 ? 9 : ia) - (ib < 0 ? 9 : ib) || a.localeCompare(b); });
    var pinnedN = S.docs.filter(function (d) { return d.pinned; }).length;
    var html = mastHtml('Strategy Library', 'The documents that set Welfare Avenue\u2019s direction: what each one says and where it stands. Pinned documents lead the Goals page.') +
      '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 6px"><label class="w205search">' + I('search') + '<input type="search" data-w205libq value="' + E(S.libQ) + '" placeholder="Search the library" aria-label="Search the Strategy Library" /></label>' +
      '<span style="font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + '">' + plural(S.docs.length, 'document') + ', ' + pinnedN + ' pinned</span><span style="flex:1"></span>' +
      (admin ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn pri" data-w205docadd>' + I('plus') + ' Add a document</button>' : '') +
      '<button type="button" class="w205btn" data-w205go="#orggoals">Back to the goals</button></div>';
    if (!list.length) {
      html += '<div class="w205empty" style="margin-top:16px"><b>' + (qv ? 'Nothing matches' : 'The library is empty') + '</b>' + (qv ? 'Try another word, or clear the search.' : (admin ? 'Add the first document.' : 'Admins add the documents that set our direction.')) + '</div>';
      return html;
    }
    shelves.forEach(function (s) {
      var ds = list.filter(function (d) { return (d.group_label || 'Other') === s; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.pinned ? 1 : 0) - (a.pinned ? 1 : 0) || (a.sort - b.sort); });
      html += '<div class="w205shelf">' + E(s) + ' <small>' + ds.length + '</small></div><div class="w205lib">' + ds.map(function (d) { return docHtml(d, false); }).join('') + '</div>';
    });
    if (S.plan.source && S.plan.source.title) html += '<p style="font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:20px 0 0">Seeded from "What our own plans already say" and the sources of the draft "' + E(S.plan.source.title) + '".</p>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---------- the retired goals ---------- */
  function retiredViewHtml() {
    var oldOrg = S.retired.filter(function (g) { return g.department === 'org'; });
    var dept = S.retired.filter(function (g) { return g.department !== 'org'; });
    var lead = isLeader();
    function row(g) {
      var to = g.maps_to_goal_id ? byId(S.goals, g.maps_to_goal_id) : null;
      var proj = g.moved_to_project_id ? byId(S.projects, g.moved_to_project_id) : null;
      var wins = S.wins[g.id] || 0, cm = (S.comments || []).filter(function (c) { return String(c.goal_id) === String(g.id); }).length;
      return '<div class="it"><i style="width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:' + E(g.department === 'org' ? GOLD : deptColor(g.department)) + ';flex:none"></i><div class="t"><b data-w198skip>' + E(g.title) + '</b>' +
        '<small>' + E(g.department === 'org' ? 'Organizational goal' : deptLabel(g.department)) + (g.quarter ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(g.quarter) : '') + (g.status_before_retire ? ' \u00b7 was ' + E(ST_LABEL[g.status_before_retire] || g.status_before_retire) : '') +
        (wins ? ' \u00b7 ' + plural(wins, 'win') : '') + (cm ? ' \u00b7 ' + plural(cm, 'comment') : '') + '</small>' +
        (g.retired_note ? '<small data-w198skip>' + E(g.retired_note) + '</small>' : '') + '</div>' +
        (to ? '<a class="w205tag g" href="#orggoals/goal/' + to.id + '" data-w205go="#orggoals/goal/' + to.id + '">Goal ' + E(to.number) + '</a>' : '<span class="w205tag">No obvious fit</span>') +
        (g.department !== 'org' ? (proj ? '<a class="w205btn sm ghost" href="#project/' + proj.id + '">Now a project</a>' : g.moved_to_project_id ? '<span class="w205tag p">Now a project</span>' : (lead ? '<button type="button" class="w205btn sm" data-w205toproj="' + g.id + '" title="Once only: a project in ' + E(deptLabel(g.department)) + (to ? ', linked to goal ' + E(to.number) : '') + '">Make it a project</button>' : '')) : '') + '</div>';
    }
    var groups = {}; dept.forEach(function (g) { var k = g.maps_to_goal_id && byId(S.goals, g.maps_to_goal_id) ? String(g.maps_to_goal_id) : 'none'; (groups[k] = groups[k] || []).push(g); });
    var html = mastHtml('Retired goals', 'Department goals are retired as a tier: departments own projects, and projects serve the five goals. Nothing was removed: each goal keeps its wins, comments and history.') +
      '<div style="margin:0 0 14px"><button type="button" class="w205btn" data-w205go="#orggoals">Back to the goals</button></div>';
    if (!S.retired.length) return html + '<div class="w205empty"><b>No retired goals</b>Goals retired later show here.</div>';
    if (oldOrg.length) html += '<div class="w205shelf">The earlier organizational goals <small>' + oldOrg.length + '</small></div><div class="w205list">' + oldOrg.map(row).join('') + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w205shelf">Department goals <small>' + dept.length + '</small></div>' +
      '<p style="font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:-4px 0 10px">Grouped by the goal each one served. ' + (lead ? 'Make it a project turns one into a project in the same team, linked to that goal (once per goal).' : 'Leaders and admins can turn one into a project.') + '</p>';
    S.goals.concat([{ id: 'none' }]).forEach(function (g) {
      var list = groups[String(g.id)]; if (!list || !list.length) return;
      html += '<div class="w205grp">' + (g.id === 'none' ? 'No obvious fit with one of the five goals' : 'Served goal ' + E(g.number) + ': <span data-w198skip>' + E(g.title) + '</span>') + '</div><div class="w205list" style="margin-bottom:10px">' + list.map(row).join('') + '</div>';
    });
    return html;
  }

  /* =====================================================================
     wave207 (04/10/26): the charitable activities and this year's goals, inside wave205's page.
     Steel: "Each org goal is anchored on one or more of WA's 8 registered charitable activities": chips on each goal (the
     reason on hover or tap), a column in the goals at a glance, and the 8 activities coverage strip; owners and admins
     anchor a goal on an activity, take one off and say why (strategy_activity_set, strategy_save 'activity' reason).

     (strategy_checkpoint_comment_add) and history; the This year view at the top of Goals: the current quarter, what is due
     next, overdue checkpoints flagged. Projects link to a checkpoint (strategy_link_checkpoint) as well as a goal.
     The words are the plan's (wa207_activities_fy27.sql); nothing here invents a target.
     ===================================================================== */

      + '#w205 select.w207st{appearance:auto;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);padding:3px 6px;background:#FFFAF2;color:' + GREEN + ';max-width:100%;}'
      + '#w205 select.w207st.s-onTrack,#w205 select.w207st.s-done{background:rgba(42,140,122,.12);border-color:rgba(42,140,122,.4);color:#1d6b5d;}'
      + '#w205 select.w207st.s-atRisk{background:rgba(179,64,47,.1);border-color:rgba(179,64,47,.4);color:' + FLAG + ';}'
      + '.w207cp .ft{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:11.5px;color:' + MUTED + ';}'
      + '.w207cp input.w207due{font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:11.5px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.15);border-radius:8px;padding:2px 5px;background:#FFFAF2;color:' + GROUND + ';max-width:140px;}'
      + '.w207cp input.w207due[readonly]{border-color:transparent;background:transparent;padding-left:0;}'
      + '.w207late{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:700;}'
      /* the activities drawer */
      + '.w207pick label{align-items:flex-start;}'
      + '.w207pick .w205ed{background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.15);border-radius:8px;margin-top:6px;font-size:13px;}'
      + '@media (max-width:900px){.w207cov{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;}.w207cps{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;}.w207alerts{grid-template-columns:1fr;}}'
      + '@media (max-width:640px){'
      + '.w207year{padding:16px 14px 14px;border-radius:16px;}'
      + '.w207qs{margin-left:0;width:100%;display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:4px;}'
      + '.w207q{min-width:0;padding:5px 6px;}'
      + '.w207q b{font-size:15px;}'
      + '.w207q small{font-size:10px;}'
      + '.w207q.now em{display:none;}'
      + '.w207grid table,.w207grid tbody,.w207grid tr,.w207grid td{display:block;width:100%;}'
      + '.w207grid thead{display:none;}'
      + '.w207grid tr{border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);padding:8px 0;}'
      + '.w207grid td{border:0;padding:3px 12px;}'
      + '.w207grid td[data-l]:before{content:attr(data-l) ": ";font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;color:' + MUTED + ';text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.8px;}'
      + '.w207grid td.now{background:rgba(220,165,39,.12);}'
      + '.w207cov{grid-template-columns:1fr;}'
      + '.w207yb .top{grid-template-columns:1fr;}'
      + '.w207cps{grid-template-columns:1fr;}'
      + '.w207chip{max-width:100%;}'
      + '}';
    document.head.appendChild(css7);
  }

  /* ---------- dates: the fiscal year starts October 1 ---------- */
  function todayStr() {
    if (window.__W207_TODAY) return String(window.__W207_TODAY);
    var d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  }
  function quarterOf(ds) {
    var p = String(ds || todayStr()).slice(0, 10).split('-'), y = Number(p[0]), m = Number(p[1]) - 1;
    var q = m >= 9 ? 1 : m <= 2 ? 2 : m <= 5 ? 3 : 4;
    return { fy: 'FY' + String(m >= 9 ? y + 1 : y).slice(2), q: q };
  }
  function daysTo(ds) { var a = new Date(todayStr() + 'T12:00:00'), b = new Date(String(ds).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00'); return Math.round((b - a) / 86400000); }
  function inDays(n) { return n === 0 ? 'today' : n === 1 ? 'tomorrow' : n > 1 ? 'in ' + n + ' days' : n === -1 ? 'yesterday' : (-n) + ' days ago'; }
  var Q_SPAN = { 1: 'Oct to Dec', 2: 'Jan to Mar', 3: 'Apr to Jun', 4: 'Jul to Sep' };

  /* ---------- the rows ---------- */
  function targetOf(gid, fy) { return S.targets.filter(function (t) { return String(t.goal_id) === String(gid) && t.fy === fy; })[0] || null; }
  function cpsOfTarget(tid) { return S.checkpoints.filter(function (c) { return String(c.target_id) === String(tid); }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.quarter - b.quarter; }); }
  function cpsOfGoal(gid) {
    return S.checkpoints.filter(function (c) { return String(c.goal_id) === String(gid); }).sort(function (a, b) {
      var ta = byId(S.targets, a.target_id) || {}, tb = byId(S.targets, b.target_id) || {};
      return String(ta.fy).localeCompare(String(tb.fy)) || a.quarter - b.quarter;
    });
  }
  function cpFy(c) { var t = c ? byId(S.targets, c.target_id) : null; return t ? t.fy : ''; }
  function cpLabel(c) { return c ? (cpFy(c) + ' Q' + c.quarter) : 'A checkpoint'; }
  function isLate(c) { return !!c && c.status !== 'done' && !!c.due_date && String(c.due_date).slice(0, 10) < todayStr(); }
  function yearFy() {
    var now = quarterOf().fy;
    if (S.targets.some(function (t) { return t.fy === now; })) return now;
    var fys = S.targets.map(function (t) { return t.fy; }).sort();
    return fys.length ? fys[fys.length - 1] : now;
  }
  function yearState() {
    var fy = yearFy(), tq = quarterOf(), today = todayStr();
    var cps = S.checkpoints.filter(function (c) { return cpFy(c) === fy && byId(S.goals, c.goal_id); });
    var open = cps.filter(function (c) { return c.status !== 'done' && String(c.due_date).slice(0, 10) >= today; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.due_date).localeCompare(String(b.due_date)); });
    var nextDue = open.length ? String(open[0].due_date).slice(0, 10) : null;
    var late = S.checkpoints.filter(function (c) { return isLate(c) && byId(S.goals, c.goal_id); }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.due_date).localeCompare(String(b.due_date)); });
    return { fy: fy, today: today, quarter: tq.fy === fy ? tq.q : null, nextDue: nextDue, next: open.filter(function (c) { return String(c.due_date).slice(0, 10) === nextDue; }), overdue: late, total: cps.length, done: cps.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'done'; }).length };
  }
  function goalNum(gid) { var g = byId(S.goals, gid); return g ? g.number : ''; }
  function pill(c) { return '<span class="w207pill s-' + stKey(c.status) + (isLate(c) ? ' late' : '') + '">' + E(isLate(c) ? 'Overdue' : (ST_LABEL[c.status] || c.status)) + '</span>'; }

  /* ---------- the This year view, at the top of Goals ---------- */
  function yearViewHtml() {
    if (!S.targets.length) return '<div data-w207year></div>';
    var Y = yearState(), plan = S.plan['year_' + Y.fy.toLowerCase()] || {};
    var cps0 = S.checkpoints.filter(function (c) { return cpFy(c) === Y.fy; });
    var qDue = {}; cps0.forEach(function (c) { if (!qDue[c.quarter] || String(c.due_date) > qDue[c.quarter]) qDue[c.quarter] = String(c.due_date).slice(0, 10); });
    var qs = [1, 2, 3, 4].map(function (q) {
      var past = Y.quarter != null ? q < Y.quarter : (qDue[q] && qDue[q] < Y.today);
      return '<div class="w207q' + (Y.quarter === q ? ' now' : '') + (past ? ' past' : '') + '"><b>Q' + q + (Y.quarter === q ? '<em>Now</em>' : '') + '</b><small>' + E(Q_SPAN[q]) + (qDue[q] ? ' \u00b7 due ' + E(fmtDay(qDue[q]).replace(/, \d{4}$/, '')) : '') + '</small></div>';
    }).join('');
    var item = function (c) {
      return '<div class="w207it" data-w207goto="' + c.goal_id + '" role="link" tabindex="0"><span class="w205num">' + E(goalNum(c.goal_id)) + '</span><span class="tx" data-w198skip>' + E(c.body) + '</span>' + pill(c) + '</div>';
    };
    var lateBox = Y.overdue.length
      ? '<div class="w207box late" data-w207overdue><h3>' + I('flag') + ' Overdue (' + Y.overdue.length + ')</h3><p class="when">Past their due date and not done.</p>' + Y.overdue.map(function (c) { return item(c).replace('<span class="tx"', '<span class="tx" title="' + E(cpLabel(c) + ', due ' + fmtDay(c.due_date)) + '"'); }).join('') + '</div>'
      : '<div class="w207box ok" data-w207overdue><h3>' + I('check') + ' Nothing overdue</h3><p class="when">Every checkpoint that has come due is done.</p></div>';
    var nextBox = Y.nextDue
      ? '<div class="w207box" data-w207next><h3>' + I('clock') + ' Due next</h3><p class="when">' + E(fmtDay(Y.nextDue)) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(inDays(daysTo(Y.nextDue))) + '</p>' + Y.next.map(item).join('') + '</div>'
      : '<div class="w207box ok" data-w207next><h3>' + I('check') + ' Nothing left to come due</h3><p class="when">' + E(Y.fy) + '\u2019s checkpoints are done or past.</p></div>';
    var rows = S.goals.map(function (g) {
      var t = targetOf(g.id, Y.fy); if (!t) return '';
      var cps = cpsOfTarget(t.id);
      return '<tr data-w207goto="' + g.id + '" tabindex="0" role="link" aria-label="Open goal ' + E(g.number) + '">' +
        '<td><span style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center"><span class="w205num">' + E(g.number) + '</span><b data-w198skip>' + E(g.title) + '</b></span></td>' +
        '<td data-l="' + E(Y.fy) + ' target" class="tg" data-w198skip>' + E(t.body) + '</td>' +
        [1, 2, 3, 4].map(function (q) {
          var c = cps.filter(function (x) { return x.quarter === q; })[0];
          return '<td data-l="Q' + q + '" class="' + (Y.quarter === q ? 'now' : '') + (c && isLate(c) ? ' late' : '') + '">' + (c ? pill(c) + '<div data-w198skip>' + E(c.body) + '</div>' : '') + '</td>';
        }).join('') +
        '<td data-l="Owners" data-w198skip>' + E(t.owners || '') + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('');
    return '<section class="w207year" data-w207year aria-label="This year">' +
      '<div class="hd"><div><div class="lab">This year</div><h2>' + E(Y.fy) + ' goals</h2><p class="sub">' + E(plan.title || ('This year\u2019s goals: ' + Y.fy)) + '</p></div><div class="w207qs" aria-label="Quarters">' + qs + '</div></div>' +
      (plan.intro ? '<p class="sub" style="margin-top:10px">' + E(plan.intro) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="w207alerts">' + nextBox + lateBox + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w207grid"><table><colgroup><col style="width:17%"><col style="width:17%"><col><col><col><col><col style="width:10%"></colgroup><thead><tr><th>Goal</th><th>' + E(Y.fy) + ' target</th>' + [1, 2, 3, 4].map(function (q) { return '<th class="' + (Y.quarter === q ? 'now' : '') + '">Q' + q + (qDue[q] ? ' (by ' + E(fmtDay(qDue[q]).replace(/, \d{4}$/, '')) + ')' : '') + '</th>'; }).join('') + '<th>Owners</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + rows + '</tbody></table></div>' +
      '<p class="note">' + E(Y.done + ' of ' + Y.total + ' checkpoints done. ') + (plan.note ? E(plan.note) : '') + '</p></section>';
  }
  function paintYear() {
    var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w207year]'); if (!el) return;
    var t = document.createElement('div'); t.innerHTML = yearViewHtml(); var n = t.firstElementChild; if (n) el.replaceWith(n);
  }
  function goYear(gid) { go('#orggoals/goal/' + gid); setTimeout(function () { var b = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w207yb="' + gid + '"]'); if (b) try { b.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 500); }

  /* ---------- a goal's year block: the target, who reports on it, the four checkpoints ---------- */
  function cpCmtBtn(c) {
    var n = S.comments.filter(function (x) { return String(x.checkpoint_id) === String(c.id); }).length;
    return '<button type="button" class="w205cnt' + (n ? ' has' : '') + '" data-w207cpcmts="' + c.id + '" aria-label="Comments on this checkpoint (' + n + ')">' + I('chat') + (n ? n : '') + '</button>';
  }
  function cpHtml(c, g, Y) {
    var can = canEdit(g), late = isLate(c), now = Y.quarter === c.quarter && cpFy(c) === Y.fy;
    var k = key('checkpoint', c.id, 'status'), kd = key('checkpoint', c.id, 'due_date');
    return '<div class="w207cp' + (now ? ' now' : '') + (late ? ' late' : '') + (c.status === 'done' ? ' done' : '') + '" data-w207cp="' + c.id + '">' +
      '<div class="qh"><b>Q' + c.quarter + '</b>' + (now ? '<em>Now</em>' : '') + '<span class="sp" style="flex:1"></span>' +
      '<select class="w207st s-' + stKey(c.status) + '" data-w205k="' + E(k) + '" aria-label="Q' + c.quarter + ' checkpoint status"' + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' +
      CP_ST.map(function (s0) { return '<option value="' + E(s0) + '"' + (s0 === c.status ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(ST_LABEL[s0] || s0) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>' +
      ed('checkpoint', c, 'body', { label: 'Q' + c.quarter + ' checkpoint', ph: 'Write the checkpoint' }) +
      '<div class="ft"><span>Due</span><input type="date" class="w207due" data-w205k="' + E(kd) + '" value="' + E(String(c.due_date || '').slice(0, 10)) + '" aria-label="Q' + c.quarter + ' due date"' + (can ? '' : ' readonly tabindex="-1"') + ' />' +
      (late ? '<span class="w207late">Overdue</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w205msft" style="margin:0" data-w207cpft="' + c.id + '">' + cpFootHtml(c) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function yearBlockHtml(g) {
    var Y = yearState(), t = targetOf(g.id, Y.fy); if (!t) return '';
    return '<div class="w205h4"><h4>This year (' + E(Y.fy) + ')</h4><span style="font-size:12px;color:' + MUTED + '">The yearly target and its quarterly checkpoints</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w207yb" data-w207yb="' + g.id + '"><div class="top"><div><div class="w205lab">' + E(Y.fy) + ' target <span class="who" data-w205who="' + E(key('target', t.id, 'body')) + '" hidden></span></div>' + ed('target', t, 'body', { label: Y.fy + ' target', ph: 'Write the target' }) + '</div>' +
      '<div><div class="w205lab">Owners who report on it <span class="who" data-w205who="' + E(key('target', t.id, 'owners')) + '" hidden></span></div>' + ed('target', t, 'owners', { label: Y.fy + ' owners', ph: 'Who reports on it' }) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w207cps">' + cpsOfTarget(t.id).map(function (c) { return cpHtml(c, g, Y); }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function afterYearSave(P) {
    if (P.e !== 'checkpoint') return;
    var c = byId(S.checkpoints, P.id), box = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w207cp="' + P.id + '"]'); if (!c || !box) return;
    var Y = yearState();
    box.classList.toggle('done', c.status === 'done'); box.classList.toggle('late', isLate(c));
    var sel = box.querySelector('select.w207st'); if (sel) sel.className = 'w207st s-' + stKey(c.status);
    var ft = box.querySelector('.ft'), lt = ft && ft.querySelector('.w207late');
    if (isLate(c) && ft && !lt) { var s0 = document.createElement('span'); s0.className = 'w207late'; s0.textContent = 'Overdue'; ft.appendChild(s0); }
    if (!isLate(c) && lt) lt.remove();
    void Y;
  }
  function cpFootHtml(c) {
    var ps = S.projects.filter(function (p) { return String(p.checkpoint_id) === String(c.id); });
    return ps.map(projChip).join('') + cpCmtBtn(c) + (canComment() ? '<button type="button" class="w205cnt" data-w207cplink="' + c.id + '" aria-label="Link a project to this checkpoint">' + I('link') + '</button>' : '');
  }
  function paintCpFoot(gid) { cpsOfGoal(gid).forEach(function (c) { var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w207cpft="' + c.id + '"]'); if (el) el.innerHTML = cpFootHtml(c); }); }
  function paintYearCounts(gid) {
    cpsOfGoal(gid).forEach(function (c) { var b = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w207cpcmts="' + c.id + '"]'); if (b) b.outerHTML = cpCmtBtn(c); });
  }

  /* ---------- the charitable activities ---------- */
  function actOf(id) { return byId(S.acts, id); }
  function gactsOf(gid) { return S.gacts.filter(function (a) { return String(a.goal_id) === String(gid); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.sort - b.sort) || (a.activity_id - b.activity_id); }); }
  function actsMini(gid) { return '<span class="w207mini">' + gactsOf(gid).map(function (a) { var x = actOf(a.activity_id) || {}; return '<i title="' + E(a.activity_id + '. ' + (x.title || '') + (a.reason ? ' (' + a.reason + ')' : '')) + '">' + E(a.activity_id) + '</i>'; }).join('') + '</span>'; }
  function actsHtml(g) {
    var list = gactsOf(g.id), can = canEdit(g);
    var note = can ? '<div class="w207note">' + ed('goal', g, 'activities_note', { label: 'Note on the charitable activities', ph: 'A note on the activities (optional)' }) + '</div>'
      : (g.activities_note ? '<div class="w207note" data-w198skip>' + E(g.activities_note) + '</div>' : '');
    return '<span class="lab">Charitable activities</span>' + list.map(function (a) {
      var x = actOf(a.activity_id) || { title: '' };
      return '<button type="button" class="w207chip" data-w207chip="' + g.id + ':' + a.activity_id + '" aria-expanded="false" title="' + E(a.activity_id + '. ' + x.title + (a.reason ? ': ' + a.reason : '')) + '"><b>' + E(a.activity_id) + '</b><span data-w198skip>' + E(x.title) + '</span></button>';
    }).join('') + (list.length ? '' : '<span style="font-size:12.5px;color:' + FLAG + '">Not anchored on an activity yet.</span>') +
      (can ? '<button type="button" class="w205chip add" data-w207manage="' + g.id + '">' + I('plus') + ' Activities</button>' : '') + note;
  }
  function paintActs(gid) {
    var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w207acts="' + gid + '"]'), g = byId(S.goals, gid); if (!el || !g) return;
    var nk = key('goal', g.id, 'activities_note'), cur = fieldEl(nk), keep = cur && (S.dirty[nk] || document.activeElement === cur) ? cur.value : null, foc = cur && document.activeElement === cur;
    el.innerHTML = actsHtml(g);
    var n = fieldEl(nk); if (n) { if (keep != null) n.value = keep; grow(n); if (foc) n.focus(); }
  }
  function chipTip(el) {
    var p = el.getAttribute('data-w207chip').split(':'), box = el.parentNode, open = el.getAttribute('aria-expanded') === 'true';
    box.querySelectorAll('.w207tip').forEach(function (t) { t.remove(); });
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-w207chip]').forEach(function (c) { c.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); });
    if (open) return;
    var a = S.gacts.filter(function (x) { return String(x.goal_id) === p[0] && String(x.activity_id) === p[1]; })[0], x = actOf(p[1]) || {};
    var tip = document.createElement('div'); tip.className = 'w207tip'; tip.setAttribute('role', 'note');
    tip.innerHTML = '<b>' + E(p[1] + '. ' + (x.title || '')) + '</b><br>' + (a && a.reason ? 'Why this goal serves it: <span data-w198skip>' + E(a.reason) + '</span>' : 'No reason written yet.');
    el.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    var note = box.querySelector('.w207note'); if (note) box.insertBefore(tip, note); else box.appendChild(tip);
  }
  function coverage() { return S.acts.map(function (a) { return { act: a, goals: S.goals.filter(function (g) { return gactsOf(g.id).some(function (x) { return String(x.activity_id) === String(a.id); }); }) }; }); }
  function coverageHtml() {
    if (!S.acts.length) return '<div data-w207cov></div>';
    var cov = coverage(), n = cov.filter(function (c) { return c.goals.length; }).length, plan = S.plan.activities || {};
    return '<div data-w207cov><div class="w205sec"><h2>' + E(S.acts.length) + ' activities</h2><span class="hint">' + E(plan.intro || 'Every goal serves at least one of the charitable activities registered with the CRA.') + '</span><span class="sp"></span>' +
      '<span class="w207covsum' + (n < S.acts.length ? ' gap' : '') + '">' + I(n < S.acts.length ? 'flag' : 'check') + ' ' + (n === S.acts.length ? 'All ' + n + ' covered' : n + ' of ' + S.acts.length + ' covered') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="w207cov" aria-label="Charitable activities and the goals that serve them">' + cov.map(function (c) {
        return '<div class="w207tile' + (c.goals.length ? '' : ' gap') + '"><span class="n">' + E(c.act.id) + '</span><span class="t" data-w198skip>' + E(c.act.title) + '</span><div class="gs">' +
          (c.goals.length ? c.goals.map(function (g) { var a = gactsOf(g.id).filter(function (x) { return String(x.activity_id) === String(c.act.id); })[0]; return '<button type="button" data-w205jump="' + g.id + '" title="' + E('Goal ' + g.number + ': ' + g.title + (a && a.reason ? ' (' + a.reason + ')' : '')) + '" aria-label="Goal ' + E(g.number) + '"><span class="w205num">' + E(g.number) + '</span></button>'; }).join('') : 'No goal serves it yet') + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function paintCoverage() { var el = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w207cov]'); if (!el) return; var t = document.createElement('div'); t.innerHTML = coverageHtml(); var n = t.firstElementChild; if (n) el.replaceWith(n); }
  function actsDrawer(st) {
    var g = byId(S.goals, st.goal); if (!g) return drawerShell('Charitable activities', '', '');
    var can = canEdit(g), on = {}; gactsOf(g.id).forEach(function (a) { on[a.activity_id] = a; });
    var body = '<p style="font-size:13px;color:' + MUTED + ';margin:0 0 10px">' + E((S.plan.activities || {}).intro || '') + '</p><div class="w205pick w207pick">' + S.acts.map(function (x) {
      var a = on[x.id];
      return '<label class="' + (a ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" data-w207act="' + x.id + '"' + (a ? ' checked' : '') + (can ? '' : ' disabled') + ' /><span class="t"><span><b>' + E(x.id) + '.</b> ' + E(x.title) + '</span>' +
        (a ? '<small>Why this goal serves it</small>' + ed('activity', a, 'reason', { label: 'Why goal ' + g.number + ' serves activity ' + x.id, ph: 'A few words, e.g. free, healthy meals' }) : '') + '</span></label>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    return drawerShell('Charitable activities', '<span data-w198skip>Goal ' + E(g.number) + ': ' + E(g.title) + '</span>', body, '<small style="color:' + MUTED + '">Tick the activities this goal serves and say why in a few words. It saves as you go; every change is in the goal\u2019s history.</small>');
  }
  function actToggle(gid, aid, on, el) {
    if (el) el.disabled = true;
    rpc('strategy_activity_set', { p_goal: Number(gid), p_activity: Number(aid), p_on: !!on, p_reason: null }).then(function (row) {
      S.gacts = S.gacts.filter(function (a) { return !(String(a.goal_id) === String(gid) && String(a.activity_id) === String(aid)); });
      if (row && !row.archived_at) { S.gacts.push(row); baseRow('activity', row, ['reason']); }
      paintActs(gid); paintCoverage(); paintGlance(); if (S.drawer && S.drawer.kind === 'acts') paintDrawer();
      toastS(on ? 'Activity added to the goal' : 'Activity taken off. History has Restore.');
    }).catch(function (e) { if (el) { el.disabled = false; el.checked = !on; } toastS('Not changed: ' + errText(e)); });
  }

  /* the link sheet: a checkpoint of the chosen goal, optional */
  function cpPickHtml(gg, st) {
    var Y = yearState(), list = cpsOfGoal(gg.id); if (!list.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w205grp">The checkpoint (optional)</div><div class="w205pick"><label class="' + (!st.cpPick ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w207lc" data-w207pickc=""' + (!st.cpPick ? ' checked' : '') + ' /><span class="t">No checkpoint</span></label>' +
      list.map(function (c) {
        var sel = String(st.cpPick) === String(c.id);
        return '<label class="' + (sel ? 'on' : '') + '"><input type="radio" name="w207lc" data-w207pickc="' + c.id + '"' + (sel ? ' checked' : '') + ' /><span class="t"><span data-w198skip>' + E(c.body) + '</span><small>' + E(cpLabel(c)) + ' \u00b7 due ' + E(fmtDay(c.due_date)) + (Y.quarter === c.quarter && cpFy(c) === Y.fy ? ' \u00b7 this quarter' : '') + '</small></span></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- presence on the page ---------- */
  function presName(p) { var m = p.mid ? memberById(p.mid) : null; return (m && m.name) || p.name || 'Someone'; }
  function presMember(p) { return (p.mid && memberById(p.mid)) || { name: presName(p) }; }
  function paintPresence() {
    var top = document.querySelector('#w205 [data-w205pres]');
    var people = S.presence;
    if (top) {
      top.innerHTML = '<span class="w205live' + (S.live ? '' : ' off') + '" title="' + (S.live ? 'Live: changes arrive as people make them' : 'Not live: changes show when you reload') + '"></span>' +
        (people.length ? '<span>Here now</span><span class="avs">' + people.slice(0, 8).map(function (p) {
          var g = p.goal ? byId(S.goals, p.goal) : null, m = presMember(p);
          return '<span title="' + E(presName(p) + (g ? ', in goal ' + g.number : '')) + '">' + av(m, 26) + '</span>';
        }).join('') + '</span>' + (people.length > 8 ? '<span>+' + (people.length - 8) + '</span>' : '') : '<span>' + (S.live ? 'Just you here' : 'Offline view') + '</span>');
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('#w205 [data-w205gpres]').forEach(function (el) {
      var gid = el.getAttribute('data-w205gpres'), here = people.filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal) === String(gid); });
      el.innerHTML = here.length ? '<span class="avs">' + here.map(function (p) { var m = presMember(p); return '<span title="' + E(presName(p)) + ' is in this goal">' + av(m, 24) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</span>' : '';
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#w205 [data-w205who]').forEach(function (el) {
      var k = el.getAttribute('data-w205who'), who = people.filter(function (p) { return p.field === k; }).map(presName);
      if (who.length) { el.hidden = false; el.textContent = who.join(', ') + ' editing'; } else el.hidden = true;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#w205 .w205ed.other').forEach(function (el) { el.classList.remove('other'); el.removeAttribute('data-w205by'); });
    people.forEach(function (p) { if (!p.field) return; var el = fieldEl(p.field); if (el) { el.classList.add('other'); el.title = presName(p) + ' is editing this'; } });
  }

  /* the boxes fit their words: again once the fonts are in and when the width changes */
  function growAll() { document.querySelectorAll('#w205 textarea.w205ed').forEach(grow); }
  function growSoon() { setTimeout(growAll, 30); setTimeout(growAll, 400); try { if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(growAll); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  var rzT = null; window.addEventListener('resize', function () { clearTimeout(rzT); rzT = setTimeout(function () { if (onPage()) growAll(); }, 150); });
  /* ---------- paint and route ---------- */
  function paint() {
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    var act = document.activeElement, actKey = act && h.contains(act) ? act.getAttribute('data-w205k') : null, sel = null;
    if (actKey && act.selectionStart != null) sel = [act.selectionStart, act.selectionEnd];
    var keep = {}; h.querySelectorAll('[data-w205k]').forEach(function (el) { var k = el.getAttribute('data-w205k'); if (S.dirty[k] || S.conflict[k]) keep[k] = el.value; });
    var libFocus = act && act.hasAttribute && act.hasAttribute('data-w205libq');
    var html;
    if (!staffOk() && !boardMode()) html = mastHtml('Goals', '') + '<div class="w205empty"><b>Sign in to read the goals</b>The Strategy app is for Welfare Avenue accounts and the board.</div>';
    else if (S.view === 'library') html = libraryViewHtml();
    else if (S.view === 'retired') html = retiredViewHtml();
    else html = goalsViewHtml();
    h.innerHTML = html;
    Object.keys(keep).forEach(function (k) { var el = fieldEl(k); if (el) el.value = keep[k]; if (S.conflict[k]) paintConflict(k); });
    h.querySelectorAll('textarea.w205ed').forEach(grow);
    Object.keys(S.errors).forEach(paintFieldError);
    S.goals.forEach(function (g) { savedState(key('goal', g.id, 'title')); });
    if (actKey) { var n = fieldEl(actKey); if (n) { n.focus(); if (sel) try { n.setSelectionRange(sel[0], sel[1]); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    if (libFocus) { var li = h.querySelector('[data-w205libq]'); if (li) { li.focus(); li.setSelectionRange(li.value.length, li.value.length); } }
    paintPresence(); growSoon();
    try { document.title = (S.view === 'library' ? 'Strategy Library' : S.view === 'retired' ? 'Retired goals' : 'Goals') + ' \u00b7 Welfare Avenue Hub'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    S.lastNoEdit = S.goals.filter(function (x) { return !canEdit(x); }).length;
  }
  function route(hh) {
    var p = String(hh || '').replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
    S.view = 'goals'; S.goalId = null; S.sub = ''; S.subId = null;
    if (p[1] === 'library') S.view = 'library';
    else if (p[1] === 'retired') S.view = 'retired';
    else if (p[1] === 'goal' && p[2]) { S.goalId = p[2]; S.sub = p[3] || ''; S.subId = p[4] || null; }
  }
  function show(hh) {
    route(hh);
    var h = host(); if (!h) return;
    if (!S.loaded) h.innerHTML = '<div class="w205empty" style="margin-top:20px"><b>Loading the goals</b>One moment.</div>';
    return loadAll().then(function () {
      paint();
      goLive(); trackSoon();
      if (S.goalId) {
        var card = document.getElementById('w205g-' + S.goalId);
        if (card) setTimeout(function () { try { card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (e) { card.scrollIntoView(); } }, 80);
        if (S.sub === 'comments') openDrawer({ kind: 'comments', goal: S.goalId, milestone: null, focusComment: S.subId });
        else if (S.sub === 'history') { openDrawer({ kind: 'history', goal: S.goalId }); loadHistory(); }
      } else window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    });
  }
  function go(h) { if (location.hash === h) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { show(h); } } else location.hash = h; }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  function inApp(t) { return !!(t && t.closest && (t.closest('#w205') || t.closest('#w205drawer') || t.closest('#w205scrim'))); }
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t)) return;
    var k = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w205k');
    if (k) {
      if (t.readOnly || t.disabled) return;
      grow(t); S.dirty[k] = true; delete S.errors[k];
      var er = t.parentNode.querySelector('.w205err[data-for="' + k + '"]'); if (er) er.remove();
      schedule(k, t.tagName === 'SELECT' ? 0 : SAVE_MS);
      return;
    }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w205libq')) { S.libQ = t.value; clearTimeout(S.libT); S.libT = setTimeout(paint, 160); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w205linkq') && S.drawer) { S.drawer.q = t.value; clearTimeout(S.dT); S.dT = setTimeout(function () { paintDrawer(); var i = document.querySelector('#w205drawer [data-w205linkq]'); if (i) { i.focus(); i.setSelectionRange(i.value.length, i.value.length); } }, 150); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w205ownq') && S.drawer) { S.drawer.q = t.value; clearTimeout(S.dT); S.dT = setTimeout(function () { paintDrawer(); var i = document.querySelector('#w205drawer [data-w205ownq]'); if (i) { i.focus(); i.setSelectionRange(i.value.length, i.value.length); } }, 150); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t)) return;
    var k = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w205k');
    if (k && t.tagName === 'SELECT') { t.className = t.className.replace(/\bs-\w+/, 's-' + stKey(t.value)); t.title = ST_LABEL[t.value] || t.value; return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w205pickp') && S.drawer) { S.drawer.project = t.getAttribute('data-w205pickp'); var p = byId(S.projects, S.drawer.project); S.drawer.goalPick = S.drawer.goal || (p && p.goal_id) || null; S.drawer.msPick = p && String(p.goal_id) === String(S.drawer.goalPick) ? p.milestone_id : (S.drawer.ms || null); S.drawer.cpPick = S.drawer.cp || (p && String(p.goal_id) === String(S.drawer.goalPick) ? p.checkpoint_id : null) || null; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w205pickg') && S.drawer) { S.drawer.goalPick = t.getAttribute('data-w205pickg'); S.drawer.msPick = null; S.drawer.cpPick = null; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w207pickc') && S.drawer) { S.drawer.cpPick = t.getAttribute('data-w207pickc') || null; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w207act') && S.drawer) { actToggle(S.drawer.goal, t.getAttribute('data-w207act'), t.checked, t); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w205pickm') && S.drawer) { S.drawer.msPick = t.getAttribute('data-w205pickm') || null; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w205focus') && S.drawer) { ownerSet(S.drawer.goal, t.getAttribute('data-w205focus'), true, null, t.value); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w205')) return;
    var card = t.closest('[data-w205goal]'), k = t.getAttribute('data-w205k');
    var g = card ? card.getAttribute('data-w205goal') : null;
    var f = k && !t.readOnly && !t.disabled ? k : null;
    if (g !== S.focus.goal || f !== S.focus.field) { S.focus = { goal: g, field: f }; trackSoon(); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w205')) return;
    var k = t.getAttribute('data-w205k');
    if (k && S.dirty[k] && t.tagName !== 'SELECT') { clearTimeout(S.timers[k]); save(k); }
    setTimeout(function () { var a = document.activeElement; if (!a || !a.closest || !a.closest('#w205')) { if (S.focus.field || S.focus.goal) { S.focus = { goal: null, field: null }; trackSoon(); } } }, 50);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && S.drawer) { e.preventDefault(); closeDrawer(); return; }
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t)) return;
    if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'Enter') {
      if (t.matches('textarea[data-w205cmt="new"]')) { e.preventDefault(); postComment(null); return; }
      if (t.matches('textarea[data-w205reply]')) { e.preventDefault(); postComment(t.getAttribute('data-w205reply')); return; }
      if (t.matches('textarea[data-w205edit]')) { e.preventDefault(); saveEdit(t.getAttribute('data-w205edit')); return; }
    }
    /* a title and a measure are one line: Enter saves and leaves, like a heading in a document */
    var k = t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w205k');
    if (k && e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && /:(title|measure|baseline|target_fy29|target_fy31)$/.test(k)) { e.preventDefault(); t.blur(); return; }
    if (k && e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey && /^(target|activity):.*:(owners|reason)$/.test(k)) { e.preventDefault(); t.blur(); return; }
    if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && t.matches('[data-w205jump],label[data-w205ownon],[data-w207goto]')) { e.preventDefault(); t.click(); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!inApp(t)) return;
    var el;
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205close]'))) { closeDrawer(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205go]'))) { e.preventDefault(); go(el.getAttribute('data-w205go')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205jump]'))) { go('#orggoals/goal/' + el.getAttribute('data-w205jump')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205keep]'))) { resolveConflict(el.getAttribute('data-w205keep'), true); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205theirs]'))) { resolveConflict(el.getAttribute('data-w205theirs'), false); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205fold]'))) {
      var gid = el.getAttribute('data-w205fold'), card = document.getElementById('w205g-' + gid);
      var f = !card.classList.contains('folded'); card.classList.toggle('folded', f); el.setAttribute('aria-expanded', f ? 'false' : 'true');
      if (f) S.folded[gid] = 1; else delete S.folded[gid]; lsSet('w205folded', JSON.stringify(S.folded));
      if (!f) card.querySelectorAll('textarea.w205ed').forEach(grow);
      return;
    }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205cmts]'))) { var p = el.getAttribute('data-w205cmts').split(':'); openDrawer({ kind: 'comments', goal: p[0], milestone: p[1] || null }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205cseg]'))) { S.drawer.milestone = el.getAttribute('data-w205cseg') || null; S.drawer.checkpoint = null; S.drawer.replyTo = null; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w207cseg]'))) { S.drawer.checkpoint = el.getAttribute('data-w207cseg') || null; S.drawer.milestone = null; S.drawer.replyTo = null; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w207cpcmts]'))) { var cpc = byId(S.checkpoints, el.getAttribute('data-w207cpcmts')); if (cpc) openDrawer({ kind: 'comments', goal: cpc.goal_id, milestone: null, checkpoint: cpc.id }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w207cplink]'))) { var cpl = byId(S.checkpoints, el.getAttribute('data-w207cplink')); if (cpl) openDrawer({ kind: 'link', goal: cpl.goal_id, ms: null, msPick: null, cp: cpl.id, cpPick: cpl.id, q: '' }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w207manage]'))) { openDrawer({ kind: 'acts', goal: el.getAttribute('data-w207manage') }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w207chip]'))) { chipTip(el); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w207goto]'))) { e.preventDefault(); goYear(el.getAttribute('data-w207goto')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205replyto]'))) { S.drawer.replyTo = el.getAttribute('data-w205replyto') || null; paintDrawer(); var rt = document.querySelector('#w205drawer textarea[data-w205reply]'); if (rt) rt.focus(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205sendreply]'))) { postComment(el.getAttribute('data-w205sendreply')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205post]'))) { postComment(null); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205editc]'))) { S.drawer.editing = el.getAttribute('data-w205editc'); paintDrawer(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205editcancel]'))) { S.drawer.editing = null; paintDrawer(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205editsave]'))) { saveEdit(el.getAttribute('data-w205editsave')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205hist]'))) { openDrawer({ kind: 'history', goal: el.getAttribute('data-w205hist') }); loadHistory(); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205restore]'))) { el.disabled = true; restore(el.getAttribute('data-w205restore')); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205link]'))) { var lp = el.getAttribute('data-w205link').split(':'); openDrawer({ kind: 'link', goal: lp[0], ms: lp[1] || null, msPick: lp[1] || null, q: '' }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205linkp]'))) { var pr = byId(S.projects, el.getAttribute('data-w205linkp')); openDrawer({ kind: 'link', goal: null, project: pr ? pr.id : null, goalPick: null, msPick: null }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205proj]'))) { var pj = byId(S.projects, el.getAttribute('data-w205proj')); if (!pj) return; if (!canComment()) { location.hash = '#project/' + pj.id; return; } openDrawer({ kind: 'link', goal: pj.goal_id, project: pj.id, goalPick: pj.goal_id, msPick: pj.milestone_id, cpPick: pj.checkpoint_id }); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205dolink]'))) { doLink(false); return; }
    if ((el = t.closest('[data-w205unlink]'))) { doLink(true); return; }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v206 a project page opens its tasks (04/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (04/10/26): on a project's own page (#project/<id>, e.g. Brand
   Refresh) he wants to open a task the way he can on a department page.
   The project page (wave27, replaced by wave99, wrapped by 100, 101 and
   104) listed each task as a checkbox, the title and an "owner, due,
   move" line; clicking the title did nothing.
   Now every task title on the project page, open and completed, opens
   the department pages' own task drawer (window.w116Board.open, wave116b,
   with everything later waves add to it: the description and subtasks
   and attachments of 116t, the profile chips of 116m, the bank button of
   132, the @mentions of 138). There is no second task view.
   - Before opening, the task's row is read again from dept_tasks and put
     into DB_TASKS (the drawer reads tasks from there): a task added with
     the project page's quick add, or ticked on it, is then current.
   - The checkbox is untouched (wave27's save: dept_tasks plus change_log).
   - While the drawer is open, every saved change repaints the project
     page in place (debounced), and once more when the drawer closes.
   - Keyboard: the title is a button (Tab, then Enter or Space).
   - On a phone, wave51's latest-comment line goes under the task instead of
     over its title.
   - A retired goal (wave205) is no goal on the project page: "Not linked to one
     of the five goals yet" instead of the archived department goal.
   Works wherever #project/<id> opens: inside the Strategy app (parts153
   APPS.strategy pages include 'project'), from Projects, Goal alignment,
   the department cards, search and the people pages.
   Test hook: window.w206 { open, repaint, state }.
   ===================================================================== */

  function drawerOpen() { var dr = document.getElementById('w116dr'); return !!(dr && dr.classList.contains('open')); }

  /* the drawer reads DB_TASKS: bring the row up to date first */
  async function freshRow(id) {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('*').eq('id', Number(id)).maybeSingle();
      var row = r && r.data; if (!row) return false;
      if (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(DB_TASKS)) {
        var i = -1; for (var k = 0; k < DB_TASKS.length; k++) { if (String(DB_TASKS[k].id) === String(row.id)) { i = k; break; } }

  }

  /* repaint the project page in place after a change in the drawer */
  function repaint(delay) {
    if (W.timer) clearTimeout(W.timer);
    W.timer = setTimeout(async function () {
      W.timer = null;
      if (!onProjectPage() || typeof WA27_VIEW === 'undefined' || !WA27_VIEW) return;
      var y = window.scrollY;
      var det = document.querySelector('#projectpage details.completed'); var wasOpen = !!(det && det.open);

      try { await wa27RenderProject(WA27_VIEW.ref, WA27_VIEW.dept); } catch (e) { return; }

      try { if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') renderAllSections(); } catch (e) { /* decorations only */ }

      if (typing !== null) { var inp = document.getElementById('w27nt'); if (inp) { inp.value = typing; inp.focus(); } }

      if (!drawerOpen()) document.querySelectorAll('#projectpage .w27-task.w206on').forEach(function (r) { r.classList.remove('w206on'); });
    }, delay == null ? 700 : delay);
  }

  /* listen to the shared drawer only while it was opened from a project page */
  function watch() {
    if (W.watching) return; W.watching = true;
    document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (W.last && onProjectPage() && e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w116dr')) repaint(); }, true);

    }, true);

  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {

  });

/* =====================================================================
   WA v207 project tasks are department tasks (04/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (04/10/26): "I need the tasks of a project to [use] the data found on the home page of each department.
   Individuals should be able to open the tasks the same way they find them in their department task page to edit
   descriptions, due dates, assignees, push to the bank etc."
   wave206 made every task title on #project/<id> open the team pages' own drawer (w116Board.open). This wave:
   - Ticking a task on the project page goes through toggleTask, the save every team page uses (dept_tasks plus the
     change_log line), with an Undo on the row for 10 seconds; the page then repaints in place (w206.repaint).
   - Link existing tasks: staff open a picker that searches the team's open tasks (or every team's) not in this

     project made from a retired goal started empty while the team's related tasks sat in other projects; this is how
     people move the right ones in, by hand (wave207 linked only the unambiguous cases in the database).
   - A project made from a retired department goal (wave205's Make it a project) says so, and lists the team's projects
     that serve the same goal, with their open tasks, so nobody reads "No tasks assigned yet" as the whole story.

   ===================================================================== */

    + '#projectpage .w207from .pj{display:inline-flex;gap:5px;align-items:center;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);border-radius:8px;padding:1px 8px;margin:4px 4px 0 0;text-decoration:none;}'
    + '#projectpage .w207cpl{font-size:12.5px;border:1px solid rgba(220,165,39,.55);background:rgba(220,165,39,.10);border-radius:9px;padding:7px 12px;margin:-6px 0 14px;max-width:720px;}'
    + '#w207sheet{font-family:' + BODY + ';}'
    + '#w207sheet h3{font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:24px;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';margin:0 0 4px;}'
    + '#w207sheet .bar{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:10px 0;}'
    + '#w207sheet input[type=search]{flex:1 1 200px;min-width:0;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);border-radius:9px;font-size:14px;font-family:' + BODY + ';}'
    + '#w207sheet select{padding:7px 8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);border-radius:9px;font-size:13px;font-family:' + BODY + ';max-width:100%;}'
    + '#w207list{max-height:min(52vh,420px);overflow-y:auto;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.12);border-radius:10px;}'
    + '#w207list label{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;padding:8px 10px;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);cursor:pointer;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.4;}'
    + '#w207list label:first-child{border-top:0;}'
    + '#w207list label:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.04);}'
    + '#w207list input{margin-top:3px;accent-color:' + GREEN + ';flex:none;width:16px;height:16px;}'
    + '#w207list .t{flex:1;min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}'
    + '#w207list small{display:block;color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:12px;margin-top:2px;}'
    + '#w207list small b{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;}'
