    + '#w207sheet .go[disabled]{opacity:.45;cursor:default;}'
    + '#w207sheet .no{background:transparent;border:0;color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13.5px;}'
    + '#w207sheet .empty{padding:18px;text-align:center;color:' + MUTED + ';font-size:13px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- ticking: toggleTask (the team pages' save) with Undo ---------- */
  function rowOfDb(id) { try { return (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' && DB_TASKS || []).filter(function (t) { return String(t.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  async function ensureRow(id) {
    if (rowOfDb(id)) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('*').eq('id', Number(id)).maybeSingle();
      if (r && r.data && typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(DB_TASKS)) DB_TASKS.push(r.data);
    } catch (e) { /* toggleTask saves without it */ }
  }
  async function tick(chk) {
    var id = Number(chk.getAttribute('data-w27t')), done = !!chk.checked, row = chk.closest('.w27-task');
    if (!staffOk() || typeof toggleTask !== 'function') { chk.checked = !done; return false; }
    chk.disabled = true;
    await ensureRow(id);
    var ok = false; try { ok = await toggleTask(id, done); } catch (e) { ok = false; }
    chk.disabled = false;
    if (!ok) { chk.checked = !done; return false; }
    P.ticks++;
    try { if (typeof wa27Load === 'function') wa27Load(true); } catch (e) { /* counts */ }
    if (row) {
      row.classList.toggle('done', done);
      var old = row.querySelector('.w207undo'); if (old) old.remove();
      var u = document.createElement('button'); u.type = 'button'; u.className = 'w207undo'; u.textContent = 'Undo';
      u.setAttribute('data-w207undo', String(id)); u.setAttribute('data-w207to', done ? '0' : '1');
      u.setAttribute('aria-label', done ? 'Undo: reopen this task' : 'Undo: mark it done again');
      var meta = row.querySelector('.meta'); (meta || row).appendChild(u);
    }
    clearTimeout(P.timers[id]);
    P.timers[id] = setTimeout(function () { delete P.timers[id]; if (onProject()) repaint(0); }, 10000);
    return true;
  }
  async function undo(btn) {
    var id = Number(btn.getAttribute('data-w207undo')), to = btn.getAttribute('data-w207to') === '1';
    btn.disabled = true; clearTimeout(P.timers[id]); delete P.timers[id];
    var ok = false; try { ok = await toggleTask(id, to); } catch (e) { ok = false; }
    if (!ok) { btn.disabled = false; return; }
    P.undos++;
    try { if (typeof wa27Load === 'function') wa27Load(true); } catch (e) { /* counts */ }
    repaint(0);
  }
  /* before wave27's own handler (on body): the project page's checkbox goes through toggleTask */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var chk = t.closest('#projectpage input[data-w27t]');
    if (chk) { e.stopPropagation(); tick(chk); return; }
    var u = t.closest('#projectpage [data-w207undo]');
    if (u) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); undo(u); return; }
    var lb = t.closest('#projectpage [data-w207link]');
    if (lb) { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); openSheet(); return; }
    var pj = t.closest('#projectpage [data-w207proj]');
    if (pj) { e.preventDefault(); location.hash = '#project/' + pj.getAttribute('data-w207proj'); return; }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- Link existing tasks ---------- */
  async function openSheet() {
    var v = view(); if (!v || v.ref === 'general' || !canLink()) return;
    P.sheet = { project: Number(v.ref), dept: v.dept, team: v.dept, q: '', rows: null, picked: {}, err: null };
    paintSheet();
    try {
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('id,title,department,project_id,subsection,owner,due_date,done,parent_task_id').eq('done', false).order('id', { ascending: false }).range(0, 999);
      if (!P.sheet) return;
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      P.sheet.rows = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (t) { return String(t.project_id || '') !== String(P.sheet.project); });
    } catch (e) { if (P.sheet) P.sheet.err = String((e && e.message) || e); }
    paintSheet();
  }
  function sheetRows() {
    var s = P.sheet; if (!s || !s.rows) return [];
    var q = s.q.trim().toLowerCase();
    return s.rows.filter(function (t) {
      if (s.team !== 'all' && t.department !== s.team) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      return (String(t.title || '') + ' ' + (t.subsection || '') + ' ' + (t.owner || '') + ' #' + t.id + ' ' + projName(t.project_id)).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0;
    });
  }
  function paintSheet(keepFocus) {
    var s = P.sheet; if (!s) return;
    var teams = []; try { teams = (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).slice(); } catch (e) { teams = []; }
    if (teams.indexOf(s.dept) < 0) teams.unshift(s.dept);
    var list = sheetRows(), n = Object.keys(s.picked).length;
    var body = s.err ? '<div class="empty">The tasks could not be read (' + E(s.err.slice(0, 120)) + ').</div>'
      : !s.rows ? '<div class="empty">Reading the open tasks\u2026</div>'
      : !list.length ? '<div class="empty">' + (s.q ? 'No open task matches.' : 'No open task to link here.') + '</div>'
      : list.slice(0, 200).map(function (t) {
          var cur = t.project_id ? projName(t.project_id) : '';
          return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w207pick="' + t.id + '"' + (s.picked[t.id] ? ' checked' : '') + ' /><span class="t"><span data-w198skip>' + E(t.title) + '</span>' +
            '<small>#' + t.id + ' \u00b7 ' + E(deptLabel(t.department)) + (t.subsection ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(t.subsection) : '') + (t.owner ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(t.owner) : '') + (t.due_date ? ' \u00b7 due ' + E(fmtDay(t.due_date)) : '') +
            ' \u00b7 ' + (cur ? '<b>moves from ' + E(cur) + '</b>' : 'no project yet') + '</small></span></label>';
        }).join('') + (list.length > 200 ? '<div class="empty">Showing 200 of ' + list.length + '. Search to narrow it.</div>' : '');
    var html = '<div id="w207sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w207sh"><h3 id="w207sh">Link existing tasks</h3>' +
      '<p class="read" style="margin:0">Open tasks not in <b data-w198skip>' + E(projName(s.project)) + '</b> yet. Tick the ones that belong here; a task in another project moves.</p>' +
      '<div class="bar"><input type="search" id="w207q" placeholder="Search by title, workstream, owner or #number" aria-label="Search the open tasks" value="' + E(s.q) + '" />' +
      '<select id="w207team" aria-label="Which team">' + teams.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + E(d) + '"' + (s.team === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(deptLabel(d)) + (d === s.dept ? ' (this project\u2019s team)' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '<option value="all"' + (s.team === 'all' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Every team</option></select></div>' +
      '<div id="w207list">' + body + '</div>' +
      '<div class="ft"><span style="font-size:12.5px;color:' + MUTED + '">' + (n ? n + ' picked' : 'Nothing picked yet') + '</span><span class="sp"></span><button type="button" class="no" data-w207close="1">Cancel</button><button type="button" class="go" data-w207do="1"' + (n ? '' : ' disabled') + '>Link ' + (n ? n + ' ' : '') + (n === 1 ? 'task' : 'tasks') + '</button></div></div>';
    var box = document.getElementById('modalBox'), back = document.getElementById('modalBack'), open = box && back && back.style.display !== 'none' && box.querySelector('#w207sheet');
    if (open) {
      var lst = document.getElementById('w207list'), top = lst ? lst.scrollTop : 0;
      open.outerHTML = html;
      var l2 = document.getElementById('w207list'); if (l2) l2.scrollTop = top;
    } else if (typeof openModal === 'function') openModal(html);
    if (keepFocus) { var q = document.getElementById('w207q'); if (q) { q.focus(); q.setSelectionRange(q.value.length, q.value.length); } }
    else if (!open) setTimeout(function () { var q = document.getElementById('w207q'); if (q) q.focus(); }, 40);
  }
  function closeSheet() { P.sheet = null; try { var el = document.getElementById('w207sheet'); if (el) { if (typeof closeModal === 'function') closeModal(); el.remove(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function doLink() {
    var s = P.sheet; if (!s) return;
    var ids = Object.keys(s.picked).map(Number); if (!ids.length) return;
    var btn = document.querySelector('#w207sheet [data-w207do]'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    var before = {}; (s.rows || []).forEach(function (t) { if (s.picked[t.id]) before[t.id] = t.project_id || null; });
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({ project_id: s.project, updated_by: who0(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).in('id', ids);
    if (r && r.error) { if (btn) btn.disabled = false; toastS('Not linked: ' + (r.error.message || r.error)); return; }
    try {
      await db.from('change_log').insert(ids.map(function (id) {
        return { entity_type: 'dept_task', entity_id: String(id), field: 'project_id', old_value: before[id] == null ? null : String(before[id]), new_value: String(s.project) + ' (linked from the project page)', changed_by: who0() };
      }));
    } catch (e) { /* logging is best effort, as every task write */ }
    try { if (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined') DB_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { if (ids.indexOf(Number(t.id)) >= 0) t.project_id = s.project; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    P.linked += ids.length;
    closeSheet();
    toastS(ids.length === 1 ? 'Task linked to the project' : ids.length + ' tasks linked to the project');
    try { if (typeof wa27Load === 'function') await wa27Load(true); } catch (e) { /* counts */ }
    repaint(0);
  }
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !P.sheet) return;
    if (t.id === 'w207q') { P.sheet.q = t.value; clearTimeout(P.qT); P.qT = setTimeout(function () { paintSheet(true); }, 140); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !P.sheet) return;
    if (t.id === 'w207team') { P.sheet.team = t.value; paintSheet(); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute && t.hasAttribute('data-w207pick')) {
      var id = t.getAttribute('data-w207pick'); if (t.checked) P.sheet.picked[id] = 1; else delete P.sheet.picked[id];
      var n = Object.keys(P.sheet.picked).length, b = document.querySelector('#w207sheet [data-w207do]'), c = document.querySelector('#w207sheet .ft > span');
      if (b) { b.disabled = !n; b.textContent = 'Link ' + (n ? n + ' ' : '') + (n === 1 ? 'task' : 'tasks'); }
      if (c) c.textContent = n ? n + ' picked' : 'Nothing picked yet';
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !P.sheet) return;
    if (t.closest('#w207sheet [data-w207close]')) { closeSheet(); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w207sheet [data-w207do]')) { doLink(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'modalBack') P.sheet = null;
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && P.sheet && document.getElementById('w207sheet')) closeSheet(); });

  /* ---------- the page: the Link button, where a project came from, its checkpoint ---------- */
  var ORIGIN = {};
  async function origin(pid) {
    if (ORIGIN[pid] !== undefined) return ORIGIN[pid];
    try {
      var r = await db.from('goals').select('id,title,department,retired_at,maps_to_goal_id').eq('moved_to_project_id', Number(pid)).limit(1);
      var g = r && r.data && r.data[0];
      if (!g) { ORIGIN[pid] = null; return null; }
      var log = await db.from('change_log').select('changed_at').eq('entity_type', 'goal').eq('entity_id', String(g.id)).eq('field', 'moved_to_project').order('changed_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1);
      g.made_at = log && log.data && log.data[0] ? log.data[0].changed_at : null;
      ORIGIN[pid] = g; return g;
    } catch (e) { ORIGIN[pid] = null; return null; }
  }
  async function decorate() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('projectpage'), v = view(); if (!sec || !v || v.ref === 'general') return;
    var card = sec.querySelector('.card'); if (!card) return;
    var p = typeof wa27Find === 'function' ? wa27Find(v.ref) : null; if (!p) return;
    /* the Link existing tasks button, under the quick add (or under the open list for people without it) */
    if (canLink() && !card.querySelector('[data-w207link]')) {
      var b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'w207linkbtn'; b.setAttribute('data-w207link', '1');
      b.innerHTML = '<span aria-hidden="true">+</span> Link existing tasks';
      var add = card.querySelector('.add-row');
      if (add) add.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', b);
      else { var det = card.querySelector('details.completed'); if (det) det.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', b); else card.appendChild(b); }
    }
    /* a project made from a retired goal: say so, and show the team's projects on the same goal */
    var g = await origin(p.id);
    if (g && !card.querySelector('.w207from') && view() && String(view().ref) === String(p.id)) {
      var sibs = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).filter(function (x) { return x.department === p.department && String(x.id) !== String(p.id) && p.goal_id && String(x.goal_id) === String(p.goal_id); });
      var counts = typeof WA27_COUNTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_COUNTS : {};
      var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'w207from';
      d.innerHTML = 'Made from the retired department goal <b data-w198skip>' + E(g.title.length > 90 ? g.title.slice(0, 88) + '\u2026' : g.title) + '</b>' + (g.made_at ? ' on ' + E(fmtDay(g.made_at)) : '') + ', so it started with no tasks. ' +
        (canLink() ? 'Use Link existing tasks to bring in the team\u2019s tasks that belong here.' : 'The team links the tasks that belong here.') +
        (sibs.length ? '<br>The team\u2019s other projects on the same goal: ' + sibs.map(function (x) { return '<a class="pj" href="#project/' + x.id + '" data-w207proj="' + x.id + '"><span data-w198skip>' + E(x.name) + '</span> <span style="opacity:.7">' + (counts['p' + x.id] || 0) + ' open</span></a>'; }).join('') : '');
      var anchor = card.querySelector('.w27-goal') || card.querySelector('.w206ng') || card.querySelector('.w27-desc');
      if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', d);
    }
    /* the quarterly checkpoint it serves */
    if (p.checkpoint_id && !card.querySelector('.w207cpl')) {
      try {
        var r = await db.from('goal_checkpoints').select('id,quarter,body,due_date,status,target_id').eq('id', Number(p.checkpoint_id)).maybeSingle();
        var c = r && r.data;
        if (c && !card.querySelector('.w207cpl')) {
          var t = await db.from('goal_year_targets').select('fy').eq('id', Number(c.target_id)).maybeSingle();
          var fy = t && t.data ? t.data.fy : '';
          var el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'w207cpl';
          el.innerHTML = 'Serves the checkpoint <b>' + E(fy + ' Q' + c.quarter) + '</b>: <span data-w198skip>' + E(c.body) + '</span> \u00b7 due ' + E(fmtDay(c.due_date));
          var a2 = card.querySelector('.w207from') || card.querySelector('.w27-goal') || card.querySelector('.w206ng') || card.querySelector('.w27-desc');
          if (a2) a2.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el);
        }
      } catch (e) { /* the line is a courtesy */ }
    }
  }
  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    var __rp207 = wa27RenderProject;
    wa27RenderProject = async function () {
      var out = await __rp207.apply(this, arguments);
      try { await decorate(); } catch (e) { /* never break the page */ }
      return out;
    };
  }

  window.w207p = { state: function () { return { ticks: P.ticks, undos: P.undos, linked: P.linked, sheet: !!P.sheet, pending: Object.keys(P.timers).length }; }, open: openSheet, close: closeSheet, origin: origin };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v208 Interviews on the application: the transcript, its review, the JD and the MCC framework (05/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (05/10/26): "when an interview comes in through the transcript archive it should be linked to the
   applicant's profile to view the interview. It should also fill in the strengths, weaknesses and insights
   from the interview. It should also compare the interviewee's answers to the job description and the MCC
   framework on hiring."
   - interview-review (edge) matches a transcript titled "... Interview" to its applicant (by name, and the
     position), then reads it against the position's JD (from the HR drive) and the MCC hiring framework
     (CMP X WA National Expansion Manual: the nine qualities, the profile for the role's level, the five
     recruitment philosophies). Rows: hub_candidate_interviews (RLS follows the transcript's own).
   - The applicant's sheet shows the interview (read it, the recording), the summary, strengths, weaknesses,
     insights, the JD requirement by requirement, the MCC qualities against the level's profile, the follow-ups
     and a suggested next step. Review again; change the level; unlink.
   - The board: cards say "Interview reviewed"; "Interviews to link (n)" lists the interviews no applicant
     could be matched to, with suggestions, to link by hand or mark "not an applicant".
   Hooks: w199.sheetBits, w199.cardBits, w199.barBits call w208 (parts199). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var I = { rows: [], tx: {}, at: 0, loading: null, body: {}, showTx: {}, busy: {}, poll: null, linkOpen: false };
  var QUAL = [['quick_learner', 'Quick learner'], ['self_starter', 'Self-starter'], ['experience', 'Experience'], ['intelligence', 'Intelligence'], ['integrity', 'Integrity'], ['energy', 'Energy'], ['problem_solver', 'Problem-solver'], ['vision', 'Vision'], ['referrals', 'Referrals']];
  var PHIL = [['active', 'Recruitment is active'], ['lean', 'Lean teams'], ['iie', 'Integrity, intelligence, energy'], ['referrals', 'Referrals over experience'], ['learn', 'Ability to learn']];
  var LEVELS = [['board', 'Board of Directors'], ['csuite', 'C-Suite'], ['staff', 'Directors and below (HQ)'], ['chapter_president', 'Chapter President'], ['chapter_vp', 'Chapter Vice-President'], ['ambassador', 'Brand Ambassador']];
  var VERDICT = { strong: ['Strong', 'ok'], partial: ['Partly', 'mid'], gap: ['Gap', 'lo'], not_covered: ['Not covered', 'mut'] };
  var JDREAD = { pdf: 'The JD is a PDF, which the review cannot read: it compared with the position\'s title only.', no_jd: 'This position has no JD in Drive yet: the review compared with its title only.', no_position: 'This application is not linked to a position: the review compared with the role they wrote.', other: 'The JD is not a Google Doc or Word file: the review compared with the title only.' };

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function btn(label, attrs, kind) { return '<button type="button" class="w197b' + (kind ? ' ' + kind : '') + '" ' + (attrs || '') + '>' + label + '</button>'; }
  function day(s) { if (!s) return ''; if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s))) s = s + 'T12:00:00'; try { return new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function cands() { try { return (window.w197 && w197.state().cands) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function lbl(list, k) { var x = list.filter(function (p) { return p[0] === k; })[0]; return x ? x[1] : k; }
  function rowsOf(cid) { return I.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'linked' && String(r.candidate_id) === String(cid); }); }
  function unmatched() { return I.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'unmatched'; }); }
  function txOf(r) { return I.tx[r.transcript_id] || { title: 'Interview', meeting_date: null }; }
  function reopen(cid) { try { var st = window.w197 && w197.state(); if (st && cid && String(st.open) === String(cid)) w197.openSheet(cid); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function repaint() { try { if (window.w197) w197.paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }

  async function q(p) { try { var r = await p; return (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  async function fn(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('interview-review', { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) {
      var msg = r.error.message || String(r.error);
      try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) { var j = await r.error.context.json(); if (j && j.error) msg = j.error; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      throw new Error(msg);
    }
    if (r && r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r ? r.data : null;
  }
  async function load(force) {
    if (I.loading) return I.loading;
    if (!force && I.at && Date.now() - I.at < 60000) return I;
    I.loading = (async function () {
      var rows = await q(db.from('hub_candidate_interviews').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1000));
      var ids = rows.map(function (r) { return r.transcript_id; });
      var ts = ids.length ? await q(db.from('transcripts').select('id,title,meeting_date,source_url,speakers').in('id', ids)) : [];
      I.rows = rows; I.tx = {}; ts.forEach(function (t) { I.tx[t.id] = t; });
      I.at = Date.now(); I.loading = null;
      return I;
    })();
    return I.loading;
  }
  /* while a review is being written, look again every 8 seconds (only while its sheet is open) */
  function watch(cid) {
    if (I.poll) return;
    I.poll = setInterval(async function () {
      var st = null; try { st = window.w197 && w197.state(); } catch (e) { st = null; }
      var open = st && st.open;
      var waiting = open && rowsOf(open).some(function (r) { return r.analysis_status === 'pending' || r.analysis_status === 'running'; });
      if (!waiting) { clearInterval(I.poll); I.poll = null; return; }
      await load(true); repaint(); reopen(open);
    }, 8000);
  }

  /* ---------- the card ---------- */
  function cardBits(c) {
    if (!c) return '';
    var rs = rowsOf(c.id); if (!rs.length) return '';
    var done = rs.filter(function (r) { return r.analysis_status === 'done'; })[0];
    if (done) { var a = done.analysis || {}, mc = a.mcc || {}; return '<span class="w208pill" title="The interview is reviewed: open the application to read it">Interview reviewed' + (a.jd_fit && a.jd_fit.score ? ' \u00b7 JD ' + E(a.jd_fit.score) + '/5' : '') + (mc.profile_score ? ' \u00b7 MCC ' + E(mc.profile_score) + '/5' : '') + '</span>'; }
    if (rs.some(function (r) { return r.analysis_status === 'failed'; })) return '<span class="w208pill lo">Interview linked (review failed)</span>';
    return '<span class="w208pill mut">Interview linked</span>';
  }

  /* ---------- the board bar: interviews no applicant was matched to ---------- */
  function barBits() {
    if (!I.at) { load().then(function () { if (unmatched().length) repaint(); }); return ''; }
    var n = unmatched().length; if (!n) return '';
    return btn('Interviews to link (' + n + ')', 'data-w208a="linklist"', 'sm w208tolink');
  }
  function candOptions(sel) {
    var cs = cands().slice().sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name || '').localeCompare(String(b.name || '')); });
    return '<option value="">Choose the applicant\u2026</option>' + cs.map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + c.id + '"' + (String(sel) === String(c.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(c.name || 'Someone') + ' \u00b7 ' + E((window.w199 ? w199.roleOf(c) : c.role) || '') + '</option>'; }).join('');
  }
  function linkListHtml() {
    var us = unmatched();
    return '<div class="bk" data-w208a="linkshut"></div><div class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Interviews to link">' + btn('Close', 'data-w208a="linkshut"', 'sm x') +
      '<h3>Interviews to link</h3><p class="w197note">Interview transcripts in the archive that no applicant could be matched to by name. Link each one to its applicant and the Hub reviews it against the JD and the MCC framework.</p>' +
      (us.length ? '<ul class="w208ul">' + us.map(function (r) {
        var t = txOf(r), sg = (r.suggestions || []).filter(function (s) { return cands().some(function (c) { return String(c.id) === String(s.id); }); });
        return '<li><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b><small>' + E(day(t.meeting_date)) + (t.speakers && t.speakers.length ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(t.speakers.join(', ')) : '') + ' \u00b7 ' + E(r.match_note || '') + '</small>' +
          (sg.length ? '<div class="w208sg">Could be: ' + sg.map(function (s) { return btn(E(s.name), 'data-w208link="' + r.transcript_id + '" data-c="' + s.id + '"', 'sm'); }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
          '<div class="w208row"><select data-w208pick="' + r.transcript_id + '" aria-label="The applicant for ' + E(t.title) + '">' + candOptions() + '</select>' + btn('Link', 'data-w208a="linkpick" data-t="' + r.transcript_id + '"', 'sm pri') + btn('Not an applicant', 'data-w208a="notapp" data-t="' + r.transcript_id + '"', 'sm') + '</div></li>';
      }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197note">Every interview is linked.</p>') + '</div>';
  }
  function openLinkList() { var s = document.getElementById('w208links'); if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w208links'; document.body.appendChild(s); } s.innerHTML = linkListHtml(); s.classList.add('open'); I.linkOpen = true; }
  function shutLinkList() { var s = document.getElementById('w208links'); if (s) s.classList.remove('open'); I.linkOpen = false; }

  /* ---------- the sheet ---------- */
  function score5(n) { n = Number(n || 0); return '<span class="w208dots" aria-label="' + (n ? n + ' of 5' : 'not covered') + '">' + [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(function (i) { return '<i class="' + (i <= n ? 'on' : '') + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</span>'; }
  function points(list) { return '<ul class="w208pts">' + (list || []).map(function (p) { return typeof p === 'string' ? '<li>' + E(p) + '</li>' : '<li><b>' + E(p.point) + '</b>' + (p.evidence ? '<small>' + E(p.evidence) + '</small>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>'; }
  function reviewHtml(r) {
    var a = r.analysis || {}, jf = a.jd_fit || {}, mc = a.mcc || {}, needs = mc.needs || [];
    var lv = r.level || mc.level || 'staff';
    var qs = QUAL.map(function (qq) { var x = (mc.qualities || []).filter(function (y) { return y.quality === qq[0]; })[0] || {}; return { k: qq[0], l: qq[1], s: Number(x.score || 0), ev: x.evidence || '', need: needs.indexOf(qq[0]) >= 0 }; });
    qs.sort(function (x, y) { return (y.need ? 1 : 0) - (x.need ? 1 : 0); });
    return '<div class="w208rev">' +
      (a.summary ? '<p class="w208sum">' + E(a.summary) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="w208two"><div><h5>Strengths</h5>' + points(a.strengths) + '</div><div><h5>Weaknesses</h5>' + points(a.weaknesses) + '</div></div>' +
      (a.insights && a.insights.length ? '<h5>Insights</h5>' + points(a.insights) : '') +
      '<h5>Against the job description' + (jf.score ? ' <span class="w208sc">' + score5(jf.score) + ' ' + E(jf.score) + '/5</span>' : '') + '</h5>' +
      (a.jd && JDREAD[a.jd.read] ? '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0 0 4px">' + E(JDREAD[a.jd.read]) + '</p>' : (a.jd && /^unreadable/.test(a.jd.read || '') ? '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0 0 4px">The JD could not be read from Drive: the review compared with the title only.</p>' : (a.jd && a.jd.name ? '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0 0 4px">Compared with ' + E(a.jd.name) + '.</p>' : ''))) +
      (jf.summary ? '<p>' + E(jf.summary) + '</p>' : '') +
      ((jf.requirements || []).length ? '<ul class="w208req">' + jf.requirements.map(function (x) { var v = VERDICT[x.verdict] || [x.verdict, 'mut']; return '<li><span class="w208v ' + v[1] + '">' + E(v[0]) + '</span><b>' + E(x.requirement) + '</b>' + (x.evidence ? '<small>' + E(x.evidence) + '</small>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') +
      '<h5>Against the MCC hiring framework' + (mc.profile_score ? ' <span class="w208sc">' + score5(Math.round(mc.profile_score)) + ' ' + E(mc.profile_score) + '/5</span>' : '') + '</h5>' +
      '<div class="w208row"><label for="w208lv' + r.transcript_id + '" class="w208in">Level</label><select id="w208lv' + r.transcript_id + '" data-w208lv="' + r.transcript_id + '">' + LEVELS.map(function (l) { return '<option value="' + l[0] + '"' + (l[0] === lv ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(l[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select><small>The profile for this level needs the qualities marked \u25cf' + (mc.covered != null && needs.length ? ' (' + E(mc.covered) + ' of ' + needs.length + ' came up in the interview)' : '') + '.</small></div>' +
      (mc.summary ? '<p>' + E(mc.summary) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<table class="w208q"><tbody>' + qs.map(function (x) { return '<tr class="' + (x.need ? 'need' : '') + '"><th>' + (x.need ? '<span title="Needed for this level">\u25cf</span> ' : '') + E(x.l) + '</th><td>' + (x.s ? score5(x.s) : '<span class="w208nc">Not covered</span>') + '</td><td><small>' + E(x.ev) + '</small></td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</tbody></table>' +
      ((mc.philosophies || []).length ? '<h5>The recruitment philosophies</h5><ul class="w208pts">' + mc.philosophies.map(function (p) { return '<li><b>' + E(lbl(PHIL, p.philosophy)) + '</b><small>' + E(p.note) + '</small></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') +
      ((a.follow_ups || []).length ? '<h5>To follow up</h5>' + points(a.follow_ups) : '') +
      (a.next_step ? '<div class="next"><b>Suggested next step:</b> ' + E(a.next_step) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<p class="w197note">Read ' + E(day(r.analysed_at)) + (r.analysis_model ? ' by ' + E(String(r.analysis_model).replace(/^[a-z-]+\//, '')) : '') + '. A suggestion from the transcript: the decision stays with the hiring team.</p></div>';
  }
  function oneHtml(r, cid) {
    var t = txOf(r), tid = r.transcript_id, st = r.analysis_status, busy = I.busy[tid];
    var head = '<div class="w208head"><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b><small>' + E(day(t.meeting_date)) + (r.match_how === 'auto' ? ' \u00b7 linked by the Hub (' + E(String(r.match_note || '').replace(/^Matched by /, '')) + ')' : (r.match_how === 'manual' ? ' \u00b7 linked by hand' : '')) + '</small></div>' +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn(I.showTx[tid] ? 'Hide the interview' : 'Read the interview', 'data-w208a="tx" data-t="' + tid + '"', 'sm pri') +
      (t.source_url && /^https?:/.test(t.source_url) && !/app\.plaud\.ai\/?$/.test(t.source_url) ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="' + E(t.source_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">The recording</a>' : '') +
      btn(busy ? 'Reviewing\u2026' : (st === 'done' ? 'Review again' : 'Review it'), 'data-w208a="again" data-t="' + tid + '"' + (busy || st === 'running' ? ' disabled' : ''), 'sm') +
      btn('Unlink', 'data-w208a="unlink" data-t="' + tid + '" data-c="' + cid + '"', 'sm') + '</div>';
    var tx = I.showTx[tid] ? '<div class="w208tx" tabindex="0" aria-label="The interview transcript">' + (I.body[tid] == null ? 'Loading the transcript\u2026' : E(I.body[tid] || 'The transcript is empty.')) + '</div>' : '';
    var body;
    if (st === 'done' && r.analysis) body = reviewHtml(r);
    else if (st === 'failed') body = '<p class="w208err">The review did not work: ' + E(r.analysis_error || 'unknown') + '. Try Review it again.</p>';
    else if (st === 'pending' || st === 'running' || busy) { body = '<p class="w197note w208wait">Reading the interview against the job description and the MCC framework. This takes about a minute.</p>'; watch(cid); }
    else body = '<p class="w197note">Not reviewed yet.</p>';
    return '<div class="w208one">' + head + tx + body + '</div>';
  }
  /* at the top of the sheet: the interview is further down */
  function topBits(c) {
    if (!c || !I.at) return '';
    var rs = rowsOf(c.id); if (!rs.length) return '';
    var r = rs.filter(function (x) { return x.analysis_status === 'done'; })[0] || rs[0], a = r.analysis || {};
    var lab = r.analysis_status === 'done' ? 'The interview and its review' + (a.jd_fit && a.jd_fit.score ? ' (JD ' + a.jd_fit.score + '/5' + (a.mcc && a.mcc.profile_score ? ', MCC ' + a.mcc.profile_score + '/5' : '') + ')' : '') : 'The interview';
    return '<div class="w208jump">' + btn(E(lab) + ' \u2193', 'data-w208a="jump"', 'sm') + '</div>';
  }
  function sheetBits(c) {
    if (!c) return '';
    if (!I.at) { load().then(function () { reopen(c.id); }); return ''; }
    var rs = rowsOf(c.id);
    var us = unmatched();
    if (!rs.length) {
      if (['interview', 'offer', 'hired'].indexOf(c.status) < 0 && !us.length) return '';
      return '<label>Interview</label><p class="w197note" style="margin:0 0 6px">No interview transcript linked yet. An interview in the transcript archive titled "\u2026 Interview" links here by itself when the name matches.</p>' +
        (us.length ? '<div class="w208row"><select id="w208pick1" data-w208c="' + c.id + '" aria-label="Link an interview from the archive"><option value="">Link an interview from the archive\u2026</option>' + us.map(function (r) { var t = txOf(r); return '<option value="' + r.transcript_id + '">' + E(t.title) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(day(t.meeting_date)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' + btn('Link', 'data-w208a="linkhere" data-c="' + c.id + '"', 'sm pri') + '</div>' : '');
    }
    return '<label>Interview' + (rs.length > 1 ? 's <small>' + rs.length + '</small>' : '') + '</label>' + rs.map(function (r) { return oneHtml(r, c.id); }).join('');
  }

  /* ---------- actions ---------- */
  async function act(body, msg, cid) {
    var tid = body.transcript_id; I.busy[tid] = true; reopen(cid);
    try {
      var r = await fn(body);
      if (body.action === 'analyse' && r && r.ok === false) throw new Error(r.error || 'the review did not work');
      if (msg) toastS(msg);
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not do that: ' + (e.message || e)); }
    I.busy[tid] = false;
    await load(true); repaint(); if (I.linkOpen) openLinkList(); reopen(cid);
  }
  async function showTx(tid, cid) {
    I.showTx[tid] = !I.showTx[tid]; reopen(cid);
    if (I.showTx[tid] && I.body[tid] == null) {
      var r = await q(db.from('transcripts').select('id,body').eq('id', tid).limit(1));
      I.body[tid] = r[0] ? String(r[0].body || '') : ''; reopen(cid);
    }
  }
  function nameOf(cid) { var c = cands().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(cid); })[0]; return c ? c.name : 'the applicant'; }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w208a],[data-w208link]') : null; if (!t) return;
    var a = t.getAttribute('data-w208a'), tid = Number(t.getAttribute('data-t') || t.getAttribute('data-w208link') || 0);
    var st = null; try { st = window.w197 && w197.state(); } catch (x) { st = null; }
    var cid = (st && st.open) || null;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w208link')) { var c1 = t.getAttribute('data-c'); act({ action: 'link', transcript_id: tid, candidate_id: Number(c1) }, 'Linked to ' + nameOf(c1) + '. The review takes about a minute.', cid); return; }
    if (a === 'jump') { var one = document.querySelector('#w197sheet .w208one'); if (one) { try { one.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (x) { one.scrollIntoView(); } } return; }
    if (a === 'linklist') { openLinkList(); return; }
    if (a === 'linkshut') { shutLinkList(); return; }
    if (a === 'linkpick') { var sel = document.querySelector('[data-w208pick="' + tid + '"]'); if (!sel || !sel.value) { toastS('Choose the applicant first'); return; } act({ action: 'link', transcript_id: tid, candidate_id: Number(sel.value) }, 'Linked to ' + nameOf(sel.value) + '. The review takes about a minute.', cid); return; }
    if (a === 'notapp') { act({ action: 'unlink', transcript_id: tid, not_applicant: true }, 'Marked: not an applicant', cid); return; }
    if (a === 'linkhere') { var s2 = document.getElementById('w208pick1'); var c2 = t.getAttribute('data-c'); if (!s2 || !s2.value) { toastS('Choose the interview first'); return; } act({ action: 'link', transcript_id: Number(s2.value), candidate_id: Number(c2) }, 'Interview linked. The review takes about a minute.', c2); return; }
    if (a === 'tx') { showTx(tid, cid); return; }
    if (a === 'again') { act({ action: 'analyse', transcript_id: tid }, 'The interview is reviewed', cid); return; }
    if (a === 'unlink') { if (!window.confirm('Unlink this interview from ' + nameOf(t.getAttribute('data-c')) + '? Its review is cleared; the transcript stays in the archive.')) return; act({ action: 'unlink', transcript_id: tid }, 'Unlinked', cid); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    var lv = t.getAttribute('data-w208lv');
    if (lv) { var st = null; try { st = window.w197 && w197.state(); } catch (x) { st = null; } act({ action: 'analyse', transcript_id: Number(lv), level: t.value }, 'Reviewed again for ' + lbl(LEVELS, t.value), st && st.open); }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && I.linkOpen) shutLinkList(); });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () { if (/^#hrcentre\/applications/.test(String(location.hash || ''))) load(); else shutLinkList(); });

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'w208css';
  css.textContent =
    '.w208pill{display:inline-block;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(42,140,122,.14);color:#1d6b5d;margin:2px 4px 0 0;}.w208pill.mut{background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);color:#4a5a56;}.w208pill.lo{background:rgba(179,64,47,.12);color:#8f2f22;}' +
    '.w208jump{margin:4px 0 2px;}' +
    '.w208tolink{border-color:#DCA527 !important;}' +
    '.w208one{border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);border-radius:12px;padding:10px 12px;margin:4px 0 10px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.035);}' +
    '.w208head b{display:block;font-size:14.5px;}.w208head small{color:#5b6b67;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w208tx{max-height:320px;overflow:auto;white-space:pre-wrap;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:8px;padding:10px;margin:6px 0;}' +
    '.w208rev h5{font-family:\'Barlow Condensed\',\'Arial Narrow\',sans-serif;font-size:15px;letter-spacing:.02em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#103F39;margin:12px 0 4px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.w208sum{font-size:14px;margin:6px 0;}.w208two{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px;}@media (max-width:640px){.w208two{grid-template-columns:1fr;}}' +
    '.w208pts{margin:0;padding-left:18px;font-size:13.5px;}.w208pts li{margin:3px 0;}.w208pts small,.w208req small{display:block;color:#5b6b67;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w208req{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;font-size:13.5px;}.w208req li{padding:5px 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);}.w208req li:first-child{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w208v{display:inline-block;min-width:76px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;border-radius:6px;padding:1px 6px;margin-right:6px;text-align:center;}.w208v.ok{background:rgba(42,140,122,.16);color:#1d6b5d;}.w208v.mid{background:rgba(220,165,39,.2);color:#7a5a0c;}.w208v.lo{background:rgba(179,64,47,.14);color:#8f2f22;}.w208v.mut{background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);color:#4a5a56;}' +
    '.w208dots{display:inline-flex;gap:3px;vertical-align:middle;}.w208dots i{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(16,63,57,.14);}.w208dots i.on{background:#DCA527;}' +
    '.w208sc{font-family:\'Space Grotesk\',sans-serif;text-transform:none;font-size:13px;color:#1F201E;letter-spacing:0;}' +
    '.w208q{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px;}.w208q th{text-align:left;font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap;padding:4px 8px 4px 0;vertical-align:top;}.w208q td{padding:4px 6px 4px 0;vertical-align:top;}.w208q tr{border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);}.w208q tr.need th{font-weight:700;color:#103F39;}.w208q small{color:#5b6b67;}.w208nc{font-size:11.5px;color:#7a8783;}' +
    '@media (max-width:560px){.w208q td:last-child{display:none;}}' +
    '.w208row{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin:4px 0;}.w208row select{max-width:100%;}.w208row small{color:#5b6b67;}.w208in{margin:0 !important;font-weight:600;}' +
    '.w208err{color:#8f2f22;font-size:13.5px;}.w208wait{font-style:italic;}' +
    '#w208links{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:1200;display:none;}#w208links.open{display:block;}#w208links .bk{position:absolute;inset:0;background:rgba(16,32,30,.4);}' +
    '#w208links .pn{position:absolute;right:0;top:0;bottom:0;width:min(560px,100%);background:#FFFAF2;overflow:auto;padding:18px 16px;box-shadow:-8px 0 24px rgba(0,0,0,.12);}#w208links .x{float:right;}' +
    '.w208ul{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;}.w208ul li{border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);padding:10px 0;}.w208ul li small{display:block;color:#5b6b67;font-size:12.5px;}.w208sg{margin:6px 0;font-size:13px;display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;}';
  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(css);

  window.w208 = { state: function () { return I; }, load: load, cardBits: cardBits, barBits: barBits, sheetBits: sheetBits, topBits: topBits, openLinkList: openLinkList, levels: LEVELS, qualities: QUAL };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v209 Applicants in: add one by hand, every application email, volunteer roles to the Volunteer Manager (06/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (06/10/26): "I also want to be able to add new applicants via the applications page. Also emails that come through
   the hub as an application should be added directly to the applications page. Volunteer cook, kitchen, driver, server,
   photographer, community lead, and team leader should be redirected to the volunteer manager to applicants received and
   should update both the crm and volunteers records to have that applicant visible in our searches for volunteers."
   - Applications (HR centre): Add an applicant (name, contact, position or role, where they applied, when, what they wrote,
     a link). A volunteer role is said as it is typed and goes to the Volunteer Manager; anything else is a New card, opened
     so the CV can be uploaded. A card for a volunteer role offers Move to the Volunteer Manager.
   - Volunteer Manager > Applicants received (#volunteering/applicants): every volunteer application (CABM, Je B\u00e9n\u00e9vole,
     email, by hand), To handle first; Contacted, Welcome, Not a fit, Withdrawn with a note; In the CRM, In Volunteers;
     Add an applicant. The person is one record in the CRM and the volunteer directory (volunteer_people), tagged
     "volunteer applicant", so the Volunteers search, the CRM and Find volunteers find them.
   - The emails: applications-intake (edge, every 10 minutes) reads CABM, VBM and Je B\u00e9n\u00e9vole emails and files them here.
   Data: volunteer_applications; functions wa209_intake, wa209_volunteer_apply, wa209_move_to_volunteers, wa209_vol_app_status,
   wa209_volunteer_roles. Hooks: w199.barBits and w199.sheetBits call w209. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A';
  var ROLES = [['cook', 'Cook'], ['driver', 'Driver'], ['server', 'Server'], ['dishwasher', 'Dishwashers'], ['photographer', 'Photographer'], ['videographer', 'Videographer'], ['community_debrief', 'Community lead'], ['team_leader', 'Team leader']];   /* wave239: videographer */
  var SOURCES = [['linkedin', 'LinkedIn'], ['cabm', 'CABM'], ['fcabq', 'Je B\u00e9n\u00e9vole'], ['email', 'Email'], ['referral', 'Referral'], ['website', 'Website'], ['event', 'An event'], ['manual', 'Other']];
  var STATUS = [['received', 'To handle'], ['contacted', 'Contacted'], ['welcomed', 'Welcomed'], ['not_fit', 'Not a fit'], ['withdrawn', 'Withdrawn']];
  var V = { list: null, at: 0, loading: null, tab: 'open', role: '', src: '', q: '', open: {}, busy: false, n: null };

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function btn(label, attrs, kind) { return '<button type="button" class="w197b' + (kind ? ' ' + kind : '') + '" ' + (attrs || '') + '>' + label + '</button>'; }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function lbl(list, k) { var x = list.filter(function (p) { return p[0] === k; })[0]; return x ? x[1] : (k || ''); }
  function roleLabel(k) { if (k === 'volunteer') return 'Volunteer'; var x = lbl(ROLES, k); if (x !== k) return x; try { if (typeof WA_VOL_ROLELABEL !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_ROLELABEL[k]) return WA_VOL_ROLELABEL[k]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return k; }
  function day(s) { if (!s) return ''; try { return new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function ago(s) { if (!s) return ''; var d = (Date.now() - new Date(s).getTime()) / 86400000; if (d < 1) return 'today'; if (d < 2) return 'yesterday'; if (d < 30) return Math.floor(d) + ' days ago'; if (d < 60) return 'a month ago'; return Math.floor(d / 30) + ' months ago'; }
  function today() { var d = new Date(); var p = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }; return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate()); }
  function msgOf(e) { return (e && (e.message || e.details)) || String(e || 'something went wrong'); }

  /* the same reading of a role as public.wa209_volunteer_roles (the database decides; this only says it as you type) */
  function volRoles(text) {
    var t = lower(text).normalize ? lower(text).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '') : lower(text);
    if (!t) return [];
    if (/(coordinat|manager|gestionnaire|director|directeur|directrice|officer|co-lead|co-responsable|assistant|specialist|specialiste|technic|developer|developp|designer|analyst|\bintern\b|stagiaire|relations|marketing|communication|writer|redact|finance|comptab|account|recruit|recrut|\bhr\b|ressources humaines|fundrais|collecte de fonds|website|site web)/.test(t)) return [];
    var r = [];
    if (/(team ?lead|chef d.?equipe|leader d.?equipe|responsable d.?equipe)/.test(t)) r.push('team_leader');
    if (/(cook|cuisin|kitchen)/.test(t) || (/\bchef\b/.test(t) && !/chef d.?equipe/.test(t))) r.push('cook');
    if (/(driver|chauffeu|livreu|livraison)/.test(t)) r.push('driver');
    if (/(\bservers?\b|serveu|\bserving\b|service des repas|service aux repas)/.test(t)) r.push('server');
    if (/(dish|vaisselle|plonge)/.test(t)) r.push('dishwasher');
    if (/photo/.test(t)) r.push('photographer');
    if (/(video|film)/.test(t)) r.push('videographer');   /* wave239 */
    if (/(community lead|community leader|responsable communaut|leader communaut)/.test(t)) r.push('community_debrief');
    if (/(community welcome|community talk|accueil)/.test(t)) { if (r.indexOf('community_debrief') < 0) r.push('community_debrief'); }   /* wave234: one role, Community lead */
    if (!r.length && /(benevol|volunteer)/.test(t)) r.push('volunteer');
    return r;
  }
  function positions() { try { return (window.w199 && w199.state().pos) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function depts() { try { return (window.w199 && w199.depts) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }

  /* ---------- Add an applicant (both pages) ---------- */
  var F = { ctx: 'applications', busy: false, err: '' };
  function formHtml() {
    var vol = F.ctx === 'volunteers';
    var pos = positions();
    var opts = depts().map(function (d) { var ps = pos.filter(function (p) { return p.dept === d[0]; }); return ps.length ? '<optgroup label="' + E(d[1]) + '">' + ps.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + E(p.title) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>' : ''; }).join('');
    return '<div class="bk" data-w209a="shut"></div><div class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w209h">' + btn('Close', 'data-w209a="shut"', 'sm x') +
      '<h3 id="w209h">Add an applicant</h3><p class="w197note">' + (vol ? 'A volunteer who applied outside the Hub (a call, an event, a referral). They join Applicants received, and the CRM and the volunteer directory, so every search finds them.' : 'Someone who applied outside the Hub\u2019s inboxes (LinkedIn, a referral, an event). Volunteer roles (cook, kitchen, driver, server, dishes, photographer, community lead, team leader) go to the Volunteer Manager instead.') + '</p>' +
      '<label for="w209n">Name</label><input id="w209n" type="text" autocomplete="off" placeholder="First and last name">' +
      '<div class="w209two"><div><label for="w209e">Email</label><input id="w209e" type="email" autocomplete="off"></div><div><label for="w209p">Phone</label><input id="w209p" type="tel" autocomplete="off"></div></div>' +
      (vol ? '<label>What they want to do</label><div class="w209roles" role="group" aria-label="Volunteer roles">' + ROLES.map(function (r) { return '<label class="w209ck"><input type="checkbox" data-w209role="' + r[0] + '"> ' + E(r[1]) + '</label>'; }).join('') + '</div>'
        : '<label for="w209pos">Position</label><select id="w209pos"><option value="">Not one of our positions (write the role below)</option>' + opts + '</select>' +
          '<label for="w209r">Or the role they applied for</label><input id="w209r" type="text" autocomplete="off" placeholder="e.g. Fundraising Co-Lead, Volunteer cook">') +
      '<p class="w209hint" id="w209hint" aria-live="polite"></p>' +
      '<div class="w209two"><div><label for="w209s">Where they applied</label><select id="w209s">' + SOURCES.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + s[0] + '">' + E(s[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>' +
      '<div><label for="w209d">Applied on</label><input id="w209d" type="date" value="' + today() + '"></div></div>' +
      '<label for="w209m">' + (vol ? 'What they told us' : 'What they wrote, or your notes') + '</label><textarea id="w209m" rows="4"></textarea>' +
      (vol ? '' : cvField() + '<label for="w209l">LinkedIn or portfolio (optional)</label><input id="w209l" type="url" autocomplete="off" placeholder="https://">') +
      (F.err ? '<p class="w209err" role="alert">' + E(F.err) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn(F.busy ? 'Adding\u2026' : 'Add the applicant', 'data-w209a="save"' + (F.busy ? ' disabled' : ''), 'pri') + btn('Cancel', 'data-w209a="shut"') + '</div>' +
      (vol ? '' : '<p class="w197note">The Hub puts a copy of the CV in the position\u2019s Resumes folder in Google Drive (Unsorted Resumes when there is no position) and reads it.</p>') + '</div>';
  }
  /* wave211 (Steel, 06/10/26): "If they add applicants manually the link field should be mandatory and be placed in the right
     folder on google drive. Please offer guidance if they don't know where to get the link from the uploaded file in google drive" */
  function resumesFolder() {
    var pid = val('w209pos') || KEEP.w209pos, p = pid ? positions().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(pid); })[0] : null;
    if (p && p.resumes_id) return { id: p.resumes_id, label: p.title + ' > Resumes' };
    var sv = null; try { sv = w199.state().sync; } catch (e) { sv = null; }
    return sv && sv.unsorted ? { id: sv.unsorted, label: 'Unsorted Resumes' } : null;
  }
  function cvField() {
    var f = resumesFolder();
    return '<label for="w209cv">Their CV in Google Drive (link) <i class="w209req">required</i></label><input id="w209cv" type="url" autocomplete="off" placeholder="https://drive.google.com/file/d/\u2026" aria-describedby="w209cvhelp">' +
      '<p class="w209cvmsg" id="w209cvmsg" aria-live="polite">' + E(F.cvMsg || '') + '</p>' +
      '<details class="w209help" id="w209cvhelp"' + (F.helpOpen ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>Where do I find the link?</summary><ol>' +
      '<li><b>The CV is already in Google Drive:</b> right-click the file (or open it), choose <b>Share</b>, then <b>Copy link</b>, and paste it here.</li>' +
      '<li><b>It came as an email attachment:</b> in Gmail, hover the attachment and click <b>Add to Drive</b> (the triangle icon), then <b>Organize</b> or open it in Drive and Copy link.</li>' +
      '<li><b>It is on your computer:</b> upload it into the position\u2019s Resumes folder' + (f ? ' (' + E(f.label) + ')' : '') + ', then Share > Copy link. Or skip this form: a CV dropped in a Resumes folder becomes a New card by itself within about 10 minutes.</li>' +
      '<li>The link must be to the file, not to a folder. If the Hub says it cannot open it, share the file with hub@welfareavenue.com.</li></ol>' +
      (f ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/' + E(f.id) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open ' + E(f.label) + '</a>' : '') + '</details>';
  }
  function isVolRole() { var pid = val('w209pos'), title = ''; if (pid) { var p = positions().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === pid; })[0]; title = p ? p.title : ''; } return volRoles(title || val('w209r')).length > 0; }
  async function recruit(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('recruit-actions', { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) { var m = r.error.message || String(r.error); try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) { var j = await r.error.context.json(); if (j && j.error) m = j.error; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } throw new Error(m); }
    return r ? r.data : null;
  }
  function openForm(ctx) {
    F = { ctx: ctx || 'applications', busy: false, err: '', cvMsg: '', helpOpen: false }; KEEP = {};
    var s = document.getElementById('w209add'); if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w209add'; document.body.appendChild(s); }
    s.innerHTML = formHtml(); s.classList.add('open');
    setTimeout(function () { var n = document.getElementById('w209n'); if (n) try { n.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 30);
  }
  function shutForm() { var s = document.getElementById('w209add'); if (s) s.classList.remove('open'); }
  function val(id) { var x = document.getElementById(id); return x ? String(x.value || '').trim() : ''; }
  function hint() {
    var h = document.getElementById('w209hint'); if (!h || F.ctx !== 'applications') return;
    var pid = val('w209pos'), title = '';
    if (pid) { var p = positions().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === pid; })[0]; title = p ? p.title : ''; }
    var r = volRoles(title || val('w209r'));
    h.innerHTML = r.length ? '<b>' + E(r.map(roleLabel).join(', ')) + '</b> is a volunteer role: this applicant goes to the Volunteer Manager, Applicants received, and into the CRM and the volunteer directory.' : '';
    h.className = 'w209hint' + (r.length ? ' on' : '');
    var rq = document.querySelector('#w209add .w209req'); if (rq) rq.textContent = r.length ? 'not needed for a volunteer role' : 'required';
  }
  async function save() {
    if (F.busy) return;
    var name = val('w209n'); if (!name) { F.err = 'Their name, please.'; repaintForm(); return; }
    var email = val('w209e'), phone = val('w209p');
    if (email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) { F.err = 'That email does not look right.'; repaintForm(); return; }
    var cvUrl = F.ctx === 'volunteers' ? '' : val('w209cv'), volRole = F.ctx !== 'volunteers' && isVolRole();
    if (F.ctx !== 'volunteers' && !volRole) {
      if (!cvUrl) { F.err = 'The link to their CV in Google Drive, please. \u201cWhere do I find the link?\u201d shows how.'; F.helpOpen = true; repaintForm(); return; }
      if (!/^https:\/\/(drive|docs)\.google\.com\//.test(cvUrl)) { F.err = 'That is not a Google Drive link. Open the CV in Drive, then Share > Copy link.'; F.helpOpen = true; repaintForm(); return; }
    }
    var src = val('w209s') || 'manual', when = val('w209d'), msg = val('w209m');
    var applied = when ? new Date(when + 'T12:00:00').toISOString() : null;
    F.busy = true; F.err = ''; keep(); repaintForm();
    try {
      var r;
      if (F.ctx === 'volunteers') {
        var roles = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w209add [data-w209role]:checked')).map(function (x) { return x.getAttribute('data-w209role'); });
        var res = await db.rpc('wa209_volunteer_apply', { p: { name: name, email: email || null, phone: phone || null, roles: roles.length ? roles : ['volunteer'], role_text: roles.length ? roles.map(roleLabel).join(', ') : 'Volunteer',
          source: src, applied_at: applied, message: msg || null, source_ref: 'hub:' + Date.now().toString(36) + ':' + lower(email || name) } });
        if (res && res.error) throw res.error; r = (res && res.data) || {};
      } else {
        if (cvUrl) {   /* the Hub must be able to open the CV before the card is made */
          F.cvMsg = 'Checking the CV link\u2026'; repaintForm();
          var chk = await recruit({ action: 'cv_check', url: cvUrl });
          if (!chk || !chk.ok) { F.busy = false; F.cvMsg = ''; F.err = (chk && chk.error) || 'The Hub could not open that link.'; F.helpOpen = true; repaintForm(); return; }
          F.cvMsg = 'Found: ' + chk.name;
        }
        var lk = [cvUrl, val('w209l')].filter(Boolean);
        var res2 = await db.rpc('wa209_intake', { p: { name: name, email: email || null, phone: phone || null, position_id: val('w209pos') || null, role: val('w209r') || null, source: src, applied_at: applied,
          message: msg || null, links: lk, practice: !!(window.w215 && w215.practice()) } });   /* wave215: added in a walkthrough = practice */
        if (res2 && res2.error) throw res2.error; r = (res2 && res2.data) || {};
        if (cvUrl && r.candidate_id && r.to !== 'volunteers' && !r.duplicate && !r.practice) {   /* wave215: practice leaves the Resumes folders alone */
          try { var fc = await recruit({ action: 'file_cv', id: r.candidate_id, url: cvUrl }); r.cvNote = fc && fc.ok ? (fc.filed ? (fc.copied ? 'CV filed in ' + fc.where : 'CV already in ' + fc.where) : (fc.note || '')) : ('CV not filed: ' + ((fc && fc.error) || 'unknown')); }
          catch (x) { r.cvNote = 'CV not filed: ' + (x.message || x); }
        }
      }
      F.busy = false; shutForm();
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: r.to === 'volunteers' ? 'volunteer_application' : 'candidate', entity_id: String(r.application_id || r.candidate_id || ''), field: 'added_by_hand', old_value: null, new_value: name, changed_by: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && (USER.name || USER.email)) || null }); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
      if (r.to === 'volunteers') {
        toastS(name + (r.duplicate ? ' was already in Applicants received' : ' added to Applicants received (Volunteer Manager)'));
        V.at = 0; if (onView()) { await load(true); paint(); } else counts();
      } else {
        toastS(r.duplicate ? name + ' already has a card for this role' : name + ' added to New' + (r.cvNote ? '. ' + r.cvNote : ''));
        try { if (window.w197) { await w197.loadCands(true); w197.paintBoard(); if (r.candidate_id) w197.openSheet(r.candidate_id); } } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      }
    } catch (e) { F.busy = false; F.err = 'Could not add them: ' + msgOf(e); repaintForm(); }
  }
  var KEEP = {};
  function keep() { ['w209n', 'w209e', 'w209p', 'w209pos', 'w209r', 'w209s', 'w209d', 'w209m', 'w209l', 'w209cv'].forEach(function (id) { var x = document.getElementById(id); if (x) KEEP[id] = x.value; }); KEEP.roles = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w209add [data-w209role]:checked')).map(function (x) { return x.getAttribute('data-w209role'); }); }
  function repaintForm() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w209add'); if (!s) return; keep(); s.innerHTML = formHtml();
    Object.keys(KEEP).forEach(function (id) { if (id === 'roles') return; var x = document.getElementById(id); if (x && KEEP[id] != null) x.value = KEEP[id]; });
    (KEEP.roles || []).forEach(function (k) { var c = s.querySelector('[data-w209role="' + k + '"]'); if (c) c.checked = true; });
    hint();
  }

  /* ---------- Applications: the bar and the card ---------- */
  function barBits() { return staff() ? btn('Add an applicant', 'data-w209a="add" data-ctx="applications"', 'sm pri') : ''; }
  function sheetBits(c) {
    if (!c || !staff()) return '';
    var title = c.role || '';
    try { var p = c.position_id && positions().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(c.position_id); })[0]; if (p) title = p.title; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var r = volRoles(title);
    if (window.w218) return w218.sheetBits(c, r);   /* wave218: one step for every card (roles, servings), the card kept as Volunteering */
    if (!r.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w209move"><b>' + E(r.map(roleLabel).join(', ')) + ' is a volunteer role.</b> Volunteer applicants are handled in the Volunteer Manager, Applicants received, with their record in the CRM and the volunteer directory.' +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn('Move to the Volunteer Manager', 'data-w209move="' + c.id + '"', 'sm pri') + '</div></div>';
  }
  async function moveCard(id) {
    if (window.w218) { w218.open(id); return; }   /* wave218 */
    var c = null; try { c = w197.state().cands.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; } catch (e) { c = null; }
    if (!window.confirm('Move ' + ((c && c.name) || 'this applicant') + ' to the Volunteer Manager? The card leaves Applications; their application, contact and message go to Applicants received.')) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa209_move_to_volunteers', { p_id: Number(id) }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var sh = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (sh) sh.classList.remove('open');
      try { var st = w197.state(); st.open = null; st.cands = st.cands.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(id); }); w197.paintBoard(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      V.at = 0; counts();
      toastS(((c && c.name) || 'The applicant') + ' moved to Applicants received (Volunteer Manager)');
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not move them: ' + msgOf(e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- the Volunteer Manager: Applicants received ---------- */
  function onView() { try { return typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_VIEW === 'applicants'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  async function load(force) {
    if (V.loading) return V.loading;
    if (!force && V.list && Date.now() - V.at < 60000) return V.list;
    V.loading = (async function () {
      try { var r = await db.from('volunteer_applications').select('*').order('applied_at', { ascending: false }).limit(2000); V.list = (r && !r.error && r.data) || []; } catch (e) { V.list = V.list || []; }
      V.at = Date.now(); V.loading = null; V.n = V.list.filter(isOpen).length;
      return V.list;
    })();
    return V.loading;
  }
  function isOpen(a) { return a.status === 'received' || a.status === 'contacted'; }
  async function counts(noLoad) { if (!staff()) return; if (!noLoad) await load(true); try { if (window.WA_APP && typeof WA_APP.renderRail === 'function') WA_APP.renderRail(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('w209count', { detail: V.n })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function shown() {
    var q = lower(V.q);
    return (V.list || []).filter(function (a) {
      if (V.tab === 'open' && !isOpen(a)) return false;
      if (V.tab === 'welcomed' && a.status !== 'welcomed') return false;
      if (V.tab === 'closed' && a.status !== 'not_fit' && a.status !== 'withdrawn') return false;
      if (V.role === '__mine') {   /* wave258: My roles (the roles this person owns) */
        var mine = []; try { mine = (window.w258v && window.w258v.mine && window.w258v.mine()) || []; } catch (e) { mine = []; }
        if (!(a.roles || []).some(function (r) { return mine.indexOf(r) >= 0; })) return false;
      } else if (V.role && (a.roles || []).indexOf(V.role) < 0) return false;
      if (V.src && a.source !== V.src) return false;
      if (q && lower([a.name, a.email, a.phone, a.role_text, a.message, (a.roles || []).map(roleLabel).join(' ')].join(' ')).indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    }).sort(function (x, y) { if (V.tab === 'open' && x.status !== y.status) return x.status === 'received' ? -1 : 1; return String(y.applied_at).localeCompare(String(x.applied_at)); });
  }
  /* wave273: an email to someone who applied elsewhere, asking them to use the volunteer form (wa273_ask_signup) */
  function askHtml(a) {
    var q = (V.ask || {})[a.id]; if (!q) return '';
    if (!q.subject) return '<div class="w273p">' + E(q.err || 'Preparing the email\u2026') + '</div>';
    return '<div class="w273p"><p class="s">' + E(q.subject) + '</p><p class="b">' + E(q.text) + '</p><p class="m">To ' + E(q.to) + ', from ' + E(q.from) + '. Replies come to ' + E(q.reply) + '.</p>' + (q.err ? '<p class="e">' + E(q.err) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="w209acts">' + btn(q.busy ? 'Sending\u2026' : 'Send the email', 'data-w273send="' + a.id + '"', 'sm pri') + btn('Cancel', 'data-w273no="' + a.id + '"', 'sm link') + '</div></div>';
  }
  async function askSignup(id, send) {
    V.ask = V.ask || {}; var q = V.ask[id] = V.ask[id] || {};
    if (q.busy) return; q.busy = send; q.err = ''; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa273_ask_signup', { p_id: Number(id), p_send: !!send }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = (r && r.data) || {}; q.busy = false;
      if (d.ok === false) { q.err = d.error || 'The email did not go.'; paint(); return; }
      if (!send) { q.subject = d.subject; q.text = d.text; q.to = d.to; q.from = d.from; q.reply = d.reply; paint(); return; }
