/* =====================================================================
   WA v210 The Transcript archive shows what each meeting made (06/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (06/10/26): "Can the transcript archive also show what tasks were created with their owner
   and due date to visualize what it processed".
   - Numbered wave210 at release (a parallel session shipped applicant intake as wave209 the same day); the
     database objects keep their wa209 names: wa209_task_link, trg_wa209_task_link, the setting wa209.tx.
   - transcript_tasks (database) links every task a meeting added, banked or closed to its transcript:
     apply_transcript_result writes it for the tasks it makes, trg_wa209_task_link catches the tasks
     process-leadership inserts and the tasks process-transcript closes; the history is backfilled.
   - Recent meetings only (Steel, 06/10/26): meetings dated from September 1, 2026 on; older rows unchanged.
   - Each archive row carries a line under its summary: "Made 4 tasks, closed 1", opened into a table of
     the tasks (title, owner, due date, where it stands today: open, overdue, done, in the bank), each
     title opening the task's drawer (#task/<id>). Read through transcript_outcomes(), which shows a task
     only when the reader may see it (the transcript's rule, then the task's own).
   Hooks: wraps renderTranscripts (after wave62's chips) and loadTranscriptsData (clears the cache). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa210) return; window.__wa210 = true;
  var C = { by: {}, pending: null, want: {} };
  /* Steel (06/10/26): "Only do it for the recent meetings please": meetings dated from September 1, 2026 on */
  var SINCE = '2026-09-01';
  function recent(row) { var d = row.meeting_date || String(row.created_at || '').slice(0, 10); return !!d && d >= SINCE; }

  var css = ''
    + '.w210line{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:0 16px 10px;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);}'
    + '.w210line b{color:var(--wa-text);font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w210line>span{line-height:1.5;}'
    + '.w210t{background:none;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:14px;padding:2px 10px;font:inherit;font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-green);cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w210t:hover{background:#F4F2EA;}'
    + '.w210box{margin:0 16px 12px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;background:#FFFAF2;}'
    + '.w210box table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px;}'
    + '.w210box th{text-align:left;font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);padding:7px 10px;background:#F4F2EA;font-weight:700;}'
    + '.w210box td{padding:7px 10px;border-top:1px solid var(--wa-line);vertical-align:top;}'
    + '.w210box td a{color:var(--wa-green);text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;}'
    + '.w210box td a:hover{text-decoration:underline;}'
    + '.w210s{display:inline-block;border-radius:10px;padding:1px 8px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;}'
    + '.w210s.open{background:#E6EFE9;color:#103F39;}'
    + '.w210s.late{background:#F9E3DC;color:#8A2E1A;}'
    + '.w210s.done{background:#EDEBE4;color:#5a5a55;}'
    + '.w210s.bank{background:#EFEBF7;color:#4b4280;}'
    + '.w210s.closed{background:#FBF0D5;color:#6b4e07;}'
    + '.w210k{font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);padding:7px 10px 2px;border-top:1px solid var(--wa-line);background:#FCFAF4;}'
    + '.w210none{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted);padding:8px 10px;}'
    + '.w210m{display:none;margin-top:4px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);}'
    + '@media (max-width:640px){.w210box th:nth-child(n+2),.w210box td:nth-child(n+2){display:none;}.w210m{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px 10px;align-items:center;}}';
  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = css; document.head.appendChild(st);

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function today() { try { return (typeof WA_TODAY === 'string' && WA_TODAY) || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); } catch (e) { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); } }
  function day(s) { if (!s) return ''; try { return new Date(s + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return s; } }
  function n(k, c) { return c + ' ' + (c === 1 ? k : k + 's'); }

  function status(r) {
    if (r.kind === 'closed') return ['closed', 'Closed by this meeting'];
    if (r.banked) return ['bank', 'In the bank' + (r.due_date ? ' until ' + day(r.due_date) : '')];
    if (r.done) return ['done', 'Done'];
    if (r.due_date && r.due_date < today()) return ['late', 'Overdue'];
    return ['open', 'Open'];
  }

  function line(id) {
    var rows = C.by[id];
    if (!rows) return '<div class="w210line">Reading what it made\u2026</div>';
    var made = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.kind === 'added'; }).length;
    var banked = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.kind === 'banked'; }).length;
    var closed = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.kind === 'closed'; }).length;
    var open = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.kind !== 'closed' && !r.done && !r.banked; }).length;
    var late = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.kind !== 'closed' && !r.done && !r.banked && r.due_date && r.due_date < today(); }).length;
    if (!rows.length) return '<div class="w210line">This meeting made no tasks.</div>';
    var bits = [];
    if (made) bits.push('<b>' + n('task', made) + '</b> made');
    if (banked) bits.push('<b>' + banked + '</b> put in the bank');
    if (closed) bits.push('<b>' + n('task', closed) + '</b> closed');
    var tail = (open ? open + ' still open' + (late ? ', ' + late + ' overdue' : '') : (made && !banked ? 'all done' : ''));
    return '<div class="w210line">' + bits.join(', ') + (tail ? ' \u00b7 ' + tail : '')
      + ' <button type="button" class="w210t" data-w210open="' + id + '">Show the tasks</button></div>';
  }

  function table(id) {
    var rows = C.by[id] || [];
    if (!rows.length) return '';
    var groups = [['added', 'Tasks this meeting made'], ['banked', 'Put in the bank until later'], ['closed', 'Tasks this meeting closed']];
    var h = '<div class="w210box" data-w210box="' + id + '"><table><thead><tr><th>Task</th><th>Owner</th><th>Due</th><th>Today</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
    groups.forEach(function (g) {
      var rs = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.kind === g[0]; });
      if (!rs.length) return;
      h += '<tr><td colspan="4" class="w210k">' + g[1] + ' (' + rs.length + ')</td></tr>';
      rs.forEach(function (r) {
        var s = status(r);
        var title = r.live && r.task_id ? '<a href="#task/' + r.task_id + '">' + E(r.title || 'Untitled') + '</a>' : E(r.title || 'Untitled');
        var dueTxt = r.due_date ? day(r.due_date) : '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">no date</span>';
        h += '<tr><td>' + title + (r.department && DEPT_LABEL && DEPT_LABEL[r.department] ? ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11px">' + E(DEPT_LABEL[r.department]) + '</span>' : '')
          + '<div class="w210m"><span>' + E(r.owner || 'No owner') + '</span><span>' + (r.due_date ? 'Due ' + day(r.due_date) : 'No due date') + '</span><span class="w210s ' + s[0] + '">' + s[1] + '</span></div></td>'
          + '<td>' + E(r.owner || '\u2014') + '</td>'
          + '<td>' + dueTxt + '</td>'
          + '<td><span class="w210s ' + s[0] + '">' + s[1] + '</span></td></tr>';
      });
    });
    return h + '</tbody></table></div>';
  }

  function paint(list) {
    list = list || document.getElementById('txList');
    if (!list) return;
    list.querySelectorAll('.tx-item[data-tx]').forEach(function (it) {
      var id = Number(it.getAttribute('data-tx'));
      var row = (typeof txCache !== 'undefined' ? txCache : []).find(function (r) { return r.id === id; });
      if (!row || !row.processed || row.archive_only || !recent(row)) return;
      var old = it.querySelector('.w210line'); if (old) old.remove();
      var ob = it.querySelector('.w210box'); var wasOpen = !!ob; if (ob) ob.remove();
      var anchor = it.querySelector('.tx-actions');
      if (!anchor) return;
      anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', line(id));
      if (wasOpen && C.by[id] && C.by[id].length) anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', table(id));
      var b = it.querySelector('[data-w210open]');
      if (b) b.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        var box = it.querySelector('.w210box');
        if (box) { box.remove(); b.textContent = 'Show the tasks'; return; }
        anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', table(id));
        b.textContent = 'Hide the tasks';
      });
      if (!C.by[id]) C.want[id] = true;
    });
    fetchWanted();
  }

  async function fetchWanted() {
    var ids = Object.keys(C.want).map(Number).filter(function (id) { return !C.by[id]; });
    if (!ids.length || C.pending) return;
    C.want = {};
    C.pending = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('transcript_outcomes', { p_ids: ids });
        var rows = (r && !r.error && r.data) || [];
        ids.forEach(function (id) { C.by[id] = []; });
        rows.forEach(function (x) { (C.by[x.transcript_id] = C.by[x.transcript_id] || []).push(x); });
      } catch (e) { ids.forEach(function (id) { C.by[id] = C.by[id] || []; }); }
      C.pending = null;
    })();
    await C.pending;
    paint();
    if (Object.keys(C.want).length) fetchWanted();
  }

  function wire() {
    if (typeof renderTranscripts === 'function' && !renderTranscripts.__w210) {
      var __rt = renderTranscripts;
      renderTranscripts = function () { __rt.apply(this, arguments); try { paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } };
      renderTranscripts.__w210 = 1;
    }
    if (typeof loadTranscriptsData === 'function' && !loadTranscriptsData.__w210) {
      var __ld = loadTranscriptsData;
      loadTranscriptsData = async function () { var r = await __ld.apply(this, arguments); C.by = {}; return r; };
      loadTranscriptsData.__w210 = 1;
    }
  }
  wire();
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { await __sa.apply(this, arguments); try { wire(); paint(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } };
  }
  window.w210 = { paint: paint, cache: C, since: SINCE };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v211 Recruitment, from the HR meeting (06/10/26): "still interested?" texts, the offer letter, the CV read
   =====================================================================
   Numbered wave211 at release (a parallel session shipped the Transcript archive's tasks as wave210 the same day); the
   database objects keep their wa210 names: wa210_stage_moved, trg_wa210_stage, build/data/wa210_recruit.sql, wa210b_call_quo_gates.
   Steel, Valex and Samson (06/10/26): text applicants from the board so a tap on their link moves them to Reviewing;
   moving a card to Offer asks "do we send them the offer now?" and the letter goes with the offer note by email;
   the team is told when a card moves; "let's be able to process every resume, no matter the file type".
   - The sheet at New or Reviewing: Text: still interested? (the exact text first, then send). The board's selection
     bar does the same for several. Cards say Texted Oct 6, or Said yes.
   - Offer: moving a card to Offer opens the offer letter (language, reports to, hours, start, reply by, who signs, the
     note), a preview, then Create the letter and email it (or the letter only). The sheet at Offer and Hired opens it again.
     wave275 (02/11/26): "Hours a week" is a choice of 4, 6 or 8 (default 4), and "Onboarding manager" is required, defaulting from the
     position (offer_preview's manager.default, how it was chosen said under it; the choices are the active roster). The server refuses an
     offer without them in a plain sentence, shown here; once the offer is out, the manager is asked for times (onboard-run ask_manager).
   - From the CV: what the CV says (current role, experience, education, languages, skills, a summary); Read the CV.
   - Slack when a card moves to Interview, Offer or Hired is server-side (recruit-actions stage_moved, trigger).
   Edge: recruit-actions. Hooks: w199.cardBits, w199.sheetBits, w199's selection bar, parts197 moveTo. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', FLAG = '#B3402F';
  var M = { kind: '', id: null, ids: [], data: null, busy: false, err: '', f: null, preview: '' };

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function btn(label, attrs, kind) { return '<button type="button" class="w197b' + (kind ? ' ' + kind : '') + '" ' + (attrs || '') + '>' + label + '</button>'; }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function cands() { try { return (window.w197 && w197.state().cands) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function cand(id) { return cands().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  function short(t) { try { return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function iso(d) { var p = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }; return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate()); }
  function myName() { try { return (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && (USER.name || USER.full_name)) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function answered(c) { var s = String((c && c.summary) || ''); return s.indexOf('\u2705 Still interested') === 0 ? 'yes' : (s.indexOf('\u274c No longer interested') === 0 ? 'no' : ''); }
  async function fn(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('recruit-actions', { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) {
      var msg = r.error.message || String(r.error);
      try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) { var j = await r.error.context.json(); if (j && j.error) msg = j.error; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      throw new Error(msg);
    }
    if (r && r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r ? r.data : null;
  }
  async function reload(id) { try { await w197.loadCands(true); w197.paintBoard(); var st = w197.state(); if (id && String(st.open) === String(id)) w197.openSheet(id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }

  /* ---------- the card and the sheet ---------- */
  function cardBits(c) {
    if (!c) return '';
    var a = answered(c), out = c.practice ? '<span class="w215prac" title="Made in an Avenue Academy walkthrough: never sent to Slack or texted">Practice</span>' : '';
    if (a === 'yes') out += '<span class="w211pill ok">Said yes</span>';
    else if (c.interest_texted_at && (c.status === 'new' || c.status === 'reviewing')) out += '<span class="w211pill">Texted ' + E(short(c.interest_texted_at)) + '</span>';
    if (c.offer_sent_at && (c.status === 'offer' || c.status === 'hired')) out += '<span class="w211pill ok">Offer sent ' + E(short(c.offer_sent_at)) + '</span>';
    else if (c.status === 'offer' && !c.offer_sent_at) out += '<span class="w211pill gold">Offer not sent</span>';
    return out;
  }
  function cvBox(c) {
    var p = c.cv_profile, has = !!(c.cv_path || c.drive_file_id);
    if (!p && !has) return '';
    if (!p) return '<div class="w211cv"><b>The CV</b> ' + (c.cv_read_error ? '<span class="w211err">Could not be read: ' + E(c.cv_read_error) + '</span> ' : '<span class="w197note">Not read yet (the Hub reads new CVs every 15 minutes).</span> ') + btn(c.cv_read_error ? 'Try again' : 'Read the CV now', 'data-w211cv="' + c.id + '"', 'sm') + '</div>';
    var line = function (k, v) { return v ? '<div><small>' + E(k) + '</small> ' + E(v) + '</div>' : ''; };
    return '<div class="w211cv"><b>From the CV</b>' + (p.summary ? '<p>' + E(p.summary) + '</p>' : '') +
      line('Now', p.current_role) + line('Experience', p.years_experience != null ? p.years_experience + ' years' : '') + line('Education', p.education) + line('Languages', (p.languages || []).join(', ')) + line('City', p.city) +
      ((p.skills || []).length ? '<div class="w211sk">' + p.skills.map(function (s) { return '<span>' + E(s) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      (p.name && c.name && String(p.name).toLowerCase() !== String(c.name).toLowerCase() ? '<p class="w197note">The CV says <b>' + E(p.name) + '</b>. ' + btn('Use this name', 'data-w211name="' + c.id + '"', 'sm link') + '</p>' : '') +
      '<p class="w197note">Read ' + E(short(c.cv_read_at)) + '. ' + btn('Read it again', 'data-w211cv="' + c.id + '"', 'sm link') + '</p></div>';
  }
  function sheetBits(c) {
    if (!c || !staff()) return '';
    var h = '';
    if (c.status === 'new' || c.status === 'reviewing') {
      var a = answered(c);
      h += '<div class="w211box"><b>Still interested?</b> ' + (a === 'yes' ? 'They said yes by text.' : a === 'no' ? 'They said no by text.' : (c.interest_texted_at ? 'Texted ' + E(short(c.interest_texted_at)) + ', no answer yet. ' : 'One text with their own link: a tap on Yes moves them to Reviewing. ')) +
        (a ? '' : btn(c.interest_texted_at ? 'Text again' : 'Text: still interested?', 'data-w211int="' + c.id + '"', 'sm' + (c.interest_texted_at ? '' : ' pri'))) + '</div>';
    }
    if (c.status === 'offer' || c.status === 'hired') {
      h += '<div class="w211box"><b>Offer letter</b> ' + (c.offer_doc_url ? '<a class="w197b sm" href="' + E(c.offer_doc_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the letter</a> ' : '') +
        (c.offer_sent_at ? 'Emailed ' + E(short(c.offer_sent_at)) + (c.offer_email_to ? ' (' + E(c.offer_email_to) + ')' : '') + '. ' : (c.offer_doc_url ? 'Created, not emailed yet. ' : 'Not sent yet. ')) +
        btn(c.offer_sent_at ? 'Send it again' : 'Send the offer letter', 'data-w211offer="' + c.id + '"', 'sm' + (c.offer_sent_at ? '' : ' pri')) + '</div>';
    }
    var rec = '';
    if (c.impressions) rec += '<div><small>Interview impressions</small><p>' + E(c.impressions) + '</p></div>';
    if (c.accepted_on || c.start_date) rec += '<div><small>Hired</small> ' + (c.accepted_on ? 'accepted ' + E(c.accepted_on) : '') + (c.accepted_on && c.start_date ? ', ' : '') + (c.start_date ? 'starts ' + E(c.start_date) : '') + '</div>';
    if (c.close_reason && (c.status === 'declined' || c.status === 'withdrawn')) rec += '<div><small>Why closed</small> ' + E(c.close_reason) + '</div>';
    if (rec) h += '<div class="w211cv">' + rec + '</div>';
    return h + cvBox(c);
  }

  /* ---------- the panel ---------- */
  function panel(inner) {
    var s = document.getElementById('w211m'); if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w211m'; document.body.appendChild(s); }
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w211a="shut"></div><div class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w211h">' + btn('Close', 'data-w211a="shut"', 'sm x') + inner + '</div>';
    s.classList.add('open');
  }
  function shut() { var s = document.getElementById('w211m'); if (s) s.classList.remove('open'); M.kind = ''; }

  /* "still interested?" */
  async function askInterest(ids) {
    M = { kind: 'int', ids: ids, data: null, busy: true, err: '' }; paintInt();
    try { M.data = await fn({ action: 'interest_text', ids: ids, dry_run: true }); } catch (e) { M.err = e.message; }
    M.busy = false; paintInt();
  }
  function paintInt() {
    if (M.kind !== 'int') return;
    var d = M.data, ready = d ? (d.texts || []).filter(function (t) { return !t.skip; }) : [], skip = d ? (d.texts || []).filter(function (t) { return t.skip; }) : [];
    panel('<h3 id="w211h">Text: still interested?</h3>' +
      (M.busy && !d ? '<p class="w197note">Preparing the texts\u2026</p>' : '') + (M.err ? '<p class="w211err" role="alert">' + E(M.err) + '</p>' : '') +
      (d ? '<p class="w197note">From the ops line, in their language, with their own link: Yes moves the card to Reviewing, No to Withdrawn. ' + E(d.window || '') + '</p>' +
        (ready.length ? '<ul class="w211txt">' + ready.map(function (t) { return '<li><b>' + E(t.name) + '</b> <small>' + E(t.phone) + ' \u00b7 ' + (t.lang === 'fr' ? 'French' : 'English') + '</small><p>' + E(t.body) + '</p></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p>Nobody to text.</p>') +
        (skip.length ? '<p class="w197note"><b>Not texted:</b> ' + skip.map(function (t) { return E(t.name) + ' (' + E(t.skip) + ')'; }).join(', ') + '</p>' : '') +
        '<div class="acts">' + (ready.length ? btn(M.busy ? 'Sending\u2026' : 'Send ' + ready.length + (ready.length === 1 ? ' text' : ' texts'), 'data-w211a="intsend"' + (M.busy ? ' disabled' : ''), 'pri') : '') + btn('Cancel', 'data-w211a="shut"') + '</div>' : ''));
  }
  async function sendInt() {
    if (M.busy) return; M.busy = true; paintInt();
    try { var r = await fn({ action: 'interest_text', ids: M.ids, dry_run: false }); shut(); toastS((r.queued || 0) + (r.queued === 1 ? ' text' : ' texts') + ' on the way (9 am to 7 pm)'); await reload(M.ids.length === 1 ? M.ids[0] : null); }
    catch (e) { M.busy = false; M.err = 'Not sent: ' + e.message; paintInt(); }
  }

  /* the offer letter */
  function defaults(c) {
    var d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() + 7);
    var fr = /\b(Fran\u00e7ais|French)\b/i.test(String(c.summary || ''));
    return { email: c.email || '', lang: fr ? 'fr' : 'en', reports_to: '', hours: '4', start: '', respond_by: iso(d), signer_email: '', signer_title: '', note: '', manager_member_id: '' };
  }
  /* wave275: the onboarding manager (required) and the hours (4, 6 or 8) */
  var HOURS = ['4', '6', '8'];
  function managerDefault(r) {   /* the position's manager, put in once the preview says who, unless HR has chosen already */
    var d = r && r.manager && r.manager.default;
    if (!M.f.manager_member_id && d && d.member_id != null && d.available !== false) M.f.manager_member_id = String(d.member_id);   /* an unavailable default stays a placeholder */
  }
  function managerBits(f, d) {
    var m = (d && d.manager) || { default: null, choices: [] }, def = m.default || {}, choices = m.choices || [];
    var who = def.how === 'team_lead' ? (def.name || 'They') + ' is the only leader of the position\u2019s department' : (def.name || 'They') + ' is who the position reports to';
    var how = def.member_id == null ? 'The Hub cannot tell who manages this position, so choose the onboarding manager.'
      : def.available === false ? who + (def.why === 'no_email' ? ', but has no email on the roster' : ', but is no longer active on the roster') + ', so choose someone else.'
      : who + '.';
    return '<label for="w211o_manager">Onboarding manager</label><select id="w211o_manager" required aria-describedby="w211o_mnote"><option value="">Choose the onboarding manager...</option>' +
      choices.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + E(p.id) + '"' + (String(p.id) === String(f.manager_member_id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<p class="w197note" id="w211o_mnote">' + (d ? E(how) + ' ' : '') + 'They are asked to propose the onboarding call and the 1-on-1 as soon as the offer goes out.</p>';
  }
  function askOffer(id) {
    var c = cand(id); if (!c || !staff()) return;
    M = { kind: 'offer', id: c.id, data: null, busy: false, err: '', f: defaults(c), preview: '' }; paintOffer();
    fn({ action: 'offer_preview', id: c.id, f: M.f }).then(function (r) { if (M.kind !== 'offer' || M.id !== c.id) return; readOffer(); M.data = r; managerDefault(r); if (!M.f.signer_email) M.f.signer_email = r.signer_email || ''; if (!M.f.signer_title && r.signer_title) M.f.signer_title = r.signer_title; paintOffer(); }).catch(function (e) { M.err = e.message; paintOffer(); });
  }
  function readOffer() {
    ['email', 'lang', 'reports_to', 'hours', 'start', 'respond_by', 'signer_email', 'signer_title', 'note'].forEach(function (k) { var x = document.getElementById('w211o_' + k); if (x) M.f[k] = x.value; });
    var mg = document.getElementById('w211o_manager'); if (mg && (mg.options.length > 1 || !M.f.manager_member_id)) M.f.manager_member_id = mg.value;   /* wave275 */
  }
  function paintOffer() {
    if (M.kind !== 'offer') return;
    var c = cand(M.id) || {}, f = M.f, d = M.data;
    var inp = function (k, label, type, extra) { return '<label for="w211o_' + k + '">' + label + '</label><input id="w211o_' + k + '" type="' + (type || 'text') + '" value="' + E(f[k]) + '"' + (extra || '') + '>'; };
    var test = d && d.mail_mode !== 'live' && !d.practice;
    var signers = (d && d.signers) || (f.signer_email ? [{ email: f.signer_email, name: f.signer_email, has_sig: true }] : []);
    panel('<h3 id="w211h">Send the offer letter to ' + E(c.name || '') + '?</h3><p class="w197note">' + E((d && d.role) || c.role || '') + '. The letter is a signed PDF in the Welfare Avenue brand, saved in Recruitment > Offer Letters and emailed with your note; the signer gets a copy and their replies.</p>' +
      (d && d.practice ? '<p class="w211warn">Practice: the letter is saved as PRACTICE and emailed to the practice applicant\u2019s own address (yours), even while email is in test mode.</p>' : '') +
      (test ? '<p class="w211warn">Email is in test mode: the email goes to the test inboxes (' + E(d.test_to) + '), not to them. Turning email on is an admin setting (CRM).</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="w211two">' + inp('email', 'Their email', 'email') + '<div><label for="w211o_lang">Language</label><select id="w211o_lang"><option value="en"' + (f.lang === 'en' ? ' selected' : '') + '>English</option><option value="fr"' + (f.lang === 'fr' ? ' selected' : '') + '>French</option></select></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w211two">' + inp('reports_to', 'Reports to (optional)') + '<label for="w211o_hours">Hours a week</label><select id="w211o_hours">' + HOURS.map(function (h) { return '<option value="' + h + '"' + (String(f.hours) === h ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + h + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>' +
      managerBits(f, d) +
      '<div class="w211two">' + inp('start', 'Start date (optional)', 'date') + inp('respond_by', 'Reply by', 'date') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w211two"><div><label for="w211o_signer_email">Signed by</label><select id="w211o_signer_email">' + signers.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + E(x.email) + '"' + (x.email === f.signer_email ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x.name) + (x.has_sig ? '' : ' (no signature yet)') + (d && x.email === d.default_signer ? ' \u00b7 default' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>' + inp('signer_title', 'Their title') + '</div>' +
      (d && d.signers && f.signer_email && d.default_signer !== f.signer_email ? '<div class="acts">' + btn('Make ' + E((signers.filter(function (x) { return x.email === f.signer_email; })[0] || {}).name || 'them') + ' the default signer', 'data-w211a="osigner"', 'sm') + '</div>' : '') +
      (d && d.signer_email === f.signer_email && !d.signer_has_sig ? '<p class="w211warn">' + E(d.signer || 'The signer') + ' has not saved a signature yet, so the letter has their name only. Save it in Volunteer Manager &gt; Sign-offs &gt; My signature.</p>' : '') +
      '<label for="w211o_note">The offer note (in the letter and the email)</label><textarea id="w211o_note" rows="4" placeholder="A personal line: why them, what comes next">' + E(f.note) + '</textarea>' +
      (M.err ? '<p class="w211err" role="alert">' + E(M.err) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn('Preview the letter', 'data-w211a="oprev"', 'sm') + '</div>' +
      (M.preview ? '<iframe class="w211prev" title="The offer letter" sandbox="" srcdoc="' + E(M.preview) + '"></iframe>' : '') +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn(M.busy ? 'Working\u2026' : 'Create the letter and email it', 'data-w211a="osend" data-mail="1"' + (M.busy ? ' disabled' : ''), 'pri') + btn('Create the letter only', 'data-w211a="osend" data-mail="0"' + (M.busy ? ' disabled' : '')) + btn('Not now', 'data-w211a="shut"') + '</div>');
  }
  async function signerChanged() {
    readOffer(); M.f.signer_title = ''; M.preview = '';
    try { var r = await fn({ action: 'offer_preview', id: M.id, f: M.f }); M.data = r; M.f.signer_title = r.signer_title || ''; } catch (e) { M.err = e.message; }
    paintOffer();
  }
  async function makeDefaultSigner() {
    readOffer();
    try { await fn({ action: 'offer_signer_set', email: M.f.signer_email }); if (M.data) M.data.default_signer = M.f.signer_email; toastS('Default signer saved'); } catch (e) { M.err = e.message; }
    paintOffer();
  }
  async function previewOffer() {
    readOffer(); M.err = '';
    try { var r = await fn({ action: 'offer_preview', id: M.id, f: M.f }); M.data = r; M.preview = r.html || ''; } catch (e) { M.err = e.message; }
    paintOffer();
  }
  async function sendOffer(mail) {
    if (M.busy) return; readOffer();
    if (mail && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(M.f.email || '')) { M.err = 'Their email, please.'; paintOffer(); return; }
    if (!M.f.respond_by) { M.err = 'The reply-by date, please.'; paintOffer(); return; }
    if (!M.f.manager_member_id) { M.err = 'Choose the onboarding manager, please: they are asked for times as soon as the offer goes out.'; paintOffer(); return; }   /* wave275 */
    M.busy = true; M.err = ''; paintOffer();
    try {
      var r = await fn({ action: 'offer_send', id: M.id, f: M.f, email: !!mail }); var id = M.id; shut();
      var asked = r.manager && r.manager.asked ? ' ' + r.manager.name + (r.manager.already ? ' was already asked to propose times.' : ' was asked to propose times.') : '', warn = (r.warnings || []).length ? ' ' + r.warnings.join(' ') : '';   /* wave275 */
      toastS((r.emailed ? (r.emailed.mode === 'live' || r.emailed.mode === 'practice' ? 'Offer letter emailed (a copy to ' + (r.signer || 'the signer') + ')' : 'Offer letter emailed to the test inboxes (test mode)') : 'Offer letter created in Offer Letters') + (asked || warn ? '.' + asked + warn : ''));
      await reload(id);
    } catch (e) { M.busy = false; M.err = e.message; paintOffer(); }
  }

  /* the CV */
  async function readCv(id) {
    toastS('Reading the CV\u2026');
    try { var r = await fn({ action: 'cv_read', id: Number(id) }); toastS(r.ok ? 'CV read' + (r.filled && r.filled.length ? ' (filled in ' + r.filled.join(' and ') + ')' : '') : 'Could not read it: ' + r.error); } catch (e) { toastS('Could not read it: ' + e.message); }
    await reload(id);
  }
  async function useName(id) {
    var c = cand(id); if (!c || !c.cv_profile || !c.cv_profile.name) return;
    try { var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').update({ name: String(c.cv_profile.name).slice(0, 120), updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: myName() }).eq('id', c.id); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; c.name = c.cv_profile.name; toastS('Name updated'); w197.paintBoard(); w197.openSheet(c.id); } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }

  /* the selection bar (w199): several at once */
  /* wave215: practice cards (Academy walkthroughs) can be cleared from the bar: your own, or an admin everyone's */
  function isAdm() { try { return !!(typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function barBits() {
    var n = cands().filter(function (c) { return c.practice; }).length;
    return n && staff() ? btn('Clear practice (' + n + ')', 'data-w211a="pclear" title="Remove the applications made in Avenue Academy walkthroughs' + (isAdm() ? ' (everyone\u2019s)' : ' (yours)') + '"', 'sm') : '';
  }
  async function clearPractice() {
    var all = isAdm();
    if (!window.confirm(all ? 'Remove every practice application (made in the Avenue Academy walkthroughs, by anyone)?' : 'Remove your practice applications?')) return;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa215_clear_practice', { p_all: all }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; var d = r.data || {}; toastS('Removed ' + (d.applications || 0) + (d.applications === 1 ? ' practice application' : ' practice applications')); await reload(null); }
    catch (e) { toastS('Could not clear: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }
  function bulkBits(ids) { return staff() ? btn('Text: still interested?', 'data-w211a="intbulk"' + (ids && ids.length ? '' : ' disabled'), 'sm') : ''; }
  function selected() { try { var s = w199.state().sel || {}; return Object.keys(s).filter(function (k) { return s[k]; }).map(Number); } catch (e) { return []; } }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w211a],[data-w211int],[data-w211offer],[data-w211cv],[data-w211name]') : null; if (!t) return;
    var a = t.getAttribute('data-w211a');
    if (a === 'shut') { shut(); return; }
    if (a === 'intsend') { sendInt(); return; }
    if (a === 'pclear') { clearPractice(); return; }
    if (a === 'intbulk') { var ids = selected(); if (ids.length) askInterest(ids); return; }
    if (a === 'oprev') { previewOffer(); return; }
    if (a === 'osigner') { makeDefaultSigner(); return; }
    if (a === 'osend') { sendOffer(t.getAttribute('data-mail') === '1'); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w211int')) { askInterest([Number(t.getAttribute('data-w211int'))]); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w211offer')) { askOffer(t.getAttribute('data-w211offer')); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w211cv')) { readCv(t.getAttribute('data-w211cv')); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w211name')) { useName(t.getAttribute('data-w211name')); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (e.target && e.target.id === 'w211o_signer_email' && M.kind === 'offer') signerChanged(); });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') { var s = document.getElementById('w211m'); if (s && s.classList.contains('open')) shut(); } });

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'w211css';
  css.textContent =
    '.w211pill{display:inline-block;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:#4a5a56;margin:2px 4px 0 0;}.w211pill.ok{background:rgba(42,140,122,.16);color:#1d6b5d;}.w211pill.gold{background:rgba(220,165,39,.24);color:#7a5a0c;}' +
    '.w211box{margin:10px 0;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(220,165,39,.12);font-size:13.5px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:center;}' +
    '.w211cv{margin:10px 0;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);font-size:13.5px;}.w211cv p{margin:6px 0;}.w211cv small{display:inline-block;min-width:78px;color:#5b6b67;font-weight:600;}.w211sk{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;margin-top:6px;}.w211sk span{font-size:12px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);border-radius:6px;padding:2px 7px;}' +
    '.w211err{color:' + FLAG + ';}.w211warn{background:rgba(220,165,39,.2);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:13.5px;color:#5c4408;}' +
    '#w211m{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9100;display:none;}#w211m.open{display:block;}#w211m .bk{position:absolute;inset:0;background:rgba(16,32,30,.4);}' +
    '#w211m .pn{position:absolute;right:0;top:0;bottom:0;width:min(600px,100%);background:#FFFAF2;overflow:auto;padding:18px 16px 28px;box-shadow:-8px 0 24px rgba(0,0,0,.12);}#w211m .x{float:right;}' +
    '#w211m label{display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';margin:12px 0 4px;}' +
    '#w211m input,#w211m select,#w211m textarea{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font:inherit;font-size:15px;padding:9px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);border-radius:8px;background:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '#w211m .acts{display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:14px;flex-wrap:wrap;}.w211two{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;}@media (max-width:520px){.w211two{grid-template-columns:1fr;}}' +
    '.w211txt{list-style:none;margin:8px 0;padding:0;}.w211txt li{border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);padding:8px 0;}.w211txt small{color:#5b6b67;}.w211txt p{margin:4px 0 0;font-size:13.5px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.04);border-radius:8px;padding:8px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;}' +
    '.w211prev{width:100%;height:420px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);border-radius:8px;margin-top:10px;background:#FFFAF2;}';
  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(css);

  window.w211 = { barBits: barBits, cardBits: cardBits, sheetBits: sheetBits, bulkBits: bulkBits, askOffer: askOffer, askInterest: askInterest, state: function () { return M; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v211b Stage gates: what a card needs before it can move on (Steel, 06/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   "When candidates have been moved from New to Reviewing it should require the user to add someone as handling the
   candidate. From New or Reviewing to Interview it should ask for the call log (which can be pulled from QUO to get the
   transcript) status, handled filled, interview time and date." and "I'd also like to enforce mandatory fields when
   moving them from one stage to the other".
   Forward moves need everything the stages before them need (New straight to Offer asks for all of it):
     Reviewing  Handled by
     Interview  + the position, a call logged (picked from Quo with its summary and transcript, or logged by hand),
                  the interview's day and time
     Offer      + their email, the interview has happened, interview impressions
     Hired      + the offer letter (or "the offer was made another way"), the day they accepted, the start date
     Declined / Withdrawn  a reason, always
   Moving a card back a stage asks nothing. When nothing is missing the card just moves. The form only shows what is
   missing; the card moves when it is complete. Used by parts197 moveTo (drag, sheet buttons) and w199's selection bar.
   Columns (wa210b): hub_candidates.close_reason, accepted_on, start_date, impressions; hub_candidate_calls.quo_call_id,
   summary, transcript, duration. Edge: recruit-actions quo_calls, quo_log. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var RANK = { 'new': 0, reviewing: 1, interview: 2, offer: 3, hired: 4, declined: -1, withdrawn: -1, volunteering: -1 };
  var LABEL = { 'new': 'New', reviewing: 'Reviewing', interview: 'Interview', offer: 'Offer', hired: 'Hired', declined: 'Declined', withdrawn: 'Withdrawn', volunteering: 'Volunteering' };
  var REASONS = {
    declined: ['Not the right fit for the role', 'Did not reply after we reached out', 'Not available when we need them', 'The position was filled', 'Did not meet the requirements', 'Other'],
    withdrawn: ['Took another role', 'No longer interested', 'Not available anymore', 'Did not reply after we reached out', 'Other']
  };
  var OUT = [['reached', 'Reached'], ['voicemail', 'Left a voicemail'], ['no_answer', 'No answer'], ['wrong_number', 'Wrong number']];
  var G = { c: null, st: '', ids: null, resolve: null, v: {}, err: '', quo: null, quoBusy: false, quoErr: '', busy: false, outside: false };

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function btn(label, attrs, kind) { return '<button type="button" class="w197b' + (kind ? ' ' + kind : '') + '" ' + (attrs || '') + '>' + label + '</button>'; }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false && m.name && !/\btest\b/i.test(m.name); }); }
  function P() { try { return w199.state(); } catch (e) { return { pos: [], calls: [] }; } }
  function cands() { try { return (window.w197 && w197.state().cands) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function myName() { try { return (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && (USER.name || USER.full_name)) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function callsOf(id) { return (P().calls || []).filter(function (k) { return String(k.candidate_id) === String(id); }); }
  function validEmail(s) { return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(String(s || '').trim()); }
  function pad(n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
  function local(t) { if (!t) return ''; var d = new Date(t); if (isNaN(d)) return ''; return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()) + 'T' + pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes()); }
  function today() { var d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
  function when(t) { try { return new Date(t).toLocaleString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function closed(st) { return st === 'declined' || st === 'withdrawn' || st === 'volunteering'; }

  /* what is missing for a move; [] means it can move now */
  function missing(c, st, v) {
    v = v || {};
    if (!c || !st || c.status === st) return [];
    if (closed(st)) return v.reason ? [] : ['reason'];
    var from = RANK[c.status || 'new'], to = RANK[st];
    if (to == null || (from != null && from >= 0 && to <= from)) return [];   /* back a stage */
    var get = function (k) { return v[k] != null && v[k] !== '' ? v[k] : c[k]; };
    var out = [];
    if (to >= 1 && !get('owner')) out.push('owner');
    if (to >= 2) {
      if (!get('position_id')) out.push('position_id');
      if (!callsOf(c.id).length) out.push('call');
      if (!get('interview_at')) out.push('interview_at');
    }
    if (to >= 3) {
      if (!validEmail(get('email'))) out.push('email');
      var iv = get('interview_at'); if (iv && new Date(iv).getTime() > Date.now()) out.push('interview_done');
      if (String(get('impressions') || '').trim().length < 15) out.push('impressions');
    }
    if (to >= 4) {
      if (!c.offer_doc_url && !c.offer_sent_at && !v.offer_outside) out.push('offer');
      if (!get('accepted_on')) out.push('accepted_on');
      if (!get('start_date')) out.push('start_date');
    }
    return out;
  }
  var WHAT = {
    owner: 'who handles them', position_id: 'the position', call: 'a call logged', interview_at: 'the interview day and time',
    email: 'their email', interview_done: 'the interview to have happened', impressions: 'interview impressions',
    offer: 'the offer letter', accepted_on: 'the day they accepted', start_date: 'the start date', reason: 'a reason'
  };
  function says(list) { var w = list.map(function (k) { return WHAT[k] || k; }); return w.length > 1 ? w.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + w[w.length - 1] : (w[0] || ''); }

  /* one card: resolves with the extra fields for the move, or null when cancelled */
  function gate(c, st) {
    if (!c) return Promise.resolve(null);
    if (!missing(c, st, {}).length && !closed(st)) return Promise.resolve({});
    return new Promise(function (res) {
      if (G.resolve) { try { G.resolve(null); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      G = { c: c, st: st, ids: null, resolve: res, v: {}, err: '', quo: null, quoBusy: false, quoErr: '', busy: false, outside: false };
      paint();
    });
  }

  function field(k) {
    var c = G.c, v = G.v;
    if (k === 'owner') {
      var ppl = members().slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.name.localeCompare(b.name); });
      return '<label for="w211g_owner">Handled by <i>required</i></label><select id="w211g_owner" data-w211gf="owner"><option value="">Choose who handles them</option>' + ppl.map(function (m) { return '<option' + (v.owner === m.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>';
    }
    if (k === 'position_id') {
      var deps = (window.w199 && w199.depts) || [];
      var pos = P().pos || [];
      var opts = deps.map(function (d) { var ps = pos.filter(function (p) { return p.dept === d[0]; }); return ps.length ? '<optgroup label="' + E(d[1]) + '">' + ps.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '"' + (String(v.position_id) === String(p.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(p.title) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>' : ''; }).join('');
      return '<label for="w211g_pos">Position <i>required</i></label><select id="w211g_pos" data-w211gf="position_id"><option value="">Which position is this for?' + (c.role ? ' (they wrote: ' + E(String(c.role).slice(0, 40)) + ')' : '') + '</option>' + opts + '</select>';
    }
    if (k === 'call') {
      var q = G.quo, list = '';
      if (q && q.calls) {
        list = q.calls.length ? '<ul class="w211gq">' + q.calls.map(function (k2) {
          return '<li><div><b>' + E(when(k2.at)) + '</b> <small>' + E(k2.direction === 'incoming' ? 'they called' : 'we called') + ' \u00b7 ' + (k2.answered ? Math.max(1, Math.round(k2.duration / 60)) + ' min' : 'not answered') + ' \u00b7 ' + E(k2.line || '') + '</small>' +
            (k2.summary ? '<p>' + E(String(k2.summary).slice(0, 260)) + '</p>' : '') + '</div>' +
            (k2.logged ? '<span class="w211pill ok">Logged</span>' : btn('Use this call', 'data-w211gq="' + E(k2.id) + '"', 'sm pri')) + '</li>';
        }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p class="w197note">No calls with ' + E(q.phone || 'their number') + ' in Quo in the last 60 days.</p>';
        if (q.note) list = '<p class="w197note">' + E(q.note) + '</p>';
      }
      return '<label>Call log <i>required</i></label><div class="w211gbox">' +
        '<p class="w197note" style="margin:0 0 6px">Pick the call from Quo (its summary and transcript are saved on the application), or log it by hand.</p>' +
        btn(G.quoBusy ? 'Looking in Quo\u2026' : (q ? 'Look again' : 'Find their calls in Quo'), 'data-w211ga="quo"' + (G.quoBusy ? ' disabled' : ''), 'sm' + (q ? '' : ' pri')) +
        (G.quoErr ? '<p class="w211err" role="alert">' + E(G.quoErr) + '</p>' : '') + list +
        '<div class="w211gman"><span>Or log it:</span>' + OUT.map(function (o) { return btn(E(o[1]), 'data-w211go="' + o[0] + '"', 'sm'); }).join('') + '</div>' +
        '<input type="text" id="w211g_callnote" placeholder="What was said (optional)"></div>';
    }
    if (k === 'interview_at' || k === 'interview_done') {
      return '<label for="w211g_iv">Interview day and time <i>required</i></label><input type="datetime-local" id="w211g_iv" data-w211gf="interview_at" value="' + E(local(v.interview_at || c.interview_at)) + '">' +
        (k === 'interview_done' ? '<p class="w197note" style="margin:4px 0 0">The interview is booked for ' + E(when(c.interview_at)) + ', which has not happened yet. Change it if it happened at another time.</p>' : '');
    }
    if (k === 'email') return '<label for="w211g_email">Their email <i>required</i></label><input type="email" id="w211g_email" data-w211gf="email" value="' + E(v.email != null ? v.email : (c.email || '')) + '" placeholder="For the offer letter">';
    if (k === 'impressions') return '<label for="w211g_imp">Interview impressions <i>required</i></label><textarea id="w211g_imp" data-w211gf="impressions" rows="4" placeholder="How the interview went, why an offer (a few lines)">' + E(v.impressions != null ? v.impressions : (c.impressions || '')) + '</textarea>';
    if (k === 'offer') return '<label>Offer letter <i>required</i></label><div class="w211gbox">No offer letter in the Hub yet. ' + btn('Create the offer letter', 'data-w211ga="offer"', 'sm pri') + '<label class="w211gck"><input type="checkbox" data-w211gf="offer_outside"' + (G.v.offer_outside ? ' checked' : '') + '> The offer was made another way</label></div>';
    if (k === 'accepted_on') return '<label for="w211g_acc">The day they accepted <i>required</i></label><input type="date" id="w211g_acc" data-w211gf="accepted_on" max="' + today() + '" value="' + E(v.accepted_on || c.accepted_on || '') + '">';
    if (k === 'start_date') return '<label for="w211g_start">Start date <i>required</i></label><input type="date" id="w211g_start" data-w211gf="start_date" value="' + E(v.start_date || c.start_date || '') + '">';
    if (k === 'reason') {
      var rs = REASONS[G.st] || REASONS.declined;
      return '<label for="w211g_reason">Why <i>required</i></label><select id="w211g_reason" data-w211gf="reason"><option value="">Choose a reason</option>' + rs.map(function (r) { return '<option' + (G.v.reason === r ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(r) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
        '<label for="w211g_rnote">A note (' + (G.v.reason === 'Other' ? '<i>required</i>' : 'optional') + ')</label><input type="text" id="w211g_rnote" data-w211gf="reason_note" value="' + E(G.v.reason_note || '') + '">';
    }
    return '';
  }

  function paint() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w211g'); if (!s) { s = document.createElement('div'); s.id = 'w211g'; document.body.appendChild(s); }
    var c = G.c, st = G.st, h = '';
    if (G.ids) {   /* several cards (the selection bar) */
      var ready = G.ids.filter(function (x) { return !missing(x, st, closed(st) ? G.v : {}).length; });
      var held = G.ids.filter(function (x) { return missing(x, st, closed(st) ? G.v : {}).length; });
      h = '<h3 id="w211gh">Move ' + G.ids.length + (G.ids.length === 1 ? ' application' : ' applications') + ' to ' + E(LABEL[st]) + '</h3>' +
        (closed(st) ? field('reason') : '') +
        (!closed(st) && held.length ? '<p class="w197note">' + held.length + ' cannot move yet. Open each card to fill in what is missing:</p><ul class="w211gheld">' + held.map(function (x) { return '<li><button type="button" class="w197b sm link" data-w211gopen="' + x.id + '">' + E(x.name || 'Someone') + '</button> <small>needs ' + E(says(missing(x, st, {}))) + '</small></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') +
        (G.err ? '<p class="w211err" role="alert">' + E(G.err) + '</p>' : '') +
        '<div class="acts">' + (closed(st) || ready.length ? btn(closed(st) ? 'Move ' + G.ids.length : 'Move the ' + ready.length + ' ready', 'data-w211ga="go"', 'pri') : '') + btn('Cancel', 'data-w211ga="cancel"') + '</div>';
    } else {
      var need = missing(c, st, G.v), all = need.slice();
      /* keep a field on screen once it is filled, so the person sees what they gave */
      ['owner', 'position_id', 'call', 'interview_at', 'email', 'impressions', 'offer', 'accepted_on', 'start_date', 'reason'].forEach(function (k) { if (G.shown && G.shown[k] && all.indexOf(k) < 0 && !(k === 'interview_at' && all.indexOf('interview_done') >= 0)) all.push(k); });
      G.shown = G.shown || {}; all.forEach(function (k) { G.shown[k === 'interview_done' ? 'interview_at' : k] = 1; });
      var order = ['owner', 'position_id', 'call', 'interview_at', 'interview_done', 'email', 'impressions', 'offer', 'accepted_on', 'start_date', 'reason'];
      all.sort(function (a, b) { return order.indexOf(a) - order.indexOf(b); });
      h = '<h3 id="w211gh">' + E(c.name || 'Someone') + ' to ' + E(LABEL[st]) + '</h3>' +
        '<p class="w197note">' + (closed(st) ? 'Closing an application needs a reason.' : (need.length ? 'Before it moves: ' + E(says(need)) + '.' : 'All set.')) + '</p>' +
        all.map(field).join('') +
        (G.err ? '<p class="w211err" role="alert">' + E(G.err) + '</p>' : '') +
        '<div class="acts">' + btn(G.busy ? 'Saving\u2026' : 'Move to ' + E(LABEL[st]), 'data-w211ga="go"' + (G.busy ? ' disabled' : ''), 'pri') + btn('Cancel', 'data-w211ga="cancel"') + '</div>';
    }
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w211ga="cancel"></div><div class="pn" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w211gh">' + btn('Close', 'data-w211ga="cancel"', 'sm x') + h + '</div>';
    s.classList.add('open');
  }
  function readForm() {
    var s = document.getElementById('w211g'); if (!s) return;
    s.querySelectorAll('[data-w211gf]').forEach(function (x) { var k = x.getAttribute('data-w211gf'); G.v[k] = x.type === 'checkbox' ? x.checked : String(x.value || '').trim(); });
  }
  function close(val) { var s = document.getElementById('w211g'); if (s) s.classList.remove('open'); var r = G.resolve; G.resolve = null; if (r) r(val); }

  function patchOf() {
    var c = G.c, v = G.v, p = {}, st = G.st;
    if (closed(st)) { p.close_reason = v.reason + (v.reason_note ? ': ' + v.reason_note : ''); return p; }
    if (v.owner && v.owner !== c.owner) p.owner = v.owner;
    if (v.position_id && String(v.position_id) !== String(c.position_id)) { p.position_id = Number(v.position_id); var pp = (P().pos || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(v.position_id); })[0]; if (pp) p.role = pp.title; }
    if (v.interview_at) { var d = new Date(v.interview_at); if (!isNaN(d) && d.toISOString() !== c.interview_at) p.interview_at = d.toISOString(); }
    if (v.email && v.email !== c.email) p.email = v.email.toLowerCase();
    if (v.impressions && v.impressions !== c.impressions) p.impressions = v.impressions.slice(0, 4000);
    if (v.accepted_on) p.accepted_on = v.accepted_on;
    if (v.start_date) p.start_date = v.start_date;
    return p;
  }
  function go() {
    readForm(); G.err = '';
    if (G.ids) {
      if (closed(G.st) && !G.v.reason) { G.err = 'Choose a reason.'; paint(); return; }
      if (closed(G.st) && G.v.reason === 'Other' && !G.v.reason_note) { G.err = 'Say why in the note.'; paint(); return; }
      var ok = G.ids.filter(function (x) { return !missing(x, G.st, closed(G.st) ? G.v : {}).length; }).map(function (x) { return Number(x.id); });
      close({ ids: ok, patch: closed(G.st) ? { close_reason: G.v.reason + (G.v.reason_note ? ': ' + G.v.reason_note : '') } : {} });
      return;
    }
    if (G.st && closed(G.st) && G.v.reason === 'Other' && !G.v.reason_note) { G.err = 'Say why in the note.'; paint(); return; }
    var need = missing(G.c, G.st, G.v);
    if (G.v.email && !validEmail(G.v.email)) { G.err = 'That email does not look right.'; paint(); return; }
    if (G.v.start_date && G.v.accepted_on && G.v.start_date < G.v.accepted_on) { G.err = 'The start date is before the day they accepted.'; paint(); return; }
    if (need.length) { G.err = 'Still needed: ' + says(need) + '.'; paint(); var f = document.querySelector('#w211g [data-w211gf], #w211g [data-w211ga="quo"]'); if (f) { try { f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    close(patchOf());
  }

  async function fn(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('recruit-actions', { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) { var msg = r.error.message || String(r.error); try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) { var j = await r.error.context.json(); if (j && j.error) msg = j.error; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } throw new Error(msg); }
    if (r && r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r ? r.data : null;
  }
  async function findQuo() {
    readForm(); G.quoBusy = true; G.quoErr = ''; paint();
    try { G.quo = await fn({ action: 'quo_calls', id: G.c.id }); } catch (e) { G.quoErr = 'Quo: ' + e.message; }
    G.quoBusy = false; paint();
  }
  function addCall(row) { var p = P(); p.calls = [row].concat(p.calls || []); try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function useQuo(callId) {
    readForm(); G.quoErr = ''; G.quoBusy = true; paint();
    try {
      var r = await fn({ action: 'quo_log', id: G.c.id, call_id: callId, stage: G.c.status || 'new' });
      if (r && r.call && !r.already) addCall(r.call);
      if (G.quo && G.quo.calls) G.quo.calls.forEach(function (k) { if (k.id === callId) k.logged = true; });
      toastS('Call logged' + (r && r.call && r.call.transcript ? ' with its transcript' : ''));
    } catch (e) { G.quoErr = 'Could not log it: ' + e.message; }
    G.quoBusy = false; paint();
  }
  async function logByHand(outcome) {
    readForm();
    var nt = document.getElementById('w211g_callnote');
    var row = { candidate_id: Number(G.c.id), outcome: outcome, note: nt && nt.value.trim() ? nt.value.trim().slice(0, 500) : null, by_email: myEmail(), by_name: myName(), stage: G.c.status || 'new', called_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_candidate_calls').insert(row).select('*').single(); if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      addCall((r && r.data && r.data.id) ? r.data : row);
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: String(G.c.id), field: 'call', old_value: null, new_value: outcome, changed_by: row.by_name }); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
      toastS('Call logged');
    } catch (e) { G.quoErr = 'Could not log the call: ' + (e.message || e); }
    paint();
  }

  /* several at once (w199's selection bar) */
  function bulkGate(ids, st) {
    var list = cands().filter(function (c) { return ids.indexOf(Number(c.id)) >= 0 && c.status !== st; });
    if (!list.length) return Promise.resolve({ ids: [], patch: {} });
    if (!closed(st) && list.every(function (c) { return !missing(c, st, {}).length; })) return Promise.resolve({ ids: list.map(function (c) { return Number(c.id); }), patch: {} });
    return new Promise(function (res) {
      if (G.resolve) { try { G.resolve(null); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
      G = { c: null, st: st, ids: list, resolve: res, v: {}, err: '', quo: null, quoBusy: false, quoErr: '', busy: false };
      paint();
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('#w211g [data-w211ga],#w211g [data-w211gq],#w211g [data-w211go],#w211g [data-w211gopen]') : null; if (!t) return;
    var a = t.getAttribute('data-w211ga');
    if (a === 'cancel') { close(null); return; }
    if (a === 'go') { go(); return; }
    if (a === 'quo') { findQuo(); return; }
    if (a === 'offer') { var id = G.c.id; close(null); try { w211.askOffer(id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w211gq')) { useQuo(t.getAttribute('data-w211gq')); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w211go')) { logByHand(t.getAttribute('data-w211go')); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w211gopen')) { var cid = t.getAttribute('data-w211gopen'); close(null); try { w197.openSheet(cid); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w211g') || !t.hasAttribute('data-w211gf')) return;
    readForm(); if (t.getAttribute('data-w211gf') === 'reason' || t.getAttribute('data-w211gf') === 'offer_outside') paint();
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') { var s = document.getElementById('w211g'); if (s && s.classList.contains('open')) close(null); } });

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'w211gcss';
  css.textContent =
    '#w211g{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9200;display:none;}#w211g.open{display:block;}#w211g .bk{position:absolute;inset:0;background:rgba(16,32,30,.4);}' +
    '#w211g .pn{position:absolute;right:0;top:0;bottom:0;width:min(560px,100%);background:#FFFAF2;overflow:auto;padding:18px 16px 28px;box-shadow:-8px 0 24px rgba(0,0,0,.12);}#w211g .x{float:right;}' +
    '#w211g label{display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#103F39;margin:14px 0 4px;}#w211g label i{font-style:normal;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;color:#B3402F;font-weight:600;margin-left:4px;}' +
    '#w211g input,#w211g select,#w211g textarea{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font:inherit;font-size:15px;padding:9px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);border-radius:8px;background:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '#w211g input[type=checkbox]{width:auto;margin-right:6px;}#w211g .w211gck{text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-weight:500;font-size:14px;display:flex;align-items:center;margin-top:8px;}' +
    '#w211g .acts{display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;}.w211gbox{padding:10px 12px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);}' +
    '.w211gq{list-style:none;margin:8px 0;padding:0;}.w211gq li{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.1);padding:8px 0;}.w211gq small{color:#5b6b67;}.w211gq p{margin:4px 0 0;font-size:13px;}' +
    '.w211gman{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:center;margin:10px 0 6px;font-size:13.5px;}.w211gheld{margin:6px 0;padding-left:18px;}.w211gheld small{color:#5b6b67;}';
  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(css);

  window.w211g = { missing: missing, gate: gate, bulkGate: bulkGate, says: says, state: function () { return G; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v212 The HR centre: brighter colours, a notification bell, time off that works (06/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Valex's voice note (06/10/26): "the colours could be brighter", "a notification box", "time off disappeared",
   "notify Valex and Samson when someone asks for time off", "a form that tracks the weeks off". Steel: "for brighter
   colors do it for the hr center only".
   - Colours (HR centre only, #hrcentre): each stage of Applications has its own colour (New teal, Reviewing gold,
     Interview purple, Offer coral, Hired green) on the column, its count and the cards' edge; the Overview tiles carry
     a coloured top edge and number; pills and the time-off chips are stronger. Nothing outside the HR centre changes.
   - The bell (the HR centre's masthead): what needs you, counted since you last opened it: time-off requests waiting
     (HR), applications you handle that need a call, an interview, or the offer letter, new applications in the last
     three days, interviews today and tomorrow. Each line opens the page or the card.
   - Time off (wave105, edited in its segment): the page shows for the HR access profile too (wa_can_timeoff in the database; policies and the discipline tracker stay with the HR access list),
     Log time off opens a proper form, a decline needs a reason, the totals add a Total and the sick days left of 7, recent
     decisions fold open; the Overview count reads 'requested' (it read 'pending', so it was always 0). A new request is
     sent to Valex and Samson by Slack DM (hr-notify, trigger on wa_timeoff; app_config hr_notify_emails).
   Hooks: parts197 mastHr (the bell). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.w212) return;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', TEAL = '#2A8C7A', PURPLE = '#6F64AB', CORAL = '#D9654B', FLAG = '#B3402F';
  var STAGE = { 'new': TEAL, reviewing: GOLD, interview: PURPLE, offer: CORAL, hired: GREEN };
  var B = { open: false, items: [] };

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function st() { try { return window.w197 ? w197.state() : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function myName() { try { return (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && (USER.name || USER.full_name)) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function firstName(n) { return String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase(); }
  function mine(c) { var o = String(c.owner || '').toLowerCase(), me = myName().toLowerCase(); return !!o && !!me && (o === me || firstName(o) === firstName(me)); }
  function key() { return 'w212seen:' + myEmail(); }
  function seenAt() { try { return Number(localStorage.getItem(key()) || 0); } catch (e) { return 0; } }
  function markSeen() { try { localStorage.setItem(key(), String(Date.now())); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function day(t) { var d = new Date(t); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + (d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + d.getDate(); }
  function when(t) { try { return new Date(t).toLocaleString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function callsOf(id) { try { return (w199.state().calls || []).filter(function (k) { return String(k.candidate_id) === String(id); }); } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function hr() { try { return typeof window.wa46IsHr === 'function' ? wa46IsHr() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }

  /* what needs the person, newest first; at = when it became true (for the unread count) */
  function items() {
    var S = st(); if (!S) return [];
    var out = [], now = Date.now();
    var tod = day(now), tom = day(now + 86400000);
    if (hr() && S.data && S.data.timeoff) S.data.timeoff.forEach(function (t) {
      out.push({ k: 'to', at: t.created_at, ic: '\ud83c\udf34', t: (t.name || 'Someone') + ' asked for time off', s: t.start_date + ' to ' + t.end_date + ' (' + t.days + ' day' + (Number(t.days) === 1 ? '' : 's') + ')', go: '#hrcentre/timeoff' });
    });
    (S.cands || []).forEach(function (c) {
      var at = c.stage_at || c.created_at;
      if (c.status === 'new' && !c.practice && c.created_at && now - new Date(c.created_at).getTime() < 3 * 86400000) out.push({ k: 'new', at: c.created_at, ic: '\u2709', t: 'New application: ' + (c.name || 'someone'), s: (window.w199 ? w199.roleOf(c) : c.role) || '', id: c.id });
      if (!mine(c)) return;
      if ((c.status === 'new' || c.status === 'reviewing') && !callsOf(c.id).length) out.push({ k: 'call', at: at, ic: '\u260e', t: 'Call ' + (c.name || 'them'), s: 'You handle this application; no call logged yet', id: c.id });
      if (c.status === 'interview' && c.interview_at && (day(c.interview_at) === tod || day(c.interview_at) === tom)) out.push({ k: 'iv', at: at, ic: '\ud83d\uddd3', t: 'Interview: ' + (c.name || 'someone'), s: when(c.interview_at), id: c.id });
      if (c.status === 'offer' && !c.offer_sent_at) out.push({ k: 'offer', at: at, ic: '\u270d', t: 'Send the offer letter to ' + (c.name || 'them'), s: 'At Offer, the letter is not sent yet', id: c.id });
    });
    out.sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.at || '').localeCompare(String(a.at || '')); });
    return out.slice(0, 30);
  }
  function unread(list) { var s = seenAt(); return list.filter(function (i) { return i.at && new Date(i.at).getTime() > s; }).length; }

  function bell(acts) {
    if (!acts) return;
    var list = items(), n = unread(list);
    var b = acts.querySelector('#w212bell');
    if (!b) { acts.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<button type="button" id="w212bell" aria-haspopup="true" aria-expanded="false"></button>'); b = acts.querySelector('#w212bell'); }
    var label = list.length + (list.length === 1 ? ' thing needs' : ' things need') + ' you' + (n ? ', ' + n + ' new' : '');
    /* only touch the DOM when something changed: the HR centre's observers watch every change */
    if (b.getAttribute('aria-label') !== 'Notifications: ' + label) { b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Notifications: ' + label); b.title = label; }
    var html = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" aria-hidden="true"><path fill="currentColor" d="M12 22a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 2.45-2h-4.9A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 12 22zm7-6V11a7 7 0 0 0-5.5-6.84V3.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 0-3 0v.66A7 7 0 0 0 5 11v5l-2 2v1h18v-1z"/></svg>' + (n ? '<span class="w212n">' + (n > 9 ? '9+' : n) + '</span>' : '');
    if (b.__w212html !== html) { b.innerHTML = html; b.__w212html = html; }
    B.items = list;
    if (B.open) paintBox();
  }
  function paintBox() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w212box'); if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w212box'; box.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); box.setAttribute('aria-label', 'What needs you'); document.body.appendChild(box); }
    var b = document.getElementById('w212bell'); if (!b) return;
    var r = b.getBoundingClientRect(), s = seenAt();
    box.style.top = Math.round(r.bottom + 8) + 'px';
    var w = Math.min(360, window.innerWidth - 16);
    box.style.left = Math.round(Math.max(8, Math.min(r.right - w, window.innerWidth - w - 8))) + 'px'; box.style.right = 'auto';
    box.innerHTML = '<h3>What needs you</h3>' + (B.items.length ? '<ul>' + B.items.map(function (i, ix) {
      var isNew = i.at && new Date(i.at).getTime() > s;
      return '<li><button type="button" data-w212i="' + ix + '" class="w212k-' + i.k + (isNew ? ' new' : '') + '"><span class="ic" aria-hidden="true">' + i.ic + '</span><span><b>' + E(i.t) + '</b><small>' + E(i.s) + '</small></span></button></li>';
    }).join('') + '</ul>' : '<p>Nothing needs you right now.</p>');
    box.classList.add('open');
  }
  function toggle() {
    B.open = !B.open;
    var b = document.getElementById('w212bell'); if (b) b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', B.open ? 'true' : 'false');
    if (B.open) { B.items = items(); paintBox(); markSeen(); setTimeout(function () { var x = document.querySelector('#w212bell .w212n'); if (x) { x.remove(); var bb = document.getElementById('w212bell'); if (bb) bb.__w212html = bb.innerHTML; } }, 0); }
    else { var box = document.getElementById('w212box'); if (box) box.classList.remove('open'); }
  }
  function shut() { if (!B.open) return; B.open = false; var box = document.getElementById('w212box'); if (box) box.classList.remove('open'); var b = document.getElementById('w212bell'); if (b) b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target : null; if (!t) return;
    if (t.closest('#w212bell')) { toggle(); return; }
    var it = t.closest('#w212box [data-w212i]');
    if (it) { var i = B.items[Number(it.getAttribute('data-w212i'))]; shut(); if (!i) return; if (i.id) { if (!/^#hrcentre\/applications/.test(location.hash)) location.hash = '#hrcentre/applications'; setTimeout(function () { try { w197.openSheet(i.id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }, 250); } else if (i.go) location.hash = i.go; return; }
    if (B.open && !t.closest('#w212box')) shut();
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && B.open) { shut(); var b = document.getElementById('w212bell'); if (b) try { b.focus(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } });
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', shut);

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'w212css';
  var stageCss = Object.keys(STAGE).map(function (k) {
    var c = STAGE[k];
    return '#hrcentre .w197col[data-w197col="' + k + '"]{background:' + c + '1f;border-top:4px solid ' + c + ';}' +
      '#hrcentre .w197col[data-w197col="' + k + '"] h4{color:' + (k === 'reviewing' ? '#7a5a0c' : c) + ';}' +

      '#hrcentre .w197col[data-w197col="' + k + '"] .w197cand{border-left:4px solid ' + c + ';}';
  }).join('');
  var tileCols = [TEAL, GOLD, PURPLE, CORAL, GREEN, FLAG];
  css.textContent = stageCss +
    tileCols.map(function (c, i) { return '#hrcentre .w197tiles > .w197tile:nth-child(6n+' + (i + 1) + '){border-top:4px solid ' + c + ';}#hrcentre .w197tiles > .w197tile:nth-child(6n+' + (i + 1) + ') b{color:' + (c === GOLD ? '#9a7110' : c) + ';}'; }).join('') +
    '#hrcentre .w197tile:hover{box-shadow:0 10px 24px -14px rgba(16,63,57,.45);}' +
    '#hrcentre .w197cand:hover{box-shadow:0 8px 18px -12px rgba(16,63,57,.5);}' +
    '#hrcentre .w105ok{background:' + TEAL + '!important;color:#FFFAF2!important;}#hrcentre .w105no{background:' + FLAG + '!important;color:#FFFAF2!important;}#hrcentre .w105pend{background:' + GOLD + '!important;color:' + GREEN + '!important;}' +
    '#hrcentre .w105ok,#hrcentre .w105no,#hrcentre .w105pend{border-radius:999px;padding:1px 8px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '#hrcentre .w212toacts{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:12px 0 4px;}' +

    '#w212bell{position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);background:var(--wa-card,#FFFAF2);color:' + GREEN + ';cursor:pointer;}' +
    '#w212bell:hover{border-color:' + GREEN + ';}#w212bell .w212n{position:absolute;top:-4px;right:-4px;min-width:18px;height:18px;border-radius:999px;background:' + CORAL + ';color:#FFFAF2;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;line-height:18px;padding:0 4px;box-sizing:border-box;}' +
    '#w212box{position:fixed;z-index:8800;width:min(360px,calc(100vw - 16px));max-height:min(70vh,520px);overflow:auto;background:var(--wa-card,#FFFAF2);border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.18);border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 24px 50px -20px rgba(16,63,57,.55);padding:12px;display:none;}' +
    '#w212box.open{display:block;}#w212box h3{margin:2px 6px 8px;font-size:15px;color:' + GREEN + ';}#w212box p{margin:6px;font-size:14px;}' +
    '#w212box ul{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;}#w212box li button{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:none;border-radius:10px;padding:8px;cursor:pointer;font:inherit;color:inherit;}' +
    '#w212box li button:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);}#w212box li button.new{background:rgba(217,101,75,.10);}#w212box .ic{font-size:16px;line-height:1.3;width:20px;text-align:center;}' +
    '#w212box b{display:block;font-size:13.5px;}#w212box small{display:block;font-size:12.5px;color:#5b6b67;}' +
    '@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark){#w212box small{color:inherit;opacity:.75;}}';
  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(css);

  /* the counts follow the data: a light refresh while the HR centre is open */
  setInterval(function () { var a = document.querySelector('#hrcentre > .w118mast .w197acts'); if (a && document.getElementById('w212bell')) bell(a); }, 20000);

  window.w212 = { bell: bell, items: items, toggle: toggle, state: function () { return B; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v213 The due-date check (06/10/26)
   WA v237 Tasks set right: the owner questions in the due-date check, Mine / Not mine and Who owns this? (Steel, 14/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (06/10/26), after wave210 showed 52 of 62 recent meeting tasks had no due date: "I'd rather a pop up when the
   user gets on to the hub that they should [set] their due dates for the tasks or confirm the due dates provided make
   sense" (new and existing open tasks); then "it should pop with the best assumptions of due dates and allow the user
   to make edits if needed but they [should not] be able to exit until they confirm their due dates. Also overdue tasks
   should also pop to ask if items are done and if they aren't to push the due date to a realistic date".

   - On opening the Hub, a pop-up (#w213pop) lists the person's open tasks that are overdue, have no due date, or
     carry a date a meeting set that nobody confirmed (my_due_date_check(): owner by first name, or assigned).
   - Each comes filled with the best guess: their team's next meeting from the Hub calendar, or the one after when
     the next is less than two days away (a date the meeting set stays as it is). Next meeting / Following meeting
     buttons and a date field change it. An overdue task asks Done or Still open (push to a date).
   - It cannot be closed (no x, no Escape, no click outside) until Save; if saving fails it says so and, only then,
     offers "Try again later" so a fault never locks anyone out. confirm_due_dates() writes each change with a
     change_log line; the pop-up comes back next visit only for what is still overdue or undated.
   wave213c (07/10/26): each task can also be Done, sent To the bank (until a day; bank_task) or Discarded (deleted, the
   row kept in change_log); saved through confirm_due_check (confirm_due_dates stays for pages still open).
   Never on top of another window: it waits for the meeting pop-up (#w177pop) or a modal to close.
   wave237 (14/10/26): the owner questions. Steel: "if confirmations are needed, put it in the pop-up that people get when they
   open the hub [...] have them just confirm, okay, this is my task. No, this is someone else's task [...] hopefully we can avoid
   that without it prompting people to clarify tasks as much." A task whose owner a meeting left unsure (dept_tasks.owner_unsure)
   opens with "Is this yours?": Mine (then the usual date choices) or Not mine (it goes to the team's lead to re-home). A team's
   lead also sees the team's tasks nobody on the roster owns and names the owner. The list comes from wa237_due_check().
   wave238 (14/10/26): it also waits for the Loop's Welcome back gate (#w238loop).
   wave258 (15/10/26, the Fundraising meeting: a new member got stuck on "this tab that only shows the tasks" and did not know how
   to leave): one plain line at the top says what to do to carry on, and "x of n answered" shows how far along you are. Still no
   way to skip it.
   wave262 (20/10/26, the 19/10 leadership meeting: Ian answered 20 of his 50 tasks and Save failed every time): the save
   goes in batches of 15 (confirm_due_check also reads the list once per call now, wave262a), and a batch that fails keeps
   what the earlier batches saved; each section has "All in this section: Done / Next meeting" for long lists; and, Steel:
   "continue the prompt ... or change due dates if it's the day that these tasks are due": tasks due today join the check
   (why due_today, kept for today unless the person changes it) until they are confirmed that day. */

    + '#w213pop .chip{border:1px solid var(--wa-line,#d8d4c8);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:14px;padding:3px 10px;font:inherit;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;color:#333;}'
    + '#w213pop .chip.on{background:' + GREEN + ';border-color:' + GREEN + ';color:#FFFAF2;}'
    + '#w213pop .seg{display:inline-flex;border:1px solid var(--wa-line,#d8d4c8);border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;}'

    + '#w213pop .ft{padding:12px 22px;border-top:1px solid var(--wa-line,#e4e0d4);display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;}'
    + '#w213pop .save{background:' + GOLD + ';color:#1F201E;border:0;border-radius:9px;padding:9px 18px;font:inherit;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;cursor:pointer;}'
    + '#w213pop .save[disabled]{opacity:.6;cursor:default;}'

    + '#w213pop .how{margin:0 0 8px;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';border-radius:9px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.4;}'
