    + '#w213pop .prog .bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:' + GOLD + ';}'
    + '#w213pop .prog.all b{color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '#w213pop .bulk{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:center;margin:-2px 0 8px;font-size:12px;color:#6b6a65;}'
    + '#w213pop .bulk .chip{font-size:11.5px;padding:2px 9px;}'
    + '#w213pop select{font:inherit;font-size:13px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line,#d8d4c8);border-radius:7px;padding:4px 6px;background:#FFFAF2;max-width:100%;}'
    + '@media (max-width:640px){#w213pop{padding:0;align-items:stretch;}#w213pop .dlg{max-height:100vh;border-radius:0;}#w213pop .note{margin-left:0;flex-basis:100%;}}';
  var st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = css; document.head.appendChild(st);

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function day(s, wk) { if (!s) return ''; try { return new Date(s + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString(undefined, wk ? { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' } : { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return s; } }
  function team(k) { try { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[k]) || k || ''; } catch (e) { return k || ''; } }
  /* wave213d (Steel, 07/10/26): a friendlier, clearer title than "Check your due dates" */
  function hello() { try { var n = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.name) ? String(WA_ME.name).trim().split(/\s+/)[0] : ''; return n ? 'Hi ' + n + ', ' : 'Hi, '; } catch (e) { return 'Hi, '; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function busyElsewhere() {
    var p = document.getElementById('w177pop'); if (p && p.offsetParent !== null && getComputedStyle(p).display !== 'none') return true;
    var l = document.getElementById('w238loop'); if (l && l.getBoundingClientRect().width > 0) return true;   /* wave238: the Loop's gate first */
    var m = document.getElementById('modalBack'); if (m && m.style.display === 'flex') return true;
    return false;
  }

  async function load() {
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa237_due_check');
      if (!r || r.error || !Array.isArray(r.data)) { r = await db.rpc('my_due_date_check'); if (r && r.error) return null; }   /* the old list, for a database still on wave213 */
      return (r && Array.isArray(r.data)) ? r.data : [];
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  /* wave237: the people a lead can give a task to (the task's team first, active members only) */
  function teamPeople(dept) {
    var all = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || [];
    var act = all.filter(function (m) { return m && m.name && m.active !== false; });
    var own = act.filter(function (m) { return m.department === dept; }), rest = act.filter(function (m) { return m.department !== dept; });
    return own.concat(rest);
  }
  function isUnsure(r) { return r.owner_unsure === true && !r.for_lead; }
  function isLead(r) { return r.for_lead === true; }
  function mineOf(id) { return S.mine[id] === undefined ? null : S.mine[id]; }

  /* wave213c (Steel, 07/10/26): "members should have the ability to discard a task, complete a task (it's done), or
     move the task to the task bank". Each task: Keep (with its date), Done, To the bank (until a day), Discard. */
  var ACTS = [['keep', 'Keep'], ['done', 'Done'], ['bank', 'To the bank'], ['discard', 'Discard']];
  function plusDays(n) { var d = new Date(); d.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0); d.setDate(d.getDate() + n); return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function actOf(id) { return S.act[id] || 'keep'; }

  function rowHtml(r) {
    var id = r.task_id, isOver = r.why === 'overdue', v = S.pick[id], a = actOf(id);
    var meta = [team(r.department)];
    if (isLead(r)) meta.push('owner: ' + (r.owner || 'nobody yet'));
    if (isOver) meta.push('was due ' + day(r.due_date));
    else if (r.why === 'due_today') meta.push('due today');
    else if (r.why === 'meeting_set') meta.push('the meeting set ' + day(r.due_date));
    else if (r.why === 'owner_check' && r.due_date) meta.push('due ' + day(r.due_date));
    if (r.meeting_title) meta.push('from ' + r.meeting_title + (r.meeting_date ? ' (' + day(r.meeting_date) + ')' : ''));
    var h = '<div class="row a-' + a + (a === 'done' || a === 'discard' ? ' isdone' : '') + (isUnsure(r) ? ' unsure' : '') + '" data-w213row="' + id + '"><div class="t">' + E(r.title || 'Untitled') + '</div>'
      + '<div class="m">' + E(meta.filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div>';
    /* wave237: a lead names the owner of a task nobody owns */
    if (isLead(r)) {
      var who = S.who[id] || '';
      h += '<div class="ask"><span class="q">Who owns this?</span>'
        + '<span class="seg" role="group" aria-label="What to do with it"><button type="button" data-w213act="' + id + '" data-a="assign" class="' + (a === 'assign' ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (a === 'assign') + '">Give it to</button>'
        + '<button type="button" data-w213act="' + id + '" data-a="done" class="' + (a === 'done' ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (a === 'done') + '">Done</button>'
        + '<button type="button" data-w213act="' + id + '" data-a="discard" class="' + (a === 'discard' ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (a === 'discard') + '">Discard</button></span>';
      if (a === 'assign') {
        h += '<select data-w213who="' + id + '" aria-label="Who owns ' + E(r.title || 'this task') + '"><option value="">Pick someone\u2026</option>' + teamPeople(r.department).map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '"' + (who === m.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + (m.department && m.department !== r.department ? ' (' + E(team(m.department)) + ')' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
          + '<input type="date" aria-label="Due date for ' + E(r.title || 'this task') + '" data-w213date="' + id + '" value="' + E(v) + '">';
      } else if (a === 'discard') h += '<span class="hint warn">It will be deleted, like Delete on the task.</span>';
      else h += '<span class="hint">Marked done.</span>';
      return h + '</div></div>';
    }
    /* wave237: "Is this yours?" comes first; the date choices follow a Mine */
    if (isUnsure(r)) {
      var mine = mineOf(id);
      h += '<div class="ask"><span class="q">Is this yours?</span><span class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Is this your task">'
        + '<button type="button" data-w213mine="' + id + '" data-v="1" class="' + (mine === true ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (mine === true) + '">Mine</button>'
        + '<button type="button" data-w213mine="' + id + '" data-v="0" class="' + (mine === false ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (mine === false) + '">Not mine</button></span>'
        + (mine === false ? '<span class="hint">It goes to your team\u2019s lead to give to the right person.</span>' : '') + '</div>';
      if (mine !== true) return h + '</div>';
    }
    h += '<div class="ctl">'
      + '<span class="seg" role="group" aria-label="What to do with it">' + ACTS.map(function (x) {
          return '<button type="button" data-w213act="' + id + '" data-a="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (a === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (a === x[0]) + '">' + (x[0] === 'keep' && isOver ? 'Keep, push to' : (x[0] === 'keep' && r.why === 'due_today' ? 'Keep, due' : x[1])) + '</button>';
        }).join('') + '</span>';
    if (a === 'keep') {
      h += '<input type="date" aria-label="Due date for ' + E(r.title || 'this task') + '" data-w213date="' + id + '" value="' + E(v) + '">'
        + '<button type="button" class="chip' + (v === r.next_meeting ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w213set="' + id + '" data-d="' + E(r.next_meeting) + '">Next meeting \u00b7 ' + E(day(r.next_meeting, true)) + '</button>'
        + '<button type="button" class="chip' + (v === r.following_meeting ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w213set="' + id + '" data-d="' + E(r.following_meeting) + '">Following \u00b7 ' + E(day(r.following_meeting, true)) + '</button>';
    } else if (a === 'bank') {
      h += '<label class="bk">Back on <input type="date" aria-label="Back from the bank on" data-w213bank="' + id + '" value="' + E(S.bank[id]) + '"></label><span class="hint">It leaves your boards and comes back by itself on that day.</span>';
    } else if (a === 'discard') {
      h += '<span class="hint warn">It will be deleted, like Delete on the task.</span>';
    } else {
      h += '<span class="hint">Marked done.</span>';
    }
    return h + '</div></div>';
  }

  function counts() {
    var c = { keep: 0, done: 0, bank: 0, discard: 0, assign: 0, notmine: 0, open: 0 };
    (S.rows || []).forEach(function (r) {
      var id = r.task_id;
      if (isLead(r)) { c[actOf(id) === 'keep' ? 'assign' : actOf(id)]++; return; }
      if (isUnsure(r)) { var m = mineOf(id); if (m === null) { c.open++; return; } if (m === false) { c.notmine++; return; } }
      c[actOf(id)]++;
    });
    return c;
  }
  /* wave258: is this task answered enough to save? (the same rules save() checks) */
  function answered(r) {
    var id = r.task_id, a = actOf(id), today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    function future(d, strict) { if (!d || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d)) return false; var t = new Date(d + 'T12:00:00'); return strict ? t > today : t >= today; }
    if (isLead(r)) return a === 'done' || a === 'discard' || !!S.who[id];
    if (isUnsure(r)) { var m = mineOf(id); if (m === null) return false; if (m === false) return true; }
    if (a === 'done' || a === 'discard') return true;
    if (a === 'bank') return future(S.bank[id], true);
    return future(S.pick[id], false);
  }
  function progressHtml() {
    var rows = S.rows || [], n = rows.length, x = rows.filter(answered).length, pct = n ? Math.round(x / n * 100) : 0;
    return '<div class="prog' + (x === n ? ' all' : '') + '" role="status" aria-live="polite"><b>' + (x === n ? 'All ' + n + ' answered. Save, and you are in.' : x + ' of ' + n + ' answered') + '</b><span class="bar" aria-hidden="true"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></span></div>';
  }
  function saveLabel() {
    var c = counts(), bits = [];
    if (c.keep) bits.push(c.keep + (c.keep === 1 ? ' date' : ' dates'));
    if (c.done) bits.push(c.done + ' done');
    if (c.bank) bits.push(c.bank + ' to the bank');
    if (c.discard) bits.push(c.discard + ' discarded');
    if (c.assign) bits.push(c.assign + ' given an owner');
    if (c.notmine) bits.push(c.notmine + ' not yours');
    return 'Save: ' + (bits.join(', ') || 'your answers');
  }

  /* wave262: a long section gets one line to answer it all at once (each task can still be changed after) */
  function bulkHtml(why, list) {
    if (list.length < 4) return '';
    return '<div class="bulk"><span>All ' + list.length + ' in this section:</span>'
      + '<button type="button" class="chip" data-w213bulk="' + why + '" data-a="done">Done</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="chip" data-w213bulk="' + why + '" data-a="next">Next meeting</button>'
      + (why === 'due_today' ? '<button type="button" class="chip" data-w213bulk="' + why + '" data-a="today">Keep for today</button>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  }
  function bulkSet(why, a) {
    (S.rows || []).forEach(function (r) {
      if (isUnsure(r) || isLead(r) || r.why !== why) return;
      var id = r.task_id;
      if (a === 'done') { S.act[id] = 'done'; return; }
      S.act[id] = 'keep';
      S.pick[id] = a === 'today' ? r.due_date : (r.next_meeting || r.suggested);
    });
    S.err = ''; paint();
  }

  function html() {
    var rows = S.rows || [];
    var unsure = rows.filter(isUnsure);
    var lead = rows.filter(isLead);
    var plain = rows.filter(function (r) { return !isUnsure(r) && !isLead(r); });
    var over = plain.filter(function (r) { return r.why === 'overdue'; });
    var tod = plain.filter(function (r) { return r.why === 'due_today'; });   /* wave262 */
    var none = plain.filter(function (r) { return r.why === 'no_date'; });
    var set = plain.filter(function (r) { return r.why === 'meeting_set'; });
    var bits = [];
    if (unsure.length) bits.push(unsure.length + (unsure.length === 1 ? ' task to confirm as yours' : ' tasks to confirm as yours'));
    if (over.length) bits.push(over.length + (over.length === 1 ? ' overdue task' : ' overdue tasks'));
    if (tod.length) bits.push(tod.length + (tod.length === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks') + ' due today');
    if (none.length) bits.push(none.length + (none.length === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks') + ' with no due date');
    if (set.length) bits.push(set.length + (set.length === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks') + ' with a date a meeting set');
    if (lead.length) bits.push(lead.length + (lead.length === 1 ? ' task of your team' : ' tasks of your team') + ' with no owner yet');
    var h = '<div class="dlg" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w213h"><div class="hd"><p class="how">Answer each task to carry on: Done, a new date, or Not mine. The Hub opens right after you save.</p><h2 id="w213h">' + E(hello()) + 'let\u2019s get your tasks on track</h2>'
      + '<p class="lead">You have ' + E(bits.length > 1 ? bits.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + bits[bits.length - 1] : bits.join('')) + '. '
      + (unsure.length ? 'A meeting gave you a task without being sure it was yours: say if it is. ' : '')
      + 'We\u2019ve suggested a due date for each one, usually your team\u2019s next meeting. For each task, keep it with a date that works, mark it done, move it to the task bank for later, or discard it.'
      + (lead.length ? ' For your team\u2019s tasks nobody owns yet, name who takes them.' : '') + ' Then save, and you\u2019re all set.</p>' + progressHtml() + '</div><div class="bd">';
    if (unsure.length) h += '<h3>Is this yours? A meeting was not sure</h3>' + unsure.map(rowHtml).join('');
    if (over.length) h += '<h3>Overdue: is it done, or does it need a new date?</h3>' + bulkHtml('overdue', over) + over.map(rowHtml).join('');
    if (tod.length) h += '<h3>Due today: done, still on for today, or a new date?</h3>' + bulkHtml('due_today', tod) + tod.map(rowHtml).join('');
    if (none.length) h += '<h3>No due date yet: here\u2019s our suggestion</h3>' + bulkHtml('no_date', none) + none.map(rowHtml).join('');
    if (set.length) h += '<h3>Dates a meeting set: do they still work?</h3>' + set.map(rowHtml).join('');
    if (lead.length) h += '<h3>Your team\u2019s tasks with no owner yet: who takes them?</h3>' + lead.map(rowHtml).join('');
    h += '</div><div class="ft"><button type="button" class="save" data-w213save="1"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy ? 'Saving\u2026' : E(saveLabel())) + '</button>'
      + (S.failed ? '<button type="button" class="later" data-w213later="1">Try again later</button>' : '')
      + '<span class="note">You can change any of this later on the task itself.</span>'
      + (S.err ? '<p class="err" role="alert">' + E(S.err) + '</p>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    return h;
  }

  function paint() {
    var el = document.getElementById('w213pop');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w213pop'; document.body.appendChild(el); wire(el); }
    var sc = el.querySelector('.bd'), top = sc ? sc.scrollTop : 0;
    el.innerHTML = html();
    var sc2 = el.querySelector('.bd'); if (sc2) sc2.scrollTop = top;
  }
  function close() { var el = document.getElementById('w213pop'); if (el) el.remove(); S.gone = true; document.removeEventListener('keydown', trapKey, true); }
  function trapKey(e) {
    if (!document.getElementById('w213pop')) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); }
  }

  function wire(el) {
    el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target, b;
      if (t === el) { e.stopPropagation(); return; }   /* a click outside does not close it */
      if ((b = t.closest('[data-w213bulk]'))) { bulkSet(b.getAttribute('data-w213bulk'), b.getAttribute('data-a')); return; }
      if ((b = t.closest('[data-w213set]'))) { S.pick[b.getAttribute('data-w213set')] = b.getAttribute('data-d'); paint(); return; }
      if ((b = t.closest('[data-w213act]'))) { S.act[b.getAttribute('data-w213act')] = b.getAttribute('data-a'); S.err = ''; paint(); return; }
      if ((b = t.closest('[data-w213mine]'))) { S.mine[b.getAttribute('data-w213mine')] = b.getAttribute('data-v') === '1'; S.err = ''; paint(); return; }
      if (t.closest('[data-w213save]')) { save(); return; }
      if (t.closest('[data-w213later]')) { close(); toastS('Your due dates are still to check: they will come back next time.'); return; }
    });
    el.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      var i = e.target.closest('[data-w213date]'); if (i) { S.pick[i.getAttribute('data-w213date')] = i.value; paint(); return; }
      var k = e.target.closest('[data-w213bank]'); if (k) { S.bank[k.getAttribute('data-w213bank')] = k.value; paint(); return; }
      var w = e.target.closest('[data-w213who]'); if (w) { S.who[w.getAttribute('data-w213who')] = w.value; S.err = ''; paint(); }
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', trapKey, true);
  }

  async function save() {
    if (S.busy) return;
    var items = [], missing = 0, past = 0, bankBad = 0, unanswered = 0, nobody = 0, today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    (S.rows || []).forEach(function (r) {
      var id = r.task_id, a = actOf(id), mine = null;
      /* wave237: the lead's rows: an owner, or done, or discard */
      if (isLead(r)) {
        if (a === 'done') { items.push({ id: id, action: 'done' }); return; }
        if (a === 'discard') { items.push({ id: id, action: 'discard' }); return; }
        if (!S.who[id]) { nobody++; return; }
        var dd = S.pick[id]; items.push({ id: id, action: 'assign', owner: S.who[id], due: dd && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dd) && new Date(dd + 'T12:00:00') >= today ? dd : undefined }); return;
      }
      /* wave237: "Is this yours?" has to be answered; Not mine needs nothing else */
      if (isUnsure(r)) {
        mine = mineOf(id);
        if (mine === null) { unanswered++; return; }
        if (mine === false) { items.push({ id: id, action: 'notmine' }); return; }
      }
      if (a === 'done') { items.push(mine ? { id: id, action: 'done', mine: true } : { id: id, action: 'done' }); return; }
      if (a === 'discard') { items.push(mine ? { id: id, action: 'discard', mine: true } : { id: id, action: 'discard' }); return; }
      if (a === 'bank') {
        var u = S.bank[id];
        if (!u || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(u) || new Date(u + 'T12:00:00') <= today) { bankBad++; return; }
        items.push(mine ? { id: id, action: 'bank', until: u, mine: true } : { id: id, action: 'bank', until: u }); return;
      }
      var v = S.pick[id];
      if (!v || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v)) { missing++; return; }
      if (new Date(v + 'T12:00:00') < today) { past++; return; }
      items.push(mine ? { id: id, due: v, mine: true } : { id: id, due: v });
    });
    if (unanswered) { S.err = unanswered === 1 ? 'One task is waiting for Mine or Not mine.' : unanswered + ' tasks are waiting for Mine or Not mine.'; paint(); return; }
    if (nobody) { S.err = nobody === 1 ? 'One of your team\u2019s tasks has nobody picked: choose who takes it, or Done or Discard.' : nobody + ' of your team\u2019s tasks have nobody picked: choose who takes each, or Done or Discard.'; paint(); return; }
    if (missing) { S.err = missing === 1 ? 'One task has no date: pick one, or choose Done, To the bank or Discard.' : missing + ' tasks have no date: pick one for each, or choose Done, To the bank or Discard.'; paint(); return; }
    if (past) { S.err = past === 1 ? 'One date is already past: push it to a date that is still ahead.' : past + ' dates are already past: push them to dates that are still ahead.'; paint(); return; }
    if (bankBad) { S.err = 'A task going to the bank needs a day in the future to come back on.'; paint(); return; }
    S.busy = true; S.err = ''; S.sent = 0; S.total = items.length; paint();
    var skipped = [];
    try {
      /* wave262: batches of 15, so a long list never runs into the database's time limit; what a batch saved stays saved */
      for (var k = 0; k < items.length; k += 15) {
        var part = items.slice(k, k + 15);
        var r = await db.rpc('confirm_due_check', { p: part });
        if (r && r.error) throw new Error(r.error.message || 'could not save');
        var res = (r && r.data) || {};
        if (Array.isArray(res.skipped)) skipped = skipped.concat(res.skipped);
        S.saved = (S.saved || []).concat(part.map(function (x) { return x.id; }));
        S.sent = Math.min(items.length, k + part.length);
        var btn = document.querySelector('#w213pop [data-w213save]'); if (btn) btn.textContent = 'Saving\u2026 ' + S.sent + ' of ' + items.length;
      }
      var c = counts(), bits = [];
      if (c.keep) bits.push(c.keep + (c.keep === 1 ? ' due date' : ' due dates'));
      if (c.done) bits.push(c.done + ' done');
      if (c.bank) bits.push(c.bank + ' to the bank');
      if (c.discard) bits.push(c.discard + ' discarded');
      if (c.assign) bits.push(c.assign + ' given an owner');
      if (c.notmine) bits.push(c.notmine + ' passed to your lead');
      close();
      toastS('Saved: ' + bits.join(', ') + '.' + (skipped.length ? ' ' + skipped.length + (skipped.length === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks') + ' could not be changed (' + String(skipped[0].why || '') + ').' : ''));
      try { if (typeof loadAll === 'function') { await loadAll(); if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') renderAllSections(); if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function') renderDashboard(); if (typeof renderNeedsMe === 'function') renderNeedsMe(); } } catch (e2) { /* the pages refresh on their next load */ }
    } catch (e) {
      /* wave262: the batches that went through are saved: take them off the list, so Save sends only the rest */
      var kept = {}; (S.saved || []).forEach(function (id) { kept[id] = 1; });
      var before = (S.rows || []).length;
      S.rows = (S.rows || []).filter(function (x) { return !kept[x.task_id]; });
      var n = before - S.rows.length; S.saved = [];
      S.busy = false; S.failed++;
      S.err = (n ? n + (n === 1 ? ' task was saved; ' : ' tasks were saved; ') + 'the rest did not save' : 'That did not save') + ' (' + (e && e.message ? e.message : 'no answer') + '). Try Save again.';
      if (!S.rows.length) { close(); toastS('Saved.'); return; }
      paint();
    }
  }

  async function start() {
    if (S.shown || S.gone) return;
    if (!staff()) return;
    var rows = await load();
    if (!rows || !rows.length) return;
    S.rows = rows; S.saved = []; S.pick = {}; S.act = {}; S.bank = {}; S.mine = {}; S.who = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r) { S.pick[r.task_id] = (r.why === 'meeting_set' || r.why === 'due_today' || (r.why === 'owner_check' && r.due_date)) ? r.due_date : r.suggested; S.bank[r.task_id] = plusDays(90); if (r.for_lead) S.act[r.task_id] = 'assign'; });
    var tries = 0;
    (function wait() {
      if (S.shown || S.gone) return;
      if (busyElsewhere() && tries++ < 120) { setTimeout(wait, 1000); return; }
      S.shown = true; paint();
      try { var f = document.querySelector('#w213pop [data-w213save]'); if (f) f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    })();
  }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa = startApp;
    startApp = async function () { await __sa.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(function () { start(); }, 5000); };   /* wave238: after the Loop's gate and the meeting pop-up have had their moment */
  }
  window.w213 = { start: start, state: function () { return S; } };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v215 Hands-on walkthroughs: the HR centre, step by step, on the real page (07/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel (07/10/26): "a walkthrough of the entire HR center to be done in the trainings and learning avenue ... really, really
   comprehensive, every step of the way ... one walkthrough is the entire recruitment process and you're helping whoever is
   going through this walkthrough literally click the buttons of a test subject ... put themselves as a test subject in their
   own email so that they get the offer letter ... Same thing with onboarding and for the other sections as well."
   - The coach: a card in the corner and a gold ring around the button or field to use next. Unlike the page tours (wave122),
     nothing is blocked: the learner clicks the real buttons, and the card moves on by itself when the Hub shows it is done
     (a card moved, a call logged, a letter sent). Every step can be skipped, and the card can be folded out of the way.
     Back, Skip, Next, Fold, End; Esc does not end it (the Hub's own panels use Esc). The run survives a page reload
     (localStorage w215run, per browser).
   - Practice: while a walkthrough that makes records runs, what the learner adds is marked practice (hub_candidates.practice,
     wa_timeoff.practice): never sent to Slack, never texted, never on the calendar, and the offer letter goes to their own
     address even while email is in test mode (recruit-actions v3). The last step clears them (wa215_clear_practice), and an
     admin can clear everyone's from Applications.
   - In the Avenue Academy: the Learning Avenue "The HR centre" (academy_paths hrcentre): the lesson How the HR centre works
     and seven walkthroughs (trainings kind tour, key tour:hrwalk-*). The walkthroughs register in wave122's TOURS so the
     Academy lists and opens them; wave122's startTour hands them to this coach. Finishing one completes the training.
   API: window.w215 = { start, end, owns, practice, defs, state, me }. Definitions: parts215/b_walks.js. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.w215) return;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', INK = '#1F201E', TEAL = '#2A8C7A';
  var DEFS = {};
  var R = null;   /* the run: { key, i, done:{}, ctx:{}, at } */
  var timer = null, okAt = 0, folded = false;

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function me() { try { return { name: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || '', email: lower(typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) }; } catch (e) { return { name: '', email: '' }; } }
  function save() { try { if (R) localStorage.setItem('w215run', JSON.stringify(R)); else localStorage.removeItem('w215run'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function q(sel) {
    if (!sel) return null;
    if (typeof sel === 'function') { try { return sel(R.ctx) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
    var parts = String(sel).split(' || ');
    for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) { var all = document.querySelectorAll(parts[i]); for (var k = 0; k < all.length; k++) if (vis(all[k])) return all[k]; }
    return null;
  }
  function vis(el) { if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false; var r = el.getBoundingClientRect(); return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 && getComputedStyle(el).visibility !== 'hidden'; }
  function val(v) { return typeof v === 'function' ? v(R.ctx) : v; }

  /* ---------- the card and the ring ---------- */
  function layer() {
    var c = document.getElementById('w215c');
    if (!c) {
      c = document.createElement('div'); c.id = 'w215c'; c.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); c.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite'); c.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Walkthrough');
      document.body.appendChild(c);
      var r = document.createElement('div'); r.id = 'w215r'; r.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); document.body.appendChild(r);
    }
    return c;
  }
  function paint() {
    if (!R) return;
    var d = DEFS[R.key], st = d.steps[R.i]; if (!st) return;
    var n = d.steps.length, isDone = !!R.done[R.i];
    var c = layer();
    c.className = (folded ? 'fold' : '') + (isDone ? ' ok' : '');
    var body = val(st.p) || '';
    var extra = '';
    if (st.copy) { var cp = val(st.copy); if (cp && cp.text) extra += '<div class="w215cp"><span>' + E(cp.label || 'Copy') + '</span><code>' + E(cp.text) + '</code><button type="button" data-w215a="copy">Copy</button></div>'; }
    if (st.fill) extra += '<button type="button" class="w215b soft" data-w215a="fill">' + E(val(st.fillLabel) || 'Fill it in for me') + '</button>';
    if (st.act) extra += '<button type="button" class="w215b" data-w215a="act">' + E(val(st.actLabel) || 'Do it') + '</button>';
    var wait = st.done ? (isDone ? '<p class="w215st ok">' + (R.hold === R.i ? 'Done.' : 'Done. Moving on\u2026') + '</p>' : '<p class="w215st">' + E(val(st.waitText) || 'Waiting for you to do it on the page\u2026') + '</p>') : '';
    c.innerHTML = '<div class="w215h"><span class="w215k">' + E(d.title) + '</span><span class="w215n">' + (R.i + 1) + ' of ' + n + '</span>' +
      '<button type="button" data-w215a="fold" title="' + (folded ? 'Open the card' : 'Fold the card out of the way') + '" aria-label="' + (folded ? 'Open the card' : 'Fold the card') + '">' + (folded ? '\u25b4' : '\u25be') + '</button>' +
      '<button type="button" data-w215a="end" title="End the walkthrough" aria-label="End the walkthrough">\u00d7</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w215bar"><i style="width:' + Math.round(100 * (R.i + (isDone ? 1 : 0)) / n) + '%"></i></div>' +
      (folded ? '<p class="w215ft">' + E(st.t) + '</p>' :
        '<h4>' + E(st.t) + '</h4><div class="w215p">' + body + '</div>' + extra + wait +
        '<div class="w215f">' + (R.i > 0 ? '<button type="button" class="w215b soft" data-w215a="back">Back</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>' +
        (st.done && !isDone ? '<button type="button" class="w215b link" data-w215a="skip">Skip this step</button>' : '') +
        (!st.done || isDone ? '<button type="button" class="w215b" data-w215a="next">' + (R.i >= n - 1 ? 'Finish' : 'Next') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>');
    ring();
  }
  function ring() {
    var r = document.getElementById('w215r'); if (!r || !R) return;
    var st = DEFS[R.key].steps[R.i], el = st && !folded ? q(st.s) : null;
    if (!el || R.done[R.i]) { r.style.display = 'none'; return; }
    var b = el.getBoundingClientRect(), pad = 6;
    r.style.display = 'block';
    r.style.left = Math.round(b.left - pad) + 'px'; r.style.top = Math.round(b.top - pad) + 'px';
    r.style.width = Math.round(b.width + pad * 2) + 'px'; r.style.height = Math.round(b.height + pad * 2) + 'px';
  }
  function scrollTo(st) {
    var el = q(st.s); if (!el) return;
    var b = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (b.top < 70 || b.bottom > innerHeight - 40) { try { el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: 'smooth' }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }

  /* ---------- moving through ---------- */
  async function show(i) {
    if (!R) return;
    var d = DEFS[R.key];
    if (i >= d.steps.length) return finish();
    if (i < 0) i = 0;
    R.i = i; okAt = 0; save();
    var st = d.steps[i];
    if (st.hash) { var h = val(st.hash); if (h && location.hash !== h) { location.hash = h; await wait(900); } }
    if (st.go) { try { await st.go(R.ctx); } catch (e) { /* the step still shows */ } await wait(500); }
    if (st.skipIf) { var sk = false; try { sk = !!st.skipIf(R.ctx); } catch (e) { sk = false; } if (sk) return show(i + 1); }
    folded = false;
    paint();
    setTimeout(function () { if (R && R.i === i) scrollTo(st); }, 250);
  }
  function wait(ms) { return new Promise(function (res) { setTimeout(res, ms); }); }
  function tick() {
    if (!R) return;
    var st = DEFS[R.key].steps[R.i]; if (!st) return;
    ring();
    if (st.done && !R.done[R.i]) {
      var ok = false; try { ok = !!st.done(R.ctx); } catch (e) { ok = false; }
      if (ok) { R.done[R.i] = 1; okAt = performance.now(); save(); paint(); }
    } else if (st.done && R.done[R.i]) {
      if (R.hold === R.i) return;   /* came back with Back: stay until Next */
      if (!okAt) okAt = performance.now();   /* done before (a reload): move on after a beat */
      else if (performance.now() - okAt > 1100) { okAt = 0; show(R.i + 1); }
    }
  }
  function start(key) {
    var d = DEFS[key]; if (!d) { toastS('That walkthrough is not available'); return; }
    if (d.avail && !d.avail()) { toastS('That walkthrough is for the HR team'); return; }
    end(true);
    R = { key: key, i: 0, done: {}, ctx: { started: new Date().toISOString() }, at: Date.now() };
    try { if (d.init) d.init(R.ctx); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (!timer) timer = setInterval(tick, 400);
    show(0);
  }
  function end(silent) {
    var c = document.getElementById('w215c'), r = document.getElementById('w215r');
    if (c) c.remove(); if (r) r.remove();
    var was = R; R = null; save();
    if (timer) { clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }
    if (was && !silent) {
      var d = DEFS[was.key];
      toastS(d && d.practice ? 'Walkthrough ended. Your practice records stay until you clear them (the last step of the walkthrough, or Clear practice on Applications).' : 'Walkthrough ended. Pick it up again from the Avenue Academy.');
    }
  }
  async function finish() {
    var key = R && R.key; end(true);
    var tr = null; try { tr = window.w122 && w122.A && w122.A.byKey ? w122.A.byKey['tour:' + key] : null; } catch (e) { tr = null; }
    if (tr && window.w122 && w122.complete) { try { await w122.complete(tr, { last_step: DEFS[key].steps.length }); return; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
    toastS('Walkthrough complete');
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('#w215c [data-w215a]') : null; if (!t || !R) return;
    var a = t.getAttribute('data-w215a'), st = DEFS[R.key].steps[R.i];
    if (a === 'end') { if (window.confirm('End this walkthrough? You can start it again from the Avenue Academy.')) end(false); return; }
    if (a === 'fold') { folded = !folded; paint(); return; }
    if (a === 'back') { R.hold = R.i - 1; show(R.i - 1); return; }
    if (a === 'next' || a === 'skip') { show(R.i + 1); return; }
    if (a === 'copy') { var cp = val(st.copy); try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(cp.text); toastS('Copied'); } catch (x) { toastS('Select the text and copy it'); } return; }
    if (a === 'fill' && st.fill) { try { await st.fill(R.ctx); } catch (x) { toastS(String((x && x.message) || x)); } return; }
    if (a === 'act' && st.act) { t.disabled = true; try { await st.act(R.ctx); } catch (x) { toastS(String((x && x.message) || x)); } t.disabled = false; return; }
  });
  window.addEventListener('scroll', function () { if (R) ring(); }, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () { if (R) ring(); });

  /* a run in progress picks up again after a reload (once the page has drawn) */
  setTimeout(function () {
    if (R) return;   /* a walkthrough already started */
    var saved = null; try { saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('w215run') || 'null'); } catch (e) { saved = null; }
    if (!saved || !DEFS[saved.key] || Date.now() - (saved.at || 0) > 6 * 3600000) { try { localStorage.removeItem('w215run'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return; }
    R = saved; R.ctx = R.ctx || {}; if (!timer) timer = setInterval(tick, 400); show(R.i);
  }, 3500);

  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'w215css';
  css.textContent =
    '#w215c{position:fixed;left:16px;bottom:16px;z-index:9700;width:min(380px,calc(100vw - 32px));max-height:min(72vh,560px);overflow:auto;background:' + CREAM + ';color:' + INK + ';border:2px solid ' + GREEN + ';border-radius:18px;box-shadow:0 24px 60px -18px rgba(16,63,57,.6);padding:12px 14px 14px;font-family:inherit;box-sizing:border-box;}' +
    '#w215c.ok{border-color:' + TEAL + ';}#w215c.fold{max-height:none;padding-bottom:10px;}' +
    '#w215c .w215h{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;color:' + GREEN + ';}#w215c .w215k{flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;}#w215c .w215n{color:#5b6b67;font-weight:600;}' +
    '#w215c .w215h button{border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);background:transparent;color:' + GREEN + ';width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer;font-size:15px;line-height:1;}' +
    '#w215c .w215bar{height:5px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);margin:8px 0 10px;overflow:hidden;}#w215c .w215bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:' + GOLD + ';transition:width .3s;}' +
    '#w215c h4{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:17px;color:' + GREEN + ';}#w215c .w215p{font-size:14px;line-height:1.5;}#w215c .w215p p{margin:0 0 8px;}#w215c .w215p ul,#w215c .w215p ol{margin:4px 0 8px;padding-left:20px;}#w215c .w215p li{margin:3px 0;}' +
    '#w215c .w215p b{color:' + GREEN + ';}#w215c .w215ft{margin:0;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;}' +
    '#w215c .w215st{font-size:13px;margin:8px 0 0;padding:7px 10px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(220,165,39,.18);color:#5c4408;}#w215c .w215st.ok{background:rgba(42,140,122,.16);color:#1d6b5d;}' +
    '#w215c .w215cp{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:center;margin:8px 0;padding:8px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);font-size:12.5px;}#w215c .w215cp code{flex:1 1 100%;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:12px;}#w215c .w215cp button{border:none;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';border-radius:999px;padding:4px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;}' +
    '#w215c .w215f{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin-top:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;}#w215c .sp{flex:1;}' +
    '.w215b{border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:8px 14px;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';font:inherit;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;margin-top:4px;}.w215b.soft{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);color:' + GREEN + ';}.w215b.link{background:none;color:' + GREEN + ';text-decoration:underline;padding:8px 4px;font-weight:600;}' +
    '#w215r{position:fixed;z-index:9690;pointer-events:none;border:3px solid ' + GOLD + ';border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(220,165,39,.28);display:none;animation:w215p 1.6s ease-in-out infinite;}' +
    '@keyframes w215p{0%,100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(220,165,39,.28);}50%{box-shadow:0 0 0 10px rgba(220,165,39,.08);}}' +
    '@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){#w215r{animation:none;}}' +
    '@media (max-width:600px){#w215c{left:8px;right:8px;bottom:8px;width:auto;max-height:46vh;}}' +
    '.w215prac{display:inline-block;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:999px;padding:1px 7px;margin:2px 4px 0 0;}';
  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(css);

  window.w215 = {
    start: start, end: end, defs: DEFS, me: me, q: q,
    owns: function (key) { return !!DEFS[key]; },
    practice: function () { return !!(R && DEFS[R.key] && DEFS[R.key].practice); },
    state: function () { return R; },
    add: function (key, d) { DEFS[key] = d; }
  };
})();
/* wave215 b_walks: the seven HR centre walkthroughs. Each step: t (title), p (what to do, HTML), s (what to ring),
   hash (open a page first), go (do something first), done (true when the learner has done it: the card moves on),
   skipIf, copy {label, text}, fill / act (a helper button). Selectors are the live ones (waves 46, 105, 116p, 169, 197, 199,
   209, 211, 212); test/w215drive.js walks every one of them. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (!window.w215) return;
  var W = window.w215;
  var SAMPLE_CV = 'https://docs.google.com/document/d/1loqe66CRVkBYTTmsJ0TQbjzlZ6UEZSDSbCisQ2Ktd3k/edit';

  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function P() { return Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments).map(function (x) { return x.charAt(0) === '<' ? x : '<p>' + x + '</p>'; }).join(''); }
  function L(items) { return '<ul>' + items.map(function (x) { return '<li>' + x + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>'; }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function S() { try { return w197.state(); } catch (e) { return { cands: [], data: null }; } }
  function cands() { return S().cands || []; }
  function mine() { var em = W.me().email; return cands().filter(function (c) { return c.practice && lower(c.email) === em; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.id - a.id; })[0] || null; }
  function cand() { var c = mine(); return c ? cands().filter(function (x) { return x.id === c.id; })[0] : null; }
  function open() { var s = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); return !!(s && s.classList.contains('open')); }
  function sheetFor(c) { return !!c && open() && String(S().open) === String(c.id); }
  function hr() { try { return typeof window.wa46IsHr === 'function' ? !!wa46IsHr() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function modalOpen() { var m = document.getElementById('modalBack'); return !!(m && getComputedStyle(m).display !== 'none'); }
  function setVal(id, v) { var x = document.getElementById(id); if (!x) return false; x.value = v; x.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); x.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); return true; }
  function iso(d) { var p = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }; return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate()); }
  function local(d) { var p = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }; return iso(d) + 'T' + p(d.getHours()) + ':' + p(d.getMinutes()); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function openMine() { var c = cand(); if (c && !sheetFor(c)) { try { w197.openSheet(c.id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  async function clearPractice() {
    var r = await db.rpc('wa215_clear_practice', { p_all: false });
    if (r && r.error) throw new Error('Could not clear: ' + r.error.message);
    try { await w197.loadCands(true); w197.paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (window.w105TimeRender) w105TimeRender(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var d = (r && r.data) || {};
    toastS('Cleared ' + (d.applications || 0) + (d.applications === 1 ? ' practice application' : ' practice applications') + ' and ' + (d.timeoff || 0) + ' practice time off');
    W.state() && (W.state().ctx.cleared = true);
  }
  var CLEAR = function (what) {
    return { t: 'Clear your practice records', p: P('That is the whole flow. Your practice ' + what + ' is marked <b>Practice</b>: it never reached Slack, a text, an applicant or the calendar.',
      'Press <b>Clear my practice records</b> to remove it now. (If you stop part way, you can always clear it later with <b>Clear practice</b> on Applications.)'),
      act: clearPractice, actLabel: 'Clear my practice records', done: function (x) { return !!x.cleared; }, waitText: 'Press Clear my practice records' };
  };

  /* ================= 1. Getting around ================= */
  W.add('hrwalk-start', { title: 'HR centre: getting around', avail: hr, steps: [

    { hash: '#hrcentre/overview', s: '#w153rail .w153sec || #w153rail', t: 'The rail: every page of the HR centre', p: P('The pages are grouped the way the work flows:') + L(['<b>People</b>: Team, Hours, Time off, Recognition', '<b>Hiring</b>: Applications, Positions, Playbook', '<b>Growth</b>: Onboarding, Onboarding plan, Training', '<b>Records</b>: Policies, Discipline (the HR access list only)']) + P('Each page has its own walkthrough in the Avenue Academy, under the Learning Avenue <b>The HR centre</b>.') },
    { s: '#w197ov .w197tiles', t: 'Overview: the numbers that matter', p: P('Six tiles: people on the roster, people in The Loop this week, new applications, people onboarding now, people with a training overdue, and time off waiting for a decision.', 'A tile with something to act on is highlighted. <b>Click any tile</b> to go to its page.'),
      done: function (x) { return location.hash !== '#hrcentre/overview' && /^#hrcentre\//.test(location.hash); }, waitText: 'Click a tile' },

      done: function () { try { return !!w212.state().open; } catch (e) { return false; } }, waitText: 'Click the bell' },
    { s: '#w212box', t: 'Each line opens what it is about', p: P('Click a line to jump to the card or the page. Click anywhere else to close the list. The count resets when you open it.') },
    { s: '#hrcentre > .w118mast .w43help', t: 'How this page works', p: P('Every page of the HR centre has this button. It explains the page you are on in a few lines.') },

    { go: openMine, s: '#w197own1', t: 'Choose who handles it', p: P('Every applicant needs someone handling them before they move to Reviewing. That person gets a Slack message when the card moves on (practice cards never do).', '<b>Choose yourself.</b>'),
      done: function () { var c = cand(); return !!(c && c.owner); }, waitText: 'Choose yourself in Handled by' },

      done: function () { var c = cand(); return !!(c && c.status === 'hired'); }, waitText: 'Click Hired and fill the two dates' },
    { go: openMine, s: '#w197sheet .next', t: 'Next: onboarding', p: P('At Hired, the card points to <b>Go to Onboarding</b>: the next walkthrough. HR assigns their onboarding plan and sends the Welcome kit there.') },
    { go: openMine, s: '#w197sheet .stg button.cl', t: 'When it is a no', p: P('<b>Declined</b> (we said no) and <b>Withdrawn</b> (they said no) close an application. Both ask for a reason, which stays on the card. A closed card can be moved back to a stage if things change.', 'Nothing to click here.') },
    CLEAR('application')
  ] });

      fill: function () { var d = new Date(Date.now() + 60 * 86400000); setVal('w105rs', iso(d)); var e = new Date(d.getTime() + 2 * 86400000); setVal('w105re', iso(e)); setVal('w105rn', 'Practice request from the HR walkthrough'); toastS('Dates filled.     { s: '#w116pq', t: 'Find someone', p: P('<b>Type a name</b> to filter the roster.'), done: function () { var x = document.getElementById('w116pq'); return !!(x && x.value.trim().length >= 2); }, waitText: 'Type a name' },
    { hash: '#hrcentre/hours', s: '#w197hours .w197tiles || #w197hours', t: 'Hours', p: P('Hours come from two places:') + L(['<b>The Loop</b>: from I\u2019m in to Close the loop (a session left open counts at most eight hours)', '<b>Slack</b>: the hours people log with their own \u201cin\u201d and \u201cout\u201d messages in the Slack check-in channels, read every morning']) + P('The tiles show this week at a glance.') },

    { s: '#w197hours .w197feed || #w197hours .w197card + .w197card || #w197hours', t: 'What people shared in The Loop', p: P('When people close the loop they can share what moved. It lands in #check-in-out-wa and here. Never times or hours.') },
    { s: '#w144hr', t: 'Meeting attendance', p: P('Who came to which team meeting, from Google Meet. Refresh it with the button.') },
    { hash: '#hrcentre/recognition', s: '#w116psf', t: 'Recognition', p: P('Shout-outs for someone who did something worth noticing.     { s: '#w116pshouts .ls || #w116pshouts', t: 'Recent shout-outs', p: P('Newest first. Admins can take any down, and authors their own.') }
  ] });

  /* ================= 6. Training and the onboarding plan ================= */

      { hash: '#hrcentre/training', s: '#w169cardT', t: 'Trainings assigned to the organization', p: P('Each assignment: what, to whom (everyone, teams or people), the due date, and a bar of who finished. <b>See who</b> lists them by name.') },

  ] });

  /* ---------- the Academy lists and opens them (wave122's TOURS) ---------- */
  function register() {
    if (!window.w122 || !w122.tours) return false;
    Object.keys(W.defs).forEach(function (k) {
      var d = W.defs[k];
      w122.tours[k] = { tab: 'hrcentre', icon: 'people', hash: '#hrcentre/overview', avail: d.avail, w215: true,
        steps: d.steps.map(function (s) { return { c: 1, t: s.t, p: String(s.p || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 160) }; }) };
    });
    return true;
  }
  if (!register()) { var n = 0, t = setInterval(function () { if (register() || ++n > 40) clearInterval(t); }, 500); }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v218 Volunteer instead: an applicant from the HR centre to the Volunteer Manager (Steel, 07/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "sometimes when we call applicants, they ended up wanting to just do volunteer work ... cook, server, driver,
   team leader, community lead ... I want the ability to send applicants that come in through the HR center over to the
   volunteer manager whenever an applicant says that they want to do something outside of the management team."
   His choices: the card closes as Volunteering (kept, linked to their volunteer record); HR may put them on upcoming
   servings in the same step (Waiting for response, no text); nobody is notified.
   - Every open card's sheet has "Volunteer instead" (a card for a volunteer role keeps wave209's box, which now opens
     the same step). The stage row's Volunteering button opens it too.
   - The step: the roles (from what they applied for when it is a volunteer role), the next servings (ticked ones put
     them on the roster as Waiting for response in their first role; nothing is texted), a note for Operations.
   - Then: one record with the CRM in Volunteers (Review flag, tags), an application in Applicants received (To handle,
     "Cook, Server (instead of Operations Manager)", with the CV and what they wrote), the card closed as Volunteering.
   - A closed Volunteering card says what they will do and opens their volunteer record or Applicants received.
   - Practice cards (Avenue Academy) only close: nothing reaches Volunteers, the CRM or a roster.
   Database: wa218_to_volunteer (build/data/wa218_to_volunteer.sql), hub_candidates.volunteer_id.
   Hooks: parts197 (CLOSED, moveTo, the closed list, NEXT), parts209 sheetBits and moveCard, parts211 (closed, labels). */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', INK = '#1F201E';
  var ROLES = [['cook', 'Cook'], ['server', 'Server'], ['driver', 'Driver'], ['team_leader', 'Team leader'], ['community_debrief', 'Community lead'],
               ['photographer', 'Photographer'], ['videographer', 'Videographer'], ['dishwasher', 'Dishwashers']];   /* wave239: videographer */
  var D = { c: null, roles: [], events: [], picked: [], note: '', busy: false, err: '', evAt: 0, evs: null };

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return '&#' + c.charCodeAt(0) + ';'; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function btn(label, attrs, kind) { return '<button type="button" class="w197b' + (kind ? ' ' + kind : '') + '" ' + (attrs || '') + '>' + label + '</button>'; }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function cand(id) { try { return (w197.state().cands || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function label(k) { var x = ROLES.filter(function (r) { return r[0] === k; })[0]; return x ? x[1] : (k === 'volunteer' ? 'Volunteer' : k); }
  function today() { var d = new Date(), p = function (n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }; return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate()); }
  function dayTxt(iso) { try { var d = new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00'); if (isNaN(d)) return String(iso); return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(iso); } }
  function msgOf(e) { var m = (e && (e.message || e.error_description || e.details)) || String(e || ''); return String(m).replace(/^.*?ERROR:\s*/, ''); }
  function titleOf(c) {
    var t = c.role || '';
    try { if (window.w199 && w199.roleOf) t = w199.roleOf(c) || t; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return t;
  }
  function suggested(c) { var r = []; try { if (window.w209 && w209.volRoles) r = w209.volRoles(titleOf(c)) || []; } catch (e) { r = []; } return r.filter(function (k) { return k !== 'volunteer'; }); }

  /* ---------- the sheet ---------- */
  function sheetBits(c, volRole) {
    if (!c || !staff()) return '';
    if (c.status === 'volunteering') {
      return '<div class="w218box done"><b>' + E(c.close_reason || 'Volunteering') + '</b> They are with the Volunteer Manager: in Volunteers, with their contact in the CRM, and in Applicants received for Operations to welcome.' +
        (c.practice ? ' <span class="w197pill mut">Practice: nothing was added to Volunteers</span>' : '') +
        '<div class="acts">' + (c.volunteer_id ? btn('Their volunteer record', 'data-w218rec="' + E(c.volunteer_id) + '"', 'sm pri') : '') + btn('Applicants received', 'data-w218go="applicants"', 'sm') + '</div></div>';
    }
    if (c.status === 'hired') return '';
    var lead = volRole && volRole.length ? '<b>' + E(volRole.map(label).join(', ')) + ' is a volunteer role.</b> Volunteer applicants are handled in the Volunteer Manager.'
      : '<b>Would they rather volunteer?</b> If they want to cook, serve, drive or lead at servings instead of joining the team, send them to the Volunteer Manager.';
    return '<div class="w218box">' + lead + '<div class="acts">' + btn(volRole && volRole.length ? 'Move to the Volunteer Manager' : 'Volunteer instead', 'data-w218open="' + c.id + '"', 'sm' + (volRole && volRole.length ? ' pri' : '')) + '</div></div>';
  }

  /* ---------- the step ---------- */
  async function servings() {
    if (D.evs && Date.now() - D.evAt < 60000) return D.evs;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id,code,title,event_date,start_time,status,location').eq('kind', 'service').gte('event_date', today())
        .not('status', 'in', '("cancelled","completed")').order('event_date', { ascending: true }).limit(10);
      D.evs = ((r && !r.error && r.data) || []).filter(function (e) { return !/^\s*test\b/i.test(e.title || ''); }).slice(0, 6);
    } catch (e) { D.evs = []; }
    D.evAt = Date.now();
    return D.evs;
  }
  function dlg() {
    var d = document.getElementById('w218dlg');
    if (!d) { d = document.createElement('div'); d.id = 'w218dlg'; d.setAttribute('role', 'dialog'); d.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true'); d.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'w218h'); document.body.appendChild(d); }
    return d;
  }
  function paint() {
    var c = D.c; if (!c) return;
    var d = dlg();
    var evs = D.events || [];
    d.innerHTML = '<div class="bk" data-w218x></div><div class="pn">' + btn('Close', 'data-w218x', 'sm x') +
      '<h3 id="w218h">' + E(c.name || 'This applicant') + ': volunteer instead</h3>' +
      '<p class="w218lead">They join Volunteers (one record with the CRM) and Applicants received for Operations to welcome. Their card closes as Volunteering. Nobody is notified and nothing is texted.</p>' +
      '<label>What they would like to do</label><div class="w218roles">' + ROLES.map(function (r) {
        return '<label class="w218chip"><input type="checkbox" data-w218role="' + r[0] + '"' + (D.roles.indexOf(r[0]) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + '> ' + E(r[1]) + '</label>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<label>Put them on a serving? <small>optional</small></label>' +
      (D.evs === null ? '<p class="w197note">Loading the servings\u2026</p>' : (evs.length ? '<div class="w218evs">' + evs.map(function (e) {
        return '<label class="w218ev"><input type="checkbox" data-w218ev="' + e.id + '"' + (D.picked.indexOf(String(e.id)) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + '> <b>' + E(dayTxt(e.event_date)) + '</b> <span>' + E([e.code, e.title].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</span></label>';
      }).join('') + '</div><p class="w197note">They go on the roster as Waiting for response in their first role. Operations confirms them; no text goes from here.</p>'
        : '<p class="w197note">No upcoming servings. Operations can invite them later with Find volunteers.</p>')) +
      '<label for="w218note">A note for Operations <small>optional</small></label><textarea id="w218note" placeholder="What they said on the call: availability, a car, languages">' + E(D.note) + '</textarea>' +
      (D.err ? '<p class="w218err" role="alert">' + E(D.err) + '</p>' : '') +
      '<div class="acts">' + btn(D.busy ? 'Moving\u2026' : 'Move to the Volunteer Manager', 'data-w218go="send"' + (D.busy || !D.roles.length ? ' disabled' : ''), 'pri') + btn('Cancel', 'data-w218x') + '</div>' +
      (!D.roles.length ? '<p class="w197note">Pick at least one role.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    d.classList.add('open');
  }
  async function open(id) {
    var c = typeof id === 'object' ? id : cand(id); if (!c) return;
    if (!staff()) { toastS('Staff only'); return; }
    if (c.status === 'volunteering') { toastS((c.name || 'They') + ' are already with the Volunteer Manager'); return; }
    D.c = c; D.roles = suggested(c); D.picked = []; D.note = ''; D.err = ''; D.busy = false;
    if (!D.evs || Date.now() - D.evAt >= 60000) D.evs = null;
    paint();
    D.events = await servings(); if (D.c === c) paint();
    var f = document.querySelector('#w218dlg [data-w218role]'); if (f) { try { f.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function shut() { var d = document.getElementById('w218dlg'); if (d) d.classList.remove('open'); D.c = null; }
  function keep() {
    var d = document.getElementById('w218dlg'); if (!d) return;
    D.roles = Array.prototype.slice.call(d.querySelectorAll('[data-w218role]:checked')).map(function (x) { return x.getAttribute('data-w218role'); });
    D.picked = Array.prototype.slice.call(d.querySelectorAll('[data-w218ev]:checked')).map(function (x) { return x.getAttribute('data-w218ev'); });
    var n = document.getElementById('w218note'); if (n) D.note = n.value;
  }
  async function send() {
    var c = D.c; if (!c || D.busy) return; keep();
    if (!D.roles.length) { D.err = 'Pick at least one role.'; paint(); return; }
    D.busy = true; D.err = ''; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa218_to_volunteer', { p_id: Number(c.id), p_roles: D.roles, p_events: D.picked.map(Number), p_note: (D.note || '').slice(0, 1500) || null });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var res = (r && r.data) || {};
      c.status = 'volunteering'; c.close_reason = res.close_reason || ('Volunteering: ' + D.roles.map(label).join(', ')); c.volunteer_id = res.person_id || c.volunteer_id;
      c.stage_at = c.updated_at = new Date().toISOString();
      shut();
      try { w197.paintBoard(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (String(w197.state().open) === String(c.id)) w197.openSheet(c.id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (window.w209) { w209.state().at = 0; w209.counts(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      toastS((c.name || 'The applicant') + ' is with the Volunteer Manager: ' + (res.labels || D.roles.map(label).join(', ')) +
        (res.practice ? ' (practice: nothing added)' : (res.added ? '; on ' + res.added + (res.added === 1 ? ' serving' : ' servings') + ', waiting for their answer' : '')));
    } catch (e) { D.busy = false; D.err = 'Could not move them: ' + msgOf(e); paint(); }
  }
  function openRecord(vid) {
    location.hash = '#volunteering/people';
    var tries = 0, tick = function () {
      var list = (typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_PEOPLE) || [];
      if (list.some(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(vid); }) && typeof wa26PersonModal === 'function') { try { wa26PersonModal(vid); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return; }
      if (++tries < 40) setTimeout(tick, 250);
    };
    setTimeout(tick, 250);
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w218open],[data-w218x],[data-w218go],[data-w218rec]') : null; if (!t) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w218open')) { e.preventDefault(); open(t.getAttribute('data-w218open')); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w218x')) { e.preventDefault(); shut(); return; }
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w218rec')) { e.preventDefault(); var sh = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (sh) sh.classList.remove('open'); openRecord(t.getAttribute('data-w218rec')); return; }
    var g = t.getAttribute('data-w218go');
    if (g === 'send') { e.preventDefault(); send(); return; }
    if (g === 'applicants') { e.preventDefault(); var s2 = document.getElementById('w197sheet'); if (s2) s2.classList.remove('open'); location.hash = '#volunteering/applicants'; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w218dlg')) return;
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w218role')) { keep(); D.err = ''; paint(); var x = document.querySelector('#w218dlg [data-w218role="' + t.getAttribute('data-w218role') + '"]'); if (x) { try { x.focus(); } catch (y) { /* fine */ } } }
    else if (t.hasAttribute('data-w218ev')) keep();
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { if (e.target && e.target.id === 'w218note') D.note = e.target.value; });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && D.c) { var d = document.getElementById('w218dlg'); if (d && d.classList.contains('open')) { e.stopPropagation(); shut(); } } }, true);

  var css = '#w218dlg{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10050;display:none;}#w218dlg.open{display:block;}' +

  try { var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'w218css'; st.textContent = css; document.head.appendChild(st); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  window.w218 = { open: open, sheetBits: sheetBits, openRecord: openRecord, label: label, state: function () { return D; } };
