/* =====================================================================
   WA v219 Hub notifications on computers and phones (Steel, 07/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I'm wondering if when I add the Hub website as an app on my Android phone, or if I could do that on Apple, um,
   and even in the browser, if it can send in-app notifications on updates, I would like to do that if it's possible,
   especially browser use for those that are using the computer, which is most right now."
   His choices: mentions and assigned work, and announcements; each person chooses Hub notifications, Slack or both (both
   by default); one gentle prompt on Home the first time the Hub opens on a device, and a switch in Settings any time (on
   iPhone, Add to Home Screen first).
   - The Hub is an app: the manifest (/manifest.webmanifest), the icons (/icons/), the service worker (/sw.js:
     notifications only, no caching), all deployed by host-sync v12 (build/site). Chrome and Edge offer Install; Settings
     has an Install button where the browser allows it; on iPhone, Share, Add to Home Screen.
   - Home (#w219card): "Get Hub notifications on this computer?" (or phone, or iPad) with Turn on and Not now, once per
     device; on an iPhone in Safari it says to add the Hub to the Home Screen first. Turn on asks the browser's permission
     (from the tap), subscribes with the Hub's public key (app_config push_vapid_public) and saves the device
     (wa219_push_save); Turn off and Remove go through hub-push (drop).
   - Settings, Notifications (#w219set, through wave138's afterPaint): this device on or off, Send me a test, the other
     devices (remove one), where mentions and work for you arrive (In the Hub and on Slack, In the Hub only, On Slack only:
     wa219_channel_set), new announcements on or off (wave116j's switch list, key announcement), Install the Hub app.
   - Every visit re-saves this device when the person turned it on here (localStorage w219on:<email>), and subscribes again
     if the browser dropped it. Sign out turns this device off first.
   - A notification click brings the open Hub forward on its link (wa-open), or opens the Hub there. A push that arrives
     while the Hub is open fires the window event wa-push.
   Data and sending: build/data/wa219_push.sql, fn/hub-push (v1), notify v12 (Hub only skips Slack when a device is on).
   window.__wa219: state, check, turnOn, turnOff, test, sync, paintCard, paintSettings, deviceLabel. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa219) return;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', INK = '#1F201E';
  var W = { reg: null, regP: null, sub: null, mine: false, checked: false, checking: null, key: null, keyAt: 0, bip: null, busy: false,
            channel: 'both', disabled: [], devices: [], prefsAt: 0, installed: false };
  var BELL = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path fill="currentColor" d="M12 22a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 2.45-2h-4.9A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 12 22Zm7-6V11a7 7 0 0 0-5.5-6.84V3.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 0-3 0v.66A7 7 0 0 0 5 11v5l-1.7 1.7A1 1 0 0 0 4 19.4h16a1 1 0 0 0 .7-1.7Z"/></svg>';

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function say(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function myEmail() { try { return String((USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? !!isStaff() : false; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function lsGet(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function lsSet(k, v) { try { if (v == null) localStorage.removeItem(k); else localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function wait(ms) { return new Promise(function (r) { setTimeout(r, ms); }); }

  var UA = navigator.userAgent || '';
  var IPAD = /iPad/.test(UA) || (/Macintosh/.test(UA) && (navigator.maxTouchPoints || 0) > 1);
  var IOS = IPAD || /iPhone|iPod/.test(UA);
  function standalone() { try { return (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches) || navigator.standalone === true; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function supported() { return ('serviceWorker' in navigator) && ('PushManager' in window) && ('Notification' in window); }
  function perm() { try { return window.Notification ? Notification.permission : 'default'; } catch (e) { return 'default'; } }
  function here() { return IPAD ? 'this iPad' : (/Android|iPhone|iPod|Mobi/.test(UA) ? 'this phone' : 'this computer'); }
  function evenWhen() { return here() === 'this computer' ? 'even with the Hub in another tab' : 'even when the Hub is closed'; }
  function deviceLabel() {
    var b = /Edg[A]?\//.test(UA) ? 'Edge' : /OPR\//.test(UA) ? 'Opera' : /SamsungBrowser/.test(UA) ? 'Samsung Internet' : /Firefox\//.test(UA) ? 'Firefox'
      : /Chrome\/|CriOS/.test(UA) ? 'Chrome' : /Safari\//.test(UA) ? 'Safari' : 'A browser';
    var o = IPAD ? 'iPad' : /iPhone|iPod/.test(UA) ? 'iPhone' : /Android/.test(UA) ? 'Android' : /Windows/.test(UA) ? 'Windows'
      : /CrOS/.test(UA) ? 'Chromebook' : /Mac OS X|Macintosh/.test(UA) ? 'Mac' : /Linux/.test(UA) ? 'Linux' : '';
    return (standalone() ? 'The Hub app' : b) + (o ? ' on ' + o : '');
  }

  /* ---------- the head: the app manifest and icons ---------- */
  function headLinks() {
    try {
      var h = document.head; if (!h) return;
      if (!h.querySelector('link[rel="manifest"]')) { var m = document.createElement('link'); m.rel = 'manifest'; m.href = '/manifest.webmanifest'; h.appendChild(m); }
      [['apple-mobile-web-app-capable', 'yes'], ['mobile-web-app-capable', 'yes'], ['apple-mobile-web-app-title', 'WA Hub'], ['apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style', 'default'], ['application-name', 'WA Hub']].forEach(function (x) {
        if (h.querySelector('meta[name="' + x[0] + '"]')) return;
        var t = document.createElement('meta'); t.name = x[0]; t.content = x[1]; h.appendChild(t);
      });
      /* iOS reads the touch icon when someone adds the Hub to the Home Screen: the real file (wave118 draws a data: copy) */
      var ti = h.querySelectorAll('link[rel="apple-touch-icon"]');
      if (!ti.length) { var l = document.createElement('link'); l.rel = 'apple-touch-icon'; h.appendChild(l); ti = [l]; }
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(ti, function (l) { if (l.getAttribute('href') !== '/icons/apple-touch-180.png') { l.setAttribute('href', '/icons/apple-touch-180.png'); l.setAttribute('sizes', '180x180'); } });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  headLinks();
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', headLinks);
  window.addEventListener('load', function () { headLinks(); setTimeout(headLinks, 3000); setTimeout(headLinks, 9000); });

  /* the browser's own Install offer (Chrome, Edge, Android); kept for the Settings button */
  window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', function (e) { W.bip = e; paintSettings(); });
  window.addEventListener('appinstalled', function () { W.bip = null; W.installed = true; say('The Hub is installed. Open it from your apps like any other.'); paintSettings(); });

  /* ---------- the service worker and the subscription ---------- */
  function register() {
    if (W.regP) return W.regP;
    if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return Promise.resolve(null);
    W.regP = navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js', { scope: '/' }).then(function (r) { W.reg = r; return r; }, function () { W.regP = null; return null; });
    return W.regP;
  }
  function ready(ms) {
    if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return Promise.resolve(null);
    return Promise.race([navigator.serviceWorker.ready, wait(ms || 8000).then(function () { return null; })]);
  }
  function b64ToBytes(s) {
    var t = String(s || '').replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'); while (t.length % 4) t += '=';
    var bin = atob(t), out = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
    for (var i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    return out;
  }
  function sameKey(sub, key) {
    try {
      var k = sub && sub.options && sub.options.applicationServerKey; if (!k) return true;
      var a = new Uint8Array(k), b = b64ToBytes(key); if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
      for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) if (a[i] !== b[i]) return false;
      return true;
    } catch (e) { return true; }
  }
  async function publicKey(force) {
    if (W.key && !force) return W.key;
    if (!force && W.keyAt && Date.now() - W.keyAt < 60000) return W.key;
    W.keyAt = Date.now();
    try { var r = await db.from('app_config').select('value').eq('key', 'push_vapid_public').maybeSingle(); W.key = (r && r.data && r.data.value) || null; } catch (e) { W.key = null; }
    return W.key;
  }
  async function save(sub) {
    var j = sub.toJSON ? sub.toJSON() : sub;
    var r = await db.rpc('wa219_push_save', { p_endpoint: j.endpoint, p_p256dh: j.keys && j.keys.p256dh, p_auth: j.keys && j.keys.auth, p_device: deviceLabel() });
    if (r && r.error) throw new Error(r.error.message || 'Could not save this device');
    return r && r.data;
  }
  async function loadDevices() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_push_subs').select('id, endpoint, device, created_at, last_sent_at, last_error').eq('email', myEmail()).order('created_at', { ascending: true });
      W.devices = (r && Array.isArray(r.data)) ? r.data : [];
    } catch (e) { W.devices = []; }
    W.mine = !!(W.sub && W.devices.some(function (d) { return d.endpoint === W.sub.endpoint; }));
  }
  async function loadPrefs() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_notify_prefs').select('disabled, prefs').eq('email', myEmail()).maybeSingle();
      var d = r && r.data;
      W.disabled = (d && Array.isArray(d.disabled)) ? d.disabled : [];
      var ch = d && d.prefs && d.prefs.channel; W.channel = (ch === 'hub' || ch === 'slack') ? ch : 'both';
    } catch (e) { /* keep */ }
    W.prefsAt = Date.now();
  }
  /* where this device stands: registered, subscribed, and saved for this person */
  function check() {
    if (W.checking) return W.checking;
    W.checking = (async function () {
      try {
        await publicKey();
        if (supported()) {
          var reg = await register();
          if (reg) { try { W.sub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription(); } catch (e) { W.sub = null; } }
        }
        if (myEmail()) await Promise.all([loadDevices(), loadPrefs()]);
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      W.checked = true; W.checking = null;
      paintCard(); paintSettings();
    })();
    return W.checking;
  }

  /* Turn on: the permission is asked first, straight from the tap (Safari only allows it there) */
  function askPermission() {
    if (!window.Notification) return Promise.resolve('unsupported');
    if (Notification.permission === 'granted' || Notification.permission === 'denied') return Promise.resolve(Notification.permission);
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
      var done = false; function fin(p) { if (!done) { done = true; resolve(p || Notification.permission); } }
      try { var ret = Notification.requestPermission(fin); if (ret && typeof ret.then === 'function') ret.then(fin, function () { fin('default'); }); } catch (e) { fin('default'); }
    });
  }
  async function turnOn() {
    if (W.busy) return false;
    var permP = askPermission();
    W.busy = true; paintCard(); paintSettings();
    var ok = false;
    try {
      if (!supported()) throw new Error(IOS && !standalone() ? 'On iPhone and iPad, add the Hub to your Home Screen first, then turn this on in the Hub app.' : 'This browser cannot show Hub notifications.');
      var p = await permP;
      if (p !== 'granted') {
        lsSet('w219ask', 'no');
        say(p === 'denied' ? 'Notifications are blocked for the Hub in this browser. Settings, Notifications shows how to allow them.' : 'Nothing changed. You can turn them on any time in Settings, Notifications.');
      } else {
        var key = await publicKey(true);
        if (!key) throw new Error('Hub notifications are not set up yet. Try again a little later.');
        await register();
        var reg = await ready(10000);
        if (!reg) throw new Error('This browser did not start the Hub’s notification helper. Reload the Hub and try again.');
        var sub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription();
        if (sub && !sameKey(sub, key)) { try { await sub.unsubscribe(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } sub = null; }
        if (!sub) sub = await reg.pushManager.subscribe({ userVisibleOnly: true, applicationServerKey: b64ToBytes(key) });
        await save(sub);
        W.sub = sub; W.mine = true;
        lsSet('w219on:' + myEmail(), '1'); lsSet('w219ask', 'done');
        await loadDevices();
        say('Hub notifications are on for ' + here() + '.');
        ok = true;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      say(String((e && e.message) || e || 'Could not turn them on').slice(0, 200));
    }
    W.busy = false; paintCard(); paintSettings();
    return ok;
  }
  /* a device comes off through hub-push (the caller's own rows only) */
  async function dropRow(endpoint) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-push', { body: { action: 'drop', endpoint: endpoint } });
    if (r && r.error) throw new Error(r.error.message || 'failed');
    return r && r.data;
  }
  async function dropThis(quiet) {
    var sub = W.sub;
    try { if (!sub && supported()) { var reg = await register(); sub = reg ? await reg.pushManager.getSubscription() : null; } } catch (e) { sub = null; }
    if (sub) {
      try { await dropRow(sub.endpoint); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { await sub.unsubscribe(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    lsSet('w219on:' + myEmail(), null);
    W.sub = null; W.mine = false;
    if (!quiet) { await loadDevices(); say('Hub notifications are off for ' + here() + '.'); }
  }
  async function turnOff() {
    if (W.busy) return; W.busy = true; paintSettings();
    try { await dropThis(false); } catch (e) { say('Could not turn them off'); }
    W.busy = false; paintCard(); paintSettings();
  }
  async function test() {
    if (W.busy) return; W.busy = true; paintSettings();
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-push', { body: { action: 'test', endpoint: W.sub ? W.sub.endpoint : null } });
      var d = (r && r.data) || {};
      if (r && r.error) throw new Error(r.error.message || 'failed');
      if (d.ok) say('Sent. It should pop up on ' + here() + ' in a moment.');
      else if (d.gone) { say('This browser no longer has the subscription. Turn it on again.'); W.sub = null; W.mine = false; lsSet('w219on:' + myEmail(), null); }
      else say('The test did not go through' + (d.error ? ': ' + String(d.error).slice(0, 120) : '.'));
    } catch (e) { say('The test did not go through: ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); }
    W.busy = false; await loadDevices(); paintSettings();
  }
  async function removeDevice(endpoint) {
    try { await dropRow(endpoint); say('That device will not get Hub notifications any more.'); } catch (e) { say('Could not remove it'); }
    await loadDevices(); paintSettings();
  }
  async function setChannel(ch) {
    var before = W.channel; W.channel = ch; paintSettings();
    try { var r = await db.rpc('wa219_channel_set', { p_channel: ch }); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; say(ch === 'hub' ? 'Mentions and work for you now arrive in the Hub only.' : ch === 'slack' ? 'Mentions and work for you now arrive on Slack only.' : 'Mentions and work for you arrive in the Hub and on Slack.'); }
    catch (e) { W.channel = before; say('Could not save that'); paintSettings(); }
  }
  /* every visit: this device stays saved for the person who turned it on here; a dropped subscription is made again */
  async function sync() {
    try {
      if (!staff() || !myEmail() || !supported() || perm() !== 'granted') return;
      if (lsGet('w219on:' + myEmail()) !== '1') return;
      var key = await publicKey(); if (!key) return;
      await register(); var reg = await ready(8000); if (!reg) return;
      var sub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription();
      if (sub && !sameKey(sub, key)) { try { await sub.unsubscribe(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } sub = null; }
      if (!sub) sub = await reg.pushManager.subscribe({ userVisibleOnly: true, applicationServerKey: b64ToBytes(key) });
      await save(sub); W.sub = sub; W.mine = true;
    } catch (e) { /* the next visit tries again */ }
  }

  /* ---------- Home: one gentle prompt per device ---------- */
  function cardKind() {
    if (!staff() || !myEmail() || (W.busy && !document.getElementById('w219card'))) return '';
    var asked = lsGet('w219ask');
    if (asked === 'no' || asked === 'done') return '';
    if (IOS && !standalone() && !supported()) return 'ios';
    if (!supported() || !W.checked || !W.key) return '';
    if (perm() === 'denied' || W.mine) return '';
    return 'ask';
  }
  function cardHtml(kind) {
    if (kind === 'ios') return '<div class="w219ic">' + BELL + '</div><div class="w219tx"><b>Hub notifications on your ' + (IPAD ? 'iPad' : 'iPhone') + '</b>'
      + '<p>Add the Hub to your Home Screen first: tap Share, then Add to Home Screen. Open the Hub from the new icon and tap Turn on there.</p></div>'
      + '<div class="w219act"><button type="button" class="w219btn" data-w219="later">Got it</button></div>';
    return '<div class="w219ic">' + BELL + '</div><div class="w219tx"><b>Get Hub notifications on ' + here() + '?</b>'
      + '<p>When someone mentions you, gives you work or posts an announcement for you, it pops up here, ' + evenWhen() + '.</p></div>'
      + '<div class="w219act"><button type="button" class="w219btn" data-w219="on"' + (W.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (W.busy ? 'Turning on…' : 'Turn on') + '</button>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w219lnk" data-w219="later">Not now</button></div>';
  }
  function paintCard() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('mpBody'), el = document.getElementById('w219card');
      var kind = body ? cardKind() : '';
      if (!kind) { if (el) el.remove(); return; }
      if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w219card'; el.className = 'mp-sec'; el.setAttribute('role', 'region'); el.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Hub notifications'); }
      var anchor = document.getElementById('w180card');
      if (!anchor || anchor.parentNode !== body) anchor = body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-online') || body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-chips') || body.querySelector(':scope > .mp-head');
      if (anchor) { if (anchor.nextSibling !== el) body.insertBefore(el, anchor.nextSibling); } else if (el.parentNode !== body) body.insertBefore(el, body.firstChild);
      var html = cardHtml(kind);
      if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  /* ---------- Settings, Notifications ---------- */

  function devicesHtml() {

      + (W.channel === 'hub' && !anyDevice ? '<b>None of your devices has Hub notifications on yet, so Slack keeps bringing them until one does.</b>' : 'In the Hub only: Slack stays quiet for these while a device of yours has Hub notifications on.')

  function paintSettings() {
    try {
      var box = document.getElementById('modalBox'); if (!box) return;

      if (!el) {
        el = document.createElement('div'); el.id = 'w219set';
        var tabs = root.querySelector('.tabs'); if (tabs && tabs.nextSibling) root.insertBefore(el, tabs.nextSibling); else root.appendChild(el);
        /* wave116j's line about Slack now introduces the list that covers both */
        var n = el.nextElementSibling;
        if (n && n.classList.contains('note') && /Slack DMs/.test(n.textContent || '')) {
          n.innerHTML = n.innerHTML.replace('Delivered to your Slack DMs', 'Which of these reach you. On Slack they come to your DMs');
          n.classList.add('w219what');
        }
      }
      var html = setHtml();
      if (el.__h !== html) { el.innerHTML = html; el.__h = html; }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  /* wave138's hook runs after wave116j paints Settings */
  var prevAfter = window.w138AfterPaint;
  window.w138AfterPaint = function (tab) {
    var r;
    try { if (typeof prevAfter === 'function') r = prevAfter.apply(this, arguments); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (tab === 'notifications') {
      paintSettings();
      if (!W.checking && (!W.checked || !W.prefsAt || Date.now() - W.prefsAt > 15000)) check();   /* fresh each time Settings opens */
    }

  };

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-w219], [data-w219rm]') : null;
    if (!t) return;
    var what = t.getAttribute('data-w219');
    if (t.hasAttribute('data-w219rm')) { removeDevice(t.getAttribute('data-w219rm')); return; }
    if (what === 'on') { turnOn(); return; }
    if (what === 'later') { lsSet('w219ask', 'no'); paintCard(); if (!(IOS && !standalone())) say('You can turn them on any time in Settings, Notifications.'); return; }
    if (what === 'off') { turnOff(); return; }
    if (what === 'test') { test(); return; }
    if (what === 'install' && W.bip) {
      var ev = W.bip; W.bip = null;
      try { ev.prompt(); Promise.resolve(ev.userChoice).then(function (c) { if (c && c.outcome === 'accepted') W.installed = true; paintSettings(); }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      paintSettings();
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var r = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('input[name="w219ch"]') : null;
    if (r && r.checked) { setChannel(r.value); return; }
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('#w219set [data-jev="announcement"]') : null;
    if (a) { W.disabled = W.disabled.filter(function (x) { return x !== 'announcement'; }); if (!a.checked) W.disabled.push('announcement'); }   /* wave116j saves it */
  });

  /* ---------- messages from the service worker ---------- */
  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator && navigator.serviceWorker && navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener) {
    navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('message', function (e) {
      var d = e && e.data; if (!d || typeof d !== 'object') return;
      if (d.type === 'wa-open' && d.url) {
        try { var u = new URL(d.url, location.origin); if (u.origin === location.origin) { if (u.hash && u.hash !== location.hash) location.hash = u.hash; else if (!u.hash && location.hash) location.hash = ''; } } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      }
      if (d.type === 'wa-push') { try { window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('wa-push', { detail: { tag: d.tag || '', url: d.url || '' } })); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- sign out turns this device off first ---------- */
  try {
    if (typeof signOutAll === 'function' && !signOutAll.__w219) {
      var so = signOutAll;
      signOutAll = async function () {
        try { if (W.mine || lsGet('w219on:' + myEmail()) === '1') await Promise.race([dropThis(true), wait(2000)]); } catch (e) { /* proceed */ }
        return so.apply(this, arguments);
      };
      signOutAll.__w219 = true;
    }
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- styles ---------- */
  var css = document.createElement('style'); css.id = 'w219css';

    + '#w219card{display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:center;padding:14px 16px;margin:0 0 14px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);border-left:4px solid ' + GOLD + ';border-radius:14px;background:' + CREAM + ';}'
    + '#w219card .w219tx,#w219set .w219tx{flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;}'
    + '#w219card b,#w219set .w219row b{display:block;font-family:"Barlow Condensed",sans-serif;font-weight:700;font-size:18px;letter-spacing:.2px;color:' + GREEN + ';line-height:1.15;}'
    + '#w219card p,#w219set .w219row p{margin:3px 0 0;font-size:13px;line-height:1.45;color:' + INK + ';}'
    + '.w219ic{flex:0 0 38px;width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:50%;background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;}'
    + '.w219ic svg{width:19px;height:19px;}'

    + '#w219set .w219row{flex-wrap:wrap;}#w219set .w219row .w219tx{flex-basis:calc(100% - 50px);}#w219set .w219row .w219act{width:100%;padding-left:50px;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- start: once someone is signed in ---------- */
  var started = false;
  function start() {
    if (started) return;
    if (!document.documentElement.classList.contains('wa-ready') || !myEmail() || !staff()) return;
    started = true;
    if (supported()) register();
    check().then(function () { return sync(); }).then(function () { return W.mine ? null : loadDevices(); }).then(function () { paintCard(); });
  }
  var pend = null;
  function soon() { if (pend) return; pend = setTimeout(function () { pend = null; start(); if (started) paintCard(); }, 250); }
  try { new MutationObserver(soon).observe(document.documentElement, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  try { new MutationObserver(function () { if (started && document.getElementById('mpBody') && !document.getElementById('w219card') && cardKind()) soon(); }).observe(document.body || document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', soon);
  setTimeout(soon, 1500);

  window.__wa219 = {
    state: function () { return { supported: supported(), perm: perm(), ios: IOS, standalone: standalone(), checked: W.checked, mine: W.mine, key: !!W.key, channel: W.channel, disabled: W.disabled.slice(), devices: W.devices.length, endpoint: W.sub ? W.sub.endpoint : null, busy: W.busy, bip: !!W.bip, card: cardKind() }; },
    check: check, turnOn: turnOn, turnOff: turnOff, test: test, sync: sync, paintCard: paintCard, paintSettings: paintSettings, deviceLabel: deviceLabel, here: here
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v220 Phone numbers on the Hub profile (Steel, 09/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "Phone numbers of my team - please add them to their profiles (volunteer, crm, and hub profiles). The same
   goes for new onboardees."
   One record per person: a team member's phone lives on their CRM contact (crm_contacts.member_id), which is also
   their record in the Volunteer Manager, so the three never drift.
   - A person's page (#person/<id>, wave116k's header): the main number after the email, with Call (tel:) and Text
     (sms:), and "+n more" when the CRM holds other numbers. Staff only: board viewers see no number
     (wa220_member_phone returns no row for them).
   - Edit / Add a phone number: the person themselves, HR, leaders and admins (can_edit from the database). Saving goes
     through wa220_set_member_phone, which checks the same rule, replaces the main number and keeps the others; the CRM
     and the Volunteer Manager show it at once.
   - New team members: the database links or creates their CRM contact, merges their older records, carries a hired
     applicant's phone and personal email, and gives them a volunteer record (trg_wa220_member_contact,
     trg_wa220_cand_contact; build/data/wa220_member_contact.sql).
   window.w220: state, load, paint. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa220) return;
  window.__wa220 = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', FLAG = '#B3402F';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var S = { id: null, data: null, loading: null, editing: false, busy: false, err: '' };

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function say(t) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(t); } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ } }
  function dial(p) { return String(p || '').replace(/[^\d+]/g, ''); }
  function curId() { var m = String(location.hash || '').match(/^#person\/(\d+)/); return m ? m[1] : null; }
  function realSection() { return document.querySelector('section#personpage:not([data-w116kdecoy])'); }

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'w220css';
  st.textContent = ''
    + '.w220ph{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-family:' + BODY + ';}'
    + '.w220ph svg{width:13px;height:13px;flex:none;vertical-align:-2px;margin-right:4px;}'
    + '.w220ph a{cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.w220ph .w220sub{font-weight:600;font-size:11.5px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;}'
    + '.w220ph .w220more{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);}'
    + '.w220ph input{font:inherit;font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-text);background:var(--wa-card);border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.35);border-radius:9px;padding:5px 9px;width:170px;max-width:100%;}'
    + '.w220ph input:focus{outline:2px solid ' + GREEN + ';outline-offset:1px;}'
    + '.w220ph button{font:inherit;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;border-radius:9px;padding:5px 11px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid ' + GREEN + ';background:' + GREEN + ';color:' + CREAM + ';}'
    + '.w220ph button.ghost{background:transparent;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '.w220ph button[disabled]{opacity:.6;cursor:default;}'
    + '.w220ph .w220err{flex-basis:100%;font-size:11.5px;color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;}';
  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(st);

  var ICON = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M22 16.9v3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 2 19.8 19.8 0 0 1-8.6-3.1 19.5 19.5 0 0 1-6-6A19.8 19.8 0 0 1 2.1 4.2 2 2 0 0 1 4.1 2h3a2 2 0 0 1 2 1.7c.1 1 .4 1.9.7 2.8a2 2 0 0 1-.5 2.1L8 9.9a16 16 0 0 0 6 6l1.3-1.3a2 2 0 0 1 2.1-.4c.9.3 1.8.6 2.8.7a2 2 0 0 1 1.7 2z"/></svg>';

  async function load(id) {
    id = String(id);
    if (S.id === id && S.data) return S.data;
    if (S.id !== id) { S.id = id; S.data = null; S.editing = false; S.err = ''; }
    if (S.loading && S.loading.id === id) return S.loading.p;
    var p = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('wa220_member_phone', { p_member: Number(id) });
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        var row = (r && Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : [])[0] || null;
        if (S.id === id) S.data = row ? { phones: (row.phones || []).filter(Boolean), can_edit: !!row.can_edit, contact_id: row.contact_id } : { none: true };
      } catch (e) { if (S.id === id) S.data = { none: true }; }
      return S.data;
    })();
    S.loading = { id: id, p: p };
    try { return await p; } finally { if (S.loading && S.loading.id === id) S.loading = null; }
  }

  function html() {
    var d = S.data;
    if (!d || d.none) return '';
    var phone = d.phones[0] || '';
    if (S.editing) {
      return '<span class="w220ph" data-w220="edit">' + ICON
        + '<input type="tel" inputmode="tel" autocomplete="tel" data-w220in data-w198skip aria-label="Phone number" placeholder="514-555-0123" value="' + E(phone) + '">'
        + '<button type="button" data-w220save' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy ? 'Saving...' : 'Save') + '</button>'
        + '<button type="button" class="ghost" data-w220cancel>Cancel</button>'
        + (S.err ? '<span class="w220err">' + E(S.err) + '</span>' : '') + '</span>';
    }
    if (phone) {
      var more = d.phones.length - 1;
      return '<span class="w220ph" data-w220="show"><a href="tel:' + E(dial(phone)) + '" title="Call ' + E(phone) + '">' + ICON + '<span data-w198skip>' + E(phone) + '</span></a>'
        + '<a class="w220sub" href="sms:' + E(dial(phone)) + '" title="Text ' + E(phone) + '">Text</a>'
        + (d.can_edit ? '<a class="w220sub" data-w220edit role="button" tabindex="0">Edit</a>' : '')
        + (more > 0 ? '<span class="w220more" title="Other numbers on their CRM record">+' + more + ' more in the CRM</span>' : '') + '</span>';
    }
    if (d.can_edit) return '<span class="w220ph" data-w220="add"><a data-w220edit role="button" tabindex="0">' + ICON + 'Add a phone number</a></span>';
    return '';
  }

  function paint() {
    var sec = realSection(); if (!sec) return;
    var id = curId(); if (!id || String(id) !== String(S.id) || !S.data) return;
    var meta = sec.querySelector('.w116khead .meta'); if (!meta) return;
    var old = meta.querySelector('.w220ph');
    var h = html();
    if (!h) { if (old) old.parentNode.removeChild(old); return; }
    var box = document.createElement('span'); box.innerHTML = h; var el = box.firstChild;
    if (old) old.parentNode.replaceChild(el, old);
    else {
      var mail = meta.querySelector('a[href^="mailto:"]');
      if (mail && mail.nextSibling) meta.insertBefore(el, mail.nextSibling); else if (mail) meta.appendChild(el);
      else { var pill = meta.querySelector('.pill'); if (pill && pill.nextSibling) meta.insertBefore(el, pill.nextSibling); else meta.appendChild(el); }
    }
    if (S.editing) { var inp = el.querySelector('[data-w220in]'); if (inp && document.activeElement !== inp && !S.busy) { inp.focus(); try { inp.setSelectionRange(inp.value.length, inp.value.length); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }

  async function refresh(mid) {
    var id = String(mid || curId() || '');
    if (!id) return;
    await load(id);
    if (String(curId()) === id) paint();
  }

  async function save() {
    if (S.busy || !S.data || S.data.none) return;
    var inp = document.querySelector('#personpage [data-w220in]');
    var val = inp ? String(inp.value || '').trim() : '';
    var digits = val.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (val && (digits.length < 7 || digits.length > 15 || /[^0-9 ()+.extEXT-]/.test(val))) { S.err = 'That does not look like a phone number.'; paint(); return; }
    var id = S.id;
    S.busy = true; S.err = ''; paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('wa220_set_member_phone', { p_member: Number(id), p_phone: val || null });
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      if (S.id === id) { S.data.phones = (Array.isArray(r.data) ? r.data : []).filter(Boolean); S.editing = false; }
      say(val ? 'Phone saved. The CRM and the Volunteer Manager show it too.' : 'Phone removed.');
    } catch (e) {
      S.err = 'Not saved: ' + String((e && e.message) || e);
    }
    S.busy = false;
    paint();
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var t = ev.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#personpage')) return;
    if (t.closest('[data-w220edit]')) { ev.preventDefault(); S.editing = true; S.err = ''; paint(); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w220save]')) { ev.preventDefault(); save(); return; }
    if (t.closest('[data-w220cancel]')) { ev.preventDefault(); S.editing = false; S.err = ''; paint(); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
    var t = ev.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    if (t.matches && t.matches('#personpage [data-w220in]')) {
      if (ev.key === 'Enter') { ev.preventDefault(); save(); }
      else if (ev.key === 'Escape') { ev.preventDefault(); S.editing = false; S.err = ''; paint(); }
      return;
    }
    if ((ev.key === 'Enter' || ev.key === ' ') && t.closest('#personpage [data-w220edit]')) { ev.preventDefault(); S.editing = true; S.err = ''; paint(); }
  });

  /* the header is redrawn by wave116k (and the older chain behind it): put the number back whenever it goes */
  var watched = null, pt = null;
  function watch() {
    var sec = realSection(); if (!sec || sec === watched) return;
    watched = sec;
    try {
      new MutationObserver(function () {
        clearTimeout(pt);
        pt = setTimeout(function () {
          var id = curId(); if (!id) return;
          var meta = sec.querySelector('.w116khead .meta');
          if (meta && !meta.querySelector('.w220ph')) { if (String(S.id) === String(id) && S.data) paint(); else refresh(id); }
        }, 60);
      }).observe(sec, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  if (typeof wa33Person === 'function') {
    var __pp220 = wa33Person;
    wa33Person = async function (mid) {
      var id = String(mid);
      if (!S.busy) { S.id = id; S.data = null; S.editing = false; S.err = ''; }
      var pre = load(id);
      var r = await __pp220.apply(this, arguments);
      try { watch(); await pre; if (String(curId()) === id) paint(); } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  window.w220 = { state: function () { return { id: S.id, data: S.data, editing: S.editing, busy: S.busy, err: S.err }; }, load: load, paint: paint, refresh: refresh };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v221 The Hub, not "AI" (Steel, 09/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I'd rather not say AI and use another word ... I'd rather it be more personable rather than AI anywhere in the
   hub. I just saw it in the comments under meeting AI." His choices: the Hub speaks as "the Hub" (as in Ask the Hub);
   everywhere except the Admin centre (its model, cost, activity and change pages keep "AI").
   - The words on the pages, tours and Academy readings were changed in their own segments (and parts folders).
   - Who did something: the database keeps its markers ("AI: <meeting>" in updated_by, added_by and raised_by, which
     wa209_task_link and others match), so the pages show them through waWho(): "AI: <meeting>" reads
     "the Hub, from <meeting>"; "Meeting AI" and "Slack AI" (comment authors, renamed in the data and by the trigger
     trg_wa221_comment_author) read "The Hub, from the meeting" and "The Hub, from Slack"; "Meeting AI (accepted by X)"
     reads "X (from a meeting)".
   window.waWho(text) -> text. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa221) return;
  window.__wa221 = true;
  function waWho(s) {
    s = String(s == null ? '' : s);
    if (!s) return s;
    var m;
    if (/^meeting ai$/i.test(s)) return 'The Hub, from the meeting';
    if (/^slack ai$/i.test(s)) return 'The Hub, from Slack';
    if ((m = s.match(/^Meeting AI \(accepted by (.+)\)$/))) return m[1] + ' (from a meeting)';
    if ((m = s.match(/^AI agenda \((.+)\)$/))) return 'the Hub’s agenda (' + m[1] + ')';
    if ((m = s.match(/^AI recommendation \((.+)\)$/))) return 'the Hub’s recommendation (' + m[1] + ')';
    if ((m = s.match(/^AI:\s*(.+)$/))) return 'the Hub, from ' + m[1];
    if (s === 'AI') return 'the Hub';
    return s.replace(/\bAI\b/g, 'the Hub');
  }
  window.waWho = waWho;
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v226 Ask the Hub speaks Welfare Avenue (Steel, 09/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "fix the formatting in Ask the Hub to have more of the Welfare Avenue kind of branded Slack emojis and
   GIFs ... it first of all responds to us in the Welfare Avenue voice skill that I built ... concise, to the point,
   yet friendly ... hyperlinks to things instead of putting out the full entire link ... a nicer UI overall".
   His choice: WA brand emojis, light touch (the :wa-...: set from our Slack), the animated ones moving gently.
   The answers come from ask v15 (fn/ask), which also keeps confidential topics out for everyone (leadership changes,
   board matters, anyone's personal or HR situation, one-on-ones and leaders-only meetings).
   This segment only draws what ask sends:
   - Light Markdown, made safe: the text is escaped first, then **bold**, "- " and "1. " lists, paragraphs, and
     [label](link) links (https, mailto or a Hub #page; a Hub page link closes the drawer on a phone). A bare address
     that slips through becomes a short labelled link ("the doc", "the Loom").
   - Our Slack emojis drawn from the brand kit (content-images/brand): :wa-heart-gold:, :wa-star-pop:, :wa-e-moves:
     and the rest; :wa-heart-beat:, :wa-star-pop:, :wa-arrow-go: and :wa-e-moves: move gently (still for people who
     turn motion off). An emoji we do not have is left out, never shown as code.
   - Sources as small chips under the answer; the confidential reply in a soft purple card; a friendlier error line;
     the first message in the WA voice. Answers carry data-w198skip so the French layer never treats them as
     interface words.
   Wired by a MutationObserver on #wacMsgs (as wave117 does); wave38's send path and wave116o's cards are untouched.
   window.w226: md(text) for tests. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__wa226) return;
  window.__wa226 = true;

  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', PURPLE = '#6F64AB', GROUND = '#1F201E';
  var B = 'https://zwnmulmnvopclzcvytce.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/content-images/brand/';

  /* ---------- the emoji set (our Slack names, drawn from the brand kit) ---------- */
  var IMG = {
    'wa-heart': 'doodle-heart-purple.png', 'wa-heart-gold': 'doodle-heart-gold.png', 'wa-heart-purple': 'doodle-heart-purple.png',
    'wa-heart-beat': 'doodle-heart-gold.png', 'wa-heart-chain-gold': 'doodle-heart-chain-gold.png', 'wa-heart-chain-purple': 'doodle-heart-chain-purple.png',
    'wa-star': 'doodle-star-gold.png', 'wa-star-gold': 'doodle-star-gold.png', 'wa-star-purple': 'doodle-star-purple.png', 'wa-star-pop': 'doodle-star-gold.png',
    'wa-flowers-gold': 'doodle-flowers-gold.png', 'wa-flowers-purple': 'doodle-flowers-purple.png',
    'wa-asterisk': 'doodle-asterisk-gold.png', 'wa-asterisk-gold': 'doodle-asterisk-gold.png', 'wa-asterisk-purple': 'doodle-asterisk-purple.png',
    'wa-arrow': 'doodle-arrow-gold.png', 'wa-arrow-gold': 'doodle-arrow-gold.png', 'wa-arrow-purple': 'doodle-arrow-purple.png', 'wa-arrow-go': 'doodle-arrow-gold.png'
  };
  var MOVES = { 'wa-heart-beat': 'beat', 'wa-star-pop': 'pop', 'wa-arrow-go': 'go' };
  var E_COLOR = { 'wa-e': GREEN, 'wa-e-moves': GREEN, 'wa-e-gold': GOLD, 'wa-e-purple': PURPLE, 'wa-e-tile': GREEN, 'wa-e-tile-gold': GOLD, 'wa-mark': GREEN, 'wa-logo': GREEN };
  function eSvg(color, moving) {
    return '<i class="w226e w226E' + (moving ? ' w226moves' : '') + '" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 30 24" width="20" height="16">'
      + '<rect x="0" y="0" width="4" height="24" rx="1" fill="' + color + '"/>'
      + '<rect class="b1" x="0" y="0" width="22" height="4" rx="1" fill="' + color + '"/>'
      + '<rect class="b2" x="0" y="10" width="17" height="4" rx="1" fill="' + color + '"/>'
      + '<rect class="b3" x="0" y="20" width="22" height="4" rx="1" fill="' + color + '"/></svg></i>';
  }
  function emoji(name) {
    var n = String(name || '').toLowerCase();
    if (E_COLOR[n]) return eSvg(E_COLOR[n], n === 'wa-e-moves');
    if (IMG[n]) return '<img class="w226e' + (MOVES[n] ? ' w226' + MOVES[n] : '') + '" src="' + B + IMG[n] + '" alt="" aria-hidden="true">';
    return '';
  }

  /* ---------- light Markdown, made safe ---------- */
  function escH(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
  function label(u) {
    if (/docs\.google\.com\/spreadsheets/.test(u)) return 'the sheet';
    if (/docs\.google\.com\/presentation/.test(u)) return 'the slides';
    if (/docs\.google\.com\/forms/.test(u)) return 'the form';
    if (/docs\.google\.com/.test(u)) return 'the doc';
    if (/drive\.google\.com/.test(u)) return 'the file in Drive';
    if (/loom\.com/.test(u)) return 'the Loom';
    if (/canva\.com/.test(u)) return 'the Canva design';
    if (/slack\.com/.test(u)) return 'the Slack thread';
    if (/hub\.welfareavenue\.com/.test(u)) return 'the Hub';
    if (/welfareavenue\.com/.test(u)) return 'our website';
    var m = /^https?:\/\/(?:www\.)?([^\/?#]+)/.exec(u);
    return m ? m[1] : 'the link';
  }
  function linkHtml(text, href) {
    var h = String(href || '').replace(/&amp;/g, '&');
    var internal = /^#[\w\/-]+$/.test(h);
    if (!internal && !/^(https?:\/\/|mailto:)/i.test(h)) return text;
    return '<a class="w226a' + (internal ? ' w226in' : '') + '" href="' + escH(h) + '"' + (internal ? '' : ' target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"') + '>' + text + '</a>';
  }
  /* inline: links, then bare addresses, bold, emojis; the input is already escaped */
  function inline(s) {
    var keep = [];
    function stash(html) { keep.push(html); return '\u0000' + (keep.length - 1) + '\u0000'; }
    s = s.replace(/\[([^\]\n]{1,140})\]\(((?:https?:\/\/|mailto:|#)[^\s)]+)\)/g, function (_m, t, u) { return stash(linkHtml(t, u)); });
    s = s.replace(/(^|[\s(])((?:https?:\/\/)[^\s<)]+[^\s<).,;:!?'"])/g, function (_m, pre, u) { return pre + stash(linkHtml(escH(label(u.replace(/&amp;/g, '&'))), u)); });
    s = s.replace(/\*\*([^*\n]{1,200})\*\*/g, '<b>$1</b>');
    s = s.replace(/(^|[\s(])_([^_\n]{1,120})_(?=[\s.,;:!?)]|$)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
    s = s.replace(/:(wa-[a-z-]{1,30}):/g, function (_m, n) { return stash(emoji(n)); });
    s = s.replace(/\u0000(\d+)\u0000/g, function (_m, i) { return keep[Number(i)]; });
    return s;
  }
  function md(text) {
    var src = escH(String(text || '').replace(/\r/g, '').replace(/\s*[\u2014\u2013]\s*/g, ', ')).trim();
    var lines = src.split('\n'), out = [], para = [], list = null;
    function flushPara() { if (para.length) { out.push('<p>' + para.map(inline).join('<br>') + '</p>'); para = []; } }
    function flushList() { if (list) { out.push('<' + list.tag + '>' + list.items.map(function (x) { return '<li>' + inline(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</' + list.tag + '>'); list = null; } }
    lines.forEach(function (ln) {
      var t = ln.trim();
      var ul = /^(?:[-*\u2022])\s+(.*)$/.exec(t), ol = /^\d{1,2}[.)]\s+(.*)$/.exec(t), hd = /^#{1,4}\s+(.*)$/.exec(t);
      if (!t) { flushPara(); flushList(); return; }
      if (ul || ol) {
        flushPara();
        var tag = ul ? 'ul' : 'ol';
        if (!list || list.tag !== tag) { flushList(); list = { tag: tag, items: [] }; }
        list.items.push((ul || ol)[1]);
        return;
      }
      if (hd) { flushPara(); flushList(); out.push('<p class="w226h">' + inline(hd[1]) + '</p>'); return; }
      if (list && /^\s{2,}/.test(ln)) { list.items[list.items.length - 1] += ' ' + t; return; }
      flushList();
      para.push(t);
    });
    flushPara(); flushList();
    return out.join('');
  }

  /* ---------- the drawer ---------- */
  var CONF_RE = /^That one's not something I can share here/;
  var WELCOME = "Hey! Ask me anything about what's happening across Welfare Avenue: tasks, meetings, requests, our story. I can also file a request or add a task for you to confirm. :wa-heart-gold:";
  function render(d) {
    if (!d || !d.classList || !d.classList.contains('wac-m') || !d.classList.contains('bot') || d.getAttribute('data-w226') === '1') return;
    var span = d.querySelector('span');
    if (!span) return;
    var raw = span.textContent || '';
    if (/^Thinking\.\.\.$/.test(raw) || d.classList.contains('w117think') || span.querySelector('.w117load')) return;
    d.setAttribute('data-w226', '1');
    if (/^Hi, ask me anything about what is happening across Welfare Avenue/.test(raw)) raw = WELCOME;
    if (/^Something went wrong, try again\.?$/.test(raw)) { raw = "I couldn't reach the Hub just now. Give it another try in a moment."; d.classList.add('w226err'); }
    if (CONF_RE.test(raw)) d.classList.add('w226conf');
    span.classList.add('w226rich');
    span.setAttribute('data-w198skip', '');
    span.innerHTML = md(raw);
    var src = d.querySelector('.wac-src');
    if (src && src.getAttribute('data-w226') !== '1') {
      src.setAttribute('data-w226', '1');
      src.setAttribute('data-w198skip', '');
      var items = (src.textContent || '').replace(/^Sources:\s*/, '').split(/;\s*/).filter(function (x) { return x && x.trim(); });
      if (items.length) src.innerHTML = '<b>From</b>' + items.slice(0, 6).map(function (x) { return '<i class="w226chip">' + escH(x.trim().slice(0, 70)) + '</i>'; }).join('');
      else src.remove();
    }
  }
  var observed = false;
  function wire() {
    var m = document.getElementById('wacMsgs');
    if (!m) return false;
    m.querySelectorAll('.wac-m.bot').forEach(function (d) { try { render(d); } catch (e) { /* leave it plain */ } });
    if (!observed && typeof MutationObserver !== 'undefined') {
      observed = true;
      new MutationObserver(function (recs) {
        recs.forEach(function (r) { r.addedNodes.forEach(function (n) { try { render(n); } catch (e) { /* leave it plain */ } }); });
      }).observe(m, { childList: true });
    }
    var inp = document.getElementById('w38in');
    if (inp && inp.getAttribute('data-w226') !== '1') { inp.setAttribute('data-w226', '1'); inp.placeholder = 'Ask the Hub anything...'; }
    return true;
  }
  /* a Hub page link closes the drawer on a phone, so the page is what you see */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a.w226in');
    if (!a) return;
    if (window.innerWidth < 900) { var p = document.getElementById('waChatPanel'); if (p) p.style.display = 'none'; }
  });

  /* ---------- the look ---------- */
  var css = ''
    + '#waChatPanel .wac-m.bot span.w226rich{display:block;white-space:normal;max-width:100%;padding:11px 14px;line-height:1.55;font-size:13.5px;}'
    + '.w226rich p{margin:0 0 8px;}.w226rich p:last-child{margin-bottom:0;}'
    + '.w226rich p.w226h{font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';margin-top:2px;}'

    + '.w226rich ul li::before{content:"";position:absolute;left:2px;top:.62em;width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:2px;background:' + GOLD + ';}'
    + '.w226rich ol li::marker{color:' + PURPLE + ';font-weight:700;}'
    + '.w226rich b{font-weight:700;color:' + GREEN + ';}'
    + '.w226rich a.w226a{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:600;text-decoration:none;border-bottom:2px solid rgba(220,165,39,.65);padding:0 1px;border-radius:2px;transition:background .15s ease;}'

    + '.w226rich a.w226in::after{content:"\\203A";margin-left:3px;color:' + GOLD + ';font-weight:700;}'

    + '#waChatPanel .wac-src[data-w226] b{font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1.2px;text-transform:uppercase;font-size:10px;color:' + PURPLE + ';margin-right:2px;}'
    + '.w226chip{font-style:normal;background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.10);color:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;max-width:100%;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;}'

  else { var st = document.createElement('style'); st.id = 'wa226css'; st.textContent = css; document.head.appendChild(st); }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */

/* =====================================================================
   WA v227 Someone to meet this week (Steel, 09/10/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "once every week someone gets a pop-up window when they open up the hub to introduce them to someone that they
   may have not interacted with that's at Welfare Avenue that might be worth ... introducing themselves to because of
   potential work or projects that they can work on together".
   His choices: the team plus the board; the Hub picks the person and writes why; Say hi on Slack, Book a 15-minute
   coffee chat and Tell the other person too, all on the pop-up.
   - Every Monday the intros edge function picks, for each person, someone from another team they have not worked with
     (no shared tasks, meetings or reporting line, no introduction in 12 weeks) whose goals, projects or role overlap, and
     writes a short note and a first hello in the reader's language (hub_intros; build/data/wa227_intros.sql).
   - The pop-up (#w227) opens once, the first time the person opens the Hub after it is written, after the loading
     screen and any other pop-up (the due-date check first). wa227_my_intro gives it; wa227_intro_act records seen,
     later, slack, coffee, profile and share.
   - Say hi on Slack copies the hello and opens their Slack profile (Message is one click there). Book a 15-minute
     coffee chat opens a Google Calendar invite with them, the next weekday at 10:00. Tell <name> too gives them the same
     introduction from their side in their next pop-up. Maybe later (or Esc, or the x) closes it until next week.
   - Nothing is posted or sent by the Hub: no Slack message, email or notification. Off switch: app_config intros_on.
   window.w227: state, check, show, close. */
(function () {
  'use strict';

  var SLACK = 'https://welfareavenue.slack.com';
  var S = { intro: null, open: false, checked: false, busy: false, shared: false, copied: false, timer: null };

  function say(t) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(t); } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ } }
  function first(n) { return String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || ''; }
  function initials(n) { return String(n || '?').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 2).map(function (w) { return w.charAt(0).toUpperCase(); }).join('') || '?'; }

  st.id = 'w227css';
  st.textContent = ''

    + '#w227 .w227card{position:relative;width:min(520px,100%);max-height:calc(100vh - 32px);overflow:auto;background:var(--wa-card,' + CREAM + ');color:var(--wa-text,' + INK + ');border-radius:20px;box-shadow:0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.28);}'

    + '#w227 .w227who div > span{display:block;font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-muted,#5d5c58);margin-top:2px;}'

  (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(st);

  var IC = {

  };

  function act(action) {
    var it = S.intro; if (!it) return Promise.resolve(null);
    return db.rpc('wa227_intro_act', { p_id: Number(it.id), p_action: action }).then(function (r) { if (r && r.error) throw r.error; return r && r.data; }).catch(function (e) { console.warn('wave227 act', action, e); return null; });
  }

  /* the next weekday at 10:00, 15 minutes, as Google Calendar wants it (UTC, compact) */
  function slot() {
    var d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1); d.setHours(10, 0, 0, 0);
    while (d.getDay() === 0 || d.getDay() === 6) d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
    var e = new Date(d.getTime() + 15 * 60000);
    function f(x) { return x.toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g, '').replace(/\.\d{3}/, ''); }
    return f(d) + '/' + f(e);
  }
  function calLink(it) {
    var o = it.other || {}, me = (it.me && it.me.name) || '';
    var q = 'action=TEMPLATE&text=' + encodeURIComponent('Coffee chat: ' + first(me) + ' and ' + first(o.name))
      + '&dates=' + slot()
      + '&details=' + encodeURIComponent((it.hello ? it.hello + '\n\n' : '') + 'A 15-minute chat to get to know each other at Welfare Avenue.')
      + (o.email ? '&add=' + encodeURIComponent(o.email) : '');
    return 'https://calendar.google.com/calendar/render?' + q;
  }
  function slackLink(it) { var id = it.other && it.other.slack_id; return id ? SLACK + '/team/' + encodeURIComponent(id) : SLACK + '/'; }

  function avatar(o) {
    if (o.avatar_url) return '<img class="w227av" src="' + E(o.avatar_url) + '" alt="">';
    return '<span class="w227av" style="background:' + E(o.color || GREEN) + '" aria-hidden="true">' + E(initials(o.name)) + '</span>';
  }

  function html() {
    var it = S.intro, o = it.other || {}, f = first(o.name);
    var shared = it.source === 'shared';
    return '<div class="w227card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w227h">'
      + '<div class="w227band"><small>' + (shared ? 'An introduction for you' : 'Someone to meet this week') + '</small>'
      + '<h2 id="w227h">' + (shared && it.from ? E(first(it.from)) + ' would like to meet you' : 'Say hello to ' + E(f)) + '</h2>'
      + '<button type="button" class="w227x" data-w227="later" aria-label="Close">&#10005;</button></div>'
      + '<div class="w227who">' + avatar(o) + '<div><b data-w198skip>' + E(o.name) + '</b><span>' + E(o.role || 'Team member') + (o.team ? ' &middot; ' + E(o.team) : '') + '</span>'
      + '<a data-w227="profile" role="button" tabindex="0">See ' + E(f) + '\u2019s profile</a></div></div>'
      + (it.note ? '<p class="w227note" data-w198skip>' + E(it.note) + '</p>' : '')
      + (it.hello ? '<div class="w227hello"><em>A first hello you could send</em><span data-w198skip>' + E(it.hello) + '</span></div>' : '')
      + '<div class="w227acts">'
      + '<button type="button" class="main" data-w227="slack">' + IC.slack + (S.copied ? 'Hello copied: paste it in Slack' : 'Say hi on Slack') + '</button>'
      + '<button type="button" data-w227="coffee">' + IC.cup + 'Book a 15-minute coffee chat</button>'
      + (it.can_share ? (S.shared ? '<button type="button" class="done" disabled>' + IC.check + E(f) + ' will see this too</button>' : '<button type="button" data-w227="share"' + (S.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + IC.share + 'Tell ' + E(f) + ' too</button>') : '')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w227foot"><span>A new introduction every Monday.</span><button type="button" data-w227="later">Maybe later</button></div>'
      + '</div>';
  }

  function paint() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w227');
    if (!S.open || !S.intro) { if (box) box.parentNode.removeChild(box); return; }
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('div'); box.id = 'w227'; document.body.appendChild(box); }
    box.innerHTML = html();
  }

  function close(action) {
    if (!S.open) return;
    S.open = false; paint();
    if (action) act(action);
  }

  function show(it) {
    S.intro = it; S.open = true; S.shared = false; S.copied = false; paint();
    act('seen');
    setTimeout(function () { var b = document.querySelector('#w227 [data-w227="slack"]'); if (b) try { b.focus(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 60);
  }

  /* wait for the loading screen and any other pop-up (the due-date check, a meeting pop-up) to be gone */
  function busyScreen() {
    if (!document.documentElement.classList.contains('wa-ready')) return true;
    var gate = document.getElementById('gate'); if (gate && gate.style.display !== 'none' && gate.offsetParent !== null) return true;
    if (document.getElementById('w213pop')) return true;
    return Array.prototype.some.call(document.querySelectorAll('[aria-modal="true"], .modal-bg.open, #modalBg.show'), function (el) {
      if (el.closest && el.closest('#w227')) return false;
      var cs = getComputedStyle(el); return cs.display !== 'none' && cs.visibility !== 'hidden';
    });
  }

  function watchOthers() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w227'); if (!box) return;
    (function tick() {
      if (!S.open) return;
      var b = document.getElementById('w227'); if (!b) return;
      var other = !!document.getElementById('w213pop');
      b.style.display = other ? 'none' : '';
      setTimeout(tick, 1000);
    })();
  }

  function check() {
    if (S.checked) return;
    S.checked = true;
    var tries = 0;
    db.rpc('wa227_my_intro').then(function (r) {
      var it = r && !r.error && r.data;
      if (!it || !it.id || !it.other) return;
      var calm = 0;
      (function wait() {
        if (S.open) return;
        /* two quiet checks in a row (3 s) so a pop-up that opens late, like the due-date check, goes first */
        if (busyScreen()) calm = 0; else calm++;
        if (calm < 2 && tries++ < 120) { S.timer = setTimeout(wait, 1500); return; }
        show(it);
        watchOthers();
      })();
    }).catch(function (e) { console.warn('wave227 check', e); });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var t = ev.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var box = t.closest('#w227'); if (!box) return;
    if (t === box) { close('later'); return; }
    var el = t.closest('[data-w227]'); if (!el) return;
    var a = el.getAttribute('data-w227'), it = S.intro; if (!it) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    if (a === 'later') { close('later'); return; }
    if (a === 'profile') { act('profile'); close(null); location.hash = '#person/' + it.other.id; return; }
    if (a === 'slack') {
      var done = function () { S.copied = true; paint(); say('Your hello is copied. Paste it in a message to ' + first(it.other.name) + ' on Slack.'); };
      try { window.open(slackLink(it), '_blank', 'noopener'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      if (it.hello && navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(it.hello).then(done, done); else done();
      act('slack');
      return;
    }
    if (a === 'coffee') { try { window.open(calLink(it), '_blank', 'noopener'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } act('coffee'); say('Your invite is open in Google Calendar: pick a time that suits you both and send it.'); return; }
    if (a === 'share') {
      if (S.busy) return; S.busy = true; paint();
      act('share').then(function (res) {
        S.busy = false;
        if (res && res.ok) { S.shared = true; say(first(it.other.name) + ' will see this introduction the next time they open the Hub.'); }
        else say('Could not tell ' + first(it.other.name) + ' just now. Try again in a moment.');
        paint();
      });
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (ev) {
    if (!S.open) return;
    if (ev.key === 'Escape') { ev.preventDefault(); close('later'); return; }
    var t = ev.target;
    if ((ev.key === 'Enter' || ev.key === ' ') && t && t.matches && t.matches('#w227 a[data-w227]')) { ev.preventDefault(); t.click(); }
  });

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa227 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa227.apply(this, arguments);
      try { setTimeout(check, 1200); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  window.w227 = { state: function () { return { open: S.open, intro: S.intro, shared: S.shared, copied: S.copied }; }, check: function () { S.checked = false; check(); }, show: show, close: close, calLink: calLink, slackLink: slackLink };
})();

  function I(n, cls) { return '<svg class="w225i' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">' + (P[n] || P.star) + '</svg>'; }
  function E(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).toLowerCase().trim(); }
  function clip(s, n) { s = String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1).trim() + '\u2026' : s; }
  function num(v) { v = Number(v); if (!isFinite(v)) return '\u2013'; var a = Math.abs(v); if (a >= 1e6) return (v / 1e6).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M'; if (a >= 1e4) return Math.round(v / 1e3) + 'k'; if (a >= 1e3) return (v / 1e3).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k'; return String(Math.round(v)); }
  function pct(v) { return isFinite(v) ? Math.round(v * 100) + '%' : '\u2013'; }
  function toastS(m) { try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(m); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function pad(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function ymd(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
  function today() { return ymd(new Date()); }
  function addDays(ds, n) { var d = new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00'); d.setDate(d.getDate() + n); return ymd(d); }
  function dowOf(ds) { return (new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00').getDay() + 6) % 7; }
  function mondayOf(ds) { return addDays(ds, -dowOf(ds)); }
  function dayOf(iso) { return iso ? ymd(new Date(iso)) : null; }
  function hm(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var d = new Date(iso); return pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes()); }
  function niceDate(ds, opts) { try { return new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', opts || { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ds; } }
  function niceWhen(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; return niceDate(dayOf(iso)) + ', ' + hm(iso); }
  function ago(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var m = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 60000); if (m < 1) return 'just now'; if (m < 60) return m + ' min ago'; var h = Math.round(m / 60); if (h < 24) return h + ' h ago'; var d = Math.round(h / 24); return d === 1 ? 'yesterday' : d + ' days ago'; }
  function lsGet(k) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function lsSet(k, v) { try { if (v === null) localStorage.removeItem(k); else localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function q(p) { return Promise.resolve(p).then(function (r) { return r || { data: null }; }, function (e) { return { data: null, error: e }; }); }
  function fn(name, body) { return db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body }).then(function (r) { if (r.error) { var m = r.error.message || String(r.error); try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) return r.error.context.json().then(function (j) { throw new Error((j && j.error) || m); }); } catch (x) { /* below */ } throw new Error(m); } if (r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error); return r.data; }); }
  function copyText(t) { try { if (navigator.clipboard) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(t); toastS('Copied'); return; } } catch (e) { /* below */ } window.prompt('Copy this', t); }
  function mark(k, on) { var x = PLX[k]; if (!x) return ''; return '<i class="w225pm' + (on ? ' ok' : '') + '" style="background:' + x[2] + '" title="' + E(x[0]) + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</i>'; }

  /* ---------- who ---------- */
  function staffOk() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myName() { try { return (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function members() { try { return ((typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false && m.name; }); } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function memberByEmail(e) { e = lower(e); return members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === e; })[0] || null; }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function isAdmin() { return role() === 'admin'; }

  function W() { return window.w121 || null; }
  function posts() { var w = W(); return w ? w.posts() : []; }
  function cap() { var w = W(); return (w && w.cap()) || S.cap; }
  function tagsAll() { var w = W(); return (w && w.tags()) || []; }
  function lib() { var w = W(); return (w && w.lib()) || []; }
  function slots() { var w = W(); return (w && w.slots()) || []; }
  function modeOf(k) { var w = W(); return w ? w.modeOf(k) : 'reminder'; }
  function stateOf(p) { var w = W(); return w ? w.stateOf(p) : { cls: 'draft', label: p.status, note: '', attn: false, live: {} }; }
  function titleOf(p) { var w = W(); return w ? w.titleOf(p) : (p.title || 'Untitled post'); }
  function thumbOf(p) { var w = W(); return w ? w.thumbOf(p) : null; }
  function goals() { var g = (S.settings && S.settings.goals) || {}; return g; }

  async function loadAll(force) {
    if (S.loading) return S.loading;
    if (!force && S.loaded && Date.now() - S.loaded < 45000) return;
    S.loading = (async function () {
      try {
        var w = W(), wp = w ? (async function () { await Promise.all([w.load(true), w.cap() ? null : w.loadCap()]); await w.studio(); })() : null;   /* wave306: at once, not one after the other */
        var since90 = new Date(Date.now() - 90 * 86400000).toISOString(), since30 = new Date(Date.now() - 31 * 86400000).toISOString(), since365 = new Date(Date.now() - 365 * 86400000).toISOString();
        var rs = await Promise.all([
          q(db.rpc('wa225_publisher')), q(db.rpc('wa225_marketing')),
          q(db.from('social_comments').select('*').gte('commented_at', since90).order('commented_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1500)),
          q(db.from('short_links').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(500)),
          q(db.from('short_link_clicks').select('link_id, at, referrer_host, device, country').gte('at', since30).order('at', { ascending: false }).limit(5000)),
          q(db.from('social_posts').select('id, platform, external_id, url, kind, caption, thumb_url, thumb_cached, published_at, reach, views, likes, comments, shares, saves, engagement').gte('published_at', since365).order('published_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1200)),
          q(db.from('hub_settings').select('key, value, updated_at').in('key', ['content_studio', 'link_page', 'studio_inbox_sync']))
        ]);
        if (wp) await wp;
        S.pub = rs[0].data === true; S.mk = rs[1].data === true;
        /* wave277: the marketing team, for the approvers picker (only a publisher sees it) */
        S.marketing = [];
        if (S.pub) { var tm = await q(db.from('team_members').select('id,name,email,department,role,active,nicknames').eq('active', true).eq('department', 'marketing').order('name')); S.marketing = (tm.data || []).filter(function (m) { return m.email; }); }
        S.comments = rs[2].data || []; S.links = rs[3].data || []; S.clicks = rs[4].data || []; S.social = rs[5].data || [];
        (rs[6].data || []).forEach(function (r) { if (r.key === 'content_studio') S.settings = r.value || {}; else if (r.key === 'link_page') S.linkPage = r.value || {}; else if (r.key === 'studio_inbox_sync') S.inboxLast = r.value || null; });
        S.cap = w ? w.cap() : S.cap;
        S.err = null; S.loaded = Date.now();
      } catch (e) { S.err = e.message || String(e); }
      S.loading = null;
      try { if (window.w153 && window.w153.paint) window.w153.paint(); } catch (e) { /* the rail counts */ }
    })();
    return S.loading;
  }
  async function loadConn() { try { S.conn = await fn('social-connect', { action: 'status' }); } catch (e) { S.conn = { error: e.message || String(e) }; } S.platforms = (S.conn && S.conn.platforms) || []; }   /* wave277: the registry */
  function counts() {
    var pend = posts().filter(function (p) { return p.approval_status === 'pending' && ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; }).length;
    var open = S.comments.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'open' && !c.from_us; }).length;
    return { approvals: pend || null, inbox: open || null, messages: counts250(), hashtags: counts262() };   /* wave251; wave262c hashtags */
  }

  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- the page: one panel (#studio), sections by address ---------- */
  function mast(title, blurb) {
    var sec = document.getElementById('studio'); if (!sec) return;
    var m = sec.querySelector(':scope > .w118mast');
    if (!m) { m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'w118mast'; m.setAttribute('data-w225', '1'); sec.insertBefore(m, sec.firstChild); }
    var html = '<h1>' + E(title) + '</h1><p>' + E(blurb) + '</p>';
    if (m.getAttribute('data-h') !== html) { m.innerHTML = html; m.setAttribute('data-h', html); }
  }
  function host() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('studio'); if (!sec) return null;
    var t = SECT[S.sec] || SECT.overview; mast(t[0], t[1]);
    try { document.title = t[0] + ' \u00b7 Welfare Avenue Hub'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var h = document.getElementById('w225host');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w225host'; sec.appendChild(h); }
    return h;
  }
  function go(h) { if (location.hash === h) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } else location.hash = h; }
  function postRow(p, extra) {
    var th = thumbOf(p), s = stateOf(p);
    return '<div class="pl" data-w225open="' + p.id + '">' + (th ? '<img src="' + E(th) + '" alt="" loading="lazy">' : '<div class="ph">' + I('cal') + '</div>') +
      '<div style="min-width:0"><b>' + E(titleOf(p)) + '</b><small>' + (p.platforms || []).map(function (k) { return mark(k, s.live && s.live[k]); }).join('') + ' ' + E(p.scheduled_at ? niceWhen(p.scheduled_at) : 'No date') + (p.owner ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(p.owner) : '') + '</small>' + (extra || '') + '</div>' +
      '<span class="pill ' + (s.cls === 'live' ? 'ok' : s.cls === 'sched' ? 'warn' : s.cls === 'appr' ? 'appr' : s.cls === 'bad' || s.cls === 'due' ? 'bad' : '') + '">' + E(s.label) + '</span></div>';
  }
  function weekBounds(off) { var m = addDays(mondayOf(today()), 7 * (off || 0)); return [m, addDays(m, 6)]; }
  function postsIn(a, b) { return posts().filter(function (p) { var d = dayOf(p.scheduled_at || p.published_at); return d && d >= a && d <= b && p.status !== 'cancelled'; }); }
  /* goals: posts per platform per week (hub_settings content_studio.goals); a week counts for the streak when every goal with a target is met */
  function goalStatus(off) {
    var wb = weekBounds(off), g = goals(), list = postsIn(wb[0], wb[1]).filter(function (p) { return ['published', 'posted', 'scheduled', 'publishing'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0 || (off < 0 && p.status === 'failed' && Object.keys(p.results || {}).some(function (k) { return p.results[k] && p.results[k].id; })); });
    var out = {}; PORDER.forEach(function (k) { out[k] = { goal: Number(g[k] || 0), n: list.filter(function (p) { return (p.platforms || []).indexOf(k) >= 0; }).length }; });
    return out;
  }
  function streak() {
    var n = 0; for (var w = -1; w > -53; w--) { var gs = goalStatus(w), any = false, met = true; PORDER.forEach(function (k) { if (gs[k].goal > 0) { any = true; if (gs[k].n < gs[k].goal) met = false; } }); if (!any || !met) break; n++; }
    return n;
  }
  function overviewHtml() {
    var now = Date.now(), t = today(), wb = weekBounds(0);
    var all = posts();
    var scheduled = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'scheduled' && p.scheduled_at && new Date(p.scheduled_at).getTime() > now; });
    var thisWeek = postsIn(wb[0], wb[1]);
    var pend = all.filter(function (p) { return p.approval_status === 'pending' && ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; });
    var mine = all.filter(function (p) { return p.approval_status === 'changes' && lower(p.submitted_by) === myEmail(); });
    var attn = all.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'cancelled' && stateOf(p).attn; });
    var open = S.comments.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'open' && !c.from_us; });
    var clicks30 = S.clicks.length;
    var reach30 = 0; S.social.forEach(function (sp) { if (sp.published_at && now - new Date(sp.published_at).getTime() < 30 * 86400000) reach30 += Number(sp.reach || sp.views || 0); });
    var gs = goalStatus(0), gT = 0, gN = 0; PORDER.forEach(function (k) { gT += gs[k].goal; gN += Math.min(gs[k].n, gs[k].goal); });
    var st = streak();
    var kpis = '<div class="kpis">' +
      '<div class="kpi" data-w225go="#contentcal"><b>' + scheduled.length + '</b><span>scheduled ahead</span><em>' + thisWeek.length + ' this week</em></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi" data-w225go="#studio/approvals"><b class="' + (pend.length ? 'warn' : '') + '">' + pend.length + '</b><span>waiting for approval</span>' + (mine.length ? '<em>' + mine.length + ' of yours sent back</em>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="kpi" data-w225go="#studio/inbox"><b class="' + (open.length ? 'warn' : 'ok') + '">' + open.length + '</b><span>comments to answer</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi" data-w225go="#studio/queue"><b class="' + (gT && gN >= gT ? 'ok' : '') + '">' + (gT ? gN + '/' + gT : '\u2013') + '</b><span>weekly goal</span><em>' + (st ? st + '-week streak' : 'Meet every goal to start a streak') + '</em></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi" data-w225go="#studio/links"><b>' + num(clicks30) + '</b><span>link clicks, 30 days</span></div>' +
      '<div class="kpi" data-w225go="#socialstats"><b>' + (reach30 ? num(reach30) : '\u2013') + '</b><span>reach and views, 30 days</span></div></div>';
    /* the next seven days */
    var days = ''; for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      var d = addDays(t, i), list = all.filter(function (p) { return p.scheduled_at && dayOf(p.scheduled_at) === d && p.status !== 'cancelled'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.scheduled_at).localeCompare(String(b.scheduled_at)); });
      if (!list.length) continue;
      days += '<div class="day">' + E(i === 0 ? 'Today' : i === 1 ? 'Tomorrow' : niceDate(d, { weekday: 'long', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })) + '</div>' + list.map(function (p) { return postRow(p); }).join('');
    }
    var week = '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('cal') + ' The week ahead <span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225go="#contentcal">' + I('cal') + ' Calendar</a><a class="btn225 sm pri" data-w225new="1">' + I('plus') + ' New post</a></h3>' + (days || '<div class="empty">Nothing planned for the next seven days. <a data-w225go="#studio/queue" style="cursor:pointer;color:' + ROSE + ';font-weight:700">Fill the queue</a>.</div>') + '</div>';
    var needs = attn.concat(mine.filter(function (p) { return attn.indexOf(p) < 0; }));
    var attnCard = needs.length ? '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('warn') + ' Needs attention <small>' + needs.length + '</small></h3>' + needs.slice(0, 8).map(function (p) { return postRow(p, '<small style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + E(stateOf(p).note || '') + '</small>'); }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
    var apprCard = pend.length ? '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('check') + ' Waiting for approval <small>' + pend.length + '</small><span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225go="#studio/approvals">Open</a></h3>' + pend.slice(0, 5).map(function (p) { return postRow(p, '<small>From ' + E(String(p.submitted_by || '').split('@')[0]) + '</small>'); }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
    var goalsCard = '<div class="card"><h3>' + I('bolt') + ' This week\u2019s goals <span class="sp"></span><a class="btn225 sm" data-w225go="#studio/queue">Change</a></h3>' + PORDER.filter(function (k) { return gs[k].goal > 0 || gs[k].n > 0; }).map(function (k) { var g = gs[k], w = g.goal ? Math.min(100, Math.round(g.n / g.goal * 100)) : 100; return '<div class="goal"><span>' + mark(k) + ' ' + E(PLX[k][0]) + '</span><div class="bar"><i class="' + (g.goal && g.n > g.goal ? 'over' : '') + '" style="width:' + w + '%"></i></div><span>' + g.n + (g.goal ? ' / ' + g.goal : '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '<p class="muted" style="margin:8px 0 0">' + (st ? 'A ' + st + '-week streak: every goal met ' + st + ' week' + (st === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' in a row.' : 'Counts what is scheduled or out this week (Monday to Sunday).') + '</p></div>';
